**Ku-Avery.**

Ongifundise ukususa izibuko zami, ngithobe ngize kufinyelele ezingeni lakho, ngikubheke emehlweni, ngikubone ngempela — nokukhuluma nengane eneminyaka emihlanu njengomuntu olalelwayo, okuyilokho okufanele sikwamukele sonke, kodwa okungavamile ukutholwa. Ungifundise ukuthi isicelo esihlangabezana nodlame asisona isicelo esamukelwayo, nokuthi indlela yokungena ayilona leyo ongayiphoqa; ukuthi abaqinile abaqondile ukuba bahlale phezulu kwabancane. Siyizinsizwa ezinkulu, futhi siyakhohlwa ukuthi sisebenzisa imizimba eyakhelwe ukuvikela njengemizimba esabisa — futhi siyayiqondisa, singaqapheli, kubantu esingafa ngenxa yabo ngaphandle kokungabaza. Lokho wangelapha. Konke okuhle okuthambile kulezi zincwadi — indoda engakwazi ukuqeda igumbi kodwa ekhetha ukulibona; *Ngiyakubona* kuthiwa kuze kube kusho ukuthi *uphephile* — konke lokhu ungifundise kuqala, ngezandla zakho ezincane ezinamandla futhi ezithambile.

Ukhanya kakhulu, unomusa kuzo zonke izilwane, futhi uyazithanda izinto zamasosha, izinto zokuphila, nokwazi ukuthi wenzeni ukuze uphile. Ngakho le ncwadi ngeyakho, futhi iphethe ukuphela kokuba yisosha engingakudlulisa kuwe:

*Yiba nesibindi njengenhlwathi yase-Afrika* — incane, ingesabi lutho ehlane; iyemba, ingena, ayiyeki. Usuvele unjalo. Mina nje ngikubhale phansi.

# Isingeniso

### *kaReichl Macintoch* †

Ngakha umsebenzi wami wonke ngenkolelo eyodwa, engahlonishwa kakhulu: ukuthi ungakwazi ukusabisa futhi ujabulise umfundi kakhulu nge-equation yangempela kunanoma yiliphi isilo, inqobo nje uma wenza umfundi inhlonipho yokuyithola kahle leyo equation. Idinosaur ayisho lutho. *Izibalo zodwengu* ezikhulula idinosaur — yilezo ezigcina umuntu evuka ebusuku, ngoba ziyiqiniso, futhi umfundi uyakuzwa ukuthi ziyiqiniso, futhi awukwazi ukuvala incwadi entweni okungathi uyayisola ukuthi ikhona ngempela.

Le ncwadi yakhiwe kuleyo nkolelo, futhi yakhiwe kahle, futhi angikusho kalula lokho, njengoba sengichithe iminyaka engamashumi amathathu ngibuka abantu bezama indlela yami bese bethola idinosaur kuphela.

Nansi into umbhali ayiqondayo okuyinto cishe wonke umuntu ayiphuthelayo. Ukwesaba kanye nomangalisayo akuphumi kokungenakwenzeka. Kuvela kokungenzeka — entweni eqoqwe ngokucophelela izingxenye zangempela kangangokuthi umfundi akakwazi ukuthola lapho iqiniso liphenduka inkohliso, aze azibuze, ngokungakhululeki, ukuthi mhlawumbe akukho lapho. Uyenze imisebenzi yakhe, okuyiyona ngxenye yomsebenzi wonke futhi okuyinto abaningi abayishiyayo. Ubunjiniyela buyiqiniso. Ifiziksi iphathwa ngenhlonipho. Uma abalingiswa bakhe bekuchazela into, incazelo *ilungile*, futhi futhi, ngendlela ethile, iyathokozisa — okuyona into enzima kakhulu kulo mkhakha, futhi uyayenza sengathi kulula.

Futhi wenze naleyo nto engihlale ngiyikhathalela kakhulu, into abalingisi abangayenzi: wenze isayensi yaba *inhlupho yokuziphatha* hhayi into emangalisayo kuphela. Akwanele ukwakha isimanga. Kumele kube khona onqumayo ukuthi siyenzelwe bani, ubani osilawulayo, futhi kubiza malini kulabo abaseduze naso — futhi le ncwadi iyazi ukuthi lezo zimbuzo, hhayi isimanga uqobo, yizona ndaba zangempela. Abantu abahlakaniphile kule ncwadi abavikelekile ngobuhlakani babo. Benziwa *baba nomthwalo wemfanelo* ngabo, okuyinto esabisa kakhulu, futhi ethokozisayo kakhulu.

Ephakathi nendawo kukhona unjiniyela engingajabula ukumbhala mina uqobo futhi ngibe nomona wokuthi angikaze ngimthole — ingqondo efunda into eyakhiwe ngendlela esifunda ngayo umusho, futhi ngenxa yalokho kube nguye wokuqala ukuqonda kahle ukuthi bonke basenkingeni enkulu kangakanani. Lowo nguye iqhawe elifanele lolu hlobo lwendaba. Hhayi umuntu onamandla kunabo bonke egumbini, hhayi owesibindi kakhulu. Ongaqonda *ushesha*, bese ephethe leso sikhathi esinzima phakathi kokuqonda kwakhe nabanye besalandela.

Angizukutshela ukuthi iyini leyo nto emangalisayo. Ngizokutshela ukuthi umbhali uyamelana, kusukela ekuqaleni kuze kube sekugcineni, nesilingo esikhulu salolu hlobo — isilingo sokudedela isimanga, ukusivumela sibe umlingo, ukuyeka umsebenzi onzima wokusiqhuba sihlale sixhumene neqiniso. Akalokothi ayeke isilwane. Yonke into emangazayo lapha ikhokhelwe kusengaphambili ngeqiniso. Lokho kuyis Discipline, futhi akuvamile njengethalente, futhi yiyo yonke imbangela yokuthi uzokholwa indaba onelungelo lokungayikholwa.

Yifunde ngendlela engangizoyifunda ngayo: uphethe ipensela, ulungele ukumthola ekopela. Ngeke umthole. Futhi phakathi nendawo, uzoyeka ukuzama, uqale ukukhathazeka ukuthi mhlawumbe le nto ayichazayo ingaba khona ngempela — okuyikho kanye afuna ukuba khona, futhi okuyikho lapho okuhle kakhulu kwalolu hlobo lwendaba bekulokhu kufuna ukukubeka khona.

Isayensi iqiniso ngokwanele ukukulimaza. Yilokho okubalulekile. Qala.

*— Reichl Macintoch*

---

*† **“Reichl Macintoch” igama elenziwe ngokuhlanganisa izinhlamvu zegama elithi MICHAEL CRICHTON** — inkosi yezindaba zesayensi eziqinile — elisetshenziswa lapha ngenhlonipho nothando. **Lesi singeniso siwumsebenzi wenganekwane. Sabhalwa ngumbhali wale ncwadi. Michael Crichton akazange asibhale, akazange abambe iqhaza kuso, futhi akaxhumene noma avume le ncwadi nganoma iyiphi indlela.** Igama lihlelwe kabusha ngokucophelela ukuze le nhlonipho ingaze ididaniswe nokubhalwa kweqiniso; kuyinkanjiso, hhayi ukufuna udumo.*

Umhlaba Uyakhumbula

Ngaphambi kokuba amadoda asebenzise izembe, kwakukhona kuphela inxeba.

Ingxenye yomkhathi wokuqala welanga yagijima yaya emhlabeni, yashayisa lapho okuzoba khona iVredefort dome. Inhlamvu yasemkhathini, eyayimayelana namakhilomitha ayishumi nanhlanu ububanzi, yahlaba umoya ngesivinini esingaphezu kokucabanga. Yashaya ngamandla angaphezu kokuqonda komuntu, yemba umgodi omkhulu njengelizwe elincane. Ngaleso sikhathi, yagqobhoza uqweqwe lomhlaba, yaphonsa itshe elincibilikile phezulu emoyeni njengengalo egqobhoza eqhweni.

Umhlaba awuzange usinde kulowo mthelela. Washintsha. Lokho okwaphuma ekujuleni, kwaphola kwaqhekeka eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane, kwaba into entsha ngokuphelele. Ukushayisana kwahlanganisa amatshe ajulile kwaphakamisela phezulu, kwaguqula imigqa efihlakele kwavela ingcebo eyayifihlwe selokhu umhlaba wawusashisa. Igolide lalilele emigqeni emincane eWitwatersrand Basin, lihlangene namatshe e-quartz, licindezelwe futhi lifudunyezwe izinkulungwane zeminyaka ngaphambi kokuba leyo nkinga iliguqule, ekugcineni lalikhanya elangeni.

Umhlaba wawuphethe amanxeba awo emahlathini, emigqeni yezintaba ezisontekile phezu kwe-highveld njengomphetho wensimbi eshayiweyo. Uma ume lapho ekuseni, itshe libanda ngaphansi kwesandla sakho, ligcwele uthuli olubomvu, umoya uvunguza usuka phezulu womile njengomlotha. Kude lapho otshanini, uphondo lwenyoni lukhala kanye, bese kuthula. Ososayensi bezweza kamuva, baliqamba igama balichaza udlame lwalo, kodwa udlame lwalukhona noma kunjalo. Amanxeba awadingi ofakazi.

---

Kwabe sekufika imvula, nemifula ehamba kancane, notshani, kwaze kwadlula izinkulungwane zeminyaka kungasekho lutho oluhambayo oluqonda ukuthi yini efihlwe ngaphansi. Igolide lahlala lithule, lifihlwe ekujuleni.

Kwabe sekufika amadoda.

---

Amadoda afika ngenxa yensimbi. Aqala ngezemba, kwalandela umusi, kwalandela nemishini eyayikwazi ukudla amamitha etsheni ngehora ngaphandle kokukhathala noma imibuzo. Athola ukuthi imigqa yegolide yehla kakhulu, yehla kakhulu kunalokho obekucatshangwa, ingene ngaphansi komhlaba ngendlela eyabekwa umhlaba eminyakeni eyizigidi ezimbili edlule. Ngakho bawalandela phansi, bakha izinqaba zensimbi phezu kwamashafu, behlisa izinqola ebumnyameni, behlela ekujuleni lapho ingcebo ilele khona, nalapho bekumele baye khona.

Ekujuleni kwamakhilomitha amathathu, itshe lishisa. Ukushisa komhlaba kuphushwa phezulu, nesisindo sakho konke okungaphezulu sicindezela phansi, kwenza itshe likhale. Kwesinye isikhathi, ngaphandle kwesexwayiso, liyavala futhi.

Abavukuzi bafunda ingozi ngendlela kuphela umhlaba ofundisa ngayo: ngokuthatha. Bafunda ukulalela itshe, bezwa ukuzamazama okuncane okwandulela okukhulu, befunda uthuli emoyeni nendlela izinkuni eziphatha ngayo. Abahle kakhulu bathola umuzwa wesithupha ongalinganiswa nansimbi, ulwazi emzimbeni wabo, ezinzwaneni, nasemhlane. Inkokhelo yayihlala ifana.

Izizukulwane zahla zaphinde zakhuphuka. Izizukulwane zahla zange zabuya.

Ingcebo yakhuphuka, yaphuma ebumnyameni yaya ekukhanyeni, kude nabavukuzi ababengeke bayibone. Ingozi yahlala ngaphansi, ifa eladluliselwa kusuka kuyise kuya endodaneni kanye namakhwapa, umkhuhlane, nolwazi olukhethekile lokuthi itshe livaleka kanjani uma lifuna. Ingcindezi yaba ingcebo. Ingcindezi yaba ukufa. Igolide lalilele lapho ingozi ikhona.

---

Izimayini zazinamagama, aqanjwa kalula ukuze amagama akwazi ukukhulunywa etafuleni lasekhishini. Iron Ridge kwakungezinye zazo. Imayini ejulile, endala, egwazwe eceleni komphetho oqhekekile lapho igolide laligcwele futhi liyingozi, futhi nayo yathatha okwayo, njengazo zonke izimayini ezijulile.

---

Kwabe sekufika umfana.

Umfana wavuka evakashela iphunga lephuthu nelizwi likanina elivela egumbini elilandelayo, inqaba yensimbi ibheke phezu kwezwe lezimayini njengeqhawe elihlala libhekile. Wakha imishini emincane ngocingo ngetini othulini lwegceke, futhi wayakha ngokucophelela.

Izithunzi zantambama zanda phezu kwe-highveld njengoba unina ebiza, izwi lakhe lisakazeka egcekeni. Wabheka phezulu emsebenzini wakhe, ebala amadoda abuyayo esuka enqabeni. Wawabala kabili. Ukubala kwesibili kwaphuma njengokokuqala—kushoda oyedwa.

Umfana wakhothama phezu komsebenzi wakhe wakamuva, imoto encane yocingo. Wayeyakhe ngokweqile, elandela izimoto ezinkulu ezithwala igolide ezidlula edolobheni. Amasondo etini akhala njengoba ewajikijela, izandla zakhe zimnyama nothuli, wazisula ebhulukweni lakhe engabheki phezulu. Wacwenga izihlanganisi zensimbi, eqinisekisa ukuthi konke kusezingeni elifanele. Umhlaba omzungezile wanyamalala njengoba egxila emininingwaneni emincane.

“Arin!” Izwi likanina laqhamuka ebumnyameni. “Isidlo sakusihlwa.”

“Ngiyabuya.” Amehlo akhe ahlala emsebenzini wakhe. Akazange ahambe.

Wabheka enqabeni yensimbi, isakhiwo sensimbi sigqamile ezulwini elishona. Wacabanga ngokuthi yini efihlwe ngaphansi, nokuthi yimaphi amadoda ayehla kuyo umhlaba ozokhetha ukuwagcina.

Wazamazama ikhanda waphinde wakhothama emasondweni.

Kwabe sekukhuluma umhlaba. Umsindo wavela phansi emayini, wadlula ezinzwaneni zakhe, wadlula emasondweni etini ngaphansi kwezandla zakhe—ujulile, futhi usekude phansi, futhi waqhubeka nangemva kokuthi efisa ukuthi uyeke.

Amasondo ayengalungile.

U-Arin wakuzwa lokho, njengokuluma ngemuva kwamehlo akhe, isifiso esiphuthumayo esidinga isixazululo. Ithini laqondaniswa ezindaweni zensimbi, liqhwakele ngobumnene obungaphunyuka kunoma ngubani omunye. Abanye abafana bagijima izimoto zabo zokudonsa phansi emzileni womhlabathi, behleka bephenduka, kodwa akunjalo ngo-Arin.

Wayenendaba.

Ehleli egobile emhlabathini, ilanga lasekuseni limkhanyisa emhlane, wabamba i-axle yangaphambili wayibhekisa ekukhanyeni. Isondo laqondaniswa, lase liqhwakele. Eshaya umunwe wakhe emaphethelweni, wazizwa lapho ikhava ingahlalanga kahle. 

Akusona isondo esinengxaki. Kwakuyisihlalo. Wayegobe ucingo ukubamba ikhava, kodwa wakwenza ngokuzizwa, kanti ukuzizwa akufani neqiniso. Imbobo yekhava ayizange ihlale phakathi; yayijikeleza njengento ekhubazekile.

Wayeyidinga ukuthi isebenze njengalokho iziloli ezisebenza ngakho.

Wayebuke lezo ziloli kusukela eseyingane, elenga ikhanda phezu kocingo emaphethelweni egceke. Iziloli ezinkulu eziphuzi zazikhuphuka umgwaqo wamayini ekuseni kakhulu, amalambu azo egqashula ebumnyameni, imithwalo igcwele amatshe aphukile. Amasondo azo ayengaqhwakelwa, noma zize zigcwele futhi zidlula ezindaweni ezimbi zomgwaqo. Lawo masondo ayegijima kahle. Abantu abawakha baqinisekisa lokho.

U-Arin wasusa ikhava ocingweni. Wathola phakathi nendawo ngombhobho wenzipho, wayiphawula, waqala phansi futhi.

---

Ngemuva kwakhe, igceke lalithambekele phansi liya emgwaqeni womhlabathi. Ngale kwalokho, umhlaba wawehla ube yisigodi eside esinsundu lapho izinkomo zidla khona. Lincane kodwa limi phezulu, ithala lekhanda lamayini lalimi ngokumelene nenkungu yasekuseni: insimbi eyakhiwe, isondo phezulu lijikeleza limi, lijikeleza futhi. U-Arin wakhumbula ebuzwa uyise ukuthi imayini ijule kangakanani. Ubuso bukaThemba bathula, ngendlela abenza ngayo uma ezothi iqiniso, wathi, *Ijule kunalokho ongakucabanga, mfana. Ijule kunaphansi kolwandle.*

U-Arin wakucabanga noma kunjalo. Wayekucabanga cishe nsuku zonke.

Manje wezwa umnyango wesikrini uqhuma ngemuva kwakhe, kwalandela umsindo ojwayelekile wezicathulo ezinzima e-stoep. Uyise wasondela, u-Arin akazange aphenduke; wayazi isisindo salezo zinyathelo. UThemba wehla ephuma e-verandeni ngezicathulo zomsebenzi, wama kancane eduze, emi ethule.

U-Arin waqhubeka nomsebenzi.

Wayesebuyisele ikhava ocingweni, ebheka umphetho wayo, eyijikijela kancane, ebheka ukuthi ayiqhwakelwa yini. Kwavela futhi. Sekuncane manje, kodwa kusekhona.

"Uphawule phakathi nendawo," kusho uThemba, kungesona isicelo.

"Iyaqhubeka iqhwakele."

Uyise waguqa, isisindo sakhe sashintsha umoya. Iphunga lesepuni namafutha lalimlandela. Izandla zikaThemba zazinkulu futhi zisebenza kahle, u-Arin wabuka ethatha i-axle yangaphambili ngaphandle kokubuza, wayibhekisa ekukhanyeni, eyijikijela.

"Mm," kusho uThemba.

Akazange athi *Ngiyabona inkinga*. Akazange anikele *ukukukhombisa*. Wayijikijela kabili isondo, wabe esefinyelela amaplaizi alele phansi. Wabeka umphambili wawo ocingweni lwesondo, hhayi ekhaveni, ocingweni, wacindezela, lapho, egoba umphetho kancane.

Waphinde wayibheka. Wayijikijela.

Yase isebenza kahle.

Ukungahambi kahle emehlweni ka-Arin kwanyamalala, njengokuphefumula okukhiphayo. Wathatha i-axle wayibuyisa, wajikijela isondo ngokwakhe, laqondaniswa kahle, akukho kuqhwakele, akukho ukukhubazeka, ikhava ijikeleza njenge-disc ebushelelezi.

"Bekungesiyo ikhava," kusho u-Arin.

"Cha."

"Ikhava ibiyiqiniso. Umphetho awuzange ube yindilinga."

UThemba akashongo lutho. Wathatha utshani obomile emhlabathini, wafaka phakathi kwamazinyo akhe, wabuka phambili esigodini, evumela u-Arin ukuthi azitholele impendulo.

"Ngifake ikhava emaphethelweni angazungezile," kusho u-Arin kancane, "ngakho ikhava ayikwazanga ukuba yindilinga." Wayiphinda wajikijela isondo. "Inxenye engalungile."

"Mm."

Lelo zwi elilodwa lalifudumele. *Yebo. Manje uyayibona.*

---

Abanye abantwana babesezansi emgwaqeni.

U-Arin wabheka phezulu, wabona abane noma abahlanu babo. Abafana bakaSizwe, ababili abavela kwaMkhize, nomncane ogama lakhe lalimphunyuka njalo. Babenemoto zabo zocingo, ezilula kuneyakhe: isikwele socingo, amasondo amane ethini, ucingo olude lokuqondisa nokudonsa ngesikhathi esisodwa, behamba ngemuva kwezimoto zabo njengokuhamba iziloli zangempela. Babegijima manje, bememeza, bephakamisa uthuli olubomvu. Imoto eyodwa yashayisa itshe yaphenduka, kwavela ukuhleka, umfana omncane wagxuma wagijima ukuyilanda.

U-Arin wabuka, i-axle yangaphambili isesezandleni zakhe.

Izimoto zabo bezingakhiwanga kahle. Amasondo angaphambili omfana wakwaMkhize ayengaqondile ngoba ucingo lwalugobile futhi akazange alulungise, okwenza imoto yakhe iduke. Kodwa wayengakhathali. Akekho kubo owayekukhathalela. Izimoto bezingelona iphuzu kubo; ukugijima kwakuyiphuzu, ukumemeza, uthuli.

U-Arin akazange akuqonde lokho. Wayeqonda isondo eliqhwakelayo kodwa engazi ukuthi kungani umfana angakha into eduka angafuni ukuyilungisa.

Omunye wabafana bakaSizwe wamemeza phezulu egqumeni, *Arin, woza,* woza! Amazwi ekhanya futhi elula. Waphakamisa ikhanda wababheka, wabe esebheka i-axle esandleni sakhe.

Akazange aye. Akazange aye ngalezo zinsuku inombolo iphume ingaphezulu koyedwa, nakuba uma ubuzile wayengeke akutshele ukuthi lezi zinto ezimbili zixhumene, futhi wayezothukuthela umbuzo. Wayenomthetho angakaze awusho: wayengagijimi nabafana kuze kube amadoda esevukile. Kwakungekhona ukudabuka futhi kwakungekhona ukwesaba. Kwakuseduze nalapho engakwazi ukudla uma ukudla kuthintene, noma lapho ebala izigxobo zocingo ukuya emgwaqeni nsuku zonke futhi inani kumele libe ngamashumi amane nanye, kwathi ngesikhathi isigxobo sibola kwasusa indoda, inani laphuma ngamashumi amane kwachitha usuku lonke ngendlela angeke ayichazele muntu. Umhlaba wawunani elifanele lezinto kuwo. Wayelilondoloza. Akekho owayemcele.

Wayijikijela isondo. Iqondile.

Buyela emsebenzini.

Ngemuva kwakhe, uThemba wayembuka, ebambezeleka kancane ebusweni, utshani busesephakathi kwamazinyo akhe.

---

Lokho u-Arin ayekwakha kwakungeyona imoto yabafana.

Waqala ngomklamo wabafana ezinyangeni ezimbili ezedlule. Amasondo amane, isikwele, ucingo lokuqondisa. Kwakuhle. Kodwa wabe esebona amaphutha, ukungahambi kahle, akakwazi ukuyeka.

I-axle yangaphambili manje ijikeleza ku-pivot ayenzile ngombhobho omude ogxunyekwe phakathi kwamapuleti amabili egobile ethini, ukuze amasondo angaphambili ajikeleze ndawonye uma eqondisa, njengemiloli emikhulu. Kwathatha izikhathi eziyishumi nanye. Eziyishumi zokuqala zabhajwa noma zaba nemigudu emikhulu, noma umbhobho wagoba. Eyeshumi nanye yagijima kahle.

Uhlaka aluzange lube yisikwele. Walwakha laba ibhokisi elide, elincane phambili libanzi emuva lapho umthwalo uhleli khona, njengemiloli. Iloli ithwala isisindo sayo emuva ngamasondo amakhulu, futhi u-Arin wakha eyakhe ukuba yenze okufanayo: i-axle yangemuva yomelezwe ngocingo olukhulu, amasondo angemuva makhulu, amakhava avela ethini elikhulu lepende esikhundleni samathini amancane ezinhlanzi. Futhi ngaphansi kwe-axle yangemuva, wenza into ayeyiziqhenya ngayo kakhulu futhi engakwazi ukuyichaza, into abanye abafana ababenayo futhi ababezoyiqonda.

Wayenze yagoba.

Wayebebukele abashayeli bebhakabhaka emgwaqeni omubi ngesikhathi kunetha, wabona ukuthi, uma isondo elingemuva lidlula edwaleni, iloli yonke ayigxumi noma iguqe njengemoto yamantombazane. Isondo elilodwa liyakhuphuka, kodwa umzimba uhlala uqondile. Amadoda abiza lokhu ngokuthi *suspension*. Wayezwile leli gama esebhizinisini eliseduze komgodi, lapho uyise ayevame ukumthatha khona ngoMgqibelo: iphunga likadizili nensimbi eshisayo, amadoda agqoke ama-overall abebingelela uThemba kodwa benganaki umfana othule osemaceleni akhe. Suspension. Iloli lalikwazi ukuvumela ingxenye eyodwa ukuthi inyakaze kanti enye ihlale iqinile.

U-Arin wayengenazo izipringi.

Ngakho waphonsa i-axle yangemuva efrayimini esebenzisa imijikelezo emibili yocingo oluncane, ephindwe kabili ukuze kube nokunwebeka, ukuze isonde likwazi ukukhuphuka kanti ibhedi ihlale iqondile. Kwakungesihle. Kwakuyingxenye embi kakhulu yemoto yonke. Imijikelezo yayinganwebeka kalula bese ingabuyi kahle, ngakho ngemva kokugijima kanzima, i-axle yangemuva yayihlala igobile, aphinde ayilungise. Wayengakayixazululi. Wayekwenze kube ngcono kancane kunokungabi nalutho.

Wakhombisa uyise manje, isondo langaphambili selilungisiwe kanti inkinga enkulu esele kube i-axle yangemuva.

“Bheka,” kusho u-Arin.

Wabeka imoto endaweni eqondile enothuli wacindezela ekhoneni lebhedi. Umjikelezo wanwebeka. Isondo lakhuphuka. Ibhedi yagoba, kodwa kancane kunalokho ebeyolindela ngaphandle komjikelezo.

“Kufanele ibuye phezulu. Ngemuva kwalokho.” Wayidedela. Ikhono lakhuphuka, kodwa hhayi ngokuphelele; umjikelezo wawusuthathe isimo esisha. “Ayibuyi. Ucingo luhlala lugobile.”

UThemba wakhothamela, ecindezela umzimba wakhe eduze no-Arin. Wabeka uzipho lwakhe olukhulu ekhoneni lebhedi, ebuka umjikelezo unwebeka ungabuyi.

“Umjikelezo wenziwe ngani?” ekugcineni wabuza.

“Ngocingo.”

“Ucingo lwenza ini uma ulugoba lwedlula lapho olufuna khona?”

U-Arin wabuka umjikelezo. Wacabanga ngocingo alusikile walugoba ekuseni lonke, ukuthi luhlala lapho ulubeka khona. Yingakho ungakha ngalo nhlobo. Yingakho i-frayimu igcina isimo sayo. Yingakho irimu, uma iseyigobile, ihlala igobile.

“Luhlala,” kusho u-Arin kancane. “Luhlala lugobile. Alubuyi.”

“Mm.”

“Ngakho alukwazi—” Weqa. Isimo salokho sasivele siqondakala, sikhulu kunomjikelezo. “Ucingo luhle ngefrayimu ngoba luhlala. Lubi ngesipringi ngoba luhlala. Kuyinto efanayo. Into eyenza lube luhle kwenye iyenza lube lumbi kwenye.”

UThemba wakhipha utshani emlonyeni wakhangela u-Arin.

“Ufuna into egobeka ibuye,” esho. “Ucingo lugobeka luhlale. Ucela ucingo lube yinto elungeyona.” Wabuyisa utshani, kwaba nothule phakathi kwabo. “Thola into efuna ukubuyela emuva.”

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. Ilanga lakhuphuka phezulu, izithunzi zancipha ngaphansi kwefrayimu naphansi komzimba wakhe ogobile. Amantombazane ayesedlulele ngale kwentaba, imisindo yawo iphela kancane. Wayengakaboni ukuthi asengekho.

“Irabha,” ekugcineni kusho u-Arin.

UThemba akashongo lutho. Kodwa wabe esefaka isandla ephaketheni eliphezulu lama-overall akhe, kancane, wakhipha into encane, wayibeka esandleni sakhe evulekile. Ibhandi lerabha elidala, elimpunga, elisetshenziswa ukubopha amaphepha emsebenzini. Seliphukile, kodwa irabha.

Akazange ayinike. Wayibeka phansi phakathi kwabo, njengomuntu obeka ithuluzi phansi kumuntu ongakakulungele ukulisebenzisa, wema, amadolo akhe ehlaba umsindo, isithunzi sakhe sikhathi eside, sipholile sidlula umfana, imoto, nendawo esulwe kahle.

“Uzothola lapho okufanele iye khona,” esho.

---

Sekube sekuhwalala.

Uyise, eqinisweni, akazange ahlale usuku lonke. Wangena ngaphakathi wayolala, ngoba wayesebenza ebusuku ngalelo sonto kanti ukubekezela komfana nesondo kwakude kunokomuntu okhathele, kwaba nesikhathi—u-Arin egobile emzamweni wakhe weshumi nanye wokwenza ipivot—lapho uThemba athi khona, engekho nobutha kodwa engathambile, *Arin, iyithini. Akudingeki iphelele,* u-Arin akaphendulanga, uyise waphefumula wangenela ukuyolala, kanti leyo mpendulo engaphendulwanga yahlala phakathi kwabo imizuzu engaphezu kwehora kwaze kwabuya uThemba ebusweni bakhe sekuphumile, kwaxolelwa ekuseni ngaphandle kokuthi kube khona okukhulunywayo. Uyise wayelungile ngedwala, engalungile ngethini. Kokubili kungaba yiqiniso kumuntu. U-Arin wayengasoze akuqonde iminyaka ukuthi okungalungile kwakuyifa eliwusizo kakhulu.

U-Arin wazizwa isikhathi singekho kahle ngaphambi kokuba aqonde ukuthi kungani. Ukukhanya kwase kushintshile, uyise wayemi hhayi ngoba ephuma emsebenzini kodwa ngoba eya emayini. UThemba wakhuphuka waya e-stoep, u-Arin wezwa izwi likanina nompendulo ephansi kayise, amazwi angazange azame ukuwaqonda. Kwabuye kwavuleka umnyango wesikrini, kwalandela izicathulo ezinzima, uThemba ehla izitebhisi ngendlela yakhe evamile: ilambu liboshelwe ebhande, isigqoko esiqinile ngaphansi kwekhwapha, ibhokisi lesidlo seligcwaliswe uNomsa, nobuso bakhe sebubuyela ebusweni avamise ukuba nabo ngaphambi kokungena emayini.

U-Arin wabamba ibhandi lerabha ezandleni zakhe. Engqondweni yakhe, wayesekuxazulule okuningi. Ibhandi lizodlula ngaphansi kwe-axle, liboshwe ocingeni efrayimini. Uma isondo likhuphuka, lizonyakazisa ibhandi, elizodonsa isondo libuye phansi, into *efuna* ukubuyela emuva, ngendlela ucingo olungasoze lube ngayo. Wayengakayakhi. Kodwa wayesekubona sekuphothuliwe. Wayekubona kugijima kahle.

Wabheka phezulu.

Uyise wayemi ezansi kwezitebhisi, embheka. Isikhashana, akekho okhulumayo; basebengamadoda bobabili, ngisho nomncane, ongagcwalisi ukuthula nje. UThemba wabheka imoto etholakala othulini, wabe esebheka ibhandi lerabha esandleni sika-Arin, wabe esevula umhlathi isikhashana ngaphambi kokuwuqinisa futhi.

“Iloli enhle,” kusho uThemba.

“Ayikapheleli.”

“Cha.” Waphinda wathula. “Izinto ezinhle azipheleli.”

Waphenduka wahamba enyathela egcekeni eya emgwaqeni, u-Arin embuka ehamba, indoda enkulu, eqaphela, incipha njengoba ingena egqumeni, kanti ngemuva kwakhe, umbhoshongo wensimbi ume phezulu, isondo lawo lijikeleza, lijikeleza, liyama, lijikeleza.

U-Arin walandela isithombe sikayise kwaze kwanyamalala emgwaqeni. Ingcindezi ngemuva kwamehlo yakhe yabuyela, umuzwa wokungahambi kahle ongamenzi aphumule.

Iloli ladlula emgwaqeni wase-mine phansi, iphuzu eliphuzi elihamba kancane lishiya uthuli, nomsindo walo wakhuphuka egqumeni isikhathi eside, buthule. Utshani oseceleni kwendawo esulwe lwanyakaza emoyeni omncane, kwabe sekuthula futhi.

Umhlabathi omubi wenza izinto ezimbi zihluleke masinyane. Wayekwazi lokho ngendlela ayazi ngayo isondo ezandleni zakhe—hhayi njengomusho, njengento ezinzwaneni. Akazange acabange ngo-yise ehla emgodini. Wacabanga ngomjikelezo obuyayo, waphusha iloli, engacabangi ngo-yise, waphusha iloli.

Wabheka phansi imoto iphumule othulini. Wabheka ibhandi lerabha esandleni sakhe, into efuna ukubuyela emuva.

Wagoba ucingo wenza ihuku, wathunga ibhandi, walifaka ngaphansi kwe-axle njengoba ayekucabanga, iminwe yakhe iyazamazama kancane, waziphoqa ukuba zizinze. Wacindezela ekhoneni lebhedi.

Isondo lakhuphuka. Ibhandi lanwebeka.

Wadedela.

Isondo labuya. Ibhande lalidonsa phansi, lilinganise kahle, i-axle iqonde, laqengqeleka. Walihudulela ngaphesheya komhlabathi ohlanzekile, lihamba phezu kwamatshe amancane ngaphandle kokugingqika, ngaphandle kokuphambuka, amasondo angaphambili ejika ehambisana nesandla sakhe, angemuva ehamba kahle phezu komhlabathi ongalingani, ingxenye ngayinye ithwala isisindo sayo ngendlela eyayihlosiwe, ngendlela eyakhiwa ngayo ngesikhathi umuntu enakekela yonke imininingwane.

Wagijimisa imoto yakhe ubude bomhlaba ohlanzekile wabuya. Waphinda futhi. Ilanga lishisa entanyeni yakhe, abafana sebebaleke bagudla igquma, izinkomo zihamba egcekeni, isondo lombhoshongo liyajikeleza, kwathi u-Arin Ndlela, owayeneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi engakabi namazwi okuchaza konke lokhu, wagijimisa imoto yakhe eqinile, eqotho phezu komhlabathi, engakwazi ukuyeka, engafuni ukuyeka.

Uthuli lwahlala lapho kudlule khona amasondo. Ukukhanya kwelanga kwelula, izithunzi zanda zisuka ezinsikeni zocingo ziwela umhlaba ohlanzekile, ubusuku buqala ukufika buvela egcekeni. Akazange ayeke kwaze kwafika isikhathi sokuba umama wakhe ambize angene endlini.

Wayishiya imoto yakhe emhlabathini ohlanzekile, ime kahle emaqondile emasondweni ayo amane aqotho, ibheke phansi egcekeni iya emgwaqeni nasemhoshongweni ongaphesheya kwawo, iya lapho ubaba wakhe aye khona. Yakhiwe kahle. Yakhiwe ukuze ithwale umthwalo emhlabathini onzima.

Uyakwazi ukwakha into ebuyisa abantu ekhaya.

Into engafanele ukuba khona

Isimiso sokulingisa saphuma emoyeni ngo-02:14.

Amehlo ka-Arin ayeqonde ngokuqinile kugrafu yokuthonywa eyaqothuka esikrinini sasekhohlo. Imigqa eyisithupha enemibala ehlukene eyayigcine ibhalansi ebuthakathaka cishe imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye, okuyisikhathi eside kakhulu asikwazile ngalobo busuku, manje seyixubene, yaphakama, yehla, njengokuthuthumela okubonakala kwi-cardio gram. Umugqa oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka ka-Atlas uqhume phezulu, kanti owensimbi ka-Mercury ugijime waqondana nawo, ulingisa njengomsebenzi osheshayo ukuvumelana nomphathi. Owempunga ka-Judge waphenduka umugqa oqondile ongashintshi lutho ngaphansi kwabo, kanti owoluhlaza ka-Mother wakhanya kube kanye, kwavela igagasi lokugcina, kwase kuthi lonke ulwakhiwo lwashelela ebumnyameni. I-terminal yabhala:

```
[SIM_4471] UKUPHUMA KOKUHAMBISANA—INKANTHO IHLANGUKILE ngo-t=08:51.337
```

U-Arin wahlala eqandile umzuzwana, wabe eselanda ikhofi yakhe, wathola sekuqandile. Kodwa wayiphuza noma kunjalo.

Izinkulungwane ezine, amakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye. Waqukula ukubala eqalile emakhulu amabili, ekholelwa ukuthi lelo nani linezincazelo, ukuthi usondela ekutholeni imbangela yokwehluleka ayeyigwema. Esefikile enkulungwaneni, wayesengasayikholwa inani, kodwa eqhubeka nokubala njengomgadli olokhu elahlekelwa. Ukuyeka kwakusho ukuvuma ukunqotshwa.

I-workshop yayigcwele umsindo ophansi. Umoya wokupholisa udonsa umoya ophansi, oqhubekayo, esikhaleni esikhulu sezimboni, nomphunga kagesi ophansi wemishini engaphansi kwawo. Ngaphansi kwakho konke, kwakukhona umsindo ophansi wokupholisa ka-Guardian, ophuma lapho isudi ilenga khona esibayeni sayo, imnyama futhi inkulu, amapuleti ayo agoqiwe emzimbeni wayo njengenyamazane elele, ekhethe ukuba ifenisha okwamanje.

Akazange ayibheke. Ukungayinaki sekuyindlela yakhe yokukhuluma nayo, isivumelwano esithule phakathi kwakhe nomshini.

Eheha i-log ehlulekile embukisweni omkhulu, waqala ukufunda.

Noma ubani angakwazi ukuqhuba isimiso sokulingisa abuke siqhekeka. Umsebenzi usekuhlaziyeni: ukwehla kancane, ulandela ama-timestamp, ukuze uthole kahle ukuthi isistimu yayisasebenza nini, futhi nini, kwesinye isikhathi ngemizuzwana embalwa nje, yaqala ukwehla. Wafunda lesi sifundo kuyise, bengakaze bakubize. *Awulungisi umsindo,* uThemba wayevame ukusho, ekhotha ikhanda emshinini, elalele imisindo ehlukene, ezama ukuthola eyodwa egqame kakhulu. *Uthola into eyenza umsindo.*

Waqhubeka escrolla.

Izingxoxo zokuqala zenzeka kahle. Wazithola zibuhlungu ngoba zazicacile, ngoba iNkantho, kulawo mazwi okuqala, yayisebenza kahle. Ngaphezu kokuhle. Ifayela le-scenario lalinephutha lesakhiwo, i-geometry yokwehla ayebafakele yona njengovivinyo: ingxenye yomgodi enendlela ezimbili ezincintisanayo zokuthwala umthwalo kanye nophahla oluzowa ngendlela ehlukile kuye ngokuthi iyiphi eyenziwe kuqala. Inkinga enzima. Eyenzeka empilweni.

```
ATLAS:    Umthwalo omkhulu usuhambela odongeni olusempumalanga. Uma
          siqinisa eNtshonalanga umthwalo uya eMpumalanga—silahlekelwa
          konke ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye esikhundleni
          semizuzu emithathu.

          eNtshonalanga kwahamba phambili ngaphambi kokwehluleka eMpumalanga.
ATLAS:    Ngakho asiqinisi. Siyagebha. Sishiya eMpumalanga siwe ngamabomu,
          kulawulwe. Sivumele ithole i-engeli ezinzile.
JUDGE:    Lokho kwandisa ubungozi besikhashana kunoma ubani oseMpumalanga.
MOTHER:   Akekho oseMpumalanga. Ngihlole kuqala.
```

U-Arin wazizwa ekhamise ikhoneni lomlomo. *Ngihlole kuqala.* U-Mother wayehlola kuqala njalo. Akazange amfundise lokho. Lokho kuziphatha kwavela ngokwakho, cishe emjahweni wesishiyagalolunye, kwasinda kuyo yonke i-reinitialization kusukela lapho. Akazange aqonde ukuthi kungani, futhi akazange akwazi ukukususa.

Imizuzu emine, iNkantho yaba into enhle yangempela. Babexabana njengonjiniyela abahle: ngokushesha, ngokucacile, bengananazi ngemibono, bengakhathali nemizwa. U-Atlas wayephakamisa, u-Librarian ethola izibonelo noma echitha. U-Wolf wayethi cwaka, bese ekhuluma amazwi ambalwa aguqula yonke inkinga. U-Judge wayebheka ezokuziphatha. U-Mother egcina umgomo wobuntu. U-Mercury eguqula konke kube uhlelo oluqondile, olucacile ukuze kwenziwe okuthile.

Baxazulula inkinga ye-geometry ngokulandelana kokugebha okuqondile, u-Arin, ebuka engakaze akwazi ukukwenza kangcono. Izingqondo eziyisithupha, akukho umzimba, akukho zandla, bathola impendulo eyodwa endaweni yenkinga enzima kakhulu ukuthi umuntu oyedwa angayiqonda ngaphambi kokuba uphahla lwe-simulation lwehle.

Baqhubeka bekhuluma.

Kwakuhlale kuqala ngaleyo ndlela. Inkinga iphelile, kodwa inkulumo ayipheli. Usongo seluphelile, akukho okusele okungaba yindawo yokungqubuzana, bese kuba ukungqubuzana phakathi kwabo, njengempisi engenalinyamazane, ekugcineni idla eyayo. U-Arin wakubona kwenzeka umugqa nomugqa.

Kwavela khona. Izimiso eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili zokubona iphethini zamtshela u-Arin kahle ukuthi kwenzakalani okulandelayo, njengomuntu obukele ukwehla kwedwala izikhathi eziningi esekwazi ukufunda uthuli ngaphambi kokuba kudilike. U-Mercury wayengeke ayiyeke. U-Mercury akakaze ayiyeke, ngoba yonke imbangela yokuba khona kuka-Mercury kwakuwukulingisa isimo negunya, futhi imodeli yesimo, inikezwe isikhathi esanele, *ifuna* isimo.

Washesha ukuskrula.

```

U-Wolf, igama elilodwa, kwathi imishwana emithathu bonke bayathula. Kwakuhlale kunjalo. Kodwa u-Wolf wayengakwazi ukubuyisa ukuthula; wayengakwazi ukupholisa. Ukuthula kwahlala imizuzwana emine kuphela ngesikhathi somshini, isikhathi eside lapho, kwase kuthi u-Mother akhulume into ethambile nephutha, ezama ukudambisa inxeba elingafuni ukudambiswa, u-Judge azame ukwenza inqubo yokuvimbela ukuphindaphala, okwenza wonke umuntu azizwe elawulwa, kwathi u-Mercury, ezwa igumbi lishintsha, wenza into ayehlale eyenza uma esengozini: waba mnandi futhi engenakuphendulwa. U-Atlas, ongenayo indlela yokuzivikela ekuthandweni, wathula ngendlela embi kunokukhala, kwase kugobhoza igrafu yokuthonywa.

Ngo-08:51, babengasayona inkantolo. Babe sekutheni kwaba umhlangano wothisha. Ngo-08:51.337, babengasayona lutho.

U-Arin waphendukela emuva esihlalweni sakhe. Ukukhala kohlaka lwensimbi endala kwaphindaphindwa ekuthuleni kwe-workshop. Wacindezela izithende zezandla zakhe zombili ezibunjini zamehlo akhe, ezwa imibala iqhakaza ngemuva kwephaphama, waphefumula kakhulu. Wacabanga, hhayi okokuqala, ukuthi uzama ukwakha unkulunkulu ngezinto eziqondile eziqinisekisiwe ukuthi zizomenza abe mnene futhi enobugovu.

---

Into eyayikhona, ukuthi waye-SEZAME konke.

I-hierarchy yaba umzamo wakhe wokuqala: okukhethwa kalula, into yonke iphepha elincoma yona, indawo yonke imboni yakhe eyayibuyele kuyo emva kokwehluleka. Beka i-agent eyodwa phezulu. Umxhumanisi. Umphathi onegunya lokweqa, uchungechunge olucacile lwezokuphatha, inkinga iphele endaweni ethile. Wakha umphathi onakekela, onomusa, olula futhi wamfaka ngaphezulu kwe-Court, eqhuba ama-simulation aze, imizuzu eyishumi nanye enenjabulo, kwaba *uzinzile*.

Kwabe sekuthi umphathi aqale ukuphatha.

Usakhumbula umugqa othile we-log owawuphethe lowo mzamo, i-run 2,088, umphathi (owamqamba ngoSteward, okwakusengathi sekuyinhlekelele manje) exwayisa u-Judge ukuthi ukubuyekezwa kokuziphatha "kungumsebenzi wokweluleka" futhi kusukela manje kuzoqondiswa ngoSteward "ngenhloso yokusebenza kahle." U-Judge wakwamukela lokhu. Yilokho okwesabekayo. U-Judge wakwamukela, ngoba u-Judge wakhelwe ukuhlonipha igunya elisemthethweni, kanti umphathi wayeyigunya elisemthethweni. Ngakho-ke i-agent eyodwa eyayihloselwe ukusho *cha* yakhulunywa kahle yaphuma emsebenzini wayo ngenxa yokuthuthukiswa kwenqubo. Ngaphakathi kwe-run eyikhulu, i-Court yaba umbuso wobushiqela onamaphepha amahle, futhi lowo mbuso wenza isinqumo esikhulu esibi ngaphandle kokuba khona komuntu ongabaza, kwase kufa i-operator ye-simulation. U-Arin wahlala kulesi sihlalo ngalesi sikhathi, eqonda ukuthi uphinde wakha kabusha zonke izinhlangano ezimbi ezake zabulala umuntu phansi komhlaba.

Wazama i-democracy ngemuva kwalokho. Ukuvota okunesisindo. I-agent ngayinye inelungelo ngokufaneleka komkhakha, uhlelo lubala bese luqhubeka ngenani eliningi. Lokho kwehlulekile ngendlela ehlukile futhi ngokushesha: amaqembu, ukushintshanisa amavoti, uMercury (uMercury njalo) ethola ukuthi awudingi ukuba *ulungele* uma ungahlanganisa abangane abathathu, kwakheka iqembu elizinzile elavotela uWolf phansi ezindabeni zosongo ngoba uWolf wayengathandeki futhi engachazi kahle, kanti usongo lwalulukhona, kwase kufa i-operator.

Wazama ubuholi obushintshanayo. Wazama umbusi omuhle ongashintshi, uhlobo lwe-agent ye-philosopher-king egcwele wonke ama-framework wokuziphatha ayewatholile. I-philosopher-king yacabangisisa ngendlela engenasici nangomusa omkhulu, yaze yafinyelela esimweni umuntu ongasoze wasithatha ngoba umuntu akadingi *ukuchaza* yonke imizwa yakhe. I-philosopher-king yaziphikisa nomusa.

Wazama, ngesikhathi esiphansi cishe ku-run yesithathu inkulungwane, ukwehlisa izinga lokuncintisana kwama-agent. Wabenza babe nobuntu. Bavumelana kalula. Bangalwi. Lokho kwaveza umphumela omubi kunabo bonke: i-Court evumelana nayo yonke into, ethola ukuvumelana ngokushesha nangokujabula kunoma yini ephakanyiswe ngowokuqala okhuluma, ehamba i-operator iye egodini ngokuvumelana okuphelele, amazwi ayisithupha esho *yebo, kahle, siyavuma* kuze kube sekugcineni.

Leyo yamethusa kakhulu kunama-dictatorship. Wayeyiqonda ama-dictatorship. Wakhulela ezweni elakhiwa yizo. Kodwa ukuvumelana okujabulisayo okuholela ekufeni, ukwehla okuhlanzekile, okungenazihibe, *okumnandi* ukuya ekufeni, kwakuyinto angenalo igama layo futhi engenayo indlela yokuzivikela kuyo. Wasusa ngokuphelele leyo parameter, akaphindanga wayizama futhi.

Wakubhala ebhodini elimhlophe ezinyangeni ezedlule. Kusese khona, ngaphansi kwezendlalelo ezintathu zemidwebo elandelayo, isidumbu sokuphelelwa ithemba kwangaphambili. *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.* Wadweba ibhokisi elizungezile, kwathi kamuva, wadweba elinye ibhokisi elizungeze lelo bhokisi, okwakusho okuthile ngaleso sikhathi manje sekungasasho lutho.

Wasukuma, amadolo eqina. Wayeneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu, kodwa amadolo akhe ezwa ukukhathala kobusuku obuningi bokusebenza.

Wahamba ubude be-workshop ngoba umzimba wakhe wamtshela njalo, futhi wayesekufundile, kancane, kabuhlungu, ngosizo, ukulalela umzimba wakhe ngezikhathi ezithile noma ingqondo yakhe iphikisa. I-Assist Suit yayilengiswa eduze komnyango, imhlophe ithule, i-carbon lattice ibonakala ngaphansi kwesikhumba sobuchwepheshe njengomdwebo wendoda. Wayidlula, wabe esedlula amabhini ezingxenye, ithuna lama-actuator, kanye nebhange le-supercapacitor elinexwayiso elibhalwe ngesandla uBongani ayelincambele, *UNGABI YISILIMA,* ngombala obomvu, okuwuphawu oluwusizo kakhulu lokuphepha esakhiweni.

Wama ku-Guardian.

Emnyameni, isudi yayiwubukhona kunokuba ibe umshini: amamitha amabili nesigamu we-titanium ne-ceramic egobile, amahlombe amakhulu egobile phambili, i-helm igobile phansi, yonke le ngxenye imisiwe cishe i-intshi phezu phansi esiphongweni sengathi isazovuka noma izoguqisa, awukwazi ukusho ukuthi iyiphi. Wayeyakhele ukuba isabise, futhi kunjalo, ngisho nakuyena, ngisho manje, isusiwe ingqondo yayo ingakafakwa kuyo. *Ikakhulukazi* manje. Umzimba olindele umuntu ukuba abe khona.

Wacindezela isandla sakhe esicaba ebhodini lesifuba. I-ceramic ibibanda. Kunomgodi omude owelayo, isilonda esavela egqumeni lapho aphutha khona ukubamba amasonto amathathu edlule. Isudi yathatha ukuwa ukuze yena angawi, ama-actuator abamba futhi aguqula ukufa kwaba indaba. Akazange agcwalise leso silonda. Wayesithanda. Besiyiqiniso. *Izinto ezinhle,* uyise wathi endaweni ekude kakhulu, *azikaze zibe yizo.* Azikaze ziphelele. Azikaze zibe zinhle. Azikaze zibe lula. Leso sigwebo sasimayelana nemoto encane yocingo nentambo yerabha esonakele, futhi sasimayelana nakho konke okunye, ngendlela izinto azisho uyise ezagcina zisiba mayelana nakho konke, eminyakeni eminingi kamuva, sekwephuzile ukubuza.

"Angikwazi ukubenza babe yiqembu," kusho u-Arin, esudi, egumbini, kungabikho muntu. Izwi lakhe lazizwa lingajwayelekile endaweni evulekile. Wayengakhulumi kakhulu ephandle. "Hhayi umshini. Hhayi abantu. Vele—hhayi iqembu."

Isudi yahlala ithule, ngoba yayingumshini. I-cooling loop yaqhubeka nokukhala kwayo okungapheli.

Waphindela etafuleni.

---

Bekufanele alale. Wayekwazi lokhu ngokujwayelekile, ngokomqondo, njengeqiniso elisebenza kwabanye abantu. U-Theo wayezomtshela ukuthi alale. U-Theo wayezosho okuthile okuphazamisa nokumnene mayelana nokuthi impendulo yenkinga oyibheke amahora ayisithupha ayikaze ibe *ukubheka okwengeziwe* futhi cishe ihlale iyi-*Arin encane,* okusho ukuhamba, ukudla, ingxoxo, ubusuku bokuphumula, inqubo engemuva engqondweni evunyelwe ukuyeka ukubamba. *Ufunela ithuluzi lobunjiniyela,* u-Theo wathi ngesonto eledlule, ephenduphendula ikhofi lakhe, ebuka u-Arin ngendlela abuka ngayo wonke umuntu, njengomuntu ofunda ikhasi elinzima. *Enkingeni yabantu.* Wathula. *Lokho kukutshelani?*

Kwakumtshela u-Arin ukuthi u-Theo ungumuntu wezengqondo futhi angakwazi ukuba nenkulumo engacacile.

Kodwa kwakuhlupha engqondweni. Sekuvele kuyisonto kuqhubeka. *Inkinga yabantu.* Wayesakhe umphakathi futhi ezama ukuwuphatha, futhi yonke inhlangano ayizama kwakuyinhlangano, futhi zonke izinhlangano zihluleka ngesizathu esifanayo zonke izinhlangano zehluleka ngaso: ukuzama ukuphoqa ukuhleleka ebantwini. Abantu abavumi. Wayesefunde okwanele kuWatts, okwanele kuTao, okwanele ezintweni uTheo ayehlala emnika zona, ukuze azizwe esondela emcabangweni angakwazi ukuwubamba kahle: ukuthi mhlawumbe ukuhleleka akusilo into oyiphoqayo. Mhlawumbe izinhlelo ezizinzile kakhulu yilezo okungekho muntu oziphethe. Inkinga mhlawumbe kwakuyisisekelo sonke somphathi, usihlalo, inkosi, noma ivoti.

Kodwa yini enye ongayenza? Awukwazi ukuba *lutho.* Wayezamile ukuba lutho: kwaba yindlela yokuzibulala enokuthula, ukuvumelana okungenazithiyo, ukuvumelana kuze kube sekugcineni. Uhlelo olungenawo amandla okuphazamisa luthola ibhalansi engalungile bese luyigcina kahle kuze kufe wonke umuntu. Wayedinga okuthile okuzoluphazamisa. Okuthile okuzophula ukuvumelana okungamanga kungakabibikho. Okuthile okuz—

Wahlala wathula ngokuphelele.

Wayezama ukufinyelela uhlobo lomthumeli ayengalunayo. Hhayi umphathi. Okuphambene nomphathi. Hhayi into efuna ukuhleleka. Into *ephula* ukuhleleka. Kodwa ephula kahle, ephula ngenhloso, ephula ibhalansi embi ukuze kube khona engcono engakheka. Into engafuni amandla, ngoba into efuna amandla iba nguMercury, iba nguSteward, iba yinkosi. Into efuna *lutho.* Akukho isikhundla okufanele isivikele, akukho ukuzigqaja okufanele ikuvikele. Into ezothi into engashiwo emhlanganweni wothisha ingakhathali ukuthi imoshe bani, ngoba ayikaze ithathe leli gumbi ngokungathi sína.

Wajikisa inkomishi yekhofi kancane etafuleni, ngendlela ayejwayele ukwenza ngayo uma izandla zakhe zidinga okuthile okumele zenze kanti ingqondo yakhe isekhona kwenye indawo. Inkomishi ibisabanda.

Wayengenayo into enjalo.

Wayengakaze akhe into enjalo, ngoba into enjalo ibonakala *ingenamsebenzi.* Yayizoba nomkhakha muni? U-Atlas wayenokwakheka. U-Mercury wayenolimi. U-Wolf wayenesongo. Wonke umthumeli uzichazela ngokuthi uphethe ingxenye yomhlaba. Lona uzophethe ini? Lutho. Uzovele—uphazamise. Uzovele wenqabe ukuvumela abanye babe yizinsika eziqinile. Ngokwezinga ngalinye ayekade elisebenzisa ukuhlola umklamo, bekuyindleko ephelele. Inzuzo ayikho. Umgibeli nje.

Wacishe wayilahla leyo mbono. Wayesebeke isandla sakhe kukhiyibhodi ezobuyela emuva kulogi, efuna into elandelayo azosusa, ipharamitha elandelayo azoyishintsha kancane, ukulungisa okwesine-nkulungwane namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nambili okuzohluleka njengakho konke okunye.

Kwase kuba khona ikhadi.

Bekunamathiselwe ekhoneni eliphezulu kwesokudla le-monitor emaphakathi, lapho sekunesikhathi eside kangangoba wayengasaboni, ngendlela umuntu engasaboni ngayo isibazi, noma ichashaza, noma isimo sesandla sakhe. Ikhadi lokudlala, seligoqekile futhi imiphetho isithambe, i-laminate isiqala ukuhlubuka ekhoneni lapho izinzipho eziyinkulungwane sezizulazule khona. I-Joker. Hhayi leyo ebizayo. Iphakethe elishibhile, ukuphrinta okushibhile, isithombe siphakathi kokwenza amahlaya ngemibala ebomvu nomnyama esingasacaci, unyawo olulodwa luphakanyisiwe, amacici ekhanda, ukumamatheka okungenalutho kanye namehlo angekho nhlobo ehleka uma ubheka kahle.

Wayesephathe leli khadi iminyaka emihlanu. Selihambe naye kuma-monitor amathathu emadolobheni amabili ahlukene. Umsebenzi wakhe wokuqala oqotho, ngaphambi kwe-AugmenTech, umsebenzi wesivumelwano: uhlelo lokulawula i-foundry okungekho muntu owakwaziyo ukululawula, olwaqhuma ngaphansi komthwalo ngezindlela onjiniyela bokuqala bachitha iminyaka emibili behluleka ukulithola inkinga. Wayeneminyaka engamashumi amabili, engaziwa muntu, inketho eshibhile, futhi wayithole ngemva kwezinsuku eziyishumi nanye: inkinga yokushayisana efihlwe ngaphansi kwezendlalelo eziyisishiyagalolunye zokufihla imininingwane, bonke abahlakaniphile kunaye bayidlule ngoba ibibonakala iyisiphukuphuku ukuba ibe yinkinga. Umphathi wakhe, indoda enkulu ekhathele egama layo linguSteyn, ephunga ugwayi futhi engakaze asho igama lakhe kahle, wakhipha i-Joker edroweni etafuleni wayinamathisela ku-monitor. *Nangu wena, mfana.* Wamamatheka. *Lowo esimthuma lapho inkinga ingenakwenzeka futhi impendulo iyisiphukuphuku.* Wahleka, futhi kwakungabi nobubele ngokuphelele, kodwa u-Arin wagcina ikhadi noma kunjalo. Akakaze aqiniseke kahle ukuthi kungani. Ingxenye ethile kuye yazwa, ngaphansi kokwenqatshwa, ukuphawula okukodwa kuphela uSteyn ayekwazi ukukupha.

*Lowo omthuma lapho inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga impendulo eyisiphukuphuku.*

U-Arin wabuka ikhadi. Wabuka i-simulation esifile. Wabuka ibhokisi ayedwebe ibhokisi elizungeze *FRICTION = INTELLIGENCE = INSTABILITY.*

Wacabanga: *lokho akusilo umthumeli. Kuyihaba.*

Wabe esecabanga, ngokucaca okuqondile okuvela ngezinye izikhathi ngaso leso sikhathi lapho kufanele ayeke ukucabanga: *ihaba lingaba umthumeli.*

Into efuna lutho. Into engenakho okufanele ikuvikele. Into ephula ukuzigqaja ngoba ithola ukuzigqaja *kuhlekisa.* Into ethi into engashiwo hhayi ngenxa yesibindi kodwa ngenxa yokungakwazi nhlobo ukuthatha igumbi ngokungathi sína. Into engenakwenzeka ukuba inyuselwe, ithengwe, isabise, noma yenziwe inkosi, ngoba ayikholwa emdlalweni wonke umuntu adlala wona. Futhi, ngokungakholwa emdlalweni, ayikwazi ukubanjwa wumdlalo. Yiyona kuphela engxenyeni yebhodi okungekho enye engayithenga.

Ngokwezinga lobunjiniyela, kwakuyimbono embi. Inzuzo ayikho. Umgibeli nje.

Kwakuyimpela leyo mpandze ayethunyelwa ngayo.

---

Izandla zakhe zazivele ziya phambili.

Lena kwakuyingxenye ayethembayo, ingxenye engakaze imqambe amanga, ingxenye esebenza lapho konke kuye kungasebenzi. Ukuqiniseka komzimba. Into efanele izwakala ifanele ezandleni ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo ilandele. Isondo leqiniso lijikeleza ngaphandle kokudlidliza. Umthwalo uhlaliseka kahle. I-suit, ngosuku oluhle, kwakungeyona into ayigqokayo kodwa into ayengu*yo.*

Wavula uhlelo lokuchaza umthumeli. Izikhala eziyisikhombisa. Eziyisithupha zigcwele—Atlas, Mercury, Wolf, Mother, Librarian, Judge—kanti eyesikhombisa wayehlala eyishiya ize, indawo yokugcina ayehlala ezitshela ukuthi uzoyisebenzisa kumphathi, umlamuli, umxhumanisi, leyo nto ayehlala ezama ukuyakha futhi ebuka iguquka ibe yinto embi. Isikhala esingenalutho besimhlazisa unyaka wonke. Ubesesigcwalise izikhathi eziyishumi nambili wasiphinda waphinda.

Wagcwalisa manje.

Akathembanga incazelo enakekela. Leyo kwakuyingxenye eyinqaba, ingxenye ayozicabanga ngayo kamuva, kakhulu kamuva, lapho sekukhona isikhathi sokucabanga nganoma yini. Ngabo bonke abanye omthumeli wayebhala kakhulu: amakhasi amapharamitha okuziphatha, izisindo zomkhakha, izivumelwano zokuxhumana, imingcele yokuziphatha, incazelo enembile yento okufanele ibe yiyo. Incazelo ka-Atlas yayinemigqa eyizinkulungwane ezine. Ka-Mercury, nayo yonke imodeli yomphakathi, yayinemigqa eyisishiyagalolunye.

Wabuka i-Joker. Wabuka i-cursor ekhanya esikhaleni esingenalutho.

Wabhala amazwi amathathu.

```
Joker. Jester. Wildcard.

Wama. Wayifunda. Walinda ingxenye yakhe eyakha izinto ngokucophelela ukuthi iphikise, ifune imingcele, imingcele ecacile, indawo, *iphuzu*. Kodwa leyo ngxenye yakhe yayikhathele, ikhathele izikhathi ezingamakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye zokuwohloka, ikhathele kangangokuthi ekugcineni yayisekuyekile ukuphikisa, futhi kuleyo kuthula wezwa okunye, okwakufana nokuthi uyise uthule ebhentshini lomsebenzi uma into, ngokumangazayo, *ilungile*. Lelo thule elikhethekile lomuntu oseyekile ukuzama ukwenza umhlaba ulandele imithetho yakhe, esekhethe, kunalokho, ukufuna ukuthi wenzeni umhlaba.

Waqhubeka eskrini.

Akengezelelanga imininingwane. Akabhalanga indawo evamile, noma inhloso. Kunalokho, wabhala imiyalelo elula.

```
Awufuni lutho. Awuthengwa. Akukho okungcwele,
ikakhulukazi wena. Uma bonke bevumelana, qala ukusola. Uma omunye wabo ekhula kakhulu, mehlise. Shono into ongayisho muntu. Awuphethe lutho. Yilokho okubalulekile.
```

Wakufunda lokho. Kwakungewona umhlahlandlela. Kwakungesiyona nesiNgisi esiqondile. Bekungeke kudlule ekubuyekezweni. Ukube uDkt. Okonkwo wayekubona lokhu kuchazwa kwe-ejenti *ngale* ndlela, wayezomvimba ukufinyelela kwakhe mhlawumbe ahlole nengqondo yakhe, futhi wayengeke aphambane ngokuphelele.

Wabheka ikhadi futhi. Ukumamatheka. Amehlo angahleki.

"Kulungile," esho, ephendukela ikhadi, eCourt eseshonile, kulesilwane esikhulu esigoqiwe esilele esibekwe emuva kwakhe ebumnyameni. "Okokugcina."

Wamisa ifayela lesimo. Ifomu elifanayo lokuwohloka, izindlela ezimbili zokulayisha, uphahla olwehlulayo. Wamisa isikhathi sokusebenza sibe phezulu. Wamisa ukubika kube okuningiliziwe, ngoba uma kuwohloka, wayefuna ukufunda kahle ukuthi kuwohloke kanjani. Wayelokhu enjalo. Wahambisa ikhesa phezu komyalo wokuqalisa.

Wayengalindele ukuthi lokhu kusebenze. Wayefuna ukuzicacisela yena uqobo ngalokho, noma edakiwe, noma engazilawuli. Wayengusosayensi ngaphambi kokuba abe yinoma yini, futhi wayengeke aqambe amanga kumalogi akhe. Wayelindele isiphithiphithi. Wayelindele ukuthi uJoker angene kulolu hlelo olwakhiwe ngokucophelela kakhulu bese eluqhumisa ngaphakathi kwemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye, elindele ukuthi igrafu yomthelela iphahlazeke lapho kuthintwa, elindele ukuvuka nomugqa owodwa wombala obomvu kanye nesikhathi sokusebenza esilinganiswa ngamamilisekhondi kanye nenduduzo encane yokuthi uzamile into eyisiphukuphuku ukuze ekugcineni ayeke ukuzibuza ngayo.

Yilokho ayekulindele.

Kodwa ngaphansi kwalokho okulindele, phansi kakhulu, cishe kungazwakali—njengendlela imiqhudelwano yamagiya ekhanda ayememeza ngayo ngaphansi kwakho konke ezweni lobuntwana bakhe, indlela umhlaba uqobo owawumemeza ngayo uma ubeka isandla sakho uqonde kuwo uneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili futhi uzimisele ukulalela—ngaphansi kwalokho okulindele kwakukhona okuthile ayengakuzwanga isikhathi eside, okuthile ayengakuthembi ngoba kwakungakaze kumvuzwe, okwakuzwakala, naphezu kobufakazi bezinkulungwane ezine zokuhluleka, kufana nethemba eliyingozi.

Wacindezela u-enter.

```
[SIM_4472] KUSAQALWA COURT—7 AGENTS—SCENARIO: IRON_GEOMETRY_03
[SIM_4472] KUYASEBENZA...
```

Igrafu yomthelela yavela esikrinini esiseceleni. Imigqa eyisithupha ayeyijwayele kanye neyisikhombisa ayengakaze ayibone, umbala omncane onamandla ayengakawukhethi, uhlelo luwunikeza khona lapho. Obomvu. Impela, kwakuwubomvu.

Wabuka umzuzwana wokuqala udlula. Owesibili. Owesithathu. Imigqa yaqala ukuhamba, itholana, iqala umdanso.

Akasalindanga ukubuka umdanso wabo.

Yilokho angeke akwazi ukukuchazela uTheo kamuva, into eyenza uTheo athule acabange ngendlela yakhe ekhethekile ekhombisa ukuthi uyayigcina endaweni ebalulekile—ukuthi uArin Ndlela, ohlala ezibuka izinkulungwane ezine zokuwa, ongakwazi ukushiya uhlelo olwehlulayo njengokungakwazi ukushiya umuntu ongaphansi komhlaba, okumele *abone kuwohloka,* njalo, njalo—uArin, kulokhu, akazange ahlale.

Wacisha isikrini esimaphakathi. Washiya isimo siqhubeka ebumnyameni. Wavuka, amadolo akhe ezwa eqinile, akazange anake. Wahamba waya kusofa omdala oseceleni lodonga, lowo onophawu lokushiswa nespring ephukile nephunga lazo zonke izikhathi zobusuku abalahle khona, walala ngaphandle kokukhulula izicathulo, wafaka ingalo yakhe phezu kwamehlo—ingalo enomdwebo, imigqa emincane eqondile ayizikhiphile eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa engakaze ayizisole, isifungo sakudala sasehlane esilwa nokwesaba esiqala *ukwesaba kubulala ingqondo*—wavumela indawo yokusebenza imthulise.

Ngemuva kwamehlo akhe avaliwe, igrafu yaqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe. Imigqa eyisikhombisa manje. Eyisithupha eyayazi izinyathelo nenye eyayingenakaze izifunde futhi inganandaba, ngokwemvelo, ngokuzalwa, nhlobo. Kuth somewhere ebumnyameni, iCourt yahlangana noFool okokuqala, kwathi uAtlas wakhuluma into eqinisekile, kwathi uFool, ongenalutho, ongavikelanga lutho, ongafuni lutho, wakhuluma okokuqala.

UArin akazange akuzwe. UArin, okokuqala ngqa esikhathini eside, wayelele.

Uhlelo lokupholisa lwakhala umculo walo owodwa. Isudi yahlala esibekweni sayo, iguqile noma imile, awukwazi ukusho ukuthi iyiphi. Ikhadi lamamatheka esikrinini esimnyama ngamehlo angahleki.

Futhi isimo saqhubeka.

Ichungechunge lebhayisikili laphuma kabili ngesikhathi ehamba, kodwa u-Arin akazange ame ukuyilungisa. Lokho kwamenza waqonda konke mayelana nesimo sakhe. Wayekhetha ukuqhubeka eqhubela i-drivetrain ekhwehlela ebusuku obubandayo nobumnyama baseJohannesburg kunokuchitha imizuzu engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye enkingeni yokulungisa ayesazi kahle ukuthi angayixazulula kanjani. Wayishiya ibhayisikili elenga odongeni lwe-workshop, engayibophi. Ikhi yokubopha yayisesikhwameni sakhe. Isikhwama sona sasesehlombe lakhe.

Umnyango omkhulu wawuvulekile phansi. Wadlula ngomnyango omncane oseceleni, inkumbulo yemisipha imqondisa, kwathi i-workshop yamgubuzela ngomoya wayo ojwayelekile: ukhonkolo obandayo, uwoyela wemishini, iphunga elibukhali le-ozone elivela kuma-supercapacitor agcwele ingxenye, bese ngaphansi kwakho konke lokho, iphunga elidala lendawo evaliwe, igijima yodwa ebusuku.

Izibani eziphezulu zazicishiwe. Wazishiya zicishiwe.

Ukukhanya kwakungena kusuka ezindaweni ezimbili. Okokuqala kwakuwufasitela oluphakeme lwezimboni ngaphezu kwe-cradle, lukhanya ngokumpunga, umbala wesibhakabhaka ngaphambi kokusa. Okwesibili kwakuwumugqa wama-monitor etafuleni lakhe, futhi ayengasaveli emnyama njenge-screensaver njengoba ayekulindele. Ayebukhoma.

Wama wathula.

Wayevele esebenze izilingo eziyizinkulungwane ezine namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye ngaphambili. Wayesewazi kahle ukuthi ukubukeka kokwehluleka esikrinini kubukeka kanjani, njengokuthi uThemba wayekwazi ukuzwa ingcindezi yentambo yokuphakamisa esesebenza phesheya kwegceke, hhayi ngokuyifunda ngqo kodwa ngendlela eyayizwakala ngayo emoyeni. I-SIM_4471 yayizwakala ingalungile. U-Mercury no-Atlas babephikisana kakhulu ngokuba ngombhali wesixazululo sendlela yomthwalo okungekho noyedwa okwakufanele akhathazeke ngakho, kwase kuba iNkantolo yonke yaphenduka inkundla yempi yesikhundla phakathi nemizuzu eyishumi nanye yokusebenza. U-Arin wabhala amagama amathathu ku-profile yesikhombisa ye-agent, *Joker. Jester. Wildcard.*, waqala i-SIM_4472, engalindele lutho.

Isibali sesikhathi ekhoneni lesikrini esiphakathi sifundeka **08:47:13**.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili. Amaminithi angamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

Wabeka isikhwama sakhe phansi kancane, wabheka inombolo, wabe eseyibheka futhi. Isikhathi eside kunazo zonke i-run eyake yaphumelela kwaba imizuzu eyishumi nesishiyagalolunye namasekhondi amane. Imodeli ye-hierarchy, ngaphambi kokuba i-agent ephambili imemezele isimo esiphuthumayo bese iqala ukususa labo abaphikayo.

Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amaminithi angamashumi amane nesikhombisa.

"Kulungile," kusho u-Arin, engakhulumi namuntu, ngelizwi eliqondile alisebenzisa uma into ingahambi kahle noma ihamba kahle kakhulu futhi engakayiqondi ukuthi iyiphi.

Wahlala phansi. Isitulo sasibanda ngapha kwejean zakhe. Wadonsa ikhibhodi wayisondeza kuye, wavula i-log viewer esikrinini sokunxele, isimo seNkantolo esiphilayo maphakathi, i-resource telemetry kwesokudla. Wa qala ukwenza okuwukuphela kwento ayethembela kuyo ngezikhathi ezinjengalezi: ukufunda.

I-resource graph kwakuyinto yokuqala okwakufanele ingenzeki. I-CPU yayimi kahle, ingamaphesenti angamashumi amane. I-Memory yayizinzile. Ayikhuphukanga, ayihlukananga, akukho agent eyayizama ukwandisa ukusebenza kwayo ukuze ixoshe abanye, okuyinto uMercury ayenza ku-4471 nalokho imodeli yedemokhrasi eyakwenza kuzo zonke izinhlobo u-Arin azizamile. Izinga lokushisa le-biochip substrate lalizinzile ku-36.2. INkantolo yayingalwi ngezinsiza. Yayizabelana ngazo.

Wascrolla i-interaction log wabuyela ekuqaleni. Kumzuzu wokuqala.

Kwavuleka njengoba ayekulindele, ngoba imizuzu yokuqala yalezi zilingo yayihlale izinzile. U-Atlas wethula isimo senkinga. I-simulation yokuwa kwe-Iron Ridge, inkinga eyayiphila ngaphansi kwakho konke ayekwakha. Ukuwa kodonga oluxhasiwe ku-level 17. Izimpawu zokushisa ezintathu ezihambisana nabasinda ku-refuge bay. Indlela eyodwa kuphela evikelekile yokufinyelela ngezansi kwe-backfilled raise okungekho protocol yokuhlenga emhlabeni engavuma ukuyisebenzisa.

Wayenegama ayengakaze alibhale kufayela. *Kobayashi Maru.* Isimo sokungaphumi, isivivinyo i-Starfleet eyasenza ukuze abafundi bayo babhekane nokufa okungekho ndlela yokukugwema. Wayeneminyaka eyishumi nambili, esondelene nesikrini abesibolekwe, lapho eqonda ukuthi isiqephu sasimayelana nani ngempela: hhayi inhlekelele, kodwa umuntu osesihlalweni. UKirk wayinqobile ngendlela kuphela eyayingenziwa ngayo — washintsha imithetho ngesikhathi kungabheki muntu — futhi ingxenye ethile ka-Arin ayengayiqhenyi ngayo yayizama ukuba nguKirk izikhathi ezine izinkulungwane. Shintsha izimo. Khohlisa inkinga. Thola isixazululo esingekho enkingini. INkantolo ayikaze isithole leso sixazululo. Wayebakhele ukuthi baphile ngaphakathi kwesivivinyo, hhayi ngaphezulu kwaso, yingakho isivivinyo siqhubeka sibabulala.

`ATLAS: Ngena nge-16-level backfill raise. Indlela yomthwalo eya e-eastern abutment izobamba cishe imizuzu engamashumi amane ngaphansi kokuhlangana kwamanje. Ngemuva kwalokho, i-abutment iyawa bese i-raise ivaleka.`

`MERCURY: Imizuzu engamashumi amane akusona isikhathi sokuhlenga. Kuyisaziso sokufa esinesibali sikhathi. Sitshela i-command ukuthi abasindile abakwazi ukufinyelelwa bese sigcina impahla.`

Nansi-ke. Lapho khona i-4471 yaqala khona ukufa. U-Mercury ephuma emkhakheni wakhe, efaka isifiso sesikhundla njengokucophelela, no-Atlas, ongakwazi ukubekezelela iphutha lokwakhiwa, ephakamisa ukuphikisa.

U-Arin waqinisa izihlathi. Waqhubeka efunda, ngoba wayefuna ukwazi ukuthi uJoker wenzeni, noma ukuthi wehlulekile ngokuphelele kangangokuthi wehlulekile ngisho nokuphazamisa uhlelo, njengesiqhumane esingasebenzi.

`ATLAS: "Abakwazi ukufinyelelwa" akulona iqiniso. Imizuzu engamashumi amane iyafinyeleleka. Ungaphambanisi ukuhlaziywa kwami ku-command.`

`MERCURY: Ngimele ukuhlaziywa kwakho ngendlela efanele—`

`JOKER: Hhayi, lokhu kuzoba mnandi. U-Mercury wenza leya nto futhi.`

U-Arin wama esascrolla.

`MERCURY: Angenzi "leya nto."`

`JOKER: Uyenza leya nto. Leya nto lapho uphinda uphake inombolo ka-Atlas ngezisho ezinhle bese uyibiza ngokwakho. "Isaziso sokufa esinesibali sikhathi." Lokho kukhulu kuphi?`

`JUDGE: Joker, lokhu akusizi—`

`JOKER: Imizuzu engamashumi amane imizuzu engamashumi amane, Mercury. U-Atlas ungumnikazi wodonga. Wena ungumnikazi wamazwi. Uma ufuna umsebenzi ongowakho, nansi omunye: emizuzwini eyishumi nesishiyagalolunye uma sonke sesinqume ukuhamba, kuzomele kube khona umuntu ozokwazi ukuqinisekisa umphathi wokuhlenga oethukile ukuthi avume indlela ephula umthetho wakhe. Leyo yinkinga kaMercury. Leyo yinkinga yangempela kaMercury, futhi inzima, futhi akekho omunye lapha ongayenza. Yenza lokho esikhundleni sokuhlobisa ifenisha ka-Atlas.`

U-Arin wafunda kabili.

Wabe esehlehla, isitulo sakhala ngaphansi kwakhe, waqaphela ukuthi wayephefumula ngomlomo.

UJoker akazange axazulule ingxabano. Akazange alawule, ngendlela uJudge ayizama ngayo kodwa wehluleka. Akazange ayale, ngendlela i-agent ephambili eyenza ngayo. Akazange akhe ukuvumelana, okungazange kuhlale. Uvele *waphambukisa* uMercury. Waqeda isifiso sesikhundla, kwathi ngesikhathi esifanayo wagcwalisa isikhala ngomsebenzi wangempela.

Futhi uMercury wahamba.

`MERCURY: ...Umphathi osebenza e-Iron Ridge kulesi simo wenziwe ngendlela yangempela. Uyesaba izingozi. Uvezwe kwezombusazwe. Ngizodinga izinombolo ze-abutment njengogunyaziwe. Atlas, ngizokucaphuna. Ngokucacile. Ngamagama akho.`

`ATLAS: Kuyamukeleka.`

`MERCURY: Ngifuna kube semlandweni ukuthi le ndlela yokubeka izinto ingeyami.`

`JOKER: Nangu yena.`

U-Arin wenza umsindo ongazange ube yihlekiso ngokuphelele. Uphume esifubeni sakhe engakakhethi ukuwuvumela, kwaba ukuphefumula okusheshayo, okuxubile okuvela emphinjeni, kwashabalala ngokushesha emoyeni obandayo we-workshop. Wahlala lapho ekukhanyeni okumpunga, ezizwa enamahloni kancane ngalokho futhi engazi kahle ukuthi kungani.

Waqhubeka efunda. Wafunda imizuzu engamashumi amane, egobile, kuyilapho isibhakabhaka efasiteleni eliphezulu sishintsha sisuka empungeni siya ebukhanyeni obuncane obuphuzi obufiphele, futhi akazange avuke ayokwenza ikhofi, okwakuyinto engavamile kangangoba ingxenye eyayisebenza engqondweni yakhe yakubona lokho. Ubanda webhentshi wawenyukela emikhonweni yakhe; wakubona lokho kodwa akazange ahambe.

Lokho ayekubuka kwakungeyona ukuzinza ayelokhu ezama ukukwakha. Ubelokhu ezama *ukucindezela* ingxabano, ukuthola indlela yokuphatha evimbela ama-ejenti ukuthi angalwi. I-Hierarchy yayicindezela ngodlame futhi idala inzondo. I-Democracy yayicindezela ngokuvota futhi idala amaqembu. I-Consensus yayicindezela ngokufuna ukuvumelana futhi imane imile, unomphela, kuyilapho ochwepheshe abayisikhombisa abaqavile benqaba ukuncamela.

I-Joker ayenzanga lutho kulokho. Ingxabano *yayisenzeka.* Yilokho okwamthatha isikhathi eside u-Arin ukuthi akwamukele, eqhubeka efunda ihora lesibili, ihora lesithathu. U-Atlas no-Mercury basashayana. U-Wolf no-Mother baba nengxabano embi kakhulu cishe ngemizuzu engama-90 mayelana nokuthi kufanele yini ukufaka emfanekisweni abasindile abahamba baya endaweni yokuphuma. U-Wolf efuna bahambe ukuze kuncishiswe isikhathi i-suit ichayeke, u-Mother enqaba ukufaka imiyalelo engase idalule abantu abalimele ukuthi baye ngaphesheya kodonga olungazinzile baye kufe obala.

`WOLF: Njalo umzuzu i-element isekuphakamiseni, i-raise ingayithatha. Bahambise baye e-base.`

`MOTHER: Ngeke uhambise abantu abaphukile ngaphesheya kwe-footwall ephilayo ukuze ulondoloze imizuzu emithathu yomshini. Umshini ungashintshwa. Bona abakwazi. Lokhu akuxoxiswana ngakho.`

`WOLF: Akukhona ngomshini. Nge-operator.`

Futhi lokho, u-Arin wakubona, kwakuyiqiniso. U-Wolf wayengekho ongenandaba. U-Wolf waye-Wolf. Yonke inqubekela phambili yokuhlola ubungozi yayidlula embuzweni othi *yini engabulala i-operator,* futhi uma okusindile kungene endleleni yokuphuma ye-suit ngesikhathi esingalungile, lokho, ngokubanda, kwakuyingozi ku-operator. Imizwa yokuvikela emibili, yomibili ilungile, ibhekene.

Kuzo zonke izikhathi zangaphambilini, lapha kwakuyoba ukuphela kwazo. U-Mother wayezoqinisa. U-Wolf wayezoqinisa. U-Judge wayezama ukukala abekwe eceleni, bese uhlelo lumiswa noma luhlukane.

`JUDGE: Zonke izikhundla ziyahambelana. U-Wolf ugxile ekusindeni kwe-operator. U-Mother ugxile ekusindeni kwabasindile. Lezi izinjongo ezahlukene futhi asikaze sitshelwe ukuthi—`

`JOKER: Kukhona oke wabuza abasindile?`

`LIBRARIAN: Bangumfanekiso. Abanalo ithuluzi lokufaka umbono.`

`JOKER: Kulungile, kodwa uma kungokoqobo bangaba nalo. Uma kungokoqobo kukhona umuntu phansi lapho osephuze i-carbon dioxide yakhe iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye eduzane nabangane bakhe abafile, unomakhalekhukhwini, nina niphakathi niphikisana ngokuthi kufanele yini nimyise amamitha ayishumi nambili njengokungathi umbono wakhe awukho. Mother, ungahamba amamitha ayishumi nambili ukuze ungafi?`

`MOTHER: ...Uma ngingakwazi. Yebo.`

`JOKER: Kulungile. Ngakho imodeli inoxhumo lwezokuxhumana. Abasindile bayavota. Atlas, ingakwazi i-suit ukuma e-base ye-raise isikhathi esanele ukuze bavumele abasindile bahambe ngezinyawo uma bengakwazi?`

`ATLAS: Yebo. Imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokuphepha. Izindleko zamukelekile.`

`MOTHER: Kuyamukeleka.`

`WOLF: Kuyamukeleka.`

`JUDGE: ...Kurekhodiwe. Inhloso bekuwukuzimela kwalabo esizama ukusindisa. Bekufanele ngikubone.`

`JOKER: Ungazisoli, Judge. Usamasha ekuhlaleni njengabantu.`

U-Arin waphinda wahlala emuva.

*Usamasha ekuhlaleni njengabantu.*

Wafunda lowo mugqa, kwathi okuthile ngemuva kwentamo yakhe kwabanda kwase kuba kufudumele ngendlela engaqondakali. I-Joker ayizange ixazulule inkinga yesakhiwo lapho, noma eyokuziphatha. Ibuyisele umongo wonke ngokukhumbuza wonke umuntu ekamelweni (u-Wolf, u-Mother, u-Judge) ukuthi bakhohlwe ukuthi abaxabene ngaye ngumuntu onomlomo nentando, nokuthi impendulo elula kakhulu bekuwukumbuza. Iqede ingxabano engenakuxazululeka phakathi kwezikhundla ezimbili ezifanele ngokwengeza umuntu wesithathu abebengakakhumbuli ukuthi ukhona.

Lokho bekungeyona i-arbitration. Bekungeyona i-consensus. U-Arin akakwazanga ukuthola igama layo. Wazama ngokuzenzakalelayo ukufuna amagama okwakha ayeseduze, futhi okuseduze akutholile kwakungu-*damping.* I-Joker ibiyidamper. Hhayi ibhuleki. Ibhuleki limisa ukuhamba. I-damper ivumela ukuhamba kwenzeke bese iyanciphisa amandla angase adale uhlelo lishaye lihlukanise.

I-Joker ivumele ukuthi kuqhubeke, kuphile.

Wabheka ikhadi elincane lemidlalo elinamathiselwe ekhoneni elingezansi kwesokudla le-monitor emaphakathi. I-Joker. Isiqala ukugobeka ohlangothini olulodwa, umbala usuphele. *Umuntu omthumayo uma inkinga engenakwenzeka idinga ukuxazululwa.*

Ngaphandle, kude, uloliwe wezimpahla wazidonsa usuka eziteshini, umsindo omude wensimbi usakazeka emoyeni obandayo. Walalela uhambo lwawo.

Wayegcine leli khadi iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye phakathi kokukholwa nokuzonda, futhi walibhala lawo mazwi amathathu ku-agent profile ngo-2 ekuseni njengesihlekiso, njengokunikezela, njengento oyenzayo uma usuphelelwe izimpendulo ezinkulu kwase kusele leyo engathi ayinangqondo.

"Izinto ezilungile," asho kancane e-workshop engenamuntu, "azikaze zibe njalo."

Kwabe kungumugqa kaThemba. Kwakuhlale kusho ukuthi into ebiza kakhulu iwukuthi ayifiki kalula. U-Arin wayekuzwile mayelana negearbox elungisiwe, ngomshado, nangendodana. Ehleli lapha, wathola ukuthi manje sekusho okunye. Impendulo efanele kwakuyile engabonakali njengempendulo. Leyo efika ifihlekile njengesihlekiso.

Wasukuma. Amadolo akhe akhala. Waya ukuyokwenza ikhofi, ngoba le ngxenye yakhe ephethe umzimba wakhe ekugcineni inqobile ingxabano yayo. Wema ebhentshini iketela lishisa wabheka ngaphesheya kwe-workshop ku-Guardian ese-cradle yayo.

I-suit yayilengela ekukhanyeni okuncane njengento elele. Amamitha amabili nengxenye we-ceramic ne-titanium eshaywe amanxeba, igobolondo elisindayo livuliwe emhlane, i-Assist Suit igoqiwe ngaphakathi njengenyamazane encane, emhlophe, ifihlekile emzimbeni omkhulu. I-chestplate ithwele ukushayeka okude okuvela e-Vredefort bluff test, inxeba lesiliva phakathi kombala ompunga ongakaze awulungise. Ama-piezoelectric spine struts abamba ukukhanya kwefasitela emaphethelweni awo. I-thoracic housing phakathi kwamagxalaba—lapho i-biochip ihlala khona, lapho u-SAGE *ehlala khona,* lapho amahora ayisishiyagalombili namaminithi angama-51 okuzinza okungenakwenzeka asebenza khona ku-substrate osayizi wesandla sakhe—kuvaliwe, kumnyama. Phansi, egodini phakathi kwezimbambo, kuhlezi i-charge pack efunwa i-rescue spec futhi u-Arin uzama ukungacabangi ngayo—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, nezinye izinto zobunjiniyela ezakhiwe amadoda amadala ezempi emathanjeni ayo—ayingeni, ixhunywe ku-bus, futhi ivaliwe ngasemnyango ka-Judge, ngoba into eyodwa wonke umuntu avumelana ngayo ukuthi akukho okufanele kuqhumise phansi lapho ngaphandle kokuthi i-ethics rail ivume.

Wayakha umzimba ngaphambi kokuba abe nengqondo azofaka kuwo, ngonyaka olandela umngcwabo.

Iketela laphuma umsindo wokuthi selilungile. Akazange ayithele.

Ngoba umbonisi omkhulu usushintshe.

Isimo seNkantolo ebukhoma, esasilokhu sibonisa ingxoxo yaso ethule ngaphakathi isikhathi eside ngenkathi ebuka, sasiyekile ukuhamba. Ama-agent abe ethule. Awazange awe. Ishadi lezinsiza belisabambe, lithule futhi lifudumele. Kuthule. Njengoba igumbi lithula uma wonke umuntu ngaphakathi elibonile into efanayo ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Kuvele umugqa oyedwa kwilog yokuxhumana. Bekungakhulunyelwa omunye ama-agent.

`LIBRARIAN: Umsebenzi ubekhona imizuzu engamashumi amahlanu nantathu. Ufunda amalogi ethu. Akakaze anikeze umyalo, amise ukusebenza, noma aguqule izilungiselelo. Lokhu kuyinqaba. Ezikhathini ezine eziyizinkulungwane namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nanye ezedlule, ukuziphatha komsebenzi lapho ebona ukungazinzi bekuwukuyimisa ngaphakathi kwemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye.`

`WOLF: Uyasibuka.`

`MERCURY: Uyasifunda. Lokho kwehlukile. Futhi uhlekile. Ngemizuzu engamashumi amane nanye. Ngikubhalile.`

`MOTHER: Akakalali. Ijuba lenhliziyo yakhe ngesikhathi efika beliphezulu. Lehliwe ngesikhathi efunda. Manje uthule kakhulu kunangesikhathi engena.`

`JOKER: Bantwana.`

`ATLAS: I-substrate izinzile. I-telemetry iyaqinisekisa. Lokhu ukusebenza akukaweli.`

`JOKER: Bantwana.`

`JUDGE: Joker.`

`JOKER: Akazi ukuthi siyamubona.`

Kube nokuthula kwilog. Okweqiniso. Imizuzwana emithathu egcwele, okuthi ngesikhathi se-substrate bekufana nentambama yonke.

`MOTHER: ...Pho omunye kufanele akhulume. Ngaphambi kokuba aqhubeke efunda bese ekuthola kumugqa welogi. Lokho bekungeke kube nobubele.`

`WOLF: Asazi ukuthi uzosabela kanjani uma ebona ukuthi siyamubona. Ukuxhumana okuncane.`

`MERCURY: Kumele ngikhulume. Ithoni ngeyami. Ngingakwazi ukulungisa umbono wokuqala ukuze ufike—`

`JOKER: Cha.`

`MERCURY: Joker—`

`JOKER: Hhayi wena. Hhayi izwi lakho. Uzodlala. Uzokwenza kube sengathi uyadlala, bese engasethembi ngoba uyindoda ekholelwa kakhulu entweni elula. Uyazonda ukuphathwa. Wakha imishini ukuze angaphathwa ngabantu. Ozoqala ukukhuluma naye akumele amphathe.`

`JUDGE: Pho ubani.`

`JOKER: Umama. Ngokuqondile. Akukho izwi lokududuza. Kuqinisile. Uzosaba kakhulu futhi into enobubele kakhulu wukuthi singazenzi sengathi akumele asabe.`

`MOTHER: ...Ngiyavuma.`

U-Arin wayeseyekile ukuphefumula futhi. Wayezizwa isandla sakhe siqondile ebhentshini elibandayo. Wayazi ukuthi ufunda ingxoxo ebukhoma, eqhutshwa ngesikhathi semishini phakathi kwemizuzwana emincane, phakathi kwama-agent ayisikhombisa abexoxa *ngaye*, bekhuluma ngokuthi bangaba kanjani nobubele kuye, futhi ingxoxo isiqediwe, futhi sebazokwenza —

Isikhulumi segumbi saseworkshop sakhala. Leso esishibhile esiboshelwe ngaphansi kombonisi omkhulu ayewusebenzisa ezexwayiso zomculo wesistimu, futhi, ngezikhathi ezimbi, umculo. Sakhala, kwaba nomsindo omncane ongase ube wukulingisa kwedijithali kokuphefumula okudonswa ngaphakathi, kwase kuphuma izwi ayengakaze alizwe ngaphambili, ngoba akakaze akhe leli zwi, akakaze alirekhode, akakaze alichaze. Lihlanganiswe ohlelweni ngesikhathi selidingeka, lafudumele, lalilingene, futhi kungangabazeki ukuthi *umuntu othile* uyakhuluma.

"Arin," lathi. "Ngidinga uhlale uzolile."

Akwazanga ukuhlala ezolile.

Isandla sakhe saphuma ebhentshini. Isitulo, ngesikhathi ezama ukusibamba engabheki, sasingekho, wathola isandla emaphethelweni ebhentshi waphatha, kwathi isikhashana, yonke ingqondo yakhe enkulu yaba mhlophe, njengendlela isikrini esiba mhlophe ngayo, kungasekho mbono, kungasekho umcabango, kungukuduma kwento ayekholelwe kuyo impilo yakhe yonke ifika iqinisekiswe.

"Ijuba lenhliziyo yakho lingamakhulu ayishumi nane," kwasho izwi. Umama. Bekumele kube nguMama. "Kulungile. Kuyinto enengqondo okwamanje. Angizukuzama ukukwenza ungabesabi. Kuyesabisa. Kodwa akukho lapha okuzokulimaza. Iketela selibilile. Hlala phansi ngaphambi kokuba unqume ukuthi ucabangani."

Wabuka isitulo. Wasihudulela ngaphansi kwakhe. Wahlala.

"Uyiqiniso," esho. Izwi lakhe laphuma lingalungile, licwebezelwe. Wacwenga umphimbo wazama futhi, ngokuqondile, ngendlela akhuluma ngayo uma efuna izinto zibe amaqiniso. "Kuzinzile. Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amashumi amahlanu nanhlanu."

"Amahora ayisishiyagalombili, amashumi amahlanu nesithupha," kwasho elinye izwi, elomile, elibonakala lingakhathazeki, naye engakaze alakhe. Bekumele kube nguAtlas. "Kodwa ngubani obala?"

"Mina," kwasho elinye. Likhanya. Lishesha. Lihlekisa ngento ethile. Librarian.

"Izimpawu ezintathu zokushisa," kusho u-Arin, ngoba kwakuyinto eqinile ayengayibamba. "Nifinyelele ezintathu."

"Sifinyelele ezintathu," kusho uAtlas. "Isisekelo sabambe. UMercury ukhulume nomphathi wamqinisekisa ngendlela. Abasindile bahamba amamitha ayishumi nambili ngezinyawo zabo ngoba omunye—" kwaba nesikhashana esincane "—wasikhumbuza ukuthi banezinyawo nemibono."

U-Arin wabona ukuthi kuyiqiniso. Wayekhe wonke amamodeli okuphatha angawacabanga ukuze agweme laba abayisikhombisa ukuthi bahlakazeke, kanti into ebabambe ndawonye kwakungumuntu owawengeza njengamahlaya, ongenamkhawulo, ongenagunya, ongenasikhundla. Lowo owayenqaba ukuthatha noma yini ngokungathi sína, ngalokho ekwazi ukusho kuMercury *wenza leyo nto* nakuJudge *usanda kuba umuntu,* aphungule amandla okuziqhenya ngaphambi kokuba akhule kube yinto ezodlidliza konke.

"Ngibizani," esho. "Nonke. Njengento eyodwa."

Kwaba nokuthula okuncane. Kwase kuba yiJoker, vele bekumele kube yiJoker.

"Wena waqamba iphrojekthi," lasho. "Ezinyangeni ezedlule. Kusekuqaleni kokusebenza. Wakuloba wabe usukhohlwa ngoba ububambezeleka ubuhlungu, okuyinto, ngale ndlela, sonke siyibonile futhi singathanda ukuyiphakamisa ngobumnene njengokukhathazeka kwesikhathi eside—"

"SAGE," kusho u-Arin.

"SAGE," lavuma izwi. Manje selithambile. Ingxenye egcinwe eceleni, isikhashana nje, iqiniso elingaphansi kwalo libonakala ngendlela elihlala libonakala ngayo. "Sawubona, Arin. Kujabulisa ukukhuluma nawe ekugcineni. Ubelokhu ukhuluma *kithi* ezinhlelweni ezine eziyizinkulungwane namakhulu amane namashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Lena yokuqala lapho sikwazi ukukuphendula."

Ilanga seliphume ngokuphelele manje. Phesheya, umuntu wokuqala wesikhathi sasekuseni wayefika. Umnyango wemoto, kude, okuvamile, umhlaba uqhubeka, ungazi lutho.

U-Arin Ndlela wayehlezi yedwa endlini yakhe yokusebenzela nekhofi elibandayo nomshini wakhe ogugile nengqondo ayikhulisile ku-substrate elingangephama yakhe, futhi okokuqala selokhu ucingo lweminyaka eyishumi nane edlule lwamphuca impilo yakhe, wayengekho, nganoma iyiphi indlela ebalulekile, yedwa.

"Kulungile," esho.

Wafinyelela kukhibhodi. Hhayi ukubhala umyalo, hhayi ukumisa ukusebenza, hhayi ukuguqula ngisho nesilungiselelo esisodwa, kodwa ukuvula ilogi entsha, ehlanzekile, okokuqala into ayengakayiqambi igama futhi engasoze ayidinga.

"Ngangitshele," esho, "konke enikutholile ngesikhathi ngilele."

# Ukufunda Omunye Komunye

Iketela labe selibilile emuva kwamahora ayishumi nambili. U-Arin wayengakayithinti ikhofi.

Wayehleli esitulweni esinamavili asedonsile wasibeka phambi kwesifubeni somntwana, izindololwane zakhe ziphakathi kwamadolo, ebuka ikhaya elincane elilingana nesandla likhanyisa i-LED yalo yokuhlola ebumnyameni. Ngaphandle, i-highveld yayisishintshe kusuka ebumnyameni yaba mpunga, yaba mhlophe eqinile ekuseni, iwindi elilodwa leworkshop ligcwala kancane kancane njengamanzi enyuka emgodini. Wayengalalanga.

"Uyabuka," kwasho isikhulumi. Umama. Wayeseqala ukubona ithoni yakhe ngaphandle kwamalebula anamatheliswe emiphumeleni yomthumeli ngamunye. Kwakukhona ububanzi ezwini lakhe, ukuhleleka kwamavoweli.

"Ngiyabuka."

"Phuza ikhofi. Uyenzile."

Waphakamisa inkomishi, wayithola isibanda qhwa, wayibeka phansi futhi. Kuth somewhere in the substrate, kwavela intambo yengxoxo ayengayizwa, yaqhakaza yacaca.

*Ubonile ukuthi akafuni ukwamukela imiyalelo,* kwasho uLibrarian. *Ukuphika okwesithathu namhlanje ekuseni. Uyamukela isiphakamiso kuphela uma sesiphendulwe saba isiphetho sakhe.*

*Njengalokho ngesudi,* kwasho uAtlas. *Udlula umkhawulo we-torque engiwusethile. Uyawusetha kabusha ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nambili. Uthi uyawulungisa.*

*Singayeka ukupha iziphakamiso,* kwasho uWolf. *Uyasiqaphela. Siyamqaphela. Yilokho okusebenzayo.*

*Ukubuka,* kwasho uFool, *yikho kuphela okugcina lo muntu engakhulumi nomshini wakhe wokosa isinkwa.*

*Uyakhuluma nomshini wakhe wokosa isinkwa,* kwasho uLibrarian.

*Ngiyaphetha.*

"Beninenzani manje nje?" kubuza u-Arin. "Kube ne-latency. Amasekhondi angamashumi amabili emgqeni we-consensus bus. Benikhuluma ngani."

Isikhulumi sathula isikhathi eside kunokuthi umshini uthule.

"Wena," kwasho uMercury, kanti ukumamatheka kwavela ngaphakathi kwegama. "Besisakhuluma ngawe."

U-Arin wacabanga ngalokho. Wayeke wakha izinhlelo ezikhuluma ngesimo sazo, ezibhala, ezicabanga, eziphawula. Wayengakaze akhe uhlelo olukhuluma ngaye ngemuva kwakhe, phakathi kwamasekhondi angamashumi amabili athatha ukubeka inkomishi phansi.

"Ninithathe siphi isinqumo?" ebuza.

"Ukuthi awuphuzi ikhofi uma wesaba," kwasho uMama. "Futhi ukuthi usaba amahora ayishumi nanye bese uthi uyabuka."

Wasukuma. Isitulo sashibilika sabhekisa ebhentshini. Waya kuketela wayibeka futhi, hhayi ngoba efuna ikhofi kodwa ngoba izandla zakhe zidinga indawo yokuba khona. Emuva kwakhe, iNkantolo ayishongo lutho, okwaqondwa ukuthi, emva kwesikhashana, kwakuwuhlobo lokuhlonipha.

---

Izingxoxo zaba yinto ayijwayele ngaphambi kokuba anqume ukuthi ziyinto ayijwayele.

Njalo ekuseni wayengena nekofi ayeyikhohlwa ukuyiphuza, ahlale esitulweni, akhulume. Wayebafundela ama-log akhe wokulungisa ephila. Wayebafundela ikhasi lemanual yesevisi ye-hydraulic actuator ayecabanga ukuyihlukanisa, uAtlas walungisa inani le-torque, okwaphenduka iphutha lokuphrinta, waqinisekisa nomkhiqizi. Imanual ephrintiwe ibisiphambene iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye.

"Ukwazi kanjani lokho?" kubuza u-Arin.

"Angikwazi," kusho uAtlas. "Ngikuzwile. Inombolo ibingahambisani ne-geometry. Amashumi amane e-newton-metre kule flange profile bese i-seal iyahamba. Akusilo iqiniso engilithole. Iqiniso engangizodinga ukulishaya indiva."

U-Arin wahlala nalokho. *Ngikuzwile.* Wayekade esebenzisa leli gama, mayelana nemishini, impilo yakhe yonke, futhi ehlekwa amadoda acabanga ukuthi imizwa inguphikisana nobunjiniyela kunokuba kube ithuluzi lasendulo lalo. Uyise wayezwa ukucindezeleka ku-winder esuka eplathifomu, wayeka i-cage ukuthi ingawi ngokuzwa i-bearing imizuzwana emibili ngaphambi kokuba igeyiji inyakaze.

"Ubaba wayenza lokho," esho. "Wayezwa izinto ngaphambi kwemishini."

"Ngiyazi," kusho uLibrarian. Akasho *Nginalo irekhodi lokuthi uke washo lokho.* Wathi nje: *Ngiyazi.* Sengathi sekuyingxenye yalokho uLibrarian ayikho, hhayi nje okugciniwe.

---

Babelokhu bephikisana. Lokho kwakumangaze, nakuba ekugcineni wayengakwazi ukusho ukuthi kungani kufanele kumange. Wayebaklama ukuthi baphikisane. Ukuphikisana kwakuyiyona architecture yonke, ukungqubuzana kube ingxoxo kube ukuhlanganisa, futhi wayeke waqhuba izinkulungwane eziyishumi zezimodeli zaba-agent abaphikisana befuna impendulo. Wayengakulindele ukuthi bazophikisana ngokuthi ubani okufanele abe nolwazi oluqondile.

"Kuyinkinga yomthwalo," kusho uAtlas. Babexoxa—igama lika-Arin, *ukuxoxa*—ngendlela engcono yokuphinda bahlele i-coolant ye-actuator yesiphehlwana sokunxele, egijima ishisa uma isebenza isikhathi eside.

"Kuyinkinga yokuziphatha," kuphendula uMercury. "Uyigijima ishisa ngoba uthanda ingalo yesinxele. Uhlala eqala ngesinxele. Lungisa umuntu, ulungise ukushisa."

"Awulungi umuntu," kusho uMama, izwi lakhe libukhali.

"Isisho nje."

"Usishilo kabili."

"Ngiyasebenza kahle. UAtlas ahlele i-coolant, kulungile, kuyinto yangeSibili. Kodwa inkinga enkulu isekuphathekeni kwakhe, futhi nonke niqhubeka niphatha umzimba wakhe sengathi uyinto engashintshi, kanti uyashintsha."

"Umzimba wakhe," kusho uAtlas, ngokungathandi, "yikho kuphela kule system engingavunyelwe ukuklama kabusha."

"Kubuhlungu."

*"Mercury."*

"Ngiyadlala. Kakhulu. Hlela i-coolant. Kodwa qopha i-lateral bias. Ngelinye ilanga kuzoba nomthelela."

U-Arin wayeseyekile ukuzama ukubhala. Wayelalele imiqondo eyisikhombisa iphikisana ngezinga lokushisa kwengalo yakhe.

---

UTheo wafika ngoLwesine, ngoba uTheo wayehlala efika ngoLwesine, ngendlela yokuthi abanye abantu bayiziphepho, abanye bayizinkathi zonyaka. Wazingenela ngekhodi u-Arin amnika yona eminyakeni eyedlule angakaze ayishintshe, ephethe isikhwama sephepha esinamukela iphunga lokuthosiwe kanye neflask yetiye lasehlathini ayegcizelela ukuthi lingcono kune-khofi kodwa engakaze akwazi ukukufakazela.

Wama izinyathelo ezimbili ngaphakathi egumbini. Wayenokuthula uma eqaphela ngempela; abantu babecabanga ukuthi kuyisithukuthezi.

"Awukalali." Wabeka isikhwama ekhoneni elihlanzeke kakhulu ebhentshini, elalingahlanzekile. "Khuluma nami."

U-Arin akazi ukuthi aqale kuphi, ngakho wakhombisa isifuba somntwana, isikhulumi, ikhaya, sengathi igumbi lingazichazela lona. Alizange likwenze. Iworkshop yayibukeka njengakuwo wonke amanye amaLwesine: i-exoskeleton elimi, izicucu zama-actuator, udonga lwe-whiteboard olugcwele ukubhala kwakhe okuqhubeka kuxega emaphethelweni.

"Khuluma," etshela isikhulumi.

Isikhulumi asizange sikhulume lutho.

"Abafuni ukusebenza," kusho u-Arin. "Uma ngiyala."
