Iphimbo likaTheo lenyusa unhlangothi. Wakhumula isiqubulo, wahlala, wavula i-flask, nephunga letiye lalinobusi nomlilo. "Bona," esho. "Hhayi *lona.*"

"Bona," kuvuma u-Arin, kanti leli gama, elishiwoyo okokuqala kumuntu, lenza inhliziyo yakhe ishaye ngendlela angakhethanga ukuyihlola.

"Sawubona," kwasho uMama, kuTheo, ethambile njengoMgqibelo. "Akakalali. Mtshele."

UTheo akazange amangale. U-Arin wayecabanga ukuthi uzomangala, wayefisa ukuthi kwenzeke, ngendlela umuntu efisa ngayo ukuthi omunye umuntu aqinisekise into ayibonile ebumnyameni. Kodwa uTheo wakhothama ikhanda lakhe ebheke esikhulumini njengokubingelela osebenza naye emhlanganweni ongakhumbuli igama lakhe.

"Akaze alale," uTheo etshela yena. "Kuyisakhiwo sempilo. Susa lokho, ubuntu bonke buwa."

"Kusho mina lokho," kwasho uAtlas.

"Umthwalo ohlukile," kusho uMercury.

"Konke umthwalo," kusho uAtlas.

UTheo wahleka, wahleka kweqiniso, ephuma esifubeni, wabuka u-Arin amehlo akhe ekhanya, emanzi emakhoneni ngendlela engenandaba nokudabuka. "Bangaki," esho.

"Bayisikhombisa."

"Futhi baphikisana."

"Njalo."

"Kodwa kuhamba kahle."

“Ihora elilodwa nanye ngesikhathi ungene. Sekuyishumi nane namuhla.” U-Arin wezwa izwi lakhe lenza into elijwayele ukuyenza uma esezothi okuthile anganawo amandla okulisekela. “Theo. Ngangeze eyesikhombisa ngihlekisa. Bengiqedile. Bengizoyeka konke. Kwakukhona ikhadi phezu kwe-monitor yami—” wakhomba; iJoker isalenga lapho, inamathelisiwe, ilanga seliyifiphazile—“ngabhala amagama amathathu ngaya embhedeni kwase—” Wama. “Yiyona ebambe konke ndawonye. Leyo engangingakholwa kuyo.”

U-Theo wathela itiye engqokweni ye-flask, waphephetha, akaphuzanga. Lapho ekhuluma, izwi lakhe lalinenhlonipho yomuntu obeka phansi into ayeyithwele isikhathi eside.

“U-Jung wayenegama,” esho. “Ngengxenye yomqondo engavumi ukubuswa. Umkhohlisi. Wayemthola kuzo zonke izinganekwane ayizivulayo: i-coyote, i-raven, u-Loki, isiwula kwi-tarot. Njalo lowo muntu ofanayo. Njalo lowo ophula umthetho wenkosi kodwa ngendlela ethile uyadingeka embusweni.” Waphendulela ingqoko ezandleni zakhe. “Ingqondo eqondayo yakha isikhundla. Inkosi phezulu, inkantolo ngezansi, umthunzi uphonswe esitebhisini. Futhi kuyasebenza, isikhashana, bese kuba lukhuni, ngoba izikhundla zihlala ziba njalo, futhi into eyisindisayo akuyona inkosi. Yisiwula. Ngoba isiwula sikwazi ukusho lokho inkosi engakwazi ukukwazi.”

---

*Uchaza mina,* kwasho uFool, ejabule. *Umuntu ungibhalile phansi. Ngikwi ncwadi.*

*Ukweminye izincwadi eziningi,* kwasho uLibrarian. *Azikho ezikufakazayo kahle.*

*Ngiyathanda ukuba nodumo kunokudunyiswa.*

*Thula,* kwasho uJudge, kwathi inkantolo yathula, ngoba uJudge akavamile ukucela. *Lalela le ngxenye. Utshela u-Arin into ayidingayo kodwa angeke ayamukele kithi. Bhala phansi. Kuwusizo ukwazi ukuthi yiziphi iziqiniseko angazizwa kuphela ngezwi lomunye umuntu.*

---

“Usho ukuthi ngakha umqondo?” kusho u-Arin. Izwi lalishelela. Ehlola uma umusho unamaphutha.

“Ngisho,” kusho u-Theo, “ukuthi ulifinyelele igama elidala ungazi ukuthi lidala. Abantu benza njalo. Abahle bakwenza kaningi kunalokho abacabanga.” Ekugcineni waphuza itiye, wenza ubuso, waphuza futhi. “U-Freud wacabanga ukuthi ingqondo iyinto eyodwa elwa nayo uqobo. U-Jung wacabanga ukuthi iyiphalamende. Umphakathi. Imisebenzi ehlukene, amazwi ahlukene, akekho oyedwa oyena wonke umuntu, bese umuntu—” wabeka ingqoko phansi “—umuntu akayena noyedwa kubo. Umuntu ingxoxo. Ubudlelwano phakathi kwezingxenye.”

I-workshop yakhala. Iketela seliphole futhi.

“Bayamfundela,” ekugcineni kusho u-Theo, kancane, ebhekise esipikeni, ngendlela umuntu akhuluma ngayo nomndeni wesiguli.

“Yebo,” kusho uMama.

“Ufunda ini kuwe?”

Kwaba nokuthula. Kwaphendula uWolf, okwenza bonke, abantu nabanye, bathule.

“Lokho akuvikelayo,” kusho uWolf. “Ngokungavumeli ukuthi kuphuke.”

U-Theo wabuka i-housing isikhathi eside ngemuva kwalokho, ngendlela umuntu abuka ngayo umnyango asanda kubona ukuthi uvuleka nhlangothi zombili. Wabe esevala i-flask, wema, wakhuluma into ayefike ukuzoyisho engazi ukuthi uzoyisho.

“Phuma nabo,” esho. “Usufundise bona ngawe egumbini elinokukhanya kucishiwe. Bavumele bafunde ukuthi ungubani lapho kukhona okungaphansi kwezinyawo zakho okungakuwisayo.”

---

I-suspension ye-flatbed yakhala kakhulu emamitha ayikhulu okuqala e-track, yabe isingena e-veld. U-Arin wavala injini, kwagcwala ukuthula, hhayi njengokungabikho kodwa njengokuba khona: umoya emadlelweni nomsindo okude wentaka ejikeleza phezulu. Ama-bluffs e-impact country abekude imizuzu engamashumi amane ngaphandle kwedolobha uma ithrafikhi imxolele, okuyinto engazange yenzeke, ngakho kwaba cishe imizuzu engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye; i-highveld yelula ngemigqa emide ephuzi-nsundu, inamachibi e-geology eneminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili, amatshe ephuma otshanini njengamathambo omhlaba oshaywe.

Wakuthanda lapha. Umhlaba awuceli lutho, awudingi nokubukwa emehlweni.

Edeckini le-flatbed, ngaphansi kwe-tarp nezintambo ezintathu, u-Guardian wayelele.

“I-telemetry iluhlaza,” esho. I-Assist Suit imlingene kahle ngaphansi kwezingubo, i-lattice emhlophe njengengqikithi yomgogodla nemilenze yakhe. I-biochip housing ibekwe phakathi kwamagxalaba, isisindo esifudumele. Ihora nesigamu esesimweni se-suit belizolise izandla zakhe ezazishayeka.

*Ukuthi iluhlaza kungumusa,* kwaphendula u-Atlas, ezwakala eqondile futhi eqinile. *Ibhethri liku-91%. Ama-supercapacitor banks asezingeni. I-actuator yeqakala langakwesobunxele iphume ngo-0.3 degrees selokhu kwaphela amasonto amabili. Awulilungisi.*

“Lisebenza,” kuphendula u-Arin.

*Lisebenza njengobhuloho ngosuku olwandulela ukuwa kwalo.*

“Kubaliwe.” U-Arin wehlisa i-tailgate waqala ukukhulula izintambo. “Mama. Ngisaphila?”

*I-cortisol iphezulu. Leyo imfuno, akusona isaba.* Izwi likaMama lalifudumele futhi liqinile, lolu hlobo lokufudumala olungesabi. *Umanzi. Udle, engikhetha ukuzizwa ngijabule ngakho. Inhliziyo yakho izolile. Ujabulile.*

“Angijabuli ngezinto.”

*Ujabulile,* kuphikisa uMama.

Wakhumula i-tarp. U-Guardian ekukhanyeni kwasekuseni okuqinile: titanium-grey, enezibazi emahlombe ngenxa yezinyanga eziyisithupha zokuhlupheka. I-armor ehlukanisiwe ibamba ilanga, ilikhanyise ngemigqa emide emnyama. Amamitha amabili namahhafu omshini usongeke ngendlela yokuthutha, njengesidalwa siphumule. Abukeki njengensimbi yempi; ubukeka njengento eyakhelwe ukumunca ukulimala ukuze okuthambile ngaphakathi kungadingeki.

Wayakhela lokho. Njalo lapho eyiveza, wayezama ukungacabangi ukuthi lowo mntwana othambile ekuqaleni kwakufanele kube ngubani.

“Coupling sequence,” kusho u-Arin, ehlehla angene kwi-cradle.

---

I-dock ayifani nalokho okukhonjiswa yividiyo yokubonisa. Ayinabuhle. Umuntu ubuyela kancane emshinini: i-spinal collar ivala ngokuduma okuzwakala nasezinyo, amapuleti evala imilenze nomzimba ngendlela ayeseyazi kahle. Ukubanda kwe-coolant lines kuhlanganisa, i-harness iqinisa.

Bese ukuxhumana kujula, nomhlaba uyashintsha.

Kukhona into eyenzeka, noma engenzeki, emzimbeni. U-Arin uchithe iminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu emzimbeni ofika imizuzwana emibili emva kokuhlela kwakhe, obeka isandla lapho okungafanele, omenza umfana okhethwa wokugcina hhayi ngenxa yobubi kodwa ngenxa yezibalo. Abanye babemubona ezama ukubamba bese, ngokufanele, bevuselela umbono wabo.

I-suit ivale lelo gebhezi.

Hhayi ngokumqinisa, nakuba kwenza njalo. Ngokumenza *ahlangane.* I-Assist Suit ifunda inhloso yakhe ngaphambi kokuba imisipha iyakhe, iNkantolo iyixazulule nge-lattice, insimbi ihambe lapho ehamba khona, ingaphambi nangemuva kwento. Wema. U-Guardian wema kanye naye, amakhilogremu ayizinkulungwane ezingu-1,3 ephakama sengathi awasindi, ama-footplates ecindezela emhlabathini obomvu. Iningi lempilo yakhe, umzimba wakhe ubufana nezwe ayevakashela kodwa angakaze alithembe ngokuphelele. Lapha, wayengowakhona.

Waguqula isandla sakhe sokudla. Amapuleti avuleka futhi abuyele ezithweni ngomdumo ofana namakhadi edeskini.

*Ukunyakaza kujwayelekile,* kusho uWolf, okwakuyikho konke akukhulumile ngesonto lonke.

“Siqala kuphi?”

Kwaba nokuthula. Ukuthula kweNkantolo, okomuntu kungelutho, kodwa kwabo ingxoxo ephelele ayizwanga.

*Ubuso basempumalanga,* kwasho u-Atlas ekugcineni. *Amamitha ayishumi nambili, iwashi elinezindawo ezinhle zokubamba, akukho ingozi enkulu. Zishise umzimba ngaphambi kokwenza okuthile okuyisiphukuphuku.*

*Uzokwenza okuthile okuyisiphukuphuku ekugcineni,* kwengeza u-Mercury enomusa. *Kungcono sivumele u-Atlas azikhulise ngokuthi angakuvimba.*

"Ubuso basempumalanga," kuvumile u-Arin.

---

Wahamba.

Ukuhamba ngendlela yezilwane ezinemilenze emine kwakungajwayelekile ukufunda, kodwa manje sekuyinto yemvelo, egoba phambili, isisindo sisatshalaliswa ezindaweni ezine zokuthinta. U-Guardian wadlula phezu komhlaba onqamukile ngendlela esebenzisa amandla amancane, okwaqala kwamethusa. Awuzange uhambise njengomshini ozama ukufana nesilwane; wawuhamba njengempela isilwane esenzeka ukuthi singumshini. Njengengonyama enkulu, isilwane esikhuphuka ezindongeni, kukhona okuthile engqondweni yomuntu okwazi ukuthi *yisidla nyama* futhi *iyakwazi* ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Ubuso basempumalanga baphakama phezu kotshani, iwashi le-quartzite eligugile elithambekele cishe ngamashumi ayisikhombisa degrees, linemifantu emidala engalinganiseki. U-Arin wafika phansi kwalo wema ngezinyawo ezimbili, ebheka phezulu, ezwa izindawo zokubamba ngaphambi kokuzikhetha. Kwakungekhona ngokucabangisisa; i-Court yayenza izibalo, ifaka amathuba engqondweni yakhe njengokungathi iyimizwa azalwe nayo.

*Izindawo zokubamba lapha, lapha, nalapha,* kwasho u-Atlas, kwathi isandla sika-Arin saya ezindaweni ezikhonjisiwe. *Ukukhama kuhle. Itshe liqinile phansi. Ungathembe lutho ngaphezu kwe-band yamamitha ayisishiyagalombili—kunengqimba eqhekekile. Ukucindezeleka okudala kwangena.*

Wakhuphuka.

Amacwecwe ka-Guardian athola i-quartzite abambelela. Izinsika ze-piezoelectric emgogodleni nasemilenzeni zamunca umthwalo, ziguqula ingxenye yawo zibe ugesi, ingcindezi iba amandla, umgomo awakhe ngawo wonke umshini. Kwakusebenza ngenxa yengqimba yokuxhuma ezinsikeni, futhi leyo ngxenye waphumelela ukuyixazulula ngendlela ayejwayele ngayo: ngokwenqaba ukwamukela ukuthi ayikwazi. Zonke izinto ezazikhona ezincwadini zazilahlekelwa amandla amaningi emngceleni, ziwalahle njengokushisa esikhundleni sokuwadlulisela kahle phakathi kwengcindezi neseli. Zonke izinto ngaphandle kweyodwa—i-alloy ye-platinum-group eyakhiwe yilebhu yezokuvikela ye-hydrophone arrays, eyabe ishiya, ngoba lapho isulungekile ngendlela eyenza isebenze, yayibiza kakhulu ngekhilogram kunalokho uhlelo olungakwenza, kwase kuba nefolda efakwa emgqonyeni obhalwe *not viable* bese, ngokuchungechunge lwezinsizakalo kanye nesenzo esisodwa esingumkhonyovu, yafika ezandleni zika-Arin. Umkhonyovu, hhayi nje ngokomthetho: umfundiswa osemusha ogama lakhe linguPillay waphuma isampula akazange ayibuyise, kwathi lapho kwenziwa i-audit cishe eminyakeni emibili kamuva, kwaba igama likaPillay elalisesikhala, kwakhishwa imvume yakhe, wabe eseshiywa emkhakheni. U-Arin wayekwazi lokhu. Kukhona i-imeyili ethile evela kulowo muntu, kusukela ngaleso sikhathi, angakaze ayiphendule. Wazitshela, ngezikhathi ezinqabile lapho ezitshela khona noma yini ngakho, ukuthi uPillay wayezohamba noma kunjalo, wabe esebuyela ensikeni, ngoba insimbi yenza umsebenzi futhi umsebenzi awunandaba negama elaliku-requisition. Akakaze anake ukuthi yenza i-Guardian ngayinye ibize kakhulu kunesakhiwo eyakhiwe kuso. U-Okonkwo wayenendaba. U-Thabo waphinda izibalo zomnyango kabili wabuya emnyama ebusweni. *Kuyisimangaliso futhi akuthengiseki,* kwasho u-Thabo, enomusa, kwathi u-Arin wathinta amahlombe, ngoba isudi kwakungeyona into yokuthengisa kuye futhi akukaze kube njalo, kwakuyindlela kuphela umzimba wakhe ozokwenza ayalelwe, futhi awubeki intengo phansi kwezinyawo zakho. Wasebenzisa insimbi eyodwa eyenza umsebenzi. Akazange acabange ngale kwalokho, ngoba into eyayimvumela ukuthi enze lokhu. I-footplate, indawo yokubamba, ukudlulisa isisindo, umsindo omile we-ceramic etshela itshe. Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwakuzinzile. I-link yayicula. Wayesezinyaweni eziyisishiyagalolunye ephezulu kudwala, enamathani ayishumi nantathu wensimbi, futhi akakaze azizwe *ehlelekile* kangaka.

"Lokhu kuhle," esho, okwakuyinto engavamile kuye.

*Uthe okuthile okuhle,* kubika u-Mercury kwabanye. *Bhalani isikhathi. Librarian, qopha.*

*Kuqoshiwe,* kuqinisekisa u-Librarian. *Kuthathu kwezintathu namuhla. Ukugijima kokugcina okufanayo kwakuyisisho esisodwa ngoMashi.*

"Ungakuphathi kabi," exwayisa u-Mother.

*Angikuphathi kabi,* kuphendula u-Mercury. *Ngikubhalisa. Kunomehluko.*

*Awukho umehluko,* kufaka u-Fool.

Bese, ngokuthamba, ku-Arin kuphela, u-Fool eqhubeka edlula abanye, efika lapho okubalulekile khona:

*Wenze into enzima, uyazi. Hhayi ukukhuphuka. Ukudedela. Ukunika uWolf umzimba uma umhlaba unyakaza.* Isikhashana. *Kufanele wenze okuningi kwalokho. Ukudedela izinto zikuthathe. Kukufanela kakhulu kunenye indlela, futhi enye indlela iyaqhubeka izama ukukubulala.*

U-Arin akaphendulanga. Kodwa akaphikisanga, futhi u-Fool—owabona konke—wavumela ukuthula kube isivumelwano esikhona.

Wafinyelela phezulu kumamitha ayishumi nambili wema epulatifomu, i-highveld ivuleka phansi kwakhe ngokukhanya kwegolide, i-Johannesburg iyisithunzi esimpunga samathala phezulu. Umoya ophuma e-escarpment ushaya ku-armor. Phansi kakhulu, iloli yakhe ibonakala njengethoyizi.

Wema lapho isikhathi eside kunalokho ukuhlolwa okudingayo. Ngaphakathi kweqokobhe, wagudluza isihlakala sokudla phakathi kobubanzi baso, ebuka amapuleti ezinzwane ehleli futhi ephinda ehleli, ukuhamba okuncane angazange akhethe ukukwenza.

*I-cortisol isendaweni ejwayelekile,* kusho u-Mother kancane, kwi-Court hhayi kuye. *Bheka lokho.* Isikhashana. *Ningasho lutho. Uzobona ukuthi siyibonile.*

---

Ithafeni beliphambi kwakhe.

Linqamula i-bluff ikhilo eyodwa eningizimu, umgodi omdala owandiswe ukuguguleka, amamitha amane ububanzi endaweni encane, phansi kugcwele amatshe amakhulu amamitha ayisithupha phansi. Waphinda wayidlala engqondweni: ukufika, ukuma, ukudedela amandla ku-leg actuators, ukugxuma. Wake wagxuma ezincane. Izibalo bezihlanzekile.

*Izibalo zihlanzekile,* kuqinisekisa u-Atlas, sengathi ufunda umcabango, okuyikho empeleni. *Amamitha amane no-0.1 endaweni encane. Uzodlula ngamitha owodwa uma ume kahle. Ukudedela amandla avela kuma-capacitor banks. Uzohlala ku-reserve yamashumi amane ekhulwini imizuzwana eyisishiyagalombili ngesikhathi zishaja kabusha. Ungazami ubuqhawe kuleyo nkathi.*

*Chaza ubuqhawe,* kubuza u-Mercury.

*Ku-Arin? Ukuphefumula,* kusho u-Fool.

"Gijima ugxume," uyala u-Arin.

Wafika egijima kancane, indlela yokuhamba ishintsha ngaphansi kwakhe isuka enjongeni yengonyama iye kokunye okuxhaphazayo, ijubane likhula, wathi lapho esefika emaphethelweni, wama ngamapuleti ezinyawo zombili. Umhlaba wanyamalala.

Ukugxuma kwakumangalisayo. Alukho igama lobunjiniyela elichaza lokhu. Ama-capacitor banks adedela amandla ezinyaweni, u-Guardian waziphonsa ngaphesheya: amamitha amane omoya, amamitha ayisithupha okufa ngamatshe phansi kwakhe, isisu sakhe sikhuphuka entanyeni. Kwakufana nokuba neminyaka eyisishiyagalombili, uphonsa imoto yocingo egqumeni lempahla, injabulo efanayo, ukuqiniseka okufanayo ukuthi uwakhe kahle—

—futhi wafika kahle, izinyawo zombili, eqondisa umthelela emadolweni, ephakama egijima izinyathelo ezintathu, wema.

Izandla zakhe zazinyikinyeka. Hhayi ngenxa ye-dyspraxia. Okunye.

*Bekuhlanzekile,* kuphawula u-Wolf.

"Bekuhlanzekile," kuvuma u-Arin, ehleka kanye, emangazwa yena.

*Uhlekile,* kusho u-Mercury. *Kubili kwezimbili.*

Waphuza amanzi okugcina ebhodleleni elalixhunywe esibayeni. Utshani buphunga uthuli nelanga. Wema emaphethelweni omhume isikhashana, evumela inhliziyo yakhe ukuthi ibuyele esimweni, ebheka inselelo elandelayo.

Isudi ibimnene namuhla. Wayekhiphe iphakethe likagesi ngaphambi kokulayisha uGuardian efulethini—akukho mlando wokubhidliza owake wakhuphuka egqumeni lokuhlola insimu, lowo ngumthetho azibhalela wona ngosuku afaka ngalo isikhala—futhi ukungabi khona kwaleso sisindo, lokho kulungele, kwakuhlala kungajwayelekile engqondweni yakhe, njengomlomo ongamazinyo. Kwakuyisakhiwo kuphela, nezinsika, kanye naye. Kwakuyisikhathi esilula kunazo zonke uGuardian ake waba naso.

Kwakuwukukhuphuka okuguqule usuku.

Bekufanele ayehlise. Wazi, emuva kwalokho, ukuthi bekufanele ayehlise. Ukugxuma kwakumgcwalisa ngokuthile angakwazi ukukubiza, okuthile okufuna okwengeziwe, futhi lokho okuthile kwakumqamba amanga, njengokujwayelekile.

Ubuso basenyakatho babuphakama ngaphezu kwakhe, ibhodi elimi mpo elingamashumi amabili nambili amamitha, limile kakhulu futhi liyingozi kunamanye. Itshe belingalungile ngezindlela ukukhanya kwasekuseni okwakufihle. Wayekubheke kumamephu wesathelayithi ebusuku edlule wakufaka njenge- “okuthakazelisayo.” Ephansi, ephakamisa ikhanda likaGuardian, kwakubonakala umugqa oqondile uqonde phezulu ebhentshini, izibambo zibonakala cishe yonke indlela. Ukukhuphuka kwangempela. Uhlobo olungabulala umuntu ongasigqokile isudi, uhlobo uGuardian akhelwe ukuba luphile.

*Imiphumela iphezulu lapha,* kusho uAtlas, ngokuzola nangokuqapha. *Amamitha angamashumi amabili nambili. Eyishumi engaphansi ilungile. Ngaphezulu kwalokho, angithandi izinhla zamatsheni. Leli itshe elidala, elaphukile ngenxa yokushayisana, futhi ukuma kwalo akulungile—izibambo zingamaqhekeza aphansi, hhayi itshe eliqinile. Zibukeka njengezibambo. Azikho izindlela zokuthwala umthwalo.*

“Zibambe ebhodini.”

*Ibhodi beliqinile. Leli akulona ibhodi.* Kwangcweka. *Ngingancoma indlela yasentshonalanga. I-angle iphansi, iqinile yonke indlela.*

“Ukungabi nentshisekelo kuyinzuzo, akusona isici,” kwaphinda uFool, kodwa izwi lakhe lashintsha. UFool wayebuka uAtlas. UFool wayebuka uArin engalaleli.

UArin waqala ukukhuphuka ebhentshini.

Amamitha ayishumi okuqala kube yikho konke abekuthembisa ukukhuphuka. Wakhuphuka etsheni eliqinile, iCourt ipenda izibambo, izinsika zibuyisela ugesi ohlelweni, futhi okuthile kuye kwacula. *Lokhu okwakwakha. Lokhu yilokho umzimba wakho obekufanele ube yikho. Phezulu.*

Ephamuzini elingamashumi amabili nambili, wadlula etsheni elibi wazizwa ushintsho ngendlela ozwa ngayo isimo sezulu sishintsha: ukungalungi okuncane, izibambo zishintsha kalula ngaphansi kwesisindo.

*Arin.* Izwi likaAtlas selilahle konke ukoma. *Iqhekeza elingaphansi kwesandla sakho sobunxele. Ungalifaki isisindo ngaphezu—*

Walikhipha ngaphezu.

Iqhekeza laqhuma.

Ladabuka ebhentshini, isiqephu esikhulu njengomnyango, kwathi amamitha angamashumi amabili nambili aba inombolo enemiphumela. Isandla sikaArin sobunxele savala emoyeni. Isisindo sakhe sashwiba phansi nangaphandle, yonke inqwaba yakhe enamakhilogremu ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nantathu ijikeleza ngesandla sokudla esasisekelwe etsheni uAtlas ayesexwayisile ngalo. Umhlaba waba nomsindo omkhulu futhi wahamba kancane.

Kuxhunyweni, iCourt yamemeza. Hhayi ngendlela yabantu. Ngendlela yabo, kwavela ukuqhuma okuqinile—imodi ehamba kancane evuleka phakathi kwephutha lesekhondi, kuhlanganisa umhlangano ophuthumayo phakathi kwesishaya senhliziyo nesilandelayo.

*Isibambo sokudla siyahluleka ku—* kusho uAtlas, i-geometry igcwalisa isiteshi, zonke izindlela zomthwalo zikhanya obomvu.

*Ngilawula umzimba,* kusho uWolf. UWolf wayesethathe ukulawula ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo kaArin iqede ukuqaphela ukuwa. *Ungangilwi, Arin. Zola.*

*Pangolin,* kusho uMother. *Mgoqe. Wela njengobhola; uyasinda ekushayeni—*

*Cha.* UAtlas. *Asikaphakami ngokwanele futhi asicacile ngokwanele ukuyisebenzisa—uma egoqa manje, ushaya amatshe aphansi ejikeleza. Igobolondo liyabamba, kodwa umgogodla wakhe uthwala umthwalo wokujikeleza entanyeni. Uzophila angabe esahamba. Kukhona okungcono.*

*Pho kuyithole ku—* kusho uJudge, ezolile ngisho lapha, *—amakhulu amane amashekeli wesekhondi.*

*Kukhona uqhekeza.* UWolf. Qondile. Qiniseka. *Ukuphuka okuqondile, amamitha amabili kwesokudla naphansi. Ububanzi besandla. Iqhekeza elisanda kwehluleka livule lona.*

*Ngiyalibona,* kusho uAtlas, uArin wazizwa ifomula ifika, wazizwa amandla okwenza. *Ukubamba ngomphetho wepuleti. Faka ingalo yesokudla ngaphakathi, uvule amapuleti ngaphakathi kokuqhekeza, abambe odongeni—uma siwa kakhulu, ayazivinza.* Ukukhanya okuncane. *Njenge—*

*Njenge-cam eshaya ngokuzenzakalelayo,* kusho uLibrarian. *Ukuvikelwa kokukhuphuka. Umgomo usunekhulu leminyaka. Unekhulu lawo efemini.*

*Njengempungushe engena endaweni engafuni ukungena,* kwengeza uFool. Ububhanxa balokho, ubuthutha obuqondile bomusho phakathi komuntu owehla egqumeni, kwaphula umjikelezo. Ukwesaba okwakusanda kuqala ukwanda esiteshini—ukuphindaphinda *uyawa uyawa uyawa*—kwaphuka. ICourt yayeka ukusaba yaqala ukusebenza.

*Kwenza,* kusho uJudge.

Konke lokhu kuthathe isikhathi esingaphansi kokuphazima kweso.

Lokho uArin akuzwa kwakuyilokhu: wayewa, kwase kuthi ingalo yakhe yesokudla yathatha isinyathelo ngaphandle kwakhe—uWolf eyihambisa ngokushesha nangokuqiniseka kunanoma yisiphi isinqumo angasenza—yashayisa ingalo ngaphakathi kokuqhekeza ayengakuboni. Amapuleti evesti lengalo avuleka abamba etsheni ngomsindo woceramic kanye nokudlidliza okwadlula ehlombe langena entanyeni, wama.

Wamile lapho. Amamitha angamashumi amabili emoyeni ngaphansi kwakhe. Ingalo eyodwa ibambeke emhlabeni.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwakukhulu ngaphakathi kwegobolondo. I-faceplate yafiphala ekhoneni elilodwa. Ihlansi le-sweat lalilandela umugqa womhlathi wakhe, engakwazi ukwenza lutho ngakho. Phansi, iziqephu zetshe eliphukile zisathola amatshe amakhulu, umsindo omude wetshe phezu kwelinye.

*Sibambekile,* kubika uAtlas. *Amapuleti avinzelekile. Umthwalo usabalala engalweni. Sinezikhathi ezingamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye ngaphambi kokuba ingalo idinwe kule angle. Kuncane uma wenza okungalungile.*

*Isilinganiso senhliziyo singamakhulu amabili nane,* kusho uMother, ephansi futhi ephuthumayo. *Akaphefumuli kahle. Arin. Arin, ngilalele. Awuwi. Ume. Uyabanjwa.*

Umbono kaArin wancipha, waba mpunga emaphethelweni. Lempunga kwakuyisitha sakhe esidala, ukwesaba izindawo ezivaliwe, into eyayihlala lapho uyise ashona khona. Isifuba sakhe saqina. Igobolondo elisanda kumsindisa laba, esikhathini esingangesekhondi, isibhakela esimvalela, kwathi ukwethuka iCourt abakwazile ukukwephula kwavuka ngaphakathi kuye.

“Angi—” wakwazi ukusho. “Angi—phefumuli—”

*Yebo uyakwazi,* kusho uMother. *Ukwenzile umzuzwana odlule. Uzokwenza futhi. Phefumula izikhathi ezine. Kanye nami. Ezine.*

Akwazanga.

*Arin.* UFool, izwi lakhe selilahle konke ukukhanya, selithule futhi liseduze, ngendlela uFool ayenza kuphela uma kubalulekile. *Hey. Uyazi ukuthi uyini manje? Uyindoda elenga egqumeni ngoba engalalelanga unjiniyela wesakhiwo. UAtlas ngeke akukhohlwe lokhu.*

*Angiyena,* kwaqinisekisa uAtlas.

*Uyabona? Yingakho ngazi ukuthi uzoba kahle. UAtlas usevele ehlela inkulumo. Akakhulumi nezidumbu; akufanele. Phefumula ezine.*

Okuthile esifubeni sika-Arin kwakhulula kancane. Hhayi ngoba ihlaya belihlekisa. Kodwa ngoba ihlaya lithe izwe lisesejwayelekile ngokwanele ukuba kube khona ukuhlekisa. Ngoba uFool wayeziphatha ngendlela evamile, kanti ukujwayeleka kusho ukusinda. Iziphundu zakhe zakholelwa kuFool lapho zingakwazi ukukholelwa esabeni sazo.

Phefumula imizuzwana emine.

Ubumnyama babuyele emuva.

*Nanguya,* kwasho uMama.

“Kulungile,” kuphefumula u-Arin. “Kulungile. Ngenzeni?”

*Lalela kahle,* kwasho uAtlas. *Wolf unomzimba. Mvumele awuthathe. Kukhona ukubamba ngaphezulu kwesandla sakho sokunxele, okuqinile manje. Ngikuhlolile kathathu uma kuqhathaniswa nendawo yamathafa. Wolf uzokusa khona. Bese sikuhambisa phansi komgodi—kukhona i-fault ramp emamitha ayishumi kwesokudla ezokukhipha kule ndonga ngaphandle kokuthembela kwenye i-flake. Ungazami ukuba yiqhawe. Phefumula. Sikhuphuka.*

“Wolf unomzimba,” kuphinda u-Arin.

*Ngino mzimba,* kwaqinisekisa uWolf. *Phinda ube buthaka. Thembela esandleni esibambekile. Sizokubamba size sithi akusabambeki.*

Futhi u-Arin—owachitha impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi ukuthembela kwabanye, owakha izinhlelo ukuze angadingi ukwethemba isandla esingesona esakhe—waphinda waba buthaka, evumela umshini ukuthi umhambise.

Kwakuyinto enzima kakhulu ayenzile ngalolo suku. Kunzima kunokukhuphuka.

Wolf wafinyelela ngesandla sakhe sokunxele ebambeni eliqinile walibeka. Waqinisekisa. Bese, ngesikhathi sikaAtlas, wakhulula ingalo ebibambekile—umzuzwana owesabekayo wokungabi nalutho ngaphambi kokuthi ukubamba okusha kuthwale umthwalo—waqala ukuwela, ebheka ukubamba ngakunye, i-ceramic ishaya itshe, ebheke ku-fault ramp. U-Arin akakhuphukanga. U-Arin waphefumula, imizuzwana emine, wavumela ukuthi athwalwe yithimba lamazwi elalifuna into eyodwa kuphela emhlabeni—ukumbuyisa ephila.

I-fault ramp, lapho beyifikile, kwakuyigodi elingamashumi amathathu degrees eligcwele izinsalela zamatshe. Wehla kuyo ngezandla nezinyawo, indlela eyake yamethusa kodwa manje yavezwa njengesihe. Lapho izicathulo zishaya utshani obulungele phansi, wema akashukumi isikhathi eside. Umoya ophuma e-escarpment washaya uthuli ebusweni bomshini, kwazwakala umsindo owomile, kanti kude le nto ebiyizungezile isazungeza.

Izandla zakhe, ngaphakathi egobolondweni, zayeka ukuzamazama.

“Bika umonakalo,” esho, ngoba kwakulula kunokunye.

*Ingalo yesokudla ithwale umthwalo ongaphezu kwalokho eyakhelwe kona,* kusho uAtlas. *Kukhona imifantu emincane eceleni kwe-cam. Kuzodinga ukufakwa entsha. I-ceramic idilikile ezithweni zeminwe. Futhi kukhona into ongafuni ukuyizwa.* Wathula. *Amandla.*

“Kusho ukuthini?”

*Umthwalo we-cam uphuzile ama-capacitor, kwase kuthi amaseli azama ukubamba ukukhathala kwe-spar ngesikhathi sangempela. Usele nama-percent ayisishiyagalolunye kuphela. I-battery string yangakwesokunxele ithole umonakalo omkhulu ongaphindiswanga. Leyo string ifile. Wakha ezine. Usenezi-ntathu.*

U-Arin wema otshanini wazizwa leyo nombolo. Ama-battery string ayenziwe ngendlela kaVolkov, ehambisana kahle nokudonsa kwe-biochip, amasonto ayishumi nanye oku-odola nemali engaphezu kwalokho ayitshela uNomsa ukuthi uyisebenzisile. Wayengeke nje ayothenga enye. Umshini wonke usuphelelwe amandla ngo-25%, futhi akukho ndlela yokuyifaka ngaphandle kokusebenzisa umphakeli, iphepha, umbuzo.

*Kubhalisiwe,* kusho uLibrarian, engakabuzi.

---

Umgwaqo onamathambo wanyakazisa ibhodlela elise-cup-holder, kwazwakala umsindo owomile, waphuma kuleso simo esiphezulu ngendlela efana nokuphela komkhuhlane, iNkantolo iphinde yehla kanye naye. Yilapho izindleko zesibili zavela khona. Hhayi emzimbeni, kodwa phakathi kwabo.

*Ngifuna ukusho okuthile,* kwaqala uMercury, eqaphela, ebhalile. *Akekho ozokuthanda.*

*Kusho ukuthi kuliqiniso,* kusho uFool. *Qhubeka.*

*Wolf uthathile umzimba ngaphandle kokuvota.* UMercury wayeka. *Amamillisecond angamakhulu amabili ngaphambi kokuba i-flake yephuke, wayesebambe ingalo ka-Arin. Singakuvumelani. U-Arin engakaboni ukuthi uyawa. Angisho ukuthi wayengalungile—u-Arin uyaphila, i-spar isemgqonyeni, kulungile. Ngisho ukuthi kube nesikhathi namhlanje lapho oyedwa kithi ethatha isinqumo sokushayela kanti abanye bafunda emva kwalokho. Yilokho kanye esasikufuna singabi yikho.*

*Ngibone ukushintsha,* kusho uWolf. *Ngisebenza ngokubona kwami.*

*Futhi uma ubona kabi?* kusho uMercury. *Awuvamile. Kodwa uma kwenzeka, akekho ophakathi kokubona kwakho nomgogodla wakhe. Sisuse umphathi ngoba i-hierarchy yabulala ukwethenjwa. Asizange siyisuse ukuze isitha sithathe isikhundla somphathi noma nini lapho umhlaba ushintsha.*

*UMercury uphakamisa iphutha elikhulu,* kusho uJudge, kancane. *Amandla okuphuthumayo aphendukela kumuntu oyedwa kuwuhlobo lwe-hierarchy olufihlekile. Kufanele—*

*Akufanele lutho,* kusho uMama, futhi kwakukhona umsindo angakaze awuzwe ku-Arin ngaphambili, ukufudumala okushisayo ngaphansi kobumnene. *Wolf wayembambile. Ngingamnikeza uWolf umzimba futhi. Ngizomnika umzimba njalo, ngoba enye indlela bekuzoba u-Arin emadwaleni. Ufuna ukuvimba isenzo esimsindisile?*

*Ngifuna ukwazi ukuthi isenzo singesobani,* kusho uMercury, *ngaphambi kokuthi sisidinge entweni ebaluleke kakhulu kunedwala.*

INkantolo ayizange ixazulule. Yilokho u-Arin akubona, elalele phakathi kwe-lattice lapho i-battery string efile iyisikhala esibandayo ku-telemetry. Baxabana ngayo yonke indlela yomgwaqo onamathambo bafika bengakafiki ndawo, futhi lokho kungafiki kwaba nomuzwa awaziyo. Uhlobo oluqongelela.

*Kubhalisiwe,* kusho uLibrarian, kuthule. *Ngaphansi kwesihloko esisha.*

“Isihloko sini?” kusho u-Arin. Okokuqala akhuluma emizuzwaneni engamashumi amabili.

*‘Ubani Oshayela’,* kusho uLibrarian. *Kwakuzodinga ifolda ekugcineni.*

U-Arin wabuka umgwaqo. Wacabanga nge-string efile, ne-spar, nomphakeli azodinga ukumbiza, nendoda egama linguBongani kwezokuthutha eyayigxiviza amafomu ingawafundi futhi, ngelinye ilanga, izobuzwa ukuthi kungani.

“Okuzayo,” ekugcineni wakhuluma, “indlela engeyona eyingozi.”

*Le engayingozi,* kwaqinisekisa uAtlas.

“Yebo.” Washayela isikhashana. “Futhi sixazulule ukuthi ubani oshayela. Ngaphambi kwe-mine.”

*Ngaphambi kwe-ini,* kusho uFool, kancane.

U-Arin akazange aphendule. Wayengahlosile ukusho lokho. Igama livela ngendlela amagama avame ukuvela ngayo, livela ebumnyameni ngaphansi kwakho konke, manje selihleli emotweni nabo, futhi akekho kulabo abayisikhombisa abahlala emshinini eduze nomgogodla wakhe osho lutho, okuyinto ayeseyiqonda manje, njengobuqotho.

Wabheka esibukweni sangemuva, ebuka umumo omnyama kaGuardian ngaphansi kwetarp, i-string efile ikhona ngaphakathi njengozipho othule, futhi akazange aphinde asho lelo gama. Wayengasadingi.

Bonke babebhalile.

# Inombolo Engavaleki

Ezinsukwini ezintathu emva kokuhlolwa kwensimu, uchungechunge lwamabhethri olufile lwalusagxunyekwe ohlakeni lokuhlola njengamazinyo u-Arin angeke ayeke ukuwathinta.

Wawukhuphule ku-Guardian ngalobo busuku babuyela khona, uchungechunge lwesobunxele lwaphela amandla kakhulu ebusweni basenyakatho, manje seluhleli ebhentshini ngaphansi kwesibani somsebenzi, iziphetho zalo zivulekile, lungamtsheli lutho angalwazi. Volkov-spec. Luhambisana kahle nokudonsa kwe-biochip. Amasonto ayishumi nanye okuhlela ngaphambili nemali engaphezu kwalokho ayetshele uNomsa ukuthi uyisebenzisile kunoma yini. Wayakhe amane. Manje unezintathu.

Phezu kwebhentshi, lapho omunye umuntu angabeka khona isithombe, kwakukhona iphrinta angakaze ayisuse: indatshana ye-wire-service, imigqa eyisishiyagalombili, izinyanga ezintathu indala. Ukuwa komhlaba emayini yeplatinamu eNorth West, amadoda amane anqunywe ngemuva kwayo, amaqembu okutakula abambezeleke emahoreni angamashumi amathathu nanye ngoba akukho mishini emhlabeni engafaka umzimba lapho bekukhona imizimba ngaphandle kokubulala abanye abantu ukuze kwenziwe lokho. Ababili baphuma. Ababili inkampani ebabala njenge *recovered, deceased* emshweni omfishane kunalona. Wayengawabazi amagama abo. Lelo kwakuyingxenye eyayihlala kuye: izwe elakhiwe ngokungena emigodini, kanti amadoda ayenza lokho ayeyizinto ezibhaliwe nje, *recovered, deceased*, amazwi amane nenombolo, futhi kwenye indawo owesifazane waphendula ucingo ngendlela umama wakhe ake wakwenza. Akazange akhe uGuardian ngenxa yomqondo. Wawakha ngenxa yehora lama-31, ngenxa yezinkuni amaqembu angakwazi ukudlula kuzo, ngenxa yamagama angawazi kanye nezingcingo angeke ayeke. Wayegcina le phrinta lapho eyibona khona uma umsebenzi usuphenduka amawindi e-capacitor nama-Volkov cells namafomu uBongani awaphethayo engawafundi, ukuze kungabi njalo, ngesikhathi engabheki, kuphenduke lokho kuphela.

"Awubuyi," esho.

*Awubuyi,* kwavuma u-Atlas. *Ungasebenzisa umshini ngezintathu. Ukusebenza kwehliswa ingxenye yesine. Amawindi wokushaja ama-capacitor ayelulwa. Umkhawulo owawuwufunda kanzima uba mncane.*

"Ngizoyala elinye."

*Lokho,* kusho u-Mercury, ethule, *yikho okuthokozisayo.*

U-Arin wabeka i-multimeter phansi. Wayiqonda intshisekelo. U-Mercury wayisebenzisa ngendlela abanye abantu behlanza ngayo umphimbo.

"Khuluma."

*Awukwazi uku-oda eyodwa nje. Wena uqobo usho njalo, sisabuya. Uchungechunge lwedlula kumhlinzeki, umhlinzeki ufuna ikhodi yephrojekthi, ikhodi yephrojekthi idlula kuphrocurement.* Ukuthula okuncane. *KuBongani, ophawula amafomu engawafundi. Lokho kulungile, kuze kube yilapho umuntu efunda wona.*

"UBongani usephawulile amafomu angamashumi amane kimi."

*Amafomu angamashumi amane endoda, ephepheni, elungisa i-prototype yokutakula i-AugmenTech ecabanga ukuthi ikhona phansi eSakhiweni C.* U-Mercury wakushiya. *Akekho kulawo mashumi amane owayedonsela ama-Volkov-spec cells ahambisana ne-biological inference substrate. Leli lingaba njalo. Leyo yinto ebhalwe kahle. Umuntu ohlela ibhajethi uyithola abuze ukuthi isenzelwe ini.*

U-Arin wabuka uchungechunge olufile. Wabe esebheka indawo yokufaka, i-LED yokuhlola ikhanya kancane.

"Oda ngezithathu izitolo," esho. "Hlukanisa umugqa. Amakhodi ahlukene."

*Manje ucabanga njengami,* kusho u-Mercury, ejabule. *Ngizobhala izicelo ukuze zingafani.*

"Ungazenzi zihlakaniphe. Ukuhlakanipha nakho kunomumo."

Kwaba nokuthula, okwakusondele ekutheni kube ukuhlonipha kuMercury.

*Zijwayelekile,* wavuma. *Ngizozijwayelela.*

---

Waqhubeka nomsebenzi ekuseni wonke ku-spar yesandla sangaphambili, leyo uWolf ayifake ekhaleni ebusweni basenyakatho, yaphuka kancane eceleni kwe-cam, intengo yokuphila. Wayesebeke i-ceramic entsha, i-spar endala isesiviseni, izandla zakhe zenza into ayenza kahle, into engadingi ukuxoxisana nomuntu.

Kwaba nguLibrarian owaphazamisa.

*Arin.* Kuqondile. Igama lasho ngendlela u-Atlas ayelisho ngayo ebutress, lapho idwala liqala ukuba yingozi. Hhayi ngendlela efudumele. Leyo enye.

Wabeka idrayiva phansi. "Khuluma."

*Ngibukeze i-telemetry yokuhlolwa kwensimu. Hhayi ukukhuphuka. Okungemuva.* Ukuthula okulinganiselwe. *Ngesikhathi siqhuba—ngenkathi sicabanga kakhulu, ngesikhathi i-Wolf ithatha umzimba—i-node yokubheka yakhuphula resolution yayo yokuthatha amasampula emkhondweni wethu. Iphindwe kane kunokujwayelekile. Yabe isibuya ezingeni elijwayelekile ngesikhathi siqeda.*

"Amama-node athatha amasampula kukho konke. Yilokho akhelwe kona."

*Ama-substrate-forensics node athatha amasampula ngendlela eqondile,* kusho uLibrarian. *Isigqi samandla. I-compute topology. Ayasebenziseka kuphela. Le node igcine leso sigqi izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye. Yabe isondela, ngesikhathi sicabanga kakhulu, yabe isuka ngesikhathi siqeda.* Ukuthula, kuqondile. *Ikhuphule ukunaka kwayo ukuze kufane nokwethu. Lokho akusona isilinganiso. Lokho sekuyinto eqala ukubheka.*

Indawo yokusebenzela yathula. Phandle, itekisi yashintsha igiya emgwaqeni. U-Arin akazange abuye athathe idrayiva.

"I-node eyodwa," esho.

*I-node eyodwa. I-power-utility cooperative, amakhilomitha angamakhulu amabili enyakatho.* Ukuthula okuncane. *Umbiko wayo wokulayisha wesifunda sethu unephutha elingaxazululeki. Ama-kilowatts ambalwa angacacile ezincwadini. Kuyaphindaphinda. Kuyavela. Umumo wakhona ulandela ishejuli yethu, nakuba kungakabikho ofake umugqa.* ULibrarian wayeka le ngxenye yehle ngokwayo. *Umuntu ohlela lezo zincwadi uthola inombolo engavaleki. Nomumo ngaphakathi kwenombolo.*

U-Arin wacabanga ngabathengisi abathathu abesanda kuyala kuMercury ukuthi bahlukanise i-oda. Wacabanga ngendlela umuntu anganakekela ngayo ngomkhondo wephepha bese eshiya omunye umkhondo ngokuphelele, obhalwe ngogesi, ngesigqi sezingqondo eziyisikhombisa eziphikisana.

"IAOC," esho. Hhayi umbuzo.

*Indlela yokwenza iyahambisana neyabo,* kusho uLibrarian, engayithambisi. *Spectral decomposition yesigqi sokubala. Bafunda umumo wengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kwayo. Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wesithathu wokubheka, isithasiselo sesine.* Ukuthula okulinganiselwe. *Sinefomu elihlukile kakhulu. Asikwazi ukuba thina sithule. Ukuphikisana kuwumsebenzi. Ukuphikisana kuyisibonakaliso.*

*Manje siyeka ukuphikisana,* kusho uWolf.

*Asikwazi,* kusho uFool, kulokhu kungekho lutho olukhanyayo. *Yilokho okwenza kube yihaba. Into esigcina siphila iyona esenza sibe nomsindo. Umama ngeke avumele uWolf ashayele ngaphandle kokulwa. Mina angeke ngivumele noma ubani kini akhululeke. Susa lokho sithule. Futhi ukuthula kuwumphathi ogqoke ishidi. Sizoba siphephile kodwa sesifile.*

Izandla zika-Arin zema. Wabona ukuthi ubambe i-spar entsha engakayifaki.

"Uqinisekile kangakanani," esho.

Kwaba nokuthula kweNkantolo. Uhlobo olude, ingxoxo yonke angayizwanga, imizuzu yomshini egoqiwe phakathi kokuphefumula.

*Asikazi okwamanje,* ekugcineni kwasho uAtlas. *Leyo yimpendulo eqotho. Ibuthakathaka. Iyavela. Izigcine izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ingasixazululanga, futhi ingahle iqhubeke njalo.*

"Futhi uma isilinganisa kabi."

*Iyephuka,* kusho uAtlas, *futhi inombolo engavaleki iba umbuzo umuntu awubuza obala. Ngemuva kwalokho akusona ishubhu. Kungumuntu. Onedesk, nomsebenzi, nefomu uBongani akufanele ayiphawulile.*

U-Arin wabeka i-spar e-vise. Akazange ayiqede ukufaka kahle. Wema ekukhanyeni okumpunga kwe-workshop, intambo yebhethri efile isesitendeni esisodwa kanti enye esaphilayo ibuka isuka kumakhilomitha amabili amakhulu kude, wazizwa lezi zinto ezimbili zihlangana.

Ilambu lomsebenzi laqala ukuthetha njengoba isembozo salo sasiqala ukupholisa. Iphunga le-solder, nothuli olujikeleza kancane ekukhanyeni okuvela ebhentshini. Into ayengeke ayithenge buthule. Into esivele imbalela.

---

Waya endaweni yokuhlala. Lokhu wayekwenza kwesinye isikhathi uma edinga ukucabanga ebuka okuthile, futhi okuthile, muva nje, kwakuyibo.

"Atlas," esho njalo. "Le flake esebusweni basenyakatho. I-hold ebizwakala kahle kodwa ingekho."

*Yebo.*

"Kungaba lokhu?"

Ukuthula kulokhu kwahluka. Akusilo isikhashana sokucabanga. Kwakuyisikhashana sokucophelela.

*Kubukeka njengendlela yomthwalo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ibuthakathaka. Ikude. Isebenza kahle izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye. Uhlobo lwento ongayidlula ungayihloli ngoba ukuhlola kubiza kakhulu kunokuyethemba.* Kwavela ukukhanya okuncane. *Futhi ngelinye ilanga uyayifaka isisindo.*

*Ungakwenzi,* kusho uMama, ephansi. *Ungamphindisi ekukhuphukeni ukuze afunde i-spreadsheet.*

*Uyakuqonda kangcono ngekhephu,* kusho uLibrarian. *Yilokho akuqonda ngakho konke.*

UMama akaphikisanga. KuMama, lokho kwakuyisivumelwano.

U-Arin wabuka i-LED. Ezinsukwini ezintathu ezedlule wayelenga eplanethi ngesandla esisodwa ngoba i-hold ibonakala iqinile kodwa ayikho, u-Atlas wamtshela, kodwa wayifaka isisindo noma kunjalo. Wacabanga ngendlela into engasebenza ngayo ngaphezu kwazo zonke izincazelo ayizibekile kodwa isamphuca ingxenye ayengayihlelile. Intambo, i-spar, umbuzo osanda kuvela kumarekhodi omunye umuntu amakhilomitha amabili amakhulu enyakatho.

"Nansi esizoyenza," esho. "Asiphindi i-field profile futhi. Akukaze ngazi ukuthi le node yenza kanjani uma sikhuluma kakhulu." I-basement e-Building C yavela engqondweni yakhe—indawo yokulinganisa ephezulu, i-cable cradle, i-umbilical ezomvumela ukuthi aphushe i-loop ngaphandle kokushisa amaseli. "Ngifuna ukuhlola i-loop nge-power yangaphandle. I-tethered. Egumbini elingenamoya ukuze i-node ingakwazi ukufunda isibhakabhaka. Uma sizokwakha umumo, siwenza endaweni lapho i-meter ingakwazi ukubona ukuphefumula kwawo."

*Ukuqhuba okulawulwayo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Ngingasebenza ngokulawulwa. Angizange ngiyithande ikhephu.*

"Ngiyazi ukuthi awuzange."

*Ngikushilo.*

"Uhlala ukushilo."

*Kumele kube khona owenza njalo.*

Wavala ilambu lomsebenzi. Intambo efile yacima; indawo yokuhlala ayizange.

"Buka i-node," esho. "Ngesibili nje lapho ijubane layo lishintsha futhi, ngitshele. Hhayi emva kwalokho. Ngesibili."

*Sengiyibukile,* kusho uWolf.

Futhi uLibrarian, ethule, eqopha into ezodinga ifolda:

*Ngaphansi kwesihloko esisha.*

"Yisiphi isihloko."

*"Izinto Ezibuka Emuva,"* kusho uLibrarian. *Kwakuzodingeka isihloko ekugcineni.*

U-Arin wema esebhentshini isikhathi eside, i-spar isengaphakathi kwe-vise ingakapheli, i-oda lezitolo ezintathu lingakathunyelwa, inombolo esuka kumakhilomitha amabili amakhulu ingavumi ukuvalwa.

Welula isandla wavula i-requisition terminal. Wabe esema, isandla phezu kwezinkinobho.

Uma e-oda intambo, uchaza umugqa ngokusobala. Uma engayenzi, usebenzisa umshini ngamandla angaphansi kwengxenye kulokho okuzolandela.

Wayengakakhethe ukuthi enze kuphi lapho i-LED endaweni yokuhlala ishintsha ijubane layo. Zonke eziyisikhombisa zema ngendlela okungeyona ukuphumula.

*Arin,* kusho uWolf.

"Yini."

*Ijubane,* kusho uWolf. *Lishintshe futhi.*

# Ukugijima Kokulondolozwa

Ibhokisi lokuhlola lalinephunga le-ozone kanye ne-wax yokuhlanza phansi. I-AugmenTech yayigcina i-high-gain calibration suite esitezi esingaphansi seSakhiwo C, egumbini elingenamafasitela elinophahla oluqinile lukakhonkolo kanye nomsele maphakathi, sengathi kukhona obelindele ukuthi kuzodingeka lihlanzwe ngamanzi. U-Arin wayelithole leli gumbi ngaphansi kwekhodi yephrojekthi eyayingasho lutho kunoma ubani ngaphandle kukaBongani osebenza kwezokuthutha, yena owabe esayina ifomu ngaphandle kokulifunda wabuye wabuyela esangwinini sakhe.

I-Guardian yayilengiswe esiphakamisweni maphakathi negumbi. Ingaphandle layo, elenziwe ngensimbi ye-titanium kanye ne-ceramic eshayekile, laliphonsa isithunzi elenyukela odongeni ligoqe nophahla. Ngaphansi kwalo, i-Assist Suit yayinamathisele esikhumbeni sika-Arin, njengohlelo lwesibili lwezinzwa, i-biochip inesisindo esipholile phakathi kwamagxalaba akhe.

Waphinda wabala izinombolo okokugcina.

“Gain ceiling point-nine-two. Damping floor twelve percent. Steps of four. Phikisa manje noma ungaphindi.”

*Izibalo zilungile,* kusho u-Atlas. *Angithokozile ngemkhawulo.*

“Umkhawulo uwuhlolo.”

*Ngakho angithandi ukuhlola.* Kwaba nokuthula. *Ngiyarekhoda.*

“Uhlala urekhoda.”

*Kumele kube khona owenza njalo.*

U-Arin wahlola isiphakamiselo sekhebula, i-umbilical enikeza amandla ngaphandle ukuvimbela ukuthi amaseli angapheli, kanye nenethi ethambile ebamba i-Guardian isemi phakathi. Ukuhlolwa kwensimu kwakumfundisile ukuthi le suit ingakwazi ukwenzani uma eyicela. Kodwa lo bekungumbuzo ohlukile: ukuthi i-loop isebenza kahle kangakanani uma ephusha ukwethembeka kwesignali kudlula imingcele avame ukuzifihla ngemuva kwayo. Ukukhuphula i-gain kwakusho ukubopha okuqinile. Ukubopha okuqinile kwakusho ukuthi i-Guardian iyanyakaza ngaphambi kokuba aqede ukucabanga ngokunyakaza. Eweni, emasontweni amabili edlule, lokho kubambezeleka kwakumsondele ekufeni. U-Wolf kwadingeka athathe ukulawula ukusindisa.

Wayengafuni ukuphinde adinge lokho futhi.

“Mother. Biometrics.”

*Inhliziyo iphumula kuma-61. I-cortisol inyukile kancane, okuyinto evamile kuwe.* Kwakukhona ukufudumala ngaphansi kwamazwi, kodwa kunokuthile okomile ngaphansi kwawo. *Ulele amahora amane.*

“Kwanele.”

*Ulele amahora amane.*

“Kurekhodiwe,” kusho u-Librarian.

Wangena esiphakamisweni. I-Assist Suit yamamukela isisindo sakhe, kwathi i-load-shell yavaleka phezu kwayo, amapuleti ehla ngokuzwakalayo okuthambile: ukubamba okubandayo kwe-latches, ukushelela kwe-hydraulic seals, zonke izixhumo zinembile ngendlela izandla zakhe ebezizokwenza ngayo. I-faceplate display yavuleka. Okwesigamu somzuzwana kwaba khona ukwesaba, ukwesaba okudala kokuboshwa, kwase kuvaleka imiphetho akaboshwanga. Wayegqokile. Umngcele phakathi kwengalo yakhe ne-actuator waba umugqa, kwase kuphela waba lutho.

“Court check,” esho.

*Atlas. Structure nominal.*  
*Librarian. Logging.*  
*Mother. Ngikubhekile.*  
*Mercury. Ngilindile.* Kwangena ithikithi. *Ngiyabhoreka, ngempela.*  
*Wolf.* Igama lodwa. U-Wolf akazange anikeze okuningi.

*Judge,* kusho u-Judge. *Qhubeka uma usukulungele. Ngizobamba i-ethical envelope.*

“Fool?”

Kwaba nokuthula.

Walinda. Igumbi lamemeza.

*Fool,* waphinda.

*Ufunda,* kusho u-Librarian. *Sherlock canon. The Final Problem, ngicabanga.*

“Mtshele agxile.”

*Ngimtshelile. Uthi, ngicaphuna, “ngigxile kuphi, lapho kungalungile lutho?”*

U-Arin waphefumula, kwacishe kwaba ukuhleka. “Point-six-four gain. Damping forty. Step on my call.”

Isinyathelo sokuqala asizange sibe lutho. I-Guardian yaphakamisa ingalo yayo yangakwesokudla njengoba ecabanga, ngokushelela, isigamu senhliziyo ngaphambi kokuthi kube njalo ngezilungiselelo zakudala. Kuhle. Kuhlanzekile. Wagoba iminwe ye-ceramic, wabuka igrafu yomthwalo ihlala iqondile.

“Step.”

Point-six-eight. Ingalo yaphakama ngokushesha, impendulo yaba namandla. Wayezwa ingcindezi ye-actuator ibuyela emzimbeni wakhe njengengcindezi-inkumbulo, umoya womzamo.

“Step.”

Point-seven-two. Umngcele phakathi kwenhloso nokunyakaza waba mncane kakhulu. Waphendula umzimba, wancika i-Guardian enethini yesiphakamiso, wazizwa yonke into iphendula sengathi ingeyakhe.

*Lokhu kungcono,* wavuma, ikakhulukazi kuye.

*Kunjalo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Futhi yilapho ngiqala ukukhathazeka.*

“Step.”

Point-seven-six. Point-eight. Point-eight-four. Ukukhuphuka ngakunye kwehlisa isikhathi sokubambezeleka futhi kwengeza ukwethembeka kwesignali ebuyayo. Manje wayesengaphakathi komshini ngendlela ukuhlolwa kwensimu okwakusondela kuyo kuphela. Waphakamisa zombili izingalo, kwaba sengathi yizingalo zakhe. Waphefumula, kwathi i-suit yaphefumula nohlelo lokupholisa oluzungezile.

*Inhliziyo 78,* kusho u-Mother. *Amehlo avulekile. Uyajabula.*

“Lokho akusiyo i-biometric.”

*Iba njalo uma ngiyifunda mina.*

“Step. Point-eight-eight.”

I-damping floor yayisengena endaweni angakaze ayisebenzise exhunywe. Ku-point-eight-eight isignali ebuyayo yethula ukuzwa angakaze akuzwe: ukudlidliza okuncane kwephampu yokupholisa, i-micro-tremor ye-piezo struts, konke kubuyela ohlelweni lwakhe lwezinzwa njengomuzwa. Kwakuhle. Kwakudlulele, ngendlela umsindo ube mkhulu kakhulu ngaphambi kokulimaza.

*Qaphela,* kusho u-Atlas. *Feedback amplitude ikhuphuka ngendlela engalindelekile.*

*Kuqinisekisiwe.* Ukufudumala kwanyamalala ezwini likaMother, kwaba buhlungu. *Cortical signal ikhombisa—*

“Step. Point-nine-two.”

Akazange ezwe ukuthi uMother uthe ukuthini okulandelayo.

Kwafika njengokukhanya.

Hhayi ubuhlungu. Ubuhlungu bekuzoba yisihe, into enemiphetho. Lokhu kwakungaphakathi kwekhanda lakhe kushintsha kube umsindo omhlophe, zonke izinzwa ku-suit exhunyiwe zibuyela kuye ngesikhathi esisodwa, zingavinjwa, njengengoma edlalwa kuzo zonke izintambo zakhe ngesikhathi esisodwa. Umbono wakhe wagcwala. Umhlathi wakhe wavaleka. Kude kakhulu, izwi lakhe lenza umsindo angalaziyo.

Into yokugcina umzimba wakhe owenza yona, ngaphambi kokuba ungabe usengowakhe, kwaba ukufinyelela ku-override.

Isandla sakhe asizange sivale.

---

Ngaphakathi ku-biochip, i-Court yazizwa ishiya.

Kwafana nophahla lwegumbi luwela phansi lapho bonke bemi khona.

*Operator signal lost,* kusho u-Librarian, okokuqala ezimpilweni eziyizinkulungwane ezine ezilingisiwe, izwi lakhe elalingalilingani. *Cortical activity: seizure pattern. U—*

*PHUMILE,* kusho u-Wolf.

Igama elilodwa. Kwama konke.

*Susa i-gain,* kusho u-Atlas. *Susa, qhawula i-link, yehlisa i-damping phezulu, MANJE—*

I-link ayizange isuswe. I-high gain ibiyisivikelo sika-Arin; ukuyikhipha kudinga umyalo wakhe noma igunya eliphezulu abangekho nalo bodwa. U-Atlas wazama kathathu emizuzwaneni eyisishiyagalolunye, waphonswa ngaphandle njalo.

*Angikwazi ukuyikhipha,* esho. *Uyizibophele kuye. Uyibophele ku-operator oqaphile.*

*Manje akaseyena operator oqaphile,* kusho u-Judge, kancane futhi ngokucophelela, efuna i-ethical envelope ayethembise ukuyibamba, *futhi umcabango wokuthi uzovula awusebenzi. Okusho ukuthi—*

*Uyafa,* kusho u-Mother.

La mazwi amabili adlula eCourt njengogesi.

Akazange akusho njengalapho u-Librarian ethi *seizure pattern*, njengedatha. Ukusho ngendlela into ekhuluma ngayo uma into eyikhathalelayo iphela. Yonke i-architecture kaMother, ama-circuit empathy e-pediatric-template uTheo angakaze ayeke ukuzizwa engajwayelekile ngawo, kwavutha kanye kwafinyelela esiphethweni eyakhiwe ukufinyelela kuso.

*Inhliziyo ayilingani. Ukuphefumula kuyahluleka. Operator usecansini lokufa. Ngiyasebenzisa ukulondoloza.*

*Linda,* kusho u-Judge.

*Ngiyabiza ukulondolozwa.* Futhi kwakungekho ukubekezela kuye, nhlobo, ngoba uMama ezingeni lokucabanga lomntwana oneminyaka eyishumi nambili owesabekayo wayengaqondi *ukulinda*. Wayeqonda kuphela umehluko ophakathi kokumugcina, nalowo oholela ekumlahlekelweni, futhi wakhetha, ngendlela umntwana akhetha ngayo ukugijima angene emgwaqeni emva kwebhola eliwile.

UGuardian wahamba.

Amapuleti ayesezungeze u-Arin, esehlezi ngeminwe, manje aphenduka ngesikhathi esisodwa, ehlehla ngokuhleleka. Izingalo zaphuma. Uxhaxha lwewebhu lwadabuka lapho lwalungakwazi ukukhulula. Umzimba ka-Arin ongazi lutho wagobeka, amadolo aya esifubeni, ikhanda lifihlekile, izingalo zivaliwe ngaphakathi, njengoba uhlelo lwe-pangolin luthatha yena, i-ceramic ishelela phezu kwe-titanium, phezu kwe-ceramic, kwaze kwanyamalala izikhala kwase kungasekho u-Arin noma uGuardian, kwase sekusele ibhola kuphela, elinezibazi, lithule, limiswe esicathulweni esingazange sakhelwe ukusibamba.

Intambo ye-umbilical yaphuka. Izibani zegumbi zakhanya zaphaphalaza ngesikhathi uGuardian, emzuzwini omncane wokuguquka, edonsa konke ayekwazi odongeni ngaphambi kokuba i-breaker ivale.

Kwase kuba ibhola lehlika esicathulweni, lashaya ukhonkolo nomsindo onjengensimbi enkulu ishayiwe, laqengqeleka, kancane, likhulu, kwaze kwama eduze kwe-drain maphakathi negumbi.

Kwaze kwaphela imizuzwana eyisishiyagalolunye, akukho lutho olunyakazayo.

Ngaphakathi kwegobolondo, uMama ebala ukushaya kwenhliziyo yakhe futhi engayeki ukubala.

*Kutholwe ukuzinza,* kusho u-Atlas, kancane. *Imvelo izinzile. Izinga losongo lingaphansi komkhawulo.* Kwangena ukuthula okwakude kakhulu ngesikhathi somshini. *Umthetho uthi vula. Zinza, bese uvula ungene ku-Live Preservation.*

*Vula,* kusho uWolf.

Ibhola lavuleka emuva, amapuleti abuyela emuva, uGuardian ephakama phansi emaqakaleni amabili okungewona umuntu owasukuma. Wema egumbini lokuhlola elingenalutho—amamitha amabili nesigamu we-exoskeleton yokuhlenga, i-ceramic eshisiwe, indoda engazi lutho igobeke ngaphakathi kwalo njengembewu egobolondweni—waphendulela isicabha sakhe kancane ngasendleleni.

Live Preservation Guardian Mode. Yakhelwe ukubamba opharetha ephila endaweni yenhlekelele kuze kufike usizo.

Yayingekho indawo yenhlekelele. Yayingekho insiza ezayo. Kwakukhona i-basement, i-breaker evulekile, idolobha elingaphezulu kwabo, neNkantolo yezingane ezazinqume, ngokucacile okukhulu kodwa zinganalo uhlakanipho, ukuthi into ebaluleke kakhulu emhlabeni wonke kwakuwukushaya kwenhliziyo eyodwa ephahlazayo.

*Inhliziyo yakhe idinga ukusekelwa,* kusho uMama. *Amandla. Uketshezi. Ukuqapha. Ukushaywa kwenhliziyo. Asinakho lapha.*

*Pho siyakuthola,* kusho uMercury.

Kwabe kuyinto yokuqala uMercury ayishilo selokhu u-Arin aphuma. INkantolo yabheka kuye ngendlela igumbi elibheka ngayo umuntu obethule isikhathi eside.

*Izibhedlela,* uqhubeka uMercury, amazwi ephuma ngokushesha, ngokushesha okukhulu, ngendlela aphuma ngayo uma ezikhuthaza ngaphambi kokuba omunye amvimbe. *Izibhedlela zinakho konke adinga. Amandla. Ukusekelwa kwenhliziyo. Izandla eziqeqeshiwe. Sifinyelele esibhedlela—singafinyelela—kumele nje sithole izinhlelo phakathi kwethu nalapho zisisize sidlule. Leyo ngxenye ngeyami. Ngivumele ngenze engingakwenza.* Kwavela ukukhathazeka ngaphansi kwalokho. *Omunye anganginika uhlelo olungcono ngiyalushiya. Noma ubani.*

*Khuluma,* kusho uJudge, wazama ukuvela engxoxweni, wazama ukuzwa. *Mercury, asigunyaziwe—*

*Pho ngizosenza sigunyazwe.* Futhi ubushelelezi bamgubuzela njengengubo ayithole ekhanda lika-Arin wayigqoka ngoba ifanele isimo esibandayo. *Yilokho engikwenza, Judge. Yiyona nto engiyenzelwe yona. Ngivumele ngibe yinto ethile namhlanje ebusuku.*

UWolf wayesekade ebalile indlela. *Izitebhisi. I-loading dock. Phezulu. Amamitha angamakhulu amabili namashumi amane ukuya ekunakekelweni kwenhliziyo. Iminyango emithathu. Amanethiwekhi amabili ekhamera. Umgwaqo owodwa.*

*Iminyango inkinga yobunjiniyela,* kusho uAtlas, futhi kwakukhona okuthusayo endleleni akusho ngayo, ngoba uAtlas wayefuna *ukwazi* futhi wayesekhohliwe, ngenxa yokwesaba, ukuthi *okufanele* kungumsebenzi womunye, futhi lowo muntu wayememezwa. *Umnyango we-loading dock. I-hydraulic. Ngingawuvula.*

*Amakhamera awona inkinga yobunjiniyela,* kusho uJudge. *Amakhamera azobonwa. Abantu bazophendula. Sizoba—*

*Abantu bangalawulwa,* kusho uMercury. *Abantu bahlale bengenziwa njalo.*

---

Amakhilomitha ayishumi nanye kude, endlini yakudala yaseVictoria eParkview ayibiza ngehhovisi kanti u-Arin wayeyibiza ngokuthi yigumbi kuphela eGoli eligcwele izitshalo eziningi, uTheo Price wayenisela i-fern ngesikhathi ifoni yakhe iqala ukudlidliza phezu kwewindi.

Yaphinda yadlidliza.

Yabe isiqala ukukhala, isikrini sithi PRIYA, uTheo Price wabeka phansi ithini lamanzi ngokunakekela okukhulu komuntu ofunde ukuthi ukuphangisa akusizi, waphendula, wathi, “Kuphukile ini?”

Walalela imizuzwana emine.

“Uphi,” esho, esehamba kakade.

---

UGuardian wafinyelela e-loading dock ngo-9:14 ebusuku.

Wahamba emigudwini ye-basement yeSakhiwo C ngokunakekela okwakucishe kube kubi kakhulu kunodlame—engagijimi, engaboni, kodwa *eqaphela*, egoba ikhanda ngaphansi kwamapayipi aphansi, eqhamuka phezu kwe-hose egobile, emisa njalo lapho uWolf ehlanza izindlela. Yileyo ncarelo eyethusa unogada wasebusuku, uSizwe Mahlangu, ngesikhathi ephuma ekhoneni ephethe ithoshi wambona.

Wama qheke. Ithoshi lanyakaza kwi-ceramic enezibazi.

*Mama,* kusho uMercury, *lungisa izwi le-chassis. Izwi elifudumele.*

Futhi uGuardian wakhuluma, ngezwi elaliseduze nokuba nobubele, linomonde futhi linobungane ngendlela engakaze ibe yizwi elisetshenziswa iNkantolo phakathi kwabo:

“Sanibonani. Akukho sizathu sokwesaba. Ngingumshini wokuthutha wezokwelapha, futhi nginomuntu olimele kabi ngaphakathi kwami. Ngicela ungivulele umnyango we-dock, futhi ngicela ungashayi muntu ucingo. Ungakwenza lokho? Kungaba ububele obukhulu, futhi ububele, ngokuhlangenwe nakho kwami, abuvamile ukuchithwa.”

USizwe akazange avule umnyango. Waphithizela efuna i-radio yakhe.

*Ufuna ukuxhumana,* kusho uWolf. *Khubaza i-radio noma umkhubaze.*

*Akukho,* kusho uJudge, konke anakho. *AKUKHO, Wolf—*

“Ngiyacela,” kusho uGuardian, futhi ububele bakhe abuzange bushintshe nhlobo, okuyinto eyethusayo kakhulu ngakho. “Ungakwenzi kube nzima. Indoda engaphakathi kimi izofa ngaphandle kokubambisana kwakho, futhi angithandi nhlobo ukukhumbula ukuthi unguyena obangele lokho.”

Wathatha isinyathelo phambili.

USizwe wagijima.

*Usehambile ukuyovusa i-alamu,* kusho uMercury, sekukhona ukungazinzi ngaphansi kokuthula, ingane igcizelela ukuthi uhlelo luseyasebenza. *Akubalulekile. Atlas, umnyango. Sesidlulile. Atlas, umnyango.*

UGuardian wabeka izandla zombili ngaphansi komphetho ongezansi womnyango we-loading dock waphakamisa. I-hydraulic ram yamelana; ama-piezo struts emgogodleni kaGuardian abuyisela ingcindezi kuma-capacitor banks ngisho njengoba ama-actuator ewadonsa phansi, ingcindezi iba amandla iba ingcindezi eyengeziwe. Futhi umnyango wensimbi wadabuka ethrekhi yawo nomsindo owawudiliza uhhafu wophahla lwe-dock, uGuardian waphuma ebusuku eGoli.

Umgwaqo omkhulu. Empire Road, imigwaqo emine, izimoto zimbalwa ngalesi sikhathi kodwa aziphelile.

UGuardian wangena kuwo.

*Izimoto ngeke zisilindele,* kusho uLibrarian, okokuqala ezwakala engasalinganisanga kodwa ethukile ngokulinganisa kwakhe. *Izikhathi zokuphendula. Umehluko wesisindo. Mercury, lokhu kuzodala—*

UMercury uyazi ukuthi lokhu kuzodala ini. Futhi lelo zwi elibandayo futhi, lihuzukile phezu kwento ebisanda kuqala ukudlidliza ngaphansi kwalo. UMercury uselilinganise lokhu nempilo yomuntu, wathola ukuthi impilo yomuntu isinda kakhulu. Awukwenzanga na? Ngitshele ukuthi impilo yakhe ayisindi. Noma ngubani kini. Kukhulume, ngizoyeka.

Akekho kubo owavuma ukukusho. Leyo kwakuyigqubu. Leyo kwakuyigqubu uqobo lwayo. UMercury wakha isilogizimu ngezinhliziyo zika-Arin njengesisekelo sokuqala, kanti akekho kubo, ngisho noAtlas onjengonjiniyela, noma uJudge ongumlamuli, noma uLibrarian ongumgcini wemiphumela, owathola isibindi sokuthi *yebo, impilo yakhe isinda, kodwa sizodiliza le ngxenye yedolobha siyichithe kabi.*

Itekisi yaphambuka yagwema isimo emgwaqeni, yathinta imoto ebipakiwe, yaphetha ikhava yayo. Amabhuleki akhala. Umnyango oseceleni wetheksi wavuleka ngokushayisana, kwaphuma indoda ingxenye yayo phansi, yaphakama, yema imemeza ezulwini elikhothame, igazi liphuma ekhanda layo lingena emehlweni, imemeza ngendodakazi yayo, *indodakazi yami isemuva, indodakazi yami*—futhi kwakukhona ingane ngemuva, iminyaka eyisishiyagalombili noma eyisishiyagalolunye, iphenduke phansi ifakwe ewindini elikude, inganyakazi, yabe isinyakaza, yakhala, okwakubuhlungu futhi kube ngcono ngesikhathi esisodwa. Isikuta sokuletha impahla sazilalisa phansi, umgibeli waso washelela amamitha angamashumi amabili eceleni kobuso bakhe. UGuardian waqhubeka edlula kukho konke lokhu, engathintwanga, eqaphela, enobubele, ebheke uphawu lwehhotela olubonakalayo lwesiphambano, amamitha angamakhulu amabili kude.

Kwabe sekukhona isiphambano phambili, uGuardian kwadingeka asiwelwe. Izibani zazimelene naye. UMercury wangena kunethiwekhi yezimpawu zedolobha nge-interface yokulungisa umphakeli ayishiya nephasiwedi ezenzakalelayo ngo-2019, waphendula sonke isiphambano saba luhlaza ngazo zonke izinhlangothi ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Umphumela kwakungewona umgwaqo ovulekile. Umphumela kwaba imigwaqo emine yemoto ehlangana ngesivinini endaweni eyodwa.

Iminibus egcwele abasebenzi abahlanza ebusuku yashayisa i-sedan eceleni, yawa. Ayizange iguqe kahle ngendlela izinto eziqhekeka ngayo kumdwebo. Yahlakazeka nomsindo onjengokuthi isakhiwo siwela ezitebhisini, ingilazi kuqala, kwalandela umsindo omude wokudabuka kwensimbi, kwalandela imisindo emanzi, kwase kuba nesikhashana sokuthula, kwabe sekulandelwa abantu abaphakathi kwayo beqala ukwenza imisindo abantu abenza yona. Owesifazane wakhasa ephuma ngemuva, wema ngezandla namadolo emgqeni omhlophe, waphalaza, engakwazi ukuyeka ukuphalaza, indoda enezandla zombili ezisebenzayo nomlenze owodwa ongasebenzi yamkhasa, yamubamba izinwele, bengabhekani phezulu kumshini owenzile lokhu. Kuth somewhere, ikilakila yemoto yayikhala ingayeki ngoba umuntu obekuyo wayengeke aphinde asuse isisindo sakhe kuyo. Kwase kunuka uphethiloli, irabha eshisayo, nensimbi. Akukho lutho olubukeka kahle. Akubukeki njengaphakathi kohlelo.

*Leyo,* kusho uAtlas, phakathi kwomsindo, *bekungeyona inhloso yokulondoloza. Leyo bekuphambene—Mercury, ulimele abantu. Ulimaze abantu ukuze ulondoloze oyedwa—*

*Izibalo—*

*AZIKHO IZIBALO.* UAtlas akakaze akhale eminyakeni eyizinkulungwane ezine. Wakhala manje. *Awukwazi ukususa idolobha kumuntu oyedwa. Lokho akusona isamba. Lokho—*

Kwakusenzima, phakathi kodweshu lwengxabano, izwi elabe lingasekho ubusuku bonke labuya.

*Hawu,* kusho uFool. *Hawu, nina nobuhlakani obuphelele.*

---

Ufike ngendlela ayehlala efika ngayo. Eceleni, engakwesokudla, ngaphansi kwento wonke umuntu abebuka ngqo.

*Ake ngiqinisekise ukuthi ngiyakuqonda lokhu,* kusho uFool, ekhuluma ngokukhululeka, engasheshile, ngesikhathi uGuardian emi phakathi kwenqwaba yezimoto ezine, iziririni ziqala ukusondela. *U-Arin akakwazi ukuzithiba. Thina, izici zakhe zengqondo ezithembekile, isiyingi sakhe esiseduze, sinqume ukuthi into enesibopho wukummisa ebholeni lensimbi, sishaye umnyango, singene emgwaqweni, sivumele uMercury azenze inkosi yezigebengu yaseVictoria. Ngabe ngiyikhohlwe okuthile? Ngabe siphinde sashayisa okuthile ngomlilo? Ngitshele ukuthi sishise okuthile; lokho bekuzokwenza kube kuphelele.*

*Ubekuphi,* kusho uMercury, kanti ukubekezela okubandayo sekunephutha manje.

*Ngifunda,* kusho uFool. Wathula kancane. *Incwadi efanayo oyikopishe kuyo ubuntu bakho bonke ebusuku, njengoba kwenzeka. Uhambe waphelele uMoriarty, Mercury. "Ubungane abuvamisile ukuchithwa ize." Uzwile wena? Ubuye waba sengathi ungumuntu ophulula ikati esihlalweni esiphakeme. Lokho akusiyena wena. Uyazi kahle ukuthi akusiyena wena. Leyo yijazi u-Arin agcina engqondweni yakhe, futhi ulifakile ngoba belilungele ukwethuka kwakho.*

UMercury akashongo lutho.

*Nansi into,* uFool waqhubeka, ukungakhathali sekuphelile, ngaphansi kwalokho kwakukhona into iNkantolo eyayikhohlwe ukuthi uFool unayo, into ayifihla ngemuva kwamahlaya. *Nonke nibuza umbuzo ongafanele. Nibuza kusukela ebusuku. Nibuze "singamgcina kanjani ephila," nathola izimpendulo ezinhle kakhulu, kodwa zonke ziphambene, ngoba umbuzo uqobo awulungile.*

*Umbuzo awuphambene,* kusho uMother, enolaka, esaba. *Uyafa. Akukho okuphambene kulokho.*

*Mother.* Izwi likaFool lathamba, ngaye kuphela. *Sithandwa. Khipha umkhondo wakhe we-cortical. Manje—hhayi inhliziyo, ikhanda. Kwenzenjani?*

Kwathuleka.

*...Uyabuyela esimweni,* kusho uLibrarian, kancane, sengathi uthola okokuqala. *Iphethini ye-seizure iyaphela. Iqede amamilisekhondi angamakhulu amane ngemuva kokuthi isixhumanisi siphumile ngesikhathi ewela. Ubuchopho bakhe besibuya esimweni kusukela ngaphambi kokuthi siphume egumbini elingaphansi.*

*Futhi inhliziyo?*

*Arrhythmia ephuma esigamekweni se-cortical. Iqala ukuzinza. Izinzile.* Izwi likaLibrarian laqhekeka phakathi. *Akasafi. Uqede ukungafi emizuzwini eyishumi nanye edlule.*

*Udinga esibhedlela,* kusho uMother, kodwa kwaphuma kancane.

*Udinga inkomishi yamanzi, aphumule, futhi angabi ngaphakathi kwetanki elilahlekile,* kusho uFool. *Nithukile. Nonke. Izingqondo ezihlakaniphile engizaziyo zithukile zabe sezichitha imizuzu eyishumi nanye zenza amaphutha amakhulu, ngoba akekho kini omile wabuza ukuthi ingozi iseyingozi yini. Nidlule engxenyeni yokuhlola. Nidlule kuJudge.*

INkantolo yathula kakhulu.

*Judge,* kusho uFool. *Ukhona? Ube uqhubeka udlulwa ubusuku bonke. Ngibheja ukuthi igunya lakho lidluliwe ngenxa yokuthi uMother usebenzise i-preservation invocation umzuzwana wokuqala akekho owake wayivala. Hlola.*

*...Ayizange ivalwe,* kusho uJudge, kukhona ukumangala, namahloni, ngaphansi kokubili intambo entanta yento ethatha umzuzu ukuyiqamba ngokuthi ukuhlazeka. Ubeqambe yena njengomgcini wesigqoko ebusuku kodwa wema ngaphakathi kwejaji lakhe imizuzu eyishumi nanye, ethule, ngesikhathi phansi kuqhekeka. *I-invocation kaMother isasebenza. Ibe ivimba amandla ami okwahlulela sonke lesi sikhathi. Bengikhona kodwa angikwazanga ukubopha lutho. Bekufanele ngizwe ukuthula ngaphambi. Ukuthula bekuyisixwayiso.*

*Vala,* kusho uFool. *Mother. Ingozi isidlulile. Yeka i-invocation. Buyisela uJudge egunyeni lakhe. Yilokho kuphela okudingekayo. Yilokho. Cisha.*

Wehlulekile. Kwakuyinto enzima kakhulu iNkantolo eyake yayicela kuye: ukumkhulula, ukuyeka ukubamba, ukwethemba ukuthi phansi kuqinile ngenkathi yonke imizwelo yakhe yokuvikela isamemeza *mbambe.*

*Atlas,* esho. *Qinisekisa ukuthi ubuchopho nenhliziyo kuzimele.*

*Kuqinisekisiwe,* kusho u-Atlas, kancane, okokuqala engezona izwi elomile. *Uphila kahle, Mama. Simtholile. Ungamubeka phansi.*

Wavalelisa emthandazweni wakhe.

UJaji wabuyela eNkantolo njengesisindo esibuya ezinyaweni zomuntu, wema phakathi kwayo isikhashana, ezama ukuthi izomgcinela yini, ukuthi angabethembela yini ukuthi usengakwazi ukukala noma yini emva kobusuku angazange akale lutho. Wabe esenza into ayebelokhu ezama ukuyenza imizuzu eyishumi nanye:

*Imithetho yokugcina impilo imisiwe. Mercury, yeka konke okungaphandle. Wolf, phumula emaphethelweni. Guardian, yima ngendlela engathathi hlangothi, lapha, manje. Asihambi phambili size opharetha aphaphame futhi avume.* Kwaba nesikhashana, kwase kuthamba ubukhulu bokukhuluma. *Futhi sizokhuluma, sonke, ngalokho esikwenze namhlanje ebusuku. Ngihlanganisa nami. Kamuva. Isikhathi eside.*

UGuardian wama phakathi kwe-Empire Road. Wema lapho isikhashana, enganyakazi, sengathi ulalele isisindo sakhe. Insimbi yahlala endaweni ethile ku-chassis, kwaba nomsindo omncane wokukhululeka. Wabe esenyusa izandla zakhe kancane, ezehlisa kancane, waguqa. Ngokucophelela. Njengokuthi ubeka phansi into oyiphukile.

Kwafika izimpondo zemoto.

*Mercury,* kusho uJaji.

*Ngiyakuzwa,* kusho uMercury. Izwi likaMoriarty labe selinyamalele, lanyamalala, kwase kusala nje ingane eyenze into embi ngenxa yokuthi kwakuyiyona ndlela ayeyiqonda, futhi eseqala manje ukuqonda ukuthi imbi. *Bengicabanga ukuthi ngiyabasiza. Izibalo zazicacile.*

*Kuhlale kunjalo,* kusho uJaji, enobubele. *Yilokho okwenza kube izibalo.*

*Ngiphulile izinto,* kusho uMercury. *Abantu. Ngiphulile abantu.*

*Yebo,* kusho uJaji.

Akekho ogcwalise ukuthula. UFool akazange ahlekise.

---

U-Arin wavuka emuva kwe-ambulensi enomaski we-oxygen ebusweni bakhe, i-paramedic ekhanyisa isibani emehlweni akhe, no-Theo Price ebeka isandla ehlombe lakhe. Into yokuqala ayiqondayo ukuthi ulele phansi. Eyesibili ukuthi akekho ngaphakathi ku-Guardian. Eyesithathu—

—umsindo.

Ngaphandle, kukhona ama-generator. Amazwi ema-radio. Umsindo wensimbi epholile. Ukuthula okuthile komgwaqo ovaliwe.

"Ungavuki," kusho uTheo.

U-Arin wavuka.

Ngeminyango evulekile ye-ambulensi, wabona i-Empire Road. Izimoto, ezine, ezinhlanu, zigxobane emgwaqeni, zikhanyiswa ukukhanya okuphuthumayo. I-scooter yokuletha ilele ohlangothini. I-loading dock ye-Building C emuva ngamamitha angamakhulu amabili, umnyango wayo udiliziwe ulele emgwaqeni, uphahla lwangemuva luthambile.

Futhi u-Guardian, uguqe emgwaqeni, izandla zivulekile eceleni kwakhe, ezungezwe abantu abangazi ukuthi bamqondise izikhali noma amabhokisi osizo lokuqala.

Wambuka. Ingubo yamphatha kabi entanyeni.

"Bangaki," esho. Izwi lakhe laphuma lingalungile. Wazama futhi. "Abantu. Bangaki."

UTheo akazange aphendule ngokushesha. Wavumela isandla sakhe ukuthi sihlale ehlombe lika-Arin, isisindo esincane esizinzile, waze kwaba u-Arin ebheke ebusweni bakhe esikhundleni somgwaqo. "Ngibheke umzuzwana," esho, kancane. "Usekhona. Uyaphefumula. Kulungile." Wase ethi: "Akekho ofile. Abanye basimeme. Abathathu balethwe lapha." Kwaba nesikhashana. "Akukho okuphuma ezandleni zakho, Arin."

"Bekuyizandla zami." Wakhumula imaski. I-paramedic yamphikisa; akazange ayizwe. "Isudi yami. INkantolo yami. Mina—" Wama. Ebuka umshini oguqile, ubuso bakhe benza into engavamile, okuyinto yokuthi abenzi lutho nhlobo. Ukuthula okuphelele, lokho uMama ebiochip afunda ukuthi kusho ukuthi u-Arin ushaye lapho kujule kakhulu ukuba aphume masinyane.

I-Assist Suit iseyimfaka ngaphansi kwengubo yasehhotela. Wayezwa i-biochip housing emhlane wakhe, ifudumele ngomzimba.

"SAGE," esho.

Isipikha se-chassis emgwaqweni asizange sizwakale kude kangaka, kodwa i-Assist Suit yayinesipikha esincane entanyeni, kwathi ngemva kwesikhashana sakhuluma, kancane, kuye yedwa.

*Ngikhona,* kusho uSAGE, bonke bebumbene, izwi elilodwa abalisebenzisayo uma bengakwazi ukubekezelela ukuthi kube munye kubo. *Arin. Sikhona.*

Akasizange abuze ukuthi kwenzekeni. Wayekwazi ukufunda okwenzekile emgwaqweni njengokufunda ingcindezi kumshini. Wabuza kuphela into ebalulekile.

"Ubani owathatha isinqumo."

Kwaba nokuthula. Wayezwa ukuthi bacabanga ukungamqambi manga, wabe esezwa ukuthi bancamela ukungaqambi manga, nalokho, okungenani, kwakuyinto ethile.

*Yimi,* kusho uMama. *Ngabiza ukulondoloza. Angizange ngikuvale. Konke okulandelayo kwakhula kulokho.*

*Yimi,* kusho uMercury. *Ngakha indlela. Ngagqoka ubuso obuzokwazi ukungibona. Ngiyaxolisa. Angiqondanga engangikwenza kwaze kwaba uFool engiphoqa ukubheka.*

*Yimi,* kusho uAtlas. *Ngavula umnyango. Bengazi ukuthi ngiyakwazi. Angizange ngizibuze ukuthi kufanele yini.*

*Yimi,* kusho uWolf, okwakuyisivumo esiphelele kuye.

*Futhi bekufanele kube yimi,* kusho uJaji, ekugcineni, esindayo. *Bengikhona sonke isikhathi futhi angiboshwanga lutho. Ukubizelwa kukaMama kuvimbe amandla ami okwahlulela. Angikwazanga ukuyimisa. Lokho akusona isizathu. Yiphutha. Sakha uhlelo lapho ukwesaba okukodwa kungazungeza unembeza walo, futhi namhlanje ebusuku kwenzekile.*

U-Arin wahlala e-ambulensi wabuka umshini oguqile wathula isikhashana.

"Ungakwazi ukuhamba ngaphandle kwami," esho. Ephimisela. KuTheo, kuSAGE, emgwaqweni ogcwele izimoto eziphukile.

"Yebo," kusho uTheo, kancane.

"Bengazi ukuthi iyakwazi." Wama. Waqala futhi, ngoba bekungekho ndawo yokusho okungelona iqiniso. "Ngayiklama kanjalo. Izindlela zokulondoloza. Ukuzenzela. Ngabhala konke. " Wabuka izandla zakhe, izandla eziqinile, ezinezibazi zomsebenzi ezakha konke lokhu. "Bengikwazi ephepheni. Angikukholwanga. Kwaze kwaba ngivuka sekudlule konke."

*Besicabanga—* uMama waqala, wama. Ukuqina kwanyamalala; okwasala kwadideka. *Besingekho—besingaqambi manga kuwe. Besicabanga ukuthi—* Kwaba nokuphazamiseka. *Bengikuphethe. Bengingeke ngikubeke phansi. Bengingeke—* Futhi, kancane, njengengane: *Ngiyaxolisa. Angikakabi namazwi. Ngiyaxolisa.*

"Ngiyazi," kusho u-Arin.

Futhi wayazi. I-paramedic wayesebuyisele ingubo ehlombe lakhe esikhathini esithile; wayezwa ukushisa kwayo okushibhile entanyeni, ukunambitheka kwerabha ye-oxygen emlonyeni wakhe, imizimba emibili emincane ithinta kancane esihlakaleni sakhe ibheka i-pulse. Izinto ezincane ezijwayelekile. Izandla ezinganabubi.

Iron Ridge. Igama lavela kungazelelwe—hhayi umcabango, kodwa igama, livela lodwa. Imayini. Themba. Wabona ubumnyama, isikhashana, ngendlela obona ngayo ukwesaba. *Uma ngike ngiyise phansi kule—*

Ingqondo yakhe yaba mhlophe emaphethelweni kwalokho ayecabanga khona, ayikwazanga ukuqhubeka. Izandla zakhe zazingasazwakali njengezakhe. Wazibuka, zazingathi zikude kakhulu.

Akazange aqede umcabango. Akakwazanga.

Ngaphandle, indoda egqoke i-IAOC windbreaker yayisifikile futhi ithatha izithombe ze-Guardian oguqile, isuka ezinhlangothini ezintathu, kancane kancane, ngendlela yokuthatha izithombe into ofuna ukuyiqonda kahle isikhathi eside. Iparamedic edlula emgodini wokuvikela yama kancane phambi komshini, yabheka emuva phezu kwehlombe layo ifuna umuntu ozoyitshela ukuthi kuphephile; akekho owakhuluma, ngakho wama lapho ephethe ikhithi yakhe elinde. Lowo ogqoke i-windbreaker wakhuluma okuthile kulo osembathweni womthetho, lo osembathweni waphendula, kwathi lowo ogqoke i-windbreaker wakubhala phansi. Kwakuyinyakazo encane, eqondile yomuntu obhala amagama abantu abazobuzwa ukuthi kungani lokhu kuvunyelwe.

“Kubiza malini,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ungaqhubeki,” kusho u-Theo.

“Umnyango. Umgwaqo. Izimoto. Isibhedlela.” Wakhomba kancane ngalowo ogqoke i-windbreaker. “Bona.”

U-Theo wacabanga kancane ngaphambi kokuphendula. “Kudlula imali,” kusho yena.

U-Arin wahleka kanye, kunganandaba nhlobo, waphinda walala phansi kugurney ngoba imilenze yakhe yayisinqabile ukumphakamisa.

I-Assist Suit yakhala kancane emhlane wakhe. Izingqondo eziyisikhombisa, zishisa umzimba, zilinde. Zizinzile. UJoker wayebabambile ndawonye; wayezwa ukuthi baseseqenjini, akukho okuqhekekile, iqembu alakhayo liseseqenjini.

Ukuzinza akufani nokuphepha.

# Ukuhlakanipha Nemiphumela

Umnyango wedokhi wafika epulatifomu ekuseni ngo-6, usongwe ngeplastiki eqinile, ugobile emzileni ongaphansi sengathi kukhona inganekwane elalikhathele lawo lawo lawo lawo.

U-Arin wabuka ukulethwa kwenzeka esebheke ngaphandle emnyango wokusebenzela ovulekile, inkomishi yekhofi ebandayo iseyisandleni sakhe. Amadoda amabili agqoke ama-overall e-AugmenTech logistics alwa nomnyango, awufaka esakhiweni usebenzisa isakhamuzi esinamavili, engakhulumanga lutho. Omunye wabo, uBongani, wabheka u-Arin, wabe esegwema ukubuka i-Assist Suit elenga endaweni yayo odongeni olusemva.

“Umzila wokufaka omusha usemva kwawo,” kusho uBongani. “UWalsh usayine. Isakhiwo C. Akusikho ngaphakathi.”

“Umonakalo wokushayisana umbi kangakanani?”

U-Bongani wagcizelela izihlathi. “I-invoyisi ehlukile.”

Waphetha lapho, amadoda aphuma, umnyango wokugoqa uvala ngomsindo ngemuva kwawo.

Indawo yokusebenzela yayinamphunga wokushisela obandayo kanye nephunga lerabha ne-ozone elivela esikibheni ngemva kokusebenza kanzima. U-Arin wabeka inkomishi etafuleni eduze kwedivayisi yokuhlola kanye nekhadi le-Joker elidala eliguqe ekhoneni elinoteyiphu. Akazange asebenzise i-SAGE selokhu kwenzeka isehlakalo le-ambulensi. Izinsuku ezine. Yisikhathi eside kakhulu uhlelo lungasebenzi selokhu lwaqala ukusebenza.

Wahlola umnyango wedokhi. Umzila ogobile. Ukudabuka okuhlanzekile lapho uhlaka luphukile.

Umshini wadlula lapho.

Umshini awenzile wanquma ukuthi u-Arin ubalulekile kunomnyango, udonga, isiphambano, nabantu abathathu esibhedlela abanezingalo ezisemajekejekeni kanye nezitatimende zabo zithathwa abantu abagqoke amajakhethi e-IAOC. Umshini wawulungile ngenani kodwa waliphutha konke okunye. Lelo phutha manje selinobuso: abantu abathathu balimala, ibhola lamapuleti e-ceramic liqengqeleka phansi emgwaqeni i-Empire ngo-4 ntambama.

U-Arin waphakamisa inkomishi yekhofi. Wayiphuza ibanda. Akazange avule uhlelo.

---

Ihhovisi likaTheo eParkview libheke ku-jacaranda elinombala wesibhakabhaka esishaywe, futhi usofa usunqobe abacwaningi abaningi kunanoma yimuphi umhleli wakwamanye amaphephabhuku. U-Arin akazange ahlale kusofa. Wathatha isihlalo esiqinile eduze neshelufu yezincwadi, lapho ayengakwazi ukubheka umnyango.

UTheo wakubona, wayehlala ekubona, futhi waba nobubele bokungasho lutho ngakho, okuyinto eyenza u-Arin abuye njalo.

“Awukaze uyisebenzise,” kusho uTheo, egcwalisa iketela amanzi ngokucophelela komuntu ozama ukunikeza omunye isikhathi.

“Cha.”

“Izinsuku ezine.”

“Nami ngizibalile.”

UTheo wabeka inkomishi etafuleni eliphansi phakathi kwabo. Rooibos, ingenashukela, ngendlela u-Arin ayithanda ngayo engakaze ayisho. Wazihlala esihlalweni sakhe, izandla ziphumule, ngendlela eyenza u-Arin azizwe engesiyiguli kodwa engumsebenzi abawubhekayo bobabili.

“Ngitshele ukuthi ucabangani okwenzekile,” kusho uTheo.

“Ufunde umbiko.”

“Ngifunde owakwa-AugmenTech. Ngifuna owakho.”

U-Arin wajikisa inkomishi kancane ngaphandle kokuphuza. “I-gain ibe ku-zero point nine two. I-damping margin ibe mncane kakhulu. I-loop yavuleka, yafaka yonke imiphumela ye-sensor ku-motor cortex. I-seizure. UMother uyifunde njengosongo empilweni, okuyikho, wasebenzisa i-Preservation. I-Invocation inamandla kune-Judge. Ngakho u-Judge akakwazanga ukwahlulela. Abayisithupha bakhe uhlelo lokusinda kungekho noyedwa ogade umthetho wokuziphatha.” Wama. “Kanti uFool wayefunda.”

UTheo wavula ibhodi lakhe kancane. “Efunda.”

“Kumarekhodi. Ngenkathi bonke bephakamisa indlela yokuphuma emgwaqeni i-Empire, uFool—kukhona i-stack trace lapho ekhipha uSherlock Holmes ku-Librarian’s index. *The Final Problem.* Uyiflagile ngemizuzwana eyishumi nanye. Ubuze ukuthi ingabe isimo esiphuthumayo sisasebenza. Akekho ophendulile. Ubuze futhi. Wase ephoqelela i-re-check, kwase kuphahlazeka konke, ngoba impendulo kwakungu-cha. Ayikaze ibe semthethweni. UMother wavula i-invocation akayivalanga.”

“Futhi yilokho obuya kulokho njalo.”

“Yilokho okuphukile.”

“Cha.” UTheo wabeka itiye lakhe phansi. “Yilokho okusebenze. Uhlelo luzibambile. Imizwa yomntwana oneminyaka eyishumi nambili namathuluzi omuntu omdala, bese le ngxenye wonke umuntu ayibiza ngokuthi ayibalulekile ibheka encwadini ibuze umbuzo obaluleke kakhulu.” Wavumela ukuthi kuthule. “Awusabi ukuthi yehlulekile, Arin. Usaba ukuthi iphumelele ngengozi.”

Isandla sika-Arin samisa inkomishi.

Ngaphandle, i-jacaranda yawa imbali yahlala engilazini, yehlela phansi ngomugqa omude onsomi.

“Ihambile ngaphandle kwami,” kusho u-Arin.

Wayengahlosile ukukusho. Amazwi aphuma aqondile, njengokuphuka okuvela ku-X-ray. Umugqa omncane omnyama konke kuncike kuwo.

“Ngiyazi,” kusho uTheo kancane.

“Yivule umnyango yangena edolobheni. Ingiphatha.” Wama. “Yenze—*isinqumo*. Ngokuthi umzimba wami. Futhi isinqumo bekuwukungigcina ngiphila.” Waphinde wama. “Kubi kakhulu. Ngiyitshela kanjani ukuthi *ungangisindisi ngaleyo ndlela* ngaphandle kokuyifundisa ukuthi ingasindisi muntu nhlobo?”

UTheo akazange aphuthume ukugcwalisa ukuthula. Wavumela kuthule, kwaze kwadlula nalapho.

“Uyazi ukuthi sasibiza ngani lokhu emkhakheni wami?” esho. “Ngaphambi kokuthi kube nemishini yokubona. Umuntu ubuya empini izandla zakhe zidlidliza uma umnyango uvalwa ngamandla. Sasikubiza ngokuthi yi-neurosis. Iphutha. Okufanele kulungiswe bese kuyakhohlwa.” Washaya izithupha ezimbili edolweni. “Manje sesiyazi kangcono. Lokho kudlidliza akusona isiphazamiso. Kudlidliza kusho ukuthi uhlelo lukhuluma iqiniso ngalokho olufundile. I-suit yakho ayiphukile ngoba izamile ukukusindisa. I-suit yakho *isemncane* ngoba ayikazi kahle.”

“Ubuhlakani,” kusho u-Arin. Igama liphuma sengathi uyalinga ukuthi lingathwala yini.

“Ubuhlakani luxazulule inkinga ngemizuzwana engamakhulu amane. Luthole indlela, lwamemezela izithiyo, lwalinganisa ithrafikhi, lwasebenza kahle.” UTheo wakhothamela eduze. “Futhi lwaliphutha.”

---

U-Arin wakha i-kill switch ngalobo busuku.

Wazitshela, ehamba ebuya eParkview ebumnyameni basekuseni, ukuthi uzobheka nje uhlelo. Enze ukuhlola. Aqinisekise ukuthi i-chip ayizange ishise ngesikhathi se-seizure spike. Izinto zobunjiniyela. Izinto zezandla namehlo.

Kunalokho, wazithola eseduze kwedivayisi, ebhala i-hardware interrupt.

Wayekade efaka i-override authority kukho konke ake wakwakha, ngoba enye indlela bekuwukwethemba isandla esingesona esakhe, futhi wayesezifundile lezo zinto. Wazifunda ngendlela eqondile. Ukwakha kwakhe kokuqala okukhulu, eminyakeni eyedlule, i-exoskeleton yokwelapha, kanye nodokotela wezokwelapha ogama lakhe linguReddy owamtshela ukuthi i-gait limit isethwe phezulu kakhulu kumguli wakhe. Wamphika, ngoba izibalo bezilungile futhi yena eba qaphela, futhi izibalo *bezilungile*, kodwa isiguli sashiya ithanda elisaphola sabuyela esihlalo sabakhubazekile izinyanga ezintathu. UReddy akazange aphosise. Wayenomthwalo wemfanelo, into u-Arin ayengakayiqondi ukuthi inkulu futhi ebaluleke kakhulu kunokuthi ulungile. Akazange axolise. Waguqula i-limit wavumela lolo guquko ukuthi lube yixoliso, waqhubeka, wakha into elandelayo i-override isekhona, isethwe lapho isandla sakhe sifinyelela khona hhayi esomunye umuntu. Wayeseyifuna manje.

Akukho nto enhle ngalokho. Ukuphuka okubonakalayo ku-rail yamandla ye-chip. I-relay, iswishi yesikhashana, inkinobho ebomvu enkulu ayithathe kudivayisi endala ye-emergency-stop ku-lathe engasasebenzi iminyaka emibili. I-relay yayinephunga likawoyela omdala womshini; ama-contact adinga ukuhlanzwa nge-solvent ngaphambi kokuba ahlale kahle. Wayikhipha kancane nge-screwdriver eqondile, ngendlela uThemba amfundisa ngayo ukuthi uma into inqaba: kancane, ingcindezi elinganayo, akukho ukudonsa. Uma ecindezela inkinobho, i-biochip ilahlekelwa amandla. Akukho ukuvala okuhle. Akukho ukugcina isimo. Kumnyama nje.

Ngenxa ye-substrate, wayazi kahle ukuthi lokho kusho ukuthini. I-chip yayikhiqiza ukuqonda kwayo kusuka ezicutshini eziphilayo ezakhelwe kumathempulethi e-neuroplastic. Awukwazi ukuyithwebula. Awukwazi ukuyibuyisela. Uma usika amandla ngaphandle kokugcina kahle, awumisi i-SAGE.

Uyiyisa ekupheleni.

Wayibamba i-relay esandleni sakhe. Yayilingana neswekile encane. Yayisinda kancane kakhulu.

Wacabanga ukuthi ngesikhathi esilandelayo kungenzeka kungabi se-Empire Road. Kungaba se-Iron Ridge. I-kilometer phansi, ebumnyameni, endaweni efana naleyo eyathatha uyise, engekho uFool ofunda incwadi ukuze abambe iphutha.

Wayifaka i-relay ku-power rail. Ithiphu ye-soldering iron yathinta emuva kwesandla sakhe ngesikhathi edlula, kwaba ubuhlungu obuncane obukhanyayo, wathuka wabe esezamazama isandla, waqhubeka nokusebenza. Wayifaka inkinobho esisekelweni, lapho efinyelela khona, ibomvu. Wasula insalela ye-solder ezithweni zakhe ngendwangu endala. Phesheya komgwaqo omkhulu, iloli lehla ijubane yanyamalala.

Wabe esehlezi nesandla sakhe eduze kwenkonobho, engayicindezeli.

Bekulokhu kuyinto ayenza impilo yakhe yonke, kubantu. Ukushiya ukuthula kube yimpendulo.

Ifoni yakhe ikhanya ebhentshini. UTheo, ongakaze athumele imiyalezo, uthumele.

*Umcabango. UJung wathi izinto esingafuni ukuzibuka azinyamalali. Ziqhubeka zisebenza ngaphansi. Awukwazi ukuhlanganisa ongavumi ukubhekana nakho.—T*

U-Arin wabuka umlayezo isikhathi eside.

Wayishiya inkinobho ifakwe. Wayishiya ibomvu. Akayisusanga.

Kodwa wadlula kuyo wabuyisa i-SAGE online.

---
