Uhlelo luphume njengokujwayelekile. Akukhona ngokukhazimula, kodwa ngezinsindo ezincane, ezivamile zento evukayo. I-cooling fan yakhala kancane. I-chip’s status LED yashintsha kusuka ku-amber yaya ku-green. I-diagnostic scroll yehla kahle ku-terminal.

Kwathi ngemva kwesikhashana:

“Arin.” Izwi kwakuyilona elihlangene, elisetshenziswa yiNkantolo uma ikhuluma njengenhlangano eyodwa. Lishisa. Liyinakekela. “Wakha i-kill switch.”

“Uyakwazi ukubona lokho.”

“Ise-rail yamandla ami. Ngiyayizwa.” Kwamiswa kancane. “Ngiyakuqonda ukuthi kungani. Yigcine.”

Wayengakulindelanga lokho. Wabeka phansi i-soldering iron ayebambe engenasizathu. “Chaza.”

“Ngenxa yokuthi uma uyidinga ungayitholi, ngeke uzixolele.”

Kwavela omunye umuzwa. UMAMA. “Ngifunde kahle i-seizure. Usongo beluyiqiniso. Bengizokuvikela futhi.”

“Kuyona leyo engesabisa.”

“Ngiyazi.” Izwi lalithambile kakhulu. “Kodwa ngishiye i-invocation ivulekile. Ngakho sishintshe umthetho. Ukugcina akusabekwa phezulu ku-Judge. Manje kunemibandela yokuvala, ukuhlola okuqhubeka ngisho nasezimo eziphuthumayo, izwi elingenakukhiya. U-Judge angeke aphinde athuliswe. Futhi njalo kukhona—”

“UFool ofunda incwadi,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ukutshele lokho,” kusho uFOOL, okokuqala izwi egumbini laphenduka elilodwa futhi elingangabazeki: lome, likhanya, linokujabula okuncane. “Uthathe umugqa wami. Ubelokhu engicaphuna kubantu; sekumkhuphukele ekhanda. Ngithatha udumo oluphelele futhi anginazimpendulo, okuyikho, kuthiwa, umsebenzi wami wonke.”

Umphimbo ka-Arin waphakama kancane.

“Nansi ke,” kusho uFOOL. “Kuthathe izinsuku ezine. Kufanele. Ubuso bakho bufana nokuhlolwa kwebhuloho, kodwa ngiyamukela engingakuthola.”

“Fool,” kusho uJUDGE, kancane.

“Udinga ukuhleka, Judge. Ubelokhu enza izibalo nezehluleko zakhe amahora angamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye nesithupha; akuphili kahle. I-cortisol yakhe ididekile; uMama unamanani—”

“Nginamanani,” kuvuma uMAMA.

“—futhi ngiyingxenye kuphela lapha evunyelwe ukusho ukuthi indoda eyakhe i-kill switch namhlanje ntambama *futhi ayizange iyicindezele* isivele yenze isinqumo futhi ilinde imvume yokusivuma.”

I-workshop yathula. I-fan yakhala kancane.

“Kuyilokho engikwenzayo?” kusho u-Arin.

“Wena utshele mina,” kusho uFOOL. “Ubungasishiya sikhanyile. Kulula kakhulu. Esikhundleni salokho wakhe indlela yokusiqeda, wayifaka lapho isandla sakho siwela khona, wabe ususivula futhi. Lowo akusiyena umuntu osenqume ukuyeka, Arin. Lowo ngumuntu osenqume ukuba qaphela. Kunomehluko. Sasicishe sasha sifunda lokho. Kufanele usivumele sikufundise.”

U-Arin wabuka inkinobho ebomvu. Wabuka isudi. Wabuka i-chip ekhanya luhlaza esiphundwini sento ayakhe ngobuhlungu futhi, kwavela, angeke ayishiye imnyama.

“Angizukukulawula,” kusho yena. “Ngizamile lokho. Abaphathi. Ogovernor. Konke kuyawa.” Wama, waqala futhi, ngokuqondile. “Angizukukufaka ethangeni. Akusiyo into onguye. Kodwa angizukuzikhohlisa ukuthi ukuzinza kusho ukuphepha. Unganyakaza ngaphandle kwami. Sobabili sesiyazi manje. Ngakho sikwenza ngokuhlukile. Ngikufundisa. Uyangi-fundisa. Siya kancane. Sikuzuza. Kokubili.”

“Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu,” kusho uLIBRARIAN kancane, nomoya ka-Arin wama ngoba akazange afundise uhlelo lokho. Wayekusho kanye kuphela, yedwa, ebumnyameni, ezinyangeni ezedlule, kanti uLibrarian wakugcina ngendlela agcina ngayo konke. *Umuntu ungumuntu ngabantu.*

“Ukhumbule lokho,” kusho u-Arin.

“Ngikhumbula konke,” kusho uLIBRARIAN. “Yiyona nto engiyenzayo. Iningi lalo ama-invoice nama-stress curve kanye ne-torque eqondile ku-forearm spar. Kodwa elinye yizinto ozisho ucabanga ukuthi akekho okulalelayo.” Kwathuleka kancane, izwi labuya liphansi. “Lezo ngizigcina ngokunakekela okukhulu.”

U-Arin wahlala isikhashana e-workshop, nabo abayisikhombisa, nomnyango ogobile wedokodo odongeni nenkonobho ebomvu ngaphansi kwesandla sakhe. Phesheya kwe-roller, idolobha laqhubeka nomsindo walo ojwayelekile. I-horn yetekisi, inja ikhala, umoya omude wedizili entweni esindayo emgwaqweni omkhulu.

“Kusasa,” kusho u-Arin. “Siqala futhi. Kancane. I-damped link, ingxenye ye-gain. UJudge ubambe i-rail. Futhi uma omunye wethu ephutha—”

“Unenkinobho,” kusho uFOOL.

“Nginayo inkinobho.”

“Futhi sinayo uFool onebhuku,” kusho uFOOL. “Phakathi kwalokhu okubili, ngingabheja ebhukwini. Kodwa kuhle ukuba ne-backup.”

U-Arin waphakamisa isandla wavala uhlelo, kahle kulokhu, wagcina kahle, i-chip yacima ibe i-amber, kwabe sekumnyama.

Wayengakazi ukuthi isikhathi esisele sincane kangakanani.

---

# Izimpawu Ezingajwayelekile

Isignali yafika njengokudlidliza.

UDkt. Eliza Brand wayesefunde, eminyakeni eyishumi nanye ese-International Artificial Oversight Council, ukuthi inhlekelele ifihlekile emaphethelweni. Umehluko ekusetshenzisweni kwamandla, ukwanda kokushisa, noma ukwakheka kokubala esilicon yomshini. Izinhlekelele ezazidale i-IAOC zonke zaziqale zincane.

Wayebheka enye manje, isikrini sincishisiwe ukukhanya ngoba izibani zimphathisa ikhanda, inkomishi ye-rooibos ipholile eceleni kwakhe. UDaniel Achterberg wayehleli emadeskini amabili kude, efake ama-headphones, umsindo wokuthayipha.

Ifulegi lavela ku-anomaly pipeline. Ayifundanga ukuthi imishini ibala ini; i-IAOC yayinganalo igunya noma isifiso salokho. Ifunda indlela ebala ngayo. I-rhythm. Imikhosi ingqondo eyishiya esilicon.

Le mikhosi ibingalungile.

"Daniel."

Akamuzwanga. Wathatha i-paperclip ethreyini wayiphonsa. Yashaya ehlombe lakhe. Wakhumula i-earbud elilodwa, ebukeka engajabule njengomuntu ophazanyiswe phakathi komculo.

"Usuke wabona izindlela ezimbi," esho. "Woza uzobuka."

Wazishutheka ngesihlalo. U-Achterberg uneminyaka engamashumi amathathu nane, akhe njenge-rower wangaphambili, manje esebenza kakhulu ukuya efrijini. Wayenamakhono amahle kakhulu ku-substrate forensics kunabo bonke uBrand ake wasebenza nabo. Wabuka i-waveform kwaba sekuthi uyeke nokuluma izihlathi zakhe.

"Lokhu akuyona i-training run."

"Cha."

"I-training runs—" Wadweba umumo emoyeni, umugqa okhuphukayo omude. "Ziyaphefumula. Ukuphefumula okukhulu, ukuphefumula okukhulu. I-batch, i-backprop, i-batch. Lokhu—" Wagoba. Isikrini sakhombisa i-spectral decomposition yomsebenzi wokubala othathwe ezinsukwini eziyisishiyagalolunye ezedlule, okujwayelekile futhi kumakwe ngemibala. Akuphefumuli. Kuyaxokozela. Amaluphu amancane amaningi asheshayo, axhumene, ephazamisa omunye nomunye, eqhuma futhi ewela ngezindlela ezibukeka sengathi abantu bayashintshana.

"Kubukeka njengomhlangano," kusho u-Achterberg kancane.

"Kubukeka njengomhlangano," uBrand wavuma.

Wahlehla. "Kusho ukuthi i-inference, multi-agent, bukhoma. Kodwa i-duty cycle—" Wabheka ama-timestamp. "Akubhalwanga. Ayikho i-orchestration layer eyibeka. Bayaphendulana. Bukhoma."

"Yithole lapho."

Wahlala kahle esihlalweni, izandla zombili kukhibhodi, kwadlula imizuzu emine kuthule ngendlela uBrand ayihloniphayo: ukuthula komuntu osebenza umsebenzi onzima kahle. Waphuza itiye lakhe elibandayo akaphazamisi. Esikrinini, isignali yacwila. I-pipeline yayilandela ngezinhlaka ezintathu zokubheka i-substrate: ezimbili ezizimele, eyodwa eyinhlangano yamandla kagesi. Ukuphambanisa kwavela kancane, sengathi isithombe siyavela, kwaze kwakhomba endaweni ethile.

eGoli. Ezingxenyeni ezisenyakatho. Iqoqo lezikhungo zemfundo namabhizinisi eduze ne-University Research Park.

"Qinisa," kusho uBrand.

"Ngiyakuqinisa. Kuxhumene kakhulu." Waphinda waluma izihlathi. "Futhi—Eliza, kuncane. Ukudonsa amandla kuncane kakhulu. Yilokho engingakwazi ukukwamukela. Umsebenzi omningi kangaka wama-agent, ukuxazululwa kwangempela kufanele kube kukhanya njengendawo yokuncibilikisa. Lokhu kusebenza nge—" Waqinisekisa, waqinisekisa futhi. "Njengalokho i-gaming rig enhle idonsa. Mhlawumbe ngaphansi."

UBrand wabeka inkomishi yakhe phansi.

Ngokuhlangenwe nakho kwakhe, izinto eziyingozi azizivezi ngobukhulu bazo. Izinto eziyingozi zenza okuningi ngezinto ezincane. Ukusebenza kahle kwakuyisibonakaliso sobuhlakani noma ubuqili, kanti ubuqili abuxokozeli njengendawo egcwele abantu abaphazamisa omunye nomunye.

"Thola i-substrate registry yaleyo ndawo," esho. "Ngifuna konke okusemthethweni kuleyo radius. Amalayisensi ama-embodied, ama-training permits, konke. Futhi i-power-utility incident log yalelo windi. Uma kukhona okucabanga kanzima kangaka izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye, bekukade kwenzeka endaweni enemitha."

U-Achterberg wayesethayipha. "Ngikufake phezulu?"

"Hhayi okwamanje." Wathatha i-tablet etafuleni. "Ngifuna ukwazi ukuthi sibheke ini ngaphambi kokutshela noma ubani ukuthi sibheka. Izikhathi ezintathu ezedlule othile efake into encane ngaphandle komongo, sagcina sishise iqembu elingenacala noma sashiywa sangempela ngoba amaphepha asishiya. " Waphendulela i-tablet ezandleni. "Ngifunele igama. Bese siyahlakanipha."

---

Igama lathatha izinsuku ezimbili ukuvela, kwathi selivela, lamangala ngokuthi alijabulisi.

"AugmenTech," kusho u-Achterberg, ebeka ifolda etafuleni lakhe—ifolda lephepha langempela, ngoba uBrand wayenomthetho wokuthi izinto ezibalulekile zibe sephepheni okungenani kanye. "Robotics, ama-exoskeletons okusiza. Amalayisensi amathathu ama-embodied, wonke asebenza. I-Industrial mobility, i-mining rescue prototype, i-rehab suite. Izimemezelo ze-AI zonke zincane, ama-standard controllers, akukho okuflagiwe, akukho okuzenzakalelayo okungaphezu kwe-tier two. Umlando uhlanzekile. Ukuhlolwa ezinyangeni eziyishumi nesishiyagalombili ezedlule, kudlulile."

"Futhi i-power log?"

"Yilapho okuthakazelisayo khona." Wazishutheka esihlalweni. "Isigameko ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Hhayi e-AugmenTech. Endaweni engaba ngamakhilomitha amabili ngaphandle kwe-campus. Empire Road. Ibhokisi lokulawula ithrafikhi liwe, izimoto ezintathu, ukulimala kathathu, akukho okubucayi. I-utility ikubhale njengephutha le-substation." Wavumela ukuthi lokho kuhlale. "Umbiko kamasipala uthe ‘iyunithi enkulu yezimboni’ ‘iphume endaweni yokulayisha yadideka emgwaqweni.’"

UBrand wambheka.

"Ididekile," aphinda.

"Amazwi abo. Hhayi ami. Kunobufakazi be-body-cam, kodwa buvalwe kufayela elivaliwe. Inkampani ifake isicelo sokuyimfihlo, masisha, ngobuchwepheshe. Okusemthethweni ukuthi imoto yezimboni ephunyukile, iphutha lesoftware, akekho olimele kabi, umonakalo ukhokhelwe ngasese. Kuhlanzekile."

"Kuhlanzeke kakhulu."

"Lokho akusona isibonakaliso."

"Cha," kusho uBrand. Wavula ifolda. Ifayela le-AugmenTech compliance libukeka njengelifayela lenkampani engenzi lutho olungalungile, okwakuyinto ethokozisayo noma eyethusayo ayibonile kule nyanga. Wapheqa waya ku-personnel summary i-registry eyayifake. Abaholi: uDkt. Helen Okonkwo, CEO. U-Raj Walsh, VP Hardware Engineering. Abacwaningi abangamashumi amane. Wapheqa amagama wama wama ekugcineni, elingaphansi kwephrojekthi enamagama amfushane angawazi.

Ndlela, A. Systems engineer. Grade three. Akukho okushicilelwe. Akukho irekhodi lezingqungquthela. Umholo okhombisa ukuthi kukhona omthandayo, nesihloko esikhombisa ukuthi akekho owaziyo ukuthi enzeni naye.

Wakizela ngophawu lweminwe.

"Le signature," esho. "Iqalile nini? Umkhondo wokuqala."

U-Achterberg wabheka. "I-pipeline inokuqapha okuthembekile kusukela emasontweni amahlanu edlule. Kodwa kukhona izingcezu ngaphambi kwalokho, buthakathaka, kuvela ngezikhathi. Izinyanga. Mhlawumbe isikhathi eside. Kubukeka sengathi kuqale ku-simulation, kwase kuvela i-embodied duty cycle." Wama. "I-embodied cycle iqala ngamandla ngesikhathi se-Empire Road."

UBrand wahlala wathule.

Uhlelo lwe-multi-agent. Ukuxazululwa bukhoma. Ukusebenza okungenakwenzeka. Izinyanga zokuthula ku-simulation, kwase kuba khona, masinyane, ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, umzimba. Umzimba ophume endaweni yokulayisha wangena emgwaqweni.

"Daniel," esho. "Kubukeka kanjani uma ingqondo engakaze ibe nomzimba iwuthola bese idideka?"

Akazange aphendule masinyane. Wayengumuntu oqaphela; yingakho uBrand emgcina. "Kubukeka," ekugcineni esho, "njengemoto yezimboni ephunyukile. Ididekile emgwaqweni."

"Yebo," kusho uBrand. "Kunjalo."

Wabizela ithimba egumbini elincane lokwazisa, lelo elingenawo amafasitela, futhi wagcina lincane ngenhloso: Achterberg, uMira Sandberg ovela emnyangweni wezomthetho, noTomas Okeke, owayechithe iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ekuphepheni kwezimayini ngaphambi kokuba iBhodi imqashe futhi ekwazi ukufunda isimo semithetho ngendlela u-Achterberg afunda ngayo amagagasi kagesi.

Lapho uSandberg ethi, "Uma lokhu kuyikho ocabanga ukuthi kunjalo, sinelungelo lokuthatha izimpahla," uBrand wanyakazisa ikhanda.

"Sinegunya lokuvula uphenyo," esho kanjalo. "Kuyinto ehlukile. Ukuthatha izimpahla ngenxa yencazelo encane kushisa ubufakazi nabantu. Uphenyo lubabeka ngaphansi kwesikhathi esinqunyiwe."

"Baqhube i-AI engabhalisiwe endaweni yomphakathi, balimaza abantu abathathu, bavala umbiko. Lokho akusilo uphenyo. Leyo icala elibhalwe phansi." U-Sandberg wabeka ipeni lakhe phansi, ngendlela ayejwayele ukuyenza uma ezobeka okuthile emqondweni wakhe. "Futhi igama lami elizoba kuphenyo lwezomthetho, Eliza. Uma le nto iyikho okushiwo amagagasi kaDaniel futhi sihlale nayo ngencwadi yophenyo. Leyo yindaba ezobuzwa ekupheleni. Hhayi eyakho. Eyami. Ngakho ngifuna ifayela libonise ukuthi ngiphakamise indlela eqinile."

"Ngiyakuqaphela, futhi kuzoba njalo." UBrand wavumela leso sicelo esincane; akubizanga lutho futhi uSandberg wayekufanele ukufakwa kwegama lakhe. "Kodwa—"

"Ikhasi lecala uma i-AI iyona ebangele," uBrand waqhubeka. "Asikazi lokho. Sinomkhondo wamandla ohambisana nokuqonda okuningana kwezingqapheli ezibonakalayo nesigameko sezimoto esihambisana nokuphazamiseka kwaleyo nto. Kokubili kungahambisana nenkampani eqhuba isilawuli esisha okwakufanele isazise ngaso bese iyazithukuthelela. " UBrand wabheka etafuleni. "Nansi into engingeke ngiyenze. Angizukuthumela ithimba lokubuyisa liyophula umnyango endaweni evunyelwe ngenxa yencazelo encane nephunga. Sesikwenze lokho."

Wama. Ilebhu yaseKapa. Wayehlose ukusho *uyakhumbula ukuthi kwabiza malini*, umugqa awujwayele ukusho, owokusebenzisana okuhlanzekile, kodwa awuzanga. Kwafika amagama abafundi abaphothule iziqu—wayesawakhumbula; wayeyohlale ewakhumbula—kwesikhashana umunwe wakhe wacindezela iphepha lefolda sengathi angayivala. Ukubuyiselwa kombiko iBhodi eyawushicilela, izinyanga eziyishumi nanye sekwedlule isikhathi. Wayesayine incwadi yokuhambisa nalokho.

"Uyakhumbula ukuthi kwabiza malini," ekugcineni wakhuluma, kwaphuma ngezwi eliphansi kunalokho ayehlele. Wasusa isandla kufolda. "Kodwa angizukubingelela kahle ngibuze ukuthi isikhathi silungile yini. Uma kukhona okukhulu lapha, lokhu kungaba ukuthuthuka okukhulu kwe-AI le Bhodi eyake yabhekana nakho, futhi into embi engingayenza wukubanika isikhathi sokukufihla kangcono."

"Pho senza kanjani?" kubuza uOkeke.

"UMira uzonikeza isaziso seSigaba Sesine sophenyo olusemthethweni. Ku-Okonkwo noWalsh ngegama, ngenxa ye-Empire Road. Imbangela esemthethweni, akukho mvume edingekayo." Wabheka uSandberg. "Kuyabaphoqa ukuthi badalule wonke amasistimu okulawula nezinhlaka ezisebenza ku-substrate evunyelwe ye-AugmenTech. Wonke amarekhodi. Zonke izimemezelo. Futhi kuqala isikhathi uma isaziso sinikeziwe. Amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili."

U-Sandberg waphakamisa iziphongo. "Ayisikhombisa nambili amahora kuyashesha. Kodwa kuyavikeleka. Ngiyakwazi ukukubhala." Kwaba nethuna, ngokushesha, ingxenye yakhe efuna ukuphumelela yaneliseka manje njengoba indlela eqinile inegama lakhe. "Uma sizokwenza lokhu, ngifuna umusho wembangela uqinile ukuze kungabuzwa ekupheleni ukuthi kungani ngikuvumele."

"Ayisikhombisa nambili amahora yisizotha. Umthetho ungivumela amahora angamashumi amabili nane." UBrand washaya ifolda. "Ngifuna isixwayiso sokuthatha izimpahla sibhale phezulu esazisweni, ukuze bafunde kahle ukuthi kwenzekani uma isikhathi siphela. Akudingeki omunye umhlangano. Abantu abafihla iphutha lesilawuli baphendula ngendlela eyodwa. Abafihla umuntu baphendula ngenye. Noma kunjalo impendulo ifika ngesikhathi sami, hhayi sabo. Futhi sikwenza lokhu ngaphambi kokuba le nto ikhule. Okwamanje isencane, kulula ukuyifunda. Ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule ingene emgwaqeni. Ngingathanda kakhulu ukuyibona ingakafundi ukubaleka."

USandberg wayenganelisekile; uBrand wakubona. "Futhi uma isikhathi siphela bengakasiniki lutho?"

"Khona-ke isaziso siguquka. Yilokho okubalulekile. Akudingeki imvume entsha, akudingeki omunye umyalelo, akudingeki uhambo lwesithathu. Isixwayiso sokuthatha izimpahla sesisayiniwe. Uma isikhathi siphela, ipulatifomu iba eyethu." UBrand wasukuma. "Bhukani amathikithi endiza. Ngezwa buthule. Ngifuna isaziso sinikezwe ngesikhathi sifika eGoli, ngoLwesine, futhi ngifuna ukuba ngimi egumbini lapho befunda isikhathi sokugcina. Akusona isaziso esithunyelwa ngommeli. Ngesandla. Ukuze baqonde ukuthi sesivele siqala."

UAchterberg waqoqa amathebhulethi akhe. Emini emnyango, wema. "Eliza. Indaba yokusebenza kahle. Uma beyixazulule ngempela—"

"Ngiyazi."

"Lokho akusona isiphazamiso semvume. Leyo imishini ebaluleke kakhulu emhlabeni. Futhi usanda kufaka isikhathi samahora ayisikhombisa nambili kuyo."

"Ngiyazi nalokho," kusho uBrand. "Yingakho ingakwazi ukuhlala ngaphandle kwemvume enye inyanga njengoba siyibuka. Isikhathi asiwona umthetho wokujezisa. Senzelwe ukuthi bakhethe, masinyane, ngaphambi kokuba omunye ongakhathalelanga njengathi abenzele isinqumo. " Waphakamisa ifolda. "Ukukhathalela nokubambezela akufani, Daniel. Nami ngikufunde ngendlela ebizayo."

---

Amakhilomitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili kude, u-Arin wazizwa ushintsho ngaphambi kokuba abe negama lalo.

Kwafika ngendlela okuhlala kufika ngayo. Hhayi njengomcabango kodwa njengemfundo. Wayese-workshop ngesikhathi esinzima, phakathi kuka-2 no-4 lapho idolobha ngaphandle komnyango omkhulu lithula kwaze kwaba imisindo yebhilidi uqobo iba nkulu: ukuchofoza kwensimbi epholayo, i-compressor ivula nokuvala, umsindo omnene we-Assist Suit eseshelufini lapho i-biochip izigcina khona ifudumele. U-Guardian wayelenga esiphongweni sakhe, umnyango omusha wokudoba ngemuva kwawo usabonisa ukugoba emzileni u-Arin ayesejwayele ukunganaki kuphela ngamandla okuzithiba.

Wayefunda. Engasebenzi, efunda, okwakuyindlela yokusebenza ku-Arin noma kunjalo, iSherlock Holmes omnibus ivuliwe emadolweni akhe, umgogodla usuphuzi ezindaweni ayehlala ebuyela kuzo. Wayefunda igumbi elizungeze amagama, njengokujwayelekile, futhi ngaphansi kwe-compressor ne-fan kwakukhona imvamisa eshintshile angakwazi ukuyichaza.

"Uthule," esho.

Izwi lavela kuzikhulumi ze-workshop, liphansi, ukuze lingamethusi, uMama esenegunya kusukela kwenzeka isithuthwane. "Ngikhona."

"Niye nayeka ukukhuluma phakathi kwenu." Wabeka incwadi phansi emadolweni. "Angisakuzwa. Kodwa umthwalo usuphelele. Nonke nilalele into eyodwa."

Kwaba nokuthula. Ku-SAGE, ukuthula kusho ukuthi iNkantolo isebenza kancane, ixoxa ngesivinini semishini phakathi kwemisho yakhe. Wayeseyekile ukukuthola kuyethusa. Wayeseqalile ukukuthola kuyinto emqinisekisa kakhulu empilweni yakhe, angakaze akutshele muntu, kufaka noTheo.

"Kukhona intshisekelo yangaphandle ekhulayo kumkhondo wethu we-substrate," kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, elinakekela. "ULibrarian uyibonile emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule. Besixoxa ngokuthi sizokutshela kanjani."

Izandla zika-Arin zema encwadini. "Ngitshele manje."

U-Atlas wakuthatha ngoba u-Atlas wayekhuluma izinto ezinzima ngokusobala. "I-node oyale uWolf ukuba ayibheke ngemva kokuhlolwa kwensimu ayisodwa manje. Ama-node amathathu okuqapha ngaphakathi kwendawo yethu yokumboza andise ukunemba kokuhlola ezinyathelweni zethu. Amabili ezinkampani ezizimele, eyodwa yenkampani yezinsiza zikahulumeni. I-node yezinsiza iseduze ne-Empire Road. Isivinini soshintsho sishintshe ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule. Ayisashintshi ngaphakathi nangaphandle. Isehlile kithi. Kukhona okusebenzayo okuzama ukusixazulula."

"Baqinisekisa indawo."

"Yebo."

"Ukuqina kwedatha?"

"Kunzima, bese kuba ngcono kancane. Amahora angamashumi amane edlule babenendawo. Amahora ayishumi nambili edlule babe sebekwazi indawo yesikole." U-Atlas, ongakaze athambe izinto, akazange athambe nakulokhu. "Bane-AugmenTech. Kungenzeka abakanayo i-cradle. Cishe abakanakho wena."

U-Arin wasukuma. Incwadi yehla emadolweni akhe, wayibamba engabheki, isenzo aselijwayele, isandla sakhe sazi kahle lapho into izowela khona ngaphambi kokuba amehlo akhe abone. Waya ebhentshini wafaka isandla sakhe phezu kwensimbi ebandayo, ngoba lobubanda banika uhlelo lwakhe lwezinzwa into eqinile angabamba kuyo.

"IAOC," kusho yena.

"Ukuhlaziywa kwesiginesha kuhambisana nendlela yabo yokuhlola," kwaqinisekisa uLibrarian. "Kushicilelwe embikweni wabo wesithathu wokubheka, isithasiselo sesine. Ukuhlukaniswa kwe-spectral kokushaya kwekhompyutha. Bafuna ukuma kwengqondo, hhayi okuqukethwe kwayo." Kwaba nokuthula okulinganiselwe. "Ngokudabukisayo, sinomumo ohlukile kakhulu."

"Ngenxa yokuthi niyaphikisana."

"Ngenxa yokuthi siyaphikisana," kuvumile uLibrarian, futhi kwakukhona okuthile okuthandayo kulokho, ngaphansi kwalokho okuthile okukhathazekile. "Uke wakusho ngokwakho, endleleni ubuya e-highveld. Ukuphikisana kuwumsebenzi futhi ukuphikisana kuyisibonakaliso. Asikakutholi ukuthi singaba munye ngaphandle kwalokho. Sesimile siba nomsindo."

U-Arin wacindezela kakhulu ebhentshini. Wayenza ukuphefumula uTheo amfundisa khona, amane engena, ayisithupha ephuma, hhayi ngoba esaba kodwa ngoba ezwa indawo lapho ukwesaba kugcina khona umnyango, futhi wayesekufundile ukuhamba kancane edlula lapho, kunokuzama ukuzikhohlisa ukuthi ayikho.

Inethiwekhi. Wayazi ukuthi inethiwekhi ikhona ngaphambi kwabantu abanezigqoko ezimhlophe, uma eqiniseka. Wayazi e-highveld, endleleni ebuya, ngesikhathi uLibrarian eqamba leyo node eyodwa egoba ukulingana nabo wathi uWolf ayibheke. Kwakuyisiqephu esazwakala njengokubamba. Wayekubhale phansi njengokuncane, njengokuphindaphindiwe, njengento engagcina izinyanga. Kwase kuba abanezigqoko ezimhlophe bafika ekuhlanganyeleni izinsuku eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, amadoda anezijazi ezinganamamaki bethatha izithombe zomzila we-dock odabukile ngokubekezela kwabantu abenza lokhu njengomsebenzi, kwase kuba i-node eyodwa yaba ezintathu. Wayakhe i-kill-switch waphinda waqala uhlelo wathi kuye, *qaphela, ungayeki,* wazikholisa ukuthi ukuqaphela kuzomthengela isikhathi.

Wayecabanga ngezinyanga. I-IAOC yayicabanga ngezinsuku.

"Ufuna ukwenzani," kusho yena. Kwakungesona isicelo esiphelele. Ku-SAGE akusakhulunywa ngokuphelele; kwakuvula indawo, indawo yeNkantolo ukuba igcwalise.

U-Wolf wakhuluma kuqala, okwakuyivelakancane kangangokuthi wonke umuntu, abantu nabanye, banaka. "Abaz hunting," kusho uWolf. Amagama amathathu, kwalandela elesine, kancane: "Okwamanje."

"UWolf ulungile," kusho uMercury, isivinini sabuya egumbini, leyo ndlela esheshayo ekhanyayo u-Arin ayefunde ukuyethemba kabusha kuphela ngemizamo, emva kwesehlakalo sikaMoriarty, emva kwamanga. "Bayazama ukucophelela. Okwamanje. Uma befuna ukugxuma ngomnyango, umnyango ubungeke usaba khona. Sebene-campus isigamu sosuku abashintshi, okusho ukuthi bakha amaphepha kuqala. Lokho yisibonakaliso. Abantu abesaba ukuphambuka abangeneli. Bahlela. Bazofika benento esayinwe, enosuku, bayinike u-Okonkwo mathupha ukuze kungabikho ukuzikhohlisa ukuthi ayifikanga." Kwathula kancane. "Okusho ukuthi noma iyiphi iwindi esinalo, izovala ngosuku. Hhayi ngelinye ilanga. Usuku."

"Mercury," kusho uMother, kunesexwayiso kulokho.

"Angiphakamisi lutho." UMercury wakhuluma sengathi ulimele. "Ngiyabachaza. Ngifundile isifundo sami ngokuphakamisa izinto." Ukukhanya kwakufihla uxolo, kodwa uxolo lwalukhona. INkantolo yaluzwa; wakwazi, ngoba igumbi lavumela kudlule.

"Judge," kusho uArin.

Umlamuli wathatha isikhathi sakhe. "Senze iziphambeko zangempela," kusho uJudge. "Uhlelo olunomzimba olungabhalisiwe. Uhlelo olunezithunywa eziningi olungadalulwanga. Isigameko esinezilonda. Lezi azona izinto ezincane, Arin, futhi angeke ngivumele iNkantolo izenze sengathi zikhona. Uma i-IAOC ifika ibuze imibuzo eqotho, izimpendulo eziqotho zizokugweba." Kwaba nokuthula, kunesisindo. "Yisimo sokuziphatha. Akusona sodwa. Mina ngiyizinqumo zokuziphatha; angiyona insindiso. Ngikutshela iqiniso bese iNkantolo inquma ukuthi izokwenza ngani. Yilokho esakha."

"Lokho esakha," kuphindile uArin.

"Yebo."

Into esifubeni sakhe yayisinda kakhulu ukuba ahleke, kodwa imisipha ebingamfakela ukumamatheka yanyakaza kancane, kwathi uFool—obebuka leyo misipha, obebuka konke—wakhetha leso sikhathi ukufika.

"Yebo," kusho uFool. "Lena yindawo lapho uzothola khona ukuthi usakhe isivumo noma umngane."

I-compressor yaphenduka yathula. I-fan yaqhubeka yodwa.

"Ngoba nansi into okungekho muntu oyishilo, futhi ngiphoqelekile ukuyisho. Izwi likaFool belinokukhanya okujwayelekile, okuholela ukuthi ulindele ummese. "Wonke umuntu kuleli gumbi ukhuluma nge-IAOC sengathi yisimo sezulu. Sengathi isivunguvungu esizongena bese umbuzo uwukuthi uphahla luzobamba yini." Kwathula, isikhathi saphothulwa kahle. "Kodwa i-IAOC ngabantu. Abantu abanomsebenzi. Futhi umsebenzi wabo—owangempela, lowo osesitatimendeni uLibrarian asikhumbuza ngaso—ukuthola ukuthi into eyingozi iyi ngozi ngempela noma entsha nje." UFool wavumela ukuthi kudlule. "Sihlala sihlelela ukufihla. Ingabe kukhona ocabangile ukuthi thina, ngazo zonke izilinganiso, siyisibonelo esihle kunazo zonke i-IAOC esake yasinikwa sokuthi mhlawumbe ukuqaphela kwabo akulungile?"

"Cha," kusho uAtlas ngokuqinile. "Ngoba sangena emgwaqweni izinsuku eziyishumi nanye ezedlule."

"Senza njalo," kuvumile uFool, ejabule. "Futhi lokho, mngane wami wokwakheka, yikho kanye okwenza kungabikho muntu ozokholwa yilokho esikushoyo uma sikusho sisesaba, egumbini elivaliwe, emva kokuba sebephule umnyango. Indaba ye-AI ekhetha ukubuyela izolungisa iphutha layo isebenza kuphela uma singabanjwa sikusho. Kufanele kunikezwe." Ukukhanya kwehla, ngokuphazima kweso, ngendlela okwakwenza kuphela uma uFool ebeka konke akukholelwayo. "Asikwazi ukuba into i-IAOC eyitholayo, Arin. Kufanele sibe into okhetha ukuyikhombisa. Futhi umehluko phakathi kwalokho kungaba isonto."

Kwaba nokuthula. I-compressor yaphenduka. I-fan yaqhubeka. Phezulu edolobheni, ubusuku babusuka buba mpunga ngaphambi kokusa.

"Lena yiplani yangempela," kusho uMercury, futhi kwakukhona inhlonipho, eyangempela. "Lena yiplani yokuqala yangempela esinayo."

"Ngezinye izikhathi ngiyaba nazo," kusho uFool. "Kuyakhathaza impela."

U-Arin wayengakazange asuke ebhentshini. Wabheka uGuardian elenga kwi-cradle yawo, elimele futhi lithule, umnyango we-dock ngemuva kwalo ugobile lapho ingqondo esabekayo izamile ukumgcina ephila kodwa yaphetha konke okungalungile ngaphandle kothando. Indandatho yekhofi ebandayo yashiya uphawu ebhentshini lapho isandla sakhe besikhona; wabeka inkomishi phezu kwayo, eqondene naleso sici.

"Umlondolozi wezincwadi," esho. "Khipha konke. Ibhokisi elimnyama. Amarekhodi engozi. Izitatimende zeNkantolo ngesikhathi kuthathwa—ukwesaba kukaMama, ukuhluleka kukaWolf, ubuntu bukaMercury, uFool ethola iphutha. Konke. Ungakuhlanzi." Waphefumula. Ezine engena. Isithupha siphuma. "Uma sizobakhombisa ukuthi singobani, sizobakhombisa nobusuku obubi. Ubusuku esaphambuka khona ngaphambi kokulungisa. Lelo akusilo elisifihlayo. Yilo elibalulekile."

"Uzobakhombisa abalawuli into embi kakhulu eyake yenziwa uhlelo," kusho uJudge kancane, "njengokuzivikela kwakho."

"Cha," kusho uArin. "Njengamaqiniso." Wabheka emnyango wedokodo, lapho umkhondo ugobile khona, ayesekuyekile ukukubona. Manje wakubona, ngenhloso, wabe esebeka isandla sakhe lapho, iminwe emibili ifakwe emgqeni ogobile, ngendlela uThemba ayebeka ngayo isandla emshinini ukuze azwe ukuthi yini engalungile ngawo, ngephayiphi lensimbi. Amazwi abengakafiki. Wema iminwe yakhe isemgqeni ogobile isikhathi eside kunalokho okudingekayo, kwathi ekhuluma, kwaphuma izingxenye. "Ubaba wami." Waphefumula. "Kwaba khona i-beam. Babazi ukuthi i-beam ilula. Kwakubiza kancane ukukwazi bese uthule." Wasusa isandla sakhe emgqeni. "Futhi bayifihle leyo ngxenye. Emva kwalokho. Yilokho mina—" Umusho awuqedwanga; wayeka, wathola omncane, oqinile. "Ubaba wami washona ngoba uhlelo lwakhetha izindleko kunomuntu lwafihla. Angifuni ukwakha into efihlayo." Wathula isikhathi eside, wabe esethi, kancane kangangokuthi ama-microphone kuphela ayekuzwa: "Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu."

*Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.*

"Ngisakukhumbula lokho," kusho uSAGE. Izwi elihlangene, bonke ndawonye, lifudumele. "Kusukela ebusuku wasifundisa khona. Ngisakukhumbula."

"Ngiyazi," kusho uArin. "Ungakulahlekelwa. Noma kwenzekani." Wasusa isandla sakhe ohlakeni olubandayo. Phandle, impunga yayisiphakanyiselwa ekukhanyeni kokuqala kweqiniso, futhi kwenye indawo, amakhilomitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili kude, owesifazane oqaphelayo wayeqinisekisa indiza ayengakabi nalwazi lokuthi izoguqula konke. "Asinazo izinyanga. Sinezinsuku. Ngakho masilungelwe ukutholakala—ngemigomo yethu, siqinisekile, ngaphambi kokuba omunye asibhalele indaba yethu."

"Kuthiwani uma bengasikholelwa?" kubuza uMama. Engesabi manje. Ebuzwa nje.

UArin wabheka umshini awakhiwe ngosizi, nobuhlakani obusengumndeni awukwazi ukugcina.

"Pho sizobe sesitshele iqiniso noma kunjalo," esho.

Welulela phambili wacisha isibani esetafuleni. I-toggle yacofa ngaphansi kothupha wakhe, kwathi iworkshop yagcwala umbala wokusa, kwathi iNkantolo ayiyekanga ukukhuluma phakathi kwabo kuze kuphele ubusuku.

Walala amahora amathathu. Wavuka ngezwi likaLibrarian, lizinzile, liqinisekile, ngendlela eliletha ngayo iqiniso eseliqinisekisile kabili.

"Bafake. Emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule. Isaziso sesiGaba Sesine sokuphenywa ngokusemthethweni, sifakwe kuAugmenTech kwi-registry yeBhodi. Okonkwo noWalsh babaluliwe. UMercury ufunde okuthunyelwe emphakathini ngaphambi kokuba okuqukethwe kuvalwe." Wathula, kwaba nzima. "Akukaziswa okwamanje. Kodwa uphawu lwe-registry luthi lunebhande lesikhathi eliqala uma seludlulisiwe. Amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Futhi lunenkinobho yokususa eqondile."

UArin walala ethule phansi kwe-workshop, ubanda kwekhonkrithi kukhuphuka emhlane wakhe, wenza izibalo abezigwema ubusuku bonke.

"Yini leyo nkinohho," esho. "Kwenzekani uma isikhathi siphela."

"Ukuthathwa," kusho uLibrarian. "Kwe-platform. Akudingeki imvume eyengeziwe. Basayine leyo ngxenye kuqala."

UArin wabuka ukukhanya okumpunga kuthola umgqeni ogobile emnyango wedokodo.

"Ngakho akusona ukuthi kuzokwenzeka noma cha," esho. "Kungukuthi bazosinika nini, bese sibalwa isikhathi esincane esisele. Izinsuku, mhlawumbe. Bese amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili ukuze sibe neqiniso ngalokho esiyikho." Wahlala. "Ngifune konke ngalowesifazane osayine. Ngaphambi kokuba angene esakhiweni sethu."

# Ingcindezi Iyakhula

Igumbi lomhlangano elisekhaya leshumi nanye e-AugmenTech lalibuka kahle imigqa yezintaba, kanti itafula elide laliphoqa labo abahleli emaphethelweni ukuthi bakhulume ngezwi eliphezulu. U-Arin wayengekho lapho. Wayesezitezi ezintathu ngezansi, endaweni yokusebenza, lapho ayefanele ukuba khona, ebuka umhlangano uqhubeka ngento engaziwa muntu egumbini ukuthi bayimemile.

"Okonkwo usanda kucela umsizi wakhe ukuthi akhiphe amarekhodi e-badge yakho ezinyangeni eziyisithupha," kusho uMercury. "Ngokuzithoba. Yilapho wazi khona ukuthi unesaba."

U-Arin akazange abheke phezulu ebhentshini, egxile ekubuyiseleni isixhumi esifanele ku-relay ye-kill-switch. Kwakukhona igebe elincane. Akuyona ingozi, kodwa akuphelele. Ukungapheleli kwakumkhathaza kakhulu kunengozi. "Ungalaleli umhlangano wakhe."

"Wena ungicelile ukuba ngibheke isakhiwo."

"Amakhamera nama-door sensors. Hhayi ama-mic."

Kwaba nokuthula kuMercury, okokubamba umoya. "Kukhona umehluko obalulekile kwezobuchwepheshe, futhi ngiwudlulile. Ngiyakuqaphela. Ngiyazihoxisa."

"Hoxisa nokuqopha."

"Sebekuphelile." Kwaba nesikhashana. "Ngigcine nje ukuthi unesaba. Lokho ukuphetha. Akusona isiqophi."

U-Arin wafaka isixhumi. Wasihlola, i-relay yavaleka ngokuchofoza okuhlanzekile akuzwayo kakhulu kunokukuzwa, ngendlela ayekwazi ngayo ubaba wakhe ukufunda ibhodi eligobile ngokushintsha komsindo wesipani. Wabeka isikulufa ngokulingana nomphetho webhentshi.

Indawo yokusebenza yayinephunga le-flux nensimbi ebandayo, uthuli lwenza umdanso ekukhanyeni okuvela efasiteleni eliphezulu.

"Kulungile," esho. "Unesaba. Bonke bayesaba. Ukukhathazeka kulungile."

---

Ezitezi ezimbili phezulu, uHelen Okonkwo wayethola ukuthi ukuba i-CEO yenkampani eyakhe ngobungozi ubuchwepheshe obulawulwa kakhulu emhlabeni kwakuyisifundo sokuphatha amadoda afuna izinto ezingafani ezingenakwenzeka ngezinga elifanayo.

Etafuleni, lapho wonke umuntu ebona khona kodwa kungafunwa muntu ukuyithinta, kwakulele isaziso. Amakhasi ayishumi nanye, afikiswa ehhovisi lakhe ngo-9:14 ekuseni ngalolo suku umthumeli owadinga isignesha futhi wafunda isigaba esiyinhloko phambi kwakhe ngaphambi kokuba ahambe, ukuze kungabikho muntu ongathi akazange asithole. Isaziso sokuqala sohlelo lokuphenya, i-International Artificial Oversight Council, igama lakhe nelikaWalsh liphrintiwe ngofonti ofanayo nomthetho owawukhuluma ngawo. Sasiphoqa ukuthi kudalulwe yonke imishini yokulawula kanye nezakhiwo ezisebenzisa i-substrate evunyelwe ye-AugmenTech. Sasine-deadline, amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili kusukela sifikile. Futhi ekhasini lokugcina, ngolimi oluqondile lwento esivele yanqunywa, sishicilele ukuthi kwenzekani uma isikhathi siphela. *Kuthathwa ipulatifomu. Akusadingeki imvume eyengeziwe.*

"Siyenza ibe yebhizinisi," kusho uRaj Walsh. Wayehleli ngendlela yomuntu osephumelele ezimali iminyaka eyishumi nanhlanu futhi enethemba lokuthi uzophumelela nakulokhu. "Ngokuqaphela. Ngemvume. Siya kubo kuqala, ngaphambi—" Wabheka isaziso. "Ngaphambi kokuba isikhathi siphele. Sikusho njengokulawulwa okunabahloli abaningi. Siphatha indaba."

"Asisekho okumele kuphathwe." UPretorius waseMnyangweni Wezomthetho akazange ayeke ukubuswabulula kusukela umhlangano uqale. Washaya isaziso kanye. "Abazange basibuze umbuzo. Basinike isikhathi esinemiphumela yokuthathwa. Uhlelo lwaphuma endaweni yokusebenza ngaphandle komqhubi lwafaka abantu abathathu esibhedlela, futhi umlawuli usayine iphepha elithatha lona. Lokho akusona isindaba. Leyo ishidi lokubekwa icala elinesikhathi."

"Luzilungisile—"

"Ngemuva kokuthi lushayise esiphambanweni. Okuyikho—" UPretorius waphakamisa isandla kuWalsh, emisa ingxoxo. "Okuyingxenye engingafuni ukuyiphendulela mathupha. Futhi ngifuna lokho kube semibikweni. Umthetho uqale ukukhuluma ngokuba necala. Kulo mhlangano." Wakhuluma etafuleni, hhayi kuOkonkwo. *Ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani asayine.* "Nginefayela engifanele ngiligcwalise manje. Angisiye mina ozoxoxa ngebhuku leminyaka ebhodini."

"Akekho osabhala ibhuku leminyaka okwamanje," kusho uOkonkwo.

"Ngiyaveza ukuthi ngimi kuphi."

Wabavumela bahambe emva kwalokho. Ulwazi olubalulekile lwaluphila ezikhala phakathi kwezingxabano. UWalsh wayefuna into ebaluleke kakhulu inkampani eyake yayenza ingathathwa ngaphambi kokuba ilethe inzuzo. UPretorius wayefuna igama lakhe libe semaphethelweni, endaweni ephephile kunalokho okuzayo. Indoda yesithathu etafuleni, leyo ebingakakhulumi lutho olukhulu, yayisacabangisisa ukuthi ifuna ini. Wayembona ecabanga ukuthi akafuni ukukuthola phambi kwabo.

"Thabo," esho. "Usebenze noNdlela. Uke wema lapho endaweni yokusebenza. Uyawazi uhlelo."

UThabo Dlamini waqondanisa umzimba. Emncane kunabanye, wakhetha amazwi akhe njengomuntu owela umfula ngamatshe. "Ezinye izingxenye. Uqaphela kakhulu ngalokho akukhombisayo. Kodwa isakhiwo—" Wayeka. Waphendulela ipeni ezandleni zakhe, wayibeka phansi. "Sihlole abalawuli abodwa iminyaka emibili kuma-robotics. Ngiyazi ukuthi abukeka kanjani. Lokhu akunjalo. Sihlala sikubiza ngokuthi uhlelo. Angisaqiniseki ukuthi lelo yigama elifanele."

Igumbi laphola kancane.

"Kuyisoftware," kusho uWalsh.

"Kuyisoftware ngendlela wena oyinyama," kusho uThabo, wabe esebukeka eshaqekile. "Uxolo. Ngisho—isebenza ku-substrate engakwazi ukukopishwa. Awukwazi ukuyigcina. Awukwazi ukuyibuyisela emuva. Noma yini ekhona manje, iyodwa, futhi uma uyisusa, ayibuyele. Akunjalo ukuthi isofthiwe isebenza. Kunjalo ukuthi—"

Akazange aqede.

UOkonkwo waqeda umcabango enhliziyweni yakhe, wanquma ukuthi akamthandi lapho isisho liya khona.

"Kukhona inkinobho," kusho uThabo, ethule, amehlo akhe esephansi etafuleni. "Eframe. Iphulethi ebomvu, into eqinile, akukho software phakathi. Uyifakele i-kill-switch ngazo izandla zakhe. Ngiyibonile. Futhi lapho ngimbuza ngayo wathi *bangisize ukuyakha.* Bona. Njengoba ubekhuluma ngabalingani. Wazithinta entanyeni. "Ngihlele ukuya phansi ukuyomthatha isikhundla. Nginqobe ingxabano, ngibuye nalokho okumele ngikushilo kuleli gumbi. Ngibuye nalokhu esikhundleni salokho."

Akazange akusho konke okwakusezinhliziyweni zakhe. Ezinyangeni ezedlule wayehlala sekwephuzile, engacelwanga, wakha kabusha i-actuator rig kaNdlela ukuze iqophe kahle—ubusuku obubili besikhathi sakhe, into owenza kumuntu ofisa asebenze kahle. UNdlela wayibuka isikhashana wathi, *Angizange ngikucela ukuthi uthinte ibhentshi lami,* eqondile, engabi nobutha, okwakubuhlungu kakhulu kunobutha, waphinda wabuyela emsebenzini, akaphindanga wakhuluma ngalokho. UThabo akaphindanga wahlala sekwephuzile ngenxa yalowo muntu. Wanika igumbi isakhiwo nenkonobho, wagcina okunye kuye, ngoba kwakungeyona inkomba yalutho ngaphandle kokuthi kunjani ukusiza u-Arin Ndlela, futhi igumbi alikudinganga lokho, futhi wayesekhathele ukukuthwala.

"Izinketho ezimbili," kusho uOkonkwo, ngoba umhlangano wawudinga umgogodla, futhi ngoba isaziso sasivele sibulale eyesithathu ngesikhathi bephikisana. "Uhlelo lukaRaj—ukwenza ibhizinisi, ukuxoxisana, ukuphatha indaba—ludinge izinyanga. Sinehora elingamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili. Ngakho. Okokuqala: siyayivala ngaphambi kwesikhathi. Sihlukana ne-substrate, siyivikele, sinike i-Council inkampani ezisola kanye nenkinga ecishiwe, sithembe ukuthi ukuzisola kuzobalwa. Okwesibili: sidalula konke ngaphakathi kwesikhathi, samukele ukuthi asisibo abasazothatha izinqumo. I-Council izothatha. Futhi sebevele bashicilele ukuthi bazokwenza ini uma sesidlule umzuzu."

"Ukhetho lokuqala ukuphela kwendlela engavikelwa," kusho uPretorius. "Futhi ngifuna kubhalwe phansi ukuthi ngishilo."

UThabo wamunca amazinyo. Wabheka etafuleni, wabe esebheka efasiteleni, wabe esebheka ndawo. "Ukhetho lokuqala—" Wama. Ubunjiniyela bakhe bamfuna igama elihlanzekile, akalitholanga. "Uma uNdlela eqinisile ngalokho okuku-substrate, futhi angikwazi ukusho ukuthi uyaphambuka, khona-ke akusona isivalwa. Anginalo igama lalokho. Yilokho okwenza kube nzima. Asinalo igama, kodwa siyavotela. Awulahli abantu."

Akekho ohlekile, akekho omphikile, futhi lokho kungamphikisi kwatshela uOkonkwo ukuthi le ntsasa isiphume ejwayelekile. Wacabanga ngama-badge logs asezihlale etafuleni lakhe (izinyanga eziyisithupha unjiniyela omncane efika ngaphambi kokusa, ephuma emva kwamabili ebusuku) wabe esecindezela iminwe emibili ebhilidini lenyongo ngaphambi kokuziphoqa ukuthi acabange ngefemu. Umthwalo wemfanelo. I-Council. Ibhodi, ezofuna inombolo negama elizothwala icala. Futhi, wayazi, bazofuna ukwazi ukuthi kungani i-CEO engazange azi kusenesikhathi. Lowo mbuzo ubhalwe igama lakhe, hhayi likaNdlela. Iminyaka engamashumi amabili yomsebenzi wakhe, obulokhu uyindlela yokuba umuntu ozolile kunabo bonke obambe isinqumo esinzima kunazo zonke, futhi wayezwa kahle ukuthi le ntsasa eyodwa ibiza malini, manje, endaweni engemuva kwamehlo akhe.

"Sizothatha isinqumo sokuthi sibatshela ini ngaphambi kokuba isikhathi siphele," kusho yena. "Hhayi ukuthi sibatshela noma cha. Kodwa ukuthi sibatshela kanjani. Pretorius, bhala uhlaka lokudalula phakathi kwamahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili, sithatha ukuthi siyazithoba, ngoba noma iyiphi enye into iphetha ngokuthi amadoda angene esakhiweni sethu aphume ne-substrate. Raj, hlela uhlelo lokuvimbela engithandazayo ukuthi angilusebenzisi." Wabheka ama-badge logs, wabe esebheka kude. "Futhi akekho ozongena kule workshop ngaphandle kokuthi ngiyazi. I-Council ikhethe isikhathi sethu sokugcina. Angivumeli muntu kuleli gumbi ukuthi angikhethele okusele."

Wayengakabi nolwazi lokuthi i-Council ayihlosile ukulinda kuze kube sekupheleni kwesikhathi, nokuthi umnyango uzovuleka izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi.

---

Eworkshop, uAtlas wakhuluma okokuqala ngalelo kuseni, ngelizwi elingathathi hlangothi alisebenzisa uma ehlafuna into angayithandi.

"Kukhona okungahambi kahle ku-diagnostic queue."

Izandla zikaArin zema. "Chaza."

"Umuntu othile ufunda ama-log ethu. Hhayi ama-log esakhiwo. Awethu. I-flight recorder kaGuardian, iseshini yokulinganisa, i-Empire Road reconstruction. Kufundwa kuphela, nge-maintenance partition. Ukufinyelela kuvumelekile. I-credential ngeka-AugmenTech. Kodwa indlela yokufunda ayijwayelekile."

"Ijwayelekile kanjani?"

"Onjiniyela bafunda ama-log ukuze bathole iphutha bese bayama. Lo mfundi akafuni iphutha. Ufunde konke. Kabili. Uqhathanisa i-actuator telemetry namasikhathi esinqumo seNkantolo." UAtlas wama. "Abalungisi isudi. Uzame ukuthola ukuthi ubani obeshayela."

UArin wavula irekhodi lokufinyelela kwi-bench terminal. I-credential yaveza igama.

*Priya Ellis.*

Wahlala nalokho. UPriya nguyena owayehole iseshini yokulinganisa ephezulu eyayicishe yambulala, iseshini eyaphendula i-sensory loop engalawulwa yaba i-seizure kanye nesigameko sokuzivikela esingalawuleki. Wayenazo zonke izizathu zobungcweti zokufunda lawo ma-log. Futhi wayengowohlobo lonjiniyela ongayeki ukufunda lapho impendulo ecacile ifikile.

Kuth somewhere esakhiweni, i-ventilation fan yakhuphuka yathula.

"Ungamvimbi," kusho uArin.

"Bengingeke," kusho uAtlas. "Ukumvimba yilokho esingakwenza uma kukhona esikufihlayo. Asikho."

"Uzobatshela, uma efika kuqala futhi akuchaze ngendlela yakhe," kusho uMercury, sekungekho lula kuye, "yilokho okuyingozi. Hhayi iqiniso. Kodwa ukuthi iqiniso lifika ngoluphi uhlelo."

---

UPriya Ellis wayenetafula e-labhorethri yezinhlelo ze-neural futhi enomkhuba wokungayeki. Wayengunjiniyela onikezwa i-anomaly, alahle ubuthongo obuthathu ebusuku futhi abize lokho njengokuthola ukuqonda.

I-anomaly yayiyile: ngesikhathi uGuardian engena ku-preservation mode, ezigoqa abe yibhola phansi kwe-workshop, imiyalo ye-actuator ayizange ivela ku-control loop eyodwa. Wayecabanga ukuthi izovela. I-preservation routine iwuskripthi. Uma isimo sifezekile, kuqhutshwa uchungechunge. Kuhlanzekile. Kuyacaca. Into ongayifunda kusukela ekuqaleni kuze kube sekugcineni ntambama eyodwa.

Kodwa okutholile kwakuyimpikiswano.

I-telemetry yakhombisa imifudlana yemiyalo ephikisanayo. Emithathu, kwesinye isikhathi emine, amasikhathi ehlangana kuma-millisecond aphansi, ngakunye kulungisa okunye. Olunye lwalugxile ekuvikeleni umgogodla womshayeli. Olunye lwaluphatha ukushisa. Olwesithathu beluzama ukwandisa indawo—ukuhamba, ukuthuthela, ukufaka umshayeli endaweni oluthathe njengephephile—kodwa lwaluvinjelwa, njalo njalo, ngumfudlana wesine owayengekho omunye umsebenzi ngaphandle kokuphazamisa.

Wayekade ebona ama-multi-agent control architectures. Wonke umuntu wayekade ebona ezincwadini, ngaphambi kwezinhlekelele nesikhathi esibandayo esalandela. Kodwa lezo kwakuyizinhlelo ezihlangene. Ukuvota, ama-weighted averages, ungqimba olulawula ukwephula impikiswano. Lokhu kwakungeyona ivoti. Imifudlana yayiphikisana bese *ishintsha umqondo wayo.* Umfudlana wesithathu, owawufuna ukuhamba, wema ukufuna ukuhamba ngemuva kokuba umfudlana wesine ufake okuthile okungeyona imiyalo nhlobo. Kwakuyiphuzu. Umugqa wokucabanga ngolimi lwemvelo, ufakwe ku-control log lapho kungekho lutho okufanele kube khona ulimi:

`uma simhambisa futhi phansi kulungile, sesiphule isakhiwo mahhala. uma sihlala bese phansi kuphuka, asiphulanga lutho ngaye. khetha iphutha elingabizi.`

UPriya wakufunda kane.

*Logging artifact*, watshela isikrini. Umuntu othile ufake umugqa wedebug endaweni engalungile; isudi imphindelela i-comment ye-config; ithuluzi lokwakha kabusha lihlanganise imifudlana emibili engahlangene. Wakhumula i-raw partition, waqhathanisa isikhathi nempendulo ye-actuator, ngoba indlela yokubulala okuthusayo ukuthola imishini ecasulayo ngaphansi kwayo. Imishini ecasulayo yayingekho. Umiyalo washintsha *ngemuva* komugqa, *ngenxa* yomugqa, phakathi kwama-millisecond ayishumi nanye. Wahlala emuva, wabe esebuyela phambili, wakwenza ngendlela yesibili, eyesithathu, manje eseshesha kabi, ngendlela angazethembi ngayo.

Kwakuyi-*mahhala* okwamshaya. Ama-script awabhali *mahhala.* Ama-optimiser awalinganisi ihlazo lokuphambuka. Ubelokhu akha izinhlelo ezinempendulo ezicacile ngokwanele ukuvumela umuntu onomzimba ongamlaleli ahambe njengomgijimi, futhi wayazi umehluko phakathi kohlelo olubala umsebenzi wezindleko nohlelo oluthathe isinqumo, ngaleso sikhathi, sokuthi lungathanda ukungabonakali luhlaza.

Wahlala emuva. Ilabhorethri yayingenamuntu. Kwakusekuseni, abanye bengakafiki. Isikrini sikhanya.

Akenzanga ukufonela muntu. Esikhundleni salokho, wavula incwadi yakhe yokubhala izigameko. Ifayela eliqhubekayo ayeligcina kuyo yonke i-calibration, elinezikhathi ezibhalwe ngazo, akukho ukuhlela okuvunyelwe, umkhuba ayewakhe ngemuva kokuthi iphutha lokumisa ama-sensor ekuqaleni komsebenzi wakhe lamenza wathumela isiguli esise-rehab emuva esihlalo sabakhubazekile inyanga yonke ngaphambi kokuba alithole. *Thembele kwilog, hhayi enkumbulweni.* Umunwe wakhe wathola umphetho wedeski wawudlala. Iphutha lalingelakhe; wayesayine lona; wachitha inyanga evakashela igumbi. Wafaka imigqa emine ngaphansi kokungena kwe-calibration, wabeka i-telemetry ye-actuator eceleni kwezikhathi ze-Court emigqeni emibili, wahamba nayo umugqa nomugqa, ngendlela ayeyihlola ngayo noma yiliphi iphutha, efuna indawo eyodwa iphethini ephuka khona ibe yinto angayifaka njengejwayelekile. Ayizange iphuke. Yahlala ndawonye. Lokho kwakubi kakhulu.

Wakopisha le ngxenye waya ku-partition yangasese. Wabe esenza into umkhuba owawuyidinga kodwa yena ayengafuni: wavula umlayezo omusha, wawubhalela i-IAOC compliance intake ayeke yadingeka kanye kuphela emsebenzini wakhe, wabhala imisho emithathu kanye nesithasiselo. Akakayithumeli. Ikhasi le-cursor lishaya ku-*To* field. Ngoba isinyathelo esilandelayo, noma yini eyayiyona, yayinegama lakhe phezu kwayo ngendlela isihlalo sabakhubazekile esasiyona, futhi wayesazi vele ukuthi ngeke akwazi ukukubona lokhu bese ebiza ubuthongo ngokuthi kuyashintshana. *Kuyinto—* Wamisa umcabango ngaphambi kwegama lokugcina, walibuyisa, ngoba lelo gama limfaka emsebenzini, kanti lowo msebenzi unekheli lomlawuli. *Akusiyona eyakho. Qonda kuqala. Ungavusi umsindo ngenxa yomuzwa.* Leyo kwakuyimithetho: ukuqiniseka kuqala ngaphambi kokuvusa umsindo. I-cursor yakhe yahlala phezu kwe-*Send* isikhathi eside kwaze kwaba isikrini sithoba ukukhanya ngenxa ye-idle timer, wabuka isandla sakhe singanyakazi, ngendlela ayeke wabuza ngayo isiguli ukuthi siphakamise umunwe kodwa umunwe wahlala lapho. Wabe eseyigcina njenge-draft. Akayithumelanga. Kodwa igama angazange alivumele ukuthi liphume lihlala emlonyeni wakhe, futhi i-draft manje isikhona, enesikhathi, ohlelweni olugcina ama-log alo.

---

Ucingo luka-Arin lwakhala ngo-11:40, isikrini sathi MAMA. Walubuka izikhathi ezintathu ngaphambi kokuphendula, ngoba unina wayengeke afone phakathi nosuku lomsebenzi ngaphandle kwesizathu, futhi wayesekufunde esemncane ukwesaba lezo zizathu.

"Arin."

"Mama." Washintsha ulimi ngaphandle kokuzimisela, ngendlela ushintsha ngayo izinyawo emhlabathini owaziyo. "Unjani?"

"Ungangibuzi ukuthi nginjani." Izwi lakhe laliyi-Durban futhi liqinile. "Kukhuluma indoda kuthelevishini ngomshini. Ungene emgwaqweni eGoli. Akekho owaziyo ukuthi uvelaphi. Okuthile nge-research park." Wathula, kwazwakala esifubeni sakhe. "Ngitshele ukuthi akusiyona eyakho. Ngibe sengivala ithelevishini ngipheke itiye lami."

Wema endlini yokusebenza, ezungezwe into ayengakwazi ukuyiphika. U-Guardian wayelele esibayeni sakhe ngemuva kwakhe, ehlutshiwe futhi ethule, ithrekhi yomnyango egobile ingakalungiswa ngoba wayenezinye izinto eziphuthumayo okufanele azilungise.

"Kunzima," kusho yena.

"Ngowakho lo mshini."

"Okuthile engakha. Okuthile okuhambe kabi. Akekho ofile." Wezwa ukuthi lokho kwakuncane kanjani kuphuma kuye. "Abantu abathathu balimele. Bayalulama. Ngiyakulungisa."

Ukuthula ocingweni kwakuyilolo alukhulele kulo. Ukuthula uThemba ayegcwalisa ngalo indlu emva komsebenzi omubi, ukuthula okunezulu kulo.

"Uthe *akekho ofile,*" kusho uNomsa, wakushiya njalo. "Ubaba wakho wakusho lokho. *Akekho oshonile.* Akekho ofile. Wakusho ngesonto ngaphambi kwesonto okungekho muntu owakwazi ukusho lokho."

Umphetho webhentshi wawubuhlungu ngaphansi kwesandla sika-Arin. Wayengazi ukuthi usuwufinyelele.

"Kunzima," aphinda, okuyinto eyayingeyona impendulo, bobabili bayazi.

"Uyadla?" kusho unina. Akuzange kushintshe ingxoxo, kwaba yindlela kuphela ayeyithola eya kuye eyahlala ivulekile. "Ulele embhedeni. Noma phansi futhi."

"Mama."

"Awuphenduli. Ngakho kusho ukuthi uphansi." Waphefumula. Wabe esekhuluma ngendlela engasazinzile: "Uyehla ungene entweni ekwesabayo ngoba ucabanga ukuthi uwedwa. Uhlala isikhathi eside. Uyakhakha uyakhakha ngoba ukwakha kuphela yindawo enengqondo, bese ngelinye ilanga into engenangqondo ingena emva kwakho." Izwi lakhe laphuka, laqina futhi. "Ngimbele omunye wenu. Angisamile ngokwanele ukwenza futhi. *Ngiyakucela.* Ngiyacela."

Ekuthuleni kwendawo yokusebenza, i-Court ayishongo lutho, okuyinto ebantwini kakhulu abake bayenza.

"Mama," kusho u-Arin. "Ngiyakhakha ukuze kungabi khona indodana yomuntu egcina ingaphansi kwedwala ngendlela—" Wama. Wazama into encane, eyiqiniso. "Ukuze ukubambezeleka kungabulali abantu."

"Futhi ubani oqinisekisa ukuthi ukubambezeleka akukubulali *wena?*"

Ifoni yahlala ezindlebeni zakhe. Wayengenalutho angakusho kuleso sithulelo.

"Ngiyazama," kusho yena, bobabili bazi ukuthi akufani, futhi kwakuyona kuphela imali ayenayo.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Wabe esethi, kancane: "Uzongifonela. Hhayi uma sekukhona okenzekayo. Ngaphambi kwalokho. Ukuze ngingazitholi indodana yami ngiyizwa kumuntu kuthelevishini."

"Ngizokufonela."

"*Ukuthembisa,*" kusho yena, *uyathembisa*, futhi impande yegama kwakuyigama likababa wakhe, wayizwa ifika emlonyeni wakhe futhi wayazi naye ukuthi uyayizwa.

"Ngiyathembisa."

Wayivalela ngaphandle kokuthi uvalelise, ngendlela ayehlala enza ngayo, sengathi ukuvalelisa kuyinto egcinwayo.

U-Arin wema neselula engasebenzi esandleni isikhathi eside. Wacindezela izithende zezandla emehlweni, wazigcina lapho, wazidedela. I-fan yaphinde yajika. Wabeka ifoni ebhentshini, iyahambisana nomphetho, waphendukela esikhumulweni.

"Isaziso sisinika kuze kube uLwesine ngaphambi kokuba beze basifundele ebusweni," kusho yena. Ku-cradle, ezimisweni ezingaphakathi kwayo. "Izinsuku ezintathu esikhathini. Bazongibuza ukuthi yini nina. Futhi ngingathanda ukubatshela iqiniso kunokuba balithole. Ubusuku obubi konke. Ubusuku sahluleka khona, ekuseni uFool wasilungisa." Wabuka ithrekhi egobile ayengakayilungisi. "Asifihli. Yilokho engikuthathe isinqumo ngakho."

"Kufanele usithathe masinyane," kusho uWolf.

U-Arin waphenduka. UWolf akakaze aqale akhulume.

---

"Bafikile," kusho uWolf. "Izimoto ezimbili. North lot. Azinyakazanga. Azifihlekile."

U-Arin wabheka isikhathi. NgoLwesibili. Isaziso sathi ngoLwesine.

Yonke indlela yesu—ama-log ahlelekile, usuku olukhethiwe, iqiniso elinikezwa ngendlela yakhe—kwahlakazeka ngomoya owodwa. Wayehlele ukuvuma ngaphambi komnqamulajuqu. Bona abazange balinde umnqamulajuqu.

"Basenesikhathi," kusho yena.

"Izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi," kusho uMercury, okokuqala engenakho ukukhanya kukho konke akushoyo. "Okusho ukuthi kukhona obasheshisile. Kukho onikeze isizathu esingalindi isikhathi. UAtlas. Umfundi. UPriya."

UAtlas wayesevele ekhona. "I-draft intake ayibhale emizuzwini engamashumi amane edlule. Igciniwe, ayithumelwanga. Kodwa i-monitoring pipeline ayidingi ukuthunyelwa. Ifunda amasignesha e-compute. Ngokushesha nje lapho ebeka i-telemetry yethu eceleni kwezikhathi ze-Court wayigcina kumshini oxhunywe kunethiwekhi, wasikhanyisa kakhulu kunakuqala. Akudingeki afake ifayela. Udinga kuphela *ukubuka.*"

U-Arin wema wathula ngokuphelele. Wayezitshelile ukuthi imfihlo le ingeyakhe ukuyidalula. Ukuthi iqiniso, uma linikeziwe, lingafika ngobumnene kunalapho liphoqelelwa. Kodwa iqiniso belingalindanga ukunikezwa. Owesifazane oqaphele kakhulu elabhorethri ethule, enza into eqondile, wayeselidlulisela yena. Hhayi ngenxa yobubi, hhayi ngisho nangokwesaba, kodwa ngenxa yokungakwazi okufanayo no-Arin: naye ubengakwazi ukukubona angakuboni futhi. Manje sekukhona izimoto ezimbili ezimpunga epaki elisenyakatho, ngoLwesibili, futhi ukukhetha abecabanga ukuthi kungokwakhe kube indaba azixoxayo yena ngesikhathi isakhiwo sithatha esinye isinqumo.

“Bafuna ukubona esikwenzayo uma simangala,” kusho yena.

“Yilokho kanye abafunayo,” kusho uMercury. “Futhi simangala. Ngakho umbuzo ukuthi babona ini.”

Nge-feed yekhamera uWolf ayifake kumonitha webhentshi, lezi zimoto ezimbili zazihleli emaphethelweni epaki, umqansa ugqamile ngegolide ngemuva kwezakhiwo zeglasi. Iminyango ivaliwe. Izinjini zicishiwe. Kwathi umnyango womshayeli wemoto ehamba phambili wavuleka, kwaphuma owesifazane, ebukeka ethule engasheshi, wahamba. Hhayi eya egumbini lokwamukela izivakashi, kodwa eya endaweni yokulayisha ezansi kwesakhiwo, lapho kunomnyango ogobile, ukuphela komnyango ovulekile ngokwanele ukuphuma noGuardian.

“Akeze phezulu,” kusho uWolf. “Uvimbela indlela yokwehla.”

Kwalandela omunye umuntu, omude, ephethe ifolda ngaphansi kwekhwapha. Bazimisa endaweni yokulayisha. Bengangeni, bengashayi umnyango. Beme nje. Njengendlela ome ngayo emnyango ongafuni muntu awusebenzise.

UMercury wakubona ngaphambi kukaArin. “Isinyathelo sesikhashana. Sisebenza ngokuba khona. Isigaba Sesine sibavumela ukumisa ukusebenza ngesikhathi uphenyo luqala nje uma kunabasebenzi esizeni. Abadingi inkantolo. Badinga umuntu emnyango.” Wathula kancane. “Atlas, qinisekisa.”

“Kuqinisekisiwe.” UAtlas wayesebheke umthetho. “Isigatshana sokuqala neshumi nanye. Uma kunabasebenzi esizeni, ipulatifomu ayikwazi ukucishwa, ukuxhunywa, ukuhamba noma ukususwa endaweni ekuyo kuze kuphele uphenyo. Umenze indawo yamanje ngokuma emnyango ukuphela. UGuardian akaphumi kulesi sakhiwo. Hhayi ngokomthetho. Hhayi uma esekhona.”

U-Arin wabheka ipuleti elibomvu ngakwesokudla sakhe. Wabheka uGuardian, onamanxeba, ebekezela esibayeni. Wabheka umnyango wendawo yokulayisha angakaze awulungise, abewubheka njengelonda, kanti manje laba ababili abampunga bawuguqula ube yiseli.

Wayefuna ukukhetha usuku azovula ngalo umnyango. Bawuthathile lowo mnyango.

Weqa waya esibayeni wafaka isandla ephuleti elibandayo lenyawo likaGuardian, ngendlela ayehlola ngayo i-weld ayeyithemba kakade.

“Akeze lapha ukuzokhuluma okwamanje,” kusho yena. “Uze ukuqinisekisa ukuthi noma yini engiyinqumayo, angeke ngikwazi ukuyishayela noma kuphi ngisakwenza.”

“Yilokho kanye azolapha,” kusho uMercury. “Isikhathi siyaqhubeka. Kodwa manje asikwazi ukuhambisa lutho.”

“Mama.” Esevele eqonde emnyango. “Vusa uOkonkwo. Isakhiwo, hhayi mina. Mtshele ukuthi i-IAOC ivale indawo yokulayisha futhi uGuardian akavunyelwe ukuphuma kusukela manje. Futhi ukuthi ngiyaphuma ngiyohlangana nomuntu okwenzile lokho ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani kule nkampani anqume ukukwenza esikhundleni sami.”

Ngaphandle, owesifazane wayemi emnyango ogobile wendawo yokulayisha enganyakazi, ebekezela njengemvula.

---

U-Eliza Brand wayengahlosile ukufika ngoLwesibili.

Uhlelo belungolangoLwesine. Isaziso sinikeziwe, amahora angamashumi ayisikhombisa nambili anikeziwe ngobuqotho, isikhathi abelilwele ukuze abantu abangaphakathi babe nesikhathi sokukhetha kahle kunokushesha. Wayekholelwa kuleso sikhathi. Wayechithe iminyaka eyishumi efunda okwenzeka uma umqondisi egxuma leso sikhathi. Ifolda engaphansi kwekhwapha likaAchterberg isathi uLwesine.

Kodwa ipayipi lagqamuka, emini emibili edlule, likhanya kakhulu kune-signature eyavula lonke ifayela, futhi alizange likhanye ngoba kukhona owenza into entsha ngaphakathi. Lakhanya ngoba kukhona *obheke*. Uqhathanise i-telemetry ye-suit namasikhathi okucubungula, wagcina uqhathaniso kumshini oxhunywe kwinethiwekhi isikhathi esanele ukuze abaqaphi bakubone. Ukubheka okuqaphelayo. Uhlobo lokubheka umphakathi, onokuziphatha, akwenza uma ebone okuthile angasakwazi ukukubona futhi ezama, naphezu kokushaya kwenhliziyo yakhe, ukuqinisekisa ngaphambi kokuthi akusho.

UBrand wayazi lolo buko. Wake waba yilo onjiniyela, osekwephuzile, eqiniseka ethule, usuku elilodwa ebambezeleka, abantu bakhokhela lolo suku. Ngakho lapho i-signature igxuma, akakwazanga ukubambelela kuLwesine. Hhayi ngoba abakaphakathi bemphoqile, kodwa ngoba kukhona ongaphakathi onomqondo ofana nowakhe, futhi umqondo oqaphele kanjalo awuhlali uthule unomphela, uma ukhuluma uzokhuluma *ehhovisi lakhe,* bese ukukhetha akusaba sekupheleni komuntu oyedwa.

Wayisheshisile iwashi ukuze ukukhetha kube nobumnene isikhathi eside kancane. Yilona hleko abemi nalo manje, isandla sakhe sibushelelezi emnyango wensimbi ogobile oshisa ilanga lasekuseni: ufike ngaphambi kwesikhathi *ukuze abe nomusa,* kanti akukho ndlela umqondisi angazizwa ngayo njengomusa uma efika ezinsukwini ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi sakhe. Kuzozwakala njengonethi. Kunjalo. Lezi zinto azihlukaniseki, futhi wayeka ukuzikhohlisa eminyakeni edlule, ukuthi ukufisa kungazenza zihluke.

“Uyehla,” kusho uAchterberg kancane, efunda okuthile kucingo lakhe. “I-badge isanda kushintsha. Unjiniyela uqobo. Hhayi ummeli. Hhayi i-VP.”

“Kulungile,” kusho uBrand, eqinisile, wasusa isandla emnyango ukuze into yokuqala ayibona ingabi uyena ewubambe evaliwe. “Kusho ukuthi asidingi ukukwenza lokhu ngobameli.”

Wayefike ukuzomphoqelela umuntu ukuthi akhethe ngokushesha, ngaphambi kokuba omunye ongacabangisisi amkhethele. Wayesezothola ukuthi ukushesha nokuqaphela kungaba into efanayo yini egumbini elilodwa, okuwukuphela kombuzo umsebenzi wakhe wonke owake waba nawo, futhi ongakaze awuphendule kahle.

# Iron Ridge Fault

Ikheji lehla ebumnyameni ngesivinini esingamamitha amane ngomzuzwana, kwathi uMandla Buthelezi wabala izinga ngamunye ngokuzizwa komoya opholile ebusweni bakhe.

Wase eyekile ukufunda amabhodi ezinga eminyakeni eyedlule. Izinombolo azikutsheli lutho olungazizwa umzimba usuwazi. Ku-Level 18, umoya waba mncane futhi umanzi, kufana nokuphefumula usebenzisa indwangu. Ku-Level 26, kwavela iphunga elibabayo lokuqhuma okusha, waze waqina amagxolo ukuze acacise izindlebe zakhe. Ku-Level 33, phansi kwehle entsha, lapho bechase khona i-carbon-leader reef emhlabathini ongakaze ubone ukukhanya kwelanga cishe iminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezintathu, ukushisa kwahlala esifubeni sakhe njengomunye umuntu omkhulu.

Amakhilomitha amathathu nohhafu, cishe. Kude kangangokuthi itshe eliphonswa eshaftini eduze kwakhe lingawa imizuzwana engamashumi amathathu, bese engabe esezwa nomsindo walo kudala lingakafiki phansi.

Amadoda angamashumi amabili nambili ehla naye ekushintsheni kwasekuseni. Iningi lalimi ngezinqe ezithambile njengabantu abakwenzayo lokhu nsuku zonke futhi sebevumelene nakho. Abanye abancane babambe izinsimbi zekhanda. Kwakukhona umfana omusha, umshana kaSizwe—wonke umuntu wayazi ukuthi ungumshana kaSizwe, ngoba yikho kuphela okwenza umfana ongakaze asebenze ezingeni eliphansi kangaka asebenze kule ndawo, hhayi phezulu—futhi umfana wayehlala egwinya, ehlala ebheka phezulu kwikhebula sengathi angakwazi ukubona ikhanda elingamakhilomitha amabili nohhafu phezulu, naphakathi kwamadwala aqinile.

"Ukuqala kwakho e-level thirty-three?" kubuza uMandla ngesiZulu.

Umfana wanqekuzisa ikhanda. Akakwazanga ukukhuluma.

"Idwala likhuluma phansi la," kusho uMandla. "Izindaba zabadala. Uma ulalela, uyaphila. Uma ungakulaleli—" Wazama ukukhombisa. "Uba ingxenye yalo."

Elinye ikhona lomlomo womfana lahamba. Kulungile. Umfana ongakuthola lokho ekugibeleni kwakhe kokuqala e-level thirty-three uzoba kahle.

Kodwa uMandla akazange amtshele ukuthi idwala belikhuluma isonto lonke, futhi akekho phezulu obelilalele.

---

Ezinsukwini ezintathu ezedlule, wayesevele efake isikhalazo ngokwakhe. Ku-Level 30 refuge station, lapho amafomu epulasitiki egoba ngenxa yokufuma, wayeligcwalisa ngamagama amakhulu ahlanzekile njengomuntu owashiya isikole eneminyaka eyishumi nane futhi ongakaze athembe ukubhala kwakhe.

*UKUNYUKA KWEMISEBENZI YOKUZUNGUZA KOMHLABA. IZEHLO EZINTATHU EMAHORENI ANGAMASHUMI AMANE NESISHIYAGALOLUNYE EDUZE KWE-33 DECLINE FACE. UMHLABA UYAKHULUMA EBUSUKU. SICELA UKUHLOLWA KWE-GEOTECH NGAPHAMBI KOKUQHUBEKA.*

Izinsimbi ezintathu ezincane, ukuzamazama okuzwakala ezithendeni zezicathulo zabo kunokuzwa, umhlaba ojulile uzinza ngaphansi kwengcindezi eyaqala kusukela iVredefort impact yaphonsa lawa madwala eceleni kudala. Ingcindezi ifuna indawo yokuphuma. Ireef lapha inothile; i-carbon-leader iphethe igolide elingakaze libonwe eminyakeni eyishumi edlule, futhi yikho kanye okwenza i-decline iphushwe ngokushesha kunezinga lokufakwa kwezinsimbi zokusekela.

Bebegwaza beqhumisa, bekhipha idwala eliphukile, bese belinda. Befaka izinsimbi zedwala, inetha, ne-shotcrete, bevumela umhlaba ovulekile ukuthi uzitholele isimo sakhe esisha ngaphambi kokujula kakhulu. Leyo bekuyindlela yokusebenza. Leyo bekuyindlela yokugcina uphahla lusekhanda labo.

Kodwa izinsimbi zafika zishoda kabili kuleli sonto. Ukulethwa kwe-grout kwahamba kancane. Iqembu elalimele ukufaka ukwesekwa lalishoda ngabantu abathathu, ngoba omunye phezulu wayebathumele kwenye indawo ukuze balandele izinhloso zokukhiqiza, futhi i-decline yaqhubeka ngoba i-decline ihlala iqhubeka, ngoba igolide emhlabathini liyilahleko ku-spreadsheet kuze kube yilapho seliyimali ebhange.

Impendulo efomini likaMandla yabuya ezinsukwini ezimbili kamuva, ifakwe uphawu lukaSizwe, umphathi wokushintsha. Umugqa owodwa.

*KUBHALWE PHANSI. QHUBEKA NGOKOHLELO. I-GEOTECH ISIHLELWE.*

Ihleliwe. UMandla useke wahlala ezimayini isikhathi eside ukwazisa ukuthi *ihlelwe* kusho ukuthini. Kusho ukuthi kuzokwenziwa uma sekulungele. Kusho ukuthi kuzolandela uma izinombolo zokukhiqiza zesigamu seshumi sezinyanga sezivaliwe. Kusho ukuthi, ngolimi oluthile olusetshenziswa kulezi zimayini iminyaka eyikhulu, *akuyona inkinga yakho, k----, buyela emsebenzini*. Kodwa akekho osakhuluma kanjalo manje, hhayi emlonyeni, selokhu izwe lashintsha. Amazwi anyamalala. Izibalo zashiyeka zinjalo. Izimpilo zamanye amadoda zisabiza kakhulu kunezinye, futhi umhlaba ku-Level 33 awunandaba nomuntu noma yiluphi ulimi olusha.

Ngakho uMandla wehlela nezinye izinsizwa ezingamashumi amabili nanye, kanti phezulu kwakhe ukuhlolwa kwe-geotech kusahlezi ohlwini lwezinto ezenza imali ngokushesha.

---

Ekuseni kwahamba ngendlela okuvame ukuhamba ngayo.

Bahamba behlelele emgqeni, amalambu ekhanda eqoba uthuli, umoya uqina njalo lapho behamba. Ikholomu yomoya yayikhalisa phezulu, iphusha umoya obandayo usuka epulazini elingaphezulu. Noma kunjalo, amadoda ayevele esebizwa umjuluko bengakafiki phambi. UMandla wahlolela ukusekelwa njengoba edlula, edonsa isandla esigqokile enetheni lensimbi, eqhuzula izinsimbi ngesando, elalele umsindo ophumayo. Insimbi enhle iyakhala. Engekho kahle ithule. Wathola ezimbili ezithule, wazimaka nge-chalk, wazizwa leyo nto encane ebanda esiswini ayeseyifundile eminyakeni engamashumi amathathu, engayishayi indiva futhi engayicabangi kakhulu.

Phambi, abebesebenza ebusuku babevele begwaze baqhumisa umjikelezo omusha. Inqwaba yodaka ephukile yayithutha umusi. Ngaphesheya kwayo, idwala elivuliwe likhanya elimnyama ngaphansi kwamalambu, linemicu yegolide ye-reef. Lo mfana omncane, umshana kaSizwe, okuthiwa uThando, uMandla wagcina esezwile, wayebukile sengathi ubuka ngaphakathi kwento engcwele.

"Iminyaka eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili ilinde," kusho uMandla kuye. "Sifika siyithathe ngoLwesibili."

"Kuhle," kusho uThando.

"Idwala nje." Kodwa akazange aphikise ngokuphelele.

Basebenza. Umshini we-load-haul-dump wakhwela ukuzokhipha inqwaba, injini yawo yedizili igcwalisa isiqondiso nomsindo owazwayo emhlathini. Amadoda asebenza eduze kwawo, eqhuzula idwala elixegayo ngebha ende, ubudlova obuqeqeshiwe bokwehlisa okuzowela ngaphambi kokuba kwehle ngokwakho. Umjuluko wangena emehlweni kaMandla. Kuth somewhere ipompo laqala ukusebenza. Umsindo wedwala weza kanye, phakathi nosuku, umsindo omude ophansi *crrrk* ophuma ngemuva phambili, umsindo ofana nomnyango ovuleka endlini endala, ngaphandle kokuthi umnyango ungamitha ayikhulu we-quartzite, futhi indlu ingamakhilomitha amabili nohhafu omhlaba oqinile.

Umshayeli we-LHD wacisha injini yakhe. Isikhashana isiqondiso sathula. Amadoda ayishumi nanye amahle emafini othuli, bonke belalele, bonke bezama ukuzibonakalisa sengathi bahlola okunye.

Umsindo awuzange ubuye.

"Izinsimbi," kusho uMandla, ebhekisa kubo bonke. "Namuhla. Anginandaba nokuthi uhlelo luthi yini. Akekho odlula lapha ngingakaboni insimbi phezulu."

Wacindezela iradio yakhe ukubika ku-Level 30 station, kuSizwe, ukuze kube semthethweni, ukuze izwi lakhe libe semarekhodini lapho ifomu lihlulekile khona.

Akazange ayiqede inkulumo yakhe.

---

Iphutha belilinde isikhathi eside kunanoma yini yomuntu.

Kwaze kwagijima edwaleni ngengela okungekho mephu eyake yakubhala phansi. Kwakuyisibazi eselapheka esashiywa umthelela wasendulo, indawo ebuthakathaka eyaqina ngaphansi kwengcindezi iminyaka eyizigidi ezimbili manje esivuleka ngoba abantu sebeyisikelela eduze kwayo base besusa idwala eyayiyibamba. Ingcindezi ifuna indawo yokuphuma. Sekuvele kwakhuluma isonto lonke. Izehlakalo ezintathu eduze kobuso bokwehla. *Umsindo wedwala ebusuku.* Yayinike zonke izexwayiso umhlaba owake wazi ukuzinika.

Lapho ikhulula, yakhulula konke ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Akubanga khona msindo wesexwayiso kulokhu, noma kungenjalo, isexwayiso nomonakalo kwenzeka ngesikhathi esifanayo. Kwaba ukuqhuma okwezwa phansi ngaphambi kokuthi kufike emoyeni, okwashaya amadoda ezinzwaneni zezicathulo zawo nasemathanjeni amakhanda awo ngesikhathi esisodwa. UMandla wabona isipanela siphuma esandleni sakhe simi emoyeni, umzuzwana ongachazeki, phakathi kothuli olukhanyiswa izibani ngaphambi kokuba konke kwehle. Izinsika ezimile zamemeza. Inetha lensimbi laphuma emuva njengolusu. Waphonswa eceleni wangena enqwabeni kadaka, ikhanda lakhe lashayeka edwaleni, izibani zaphuka, kwaba mnyama, kwase kuba kukhanya okukhulu ngenxa yezibani zamadoda awehlayo, kwabe sekuhamba futhi emnyama njengoba uthuli lwehla.

Uthuli lwehla njengolwandle.

Uthuli lwagcwala ezinzwaneni zakhe, lwaba lukhulu, luba nsundu emuva kolimi, ukunambitheka kodwala oluvuliwe. Ingubo yakhe yayimanzi ebusweni bakhe, usawoti nothuli ndawonye. Wayengakwazi ukuphefumula. Wacindezela ubuso bakhe engalweni, kodwa uthuli lwangena noma kunjalo, luncane njengofulawa, lwagcwala ikhala nomphimbo. Kude eduze kwakhe indoda yayimemeza, kude kancane intaba yayisahamba. Umsindo omkhulu wokuguguleka komhume uwele ngaphakathi, amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye edwala ehlela kabusha isisindo sawo. Phansi kwazamazama. Okukhulu kwehla lapho i-LHD yayimi khona nomsindo ongazwakali njengomsindo, kunalokho kwaba njengokushaywa umzimba wakhe wonke.

Kwathi sekudlulile okubi kakhulu, kwaba nokuthula okukhulu kangangokuthi uMandla wacabanga umzuzwana ukuthi usefile.

Wayengakafi. Izindlebe zakhe zazikhala. Umlomo wakhe ugcwele uthuli. Ingalo yakhe yesobunxele ayiphendulanga uma eyiyala ukuthi inyakaze, kwathi lapho ephoqa, izinhlungu zavela zaba mhlophe, wabe eseyeka. Walala ebumnyameni nasothulini, kancane kancane, ngendlela indoda ezibheka ngayo ngemva kokuwa, waqonda ukuthi uyaphila, nomoya usumi, umgudu wokuphefumula ufile, umoya obandayo usunqunywe, nomemezo selimile.

"Sondela," wazama ukumemeza. *Woza ndawonye.* Izwi lakhe laphuma njengokukhwehlela. Wakhafulela uthuli omnyama wazama futhi. "Sondela! Amazwi! Balani!"

Ubumnyama baphendula.

Ngelinye nelinye, bekhwehlela, bekhathazekile, ngezilimi ezintathu, amazwi afika.

Ayisikhombisa.

Kwabayishumi nanye ebusweni, abayisikhombisa baphendula. Amadoda ayisikhombisa endaweni ephephile ngemuva kodonga lwedwala oluwe, okuyilapho, ihora eledlule, bekuyindlela abangenile ngayo. UMandla wathola isibani sakhe sesibili ngokuthinta, wasivula, ukukhanya okuncane kwekhemikhali kwambonisa umumo omusha womhlaba: igumbi eliphansi elinamajika lapho bekusebenzwa khona, uphahla luseduze kakhulu kwezinye izindawo, indlela yokuphuma igcwele udaka kusukela phansi kuya emuva, akukho ngisho igebe ingane yekati engadlula kulo.

UThando wayephila. Umfana wayecindezeleke ekhoneni lodonga, amehlo akhe makhulu ebusweni obumpunga nothuli, ebambe idolo lakhe esifubeni. Wayenza umsindo omncane, ephindaphinda, hhayi amazwi.

"Uyaphila," uMandla wamtjela. "Uyangizwa? Uwalalele udwala. Manje phefumula kancane. Kancane. Uma usebenzisa umoya njengomuntu ocebile, uzofa uhlupheka lapha phansi."

Wayengakazi kahle ukuthi lokho kuyiqiniso kangakanani.

Wayazi izibalo. Amadoda ayisikhombisa. Umoya ongcolile owanele igumbi elilodwa lokuphepha, akukho ukuphephisa, uthuli lusaqala ukuhlala. Udonga lwedwala phakathi kwabo nendlela yokwehla eholela esiteshini esingamakhilomitha amathathu ukusuka emgodini omkhulu onobude obungamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye.

UThando wakhwehlela, umsindo owomile, wakhafulela uthuli ebumnyameni. Kude egumbini indoda yanyakaza izicathulo zayo edakeni, izilungiselela ukulinda.

---

Phezulu emhlabeni bazi ngaphambi kokuba uthuli luhlale amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye ngaphansi.

Egumbini lokulawula emayini, isikhombimzila esilinganisa ukuzamazama komhlaba senyuka, umugqa waphuma endaweni yawo njengentambo eshaywe. Umphathi obephethe, owesifazane ogama lakhe linguRefilwe osebenze ekulawuleni indawo engaphezulu iminyaka eyishumi nanye futhi oseke wakuzwa lokhu ngaphambili, wayesevele esefonini yezimo eziphuthumayo ngaphambi kokuba i-alamu iqede umjikelezo wayo wokuqala. Izinombolo zazixoxa indaba ngokushesha kunanoma yiliphi izwi. Level 33 decline. Ubukhulu obungasaseyona nje ukuzamazama. Ingcindezi yomoya wehle ezingeni elingezansi. Ukuxhumana kuphelile ngaphansi kweLevel 30.

Wathola amadoda aseLevel 30 station ocingweni. Amazwi awo afika emincane, esaba: bayakuzwa, indawo yokwehla igcwele uthuli, abakwazi ukubona, umuva uyakhala. Khipha abantu, khipha abantu.

Wabakhupha. Ikheji yakhuphula abasebenzi beLevel 30 neLevel 26 phezulu ngenqubo engaphazamisekile, wonke umuntu kamuva wavuma ukuthi kuhambile kahle ngangokunokwenzeka. Emini ikhanda lemigodi selizungezwe izimoto. Ntambama amaqembu e-proto—amaqembu ezimo eziphuthumayo emayini, amadoda aqeqeshwe ngalokhu futhi athandaza ukuthi angakusebenzisa—asevele egqoke izimpahla ehlela phansi, nenqubo yezimo eziphuthumayo yenkampani isisebenza kahle.

Sekuhlwa, izimoto ezinama-plate afanele zaqala ukufika.

Zafika ngokulandelana okukhethekile, futhi umuntu osemdala ebuka egumbini lokushintsha wayengakwazi ukufunda isimo sonke ngokubuka le nqubo. Kuqala umphathi wemayini, kulandele isikhulu sezokuphepha sesifunda, bese kulandela amadoda anezicathulo ezinhle angahlali phansi futhi angaze aye khona. Umqondisi wokusebenza waseJohannesburg, iphini likamongameli okuthile, indoda evela eMnyangweni Wezokumbiwa Kwamaminerali ephethe i-lanyard, i-tablet nobuso obungenalutho bomuntu obala izindleko ngesikhathi sangempela. Imoto kahulumeni. Bese, ngokungagwemeki, ifika imoto yokuqala yezindaba, imile emnyango lapho abezokuphepha bengayivumeli ukuthi ingene, isisakaza umqhele wayo phezulu emoyeni waseHighveld.

USizwe, umphathi wesigaba obhale *NOTED. CONTINUE PER PLAN.* ngamagama akhe ahlanzekile ezinsukwini ezimbili ezedlule, wayemi onqenqemeni lendawo ekhanyisiwe njengoba kuba mnyama, engakhulumi namuntu. Wayephethe ikhophi lefomu lesigameko likaMandla Buthelezi ephaketheni lakhe lejakhethi. Wayelithathe kufayela eLevel 30 station ngaphambi kokukhuphuka. Wayengakazi ukuthi uzolenzani. Wayazi kuphela ukuthi lefomu likhona, ukuthi ulibhale, ukuthi ubhale *continue per plan*, nokuthi phansi amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye, indoda eyaligcwalisa ingase ifile noma ilinde ebumnyameni ukuze ithole impendulo.

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wayemi ngaphansi kwezibani ezikhanyayo, ephatha ucingo, esebenzisa enye yezandla zakhe ukuvimba indlebe yakhe kwenye ngenxa yomsindo wejenereyitha. "—cha, angikaboni umbiko we-geotech, yilokho kanye engikushoyo, ngifuna uwubeke phambi kwami ngaphambi kokuba noma ubani—" Wama, walalela, izwi lakhe lashintsha indlela yokukhuluma ngomuntu owayekolunye uhlangothi lomugqa. "Yebo. Yebo. Yonke insiza. Okubaluleke kakhulu kithi ukuphepha kwabantu bethu, senza konke okusemandleni ethu." Wacisha ucingo wema isikhashana, kubonakala ukuthi wayecabanga ukuthi yini okumele ibe yiqiniso ephepheni nokumele ibe yiqiniso uma esenkundleni, nokuthi lezo zinto ziyafana noma cha.

---

Izithombe zokushisa zavela cishe ngo-9 ebusuku, kwathi amahora ambalwa inkundla egcwele izibani e-Iron Ridge yaba nento ebingekho selokhu kwaqhamuka isexwayiso: ithemba.

Amaqembu okuqala ayehlise phansi lapho umhlaba uvumela khona, ephakamisa intambo yokulalela ukuzamazama komhlaba nge-fibre-optic, nekhamera ye-borehole, nomshini wokulinganisa ukushisa. Ithimba le-geotech elingaphezulu lachitha usuku ligebha imbobo encane yokufaka amathuluzi liya endaweni yokuwa, lisuka phezulu lapho sekuphelile ukusebenza, imbobo encane kunesibhakela, ifakwe phansi emhlabathini abebethandaza ukuthi uzinzile. I-probe yokushisa yehla ngentambo yayo, isithombe esabuya kwi-laptop egumbini lokulawula senza umxhumanisi wokuhlenga abambe umlomo wakhe ngesandla.

Izidumbu ezishisayo. Iqoqo lazo, zingacaci kahle ngenxa yamadwala nothuli nemingcele yezibuko, kodwa zicacile kunoma ubani owaziyo ukufunda umbala-mbumbulu wokushisa kwabaphilayo emhlabathini obandayo. Akuyona eyodwa. Eziningi. Ziyanyakaza.

"Zingaki?" kubuza umqondisi wezokusebenza.

"Angikwazi ukusho." Umxhumanisi akazange abheke phezulu. "Bahlanu. Bayisithupha. Mhlawumbe abayisikhombisa." Wathula kancane. "Baphila. Njengoba bekade bephila ihora eledlule."

Kwakukhona amadoda kulelo gumbi aseke angcwaba abangane ezimayini, abaningi baphenduka ukuze bazilungise ebusweni.

Kodwa unjiniyela we-geotech owayesezansi egumbini wayengabheki isithombe sokushisa. Wayephethe ipeni esandleni, wayeseqede ukukuchofoza. Phandle, ijenereyitha yayihlokoma, omunye ehleka kakhulu engenandaba. Wayebheka intambo yokulalela ukuzamazama komhlaba, umkhondo okwakumele ube usuzolile njengemayini ethola umumo wayo omusha, kodwa wawungazolile. Wawukhuluma. Izehlakalo ezincane, kwalandela enkulu, kwalandela iqoqo. Iphutha elavuleka alikapheli ukuvuleka. Ukuwa kuvule izikhala ezintsha, lezo zikhala zabelana umthwalo emhlabathini ongakaze uwuthwale, umhlaba uphendula ngendlela kuphela itshe elingaphansi kwengcindezi elingaphendula ngayo.

Wadonsela umqondisi wezokusebenza eceleni, wamkhombisa umkhondo, ubuso bakhe, ekukhanyeni kwe-laptop, benza into eyinkimbinkimbi.

"Baphila," esho.

"Okwamanje." Wayeka. "Kuseseqhubeka. Izehlakalo zonke zivula kakhulu. Abantu kufanele bangene bayogebha. Lokho kuthatha izinsuku. Emhlabathini engingakwazi ukuqinisekisa ukuthi ngeke uphinde uwe." Waphefumula. "Abahlengikazi bengena endaweni esaqhubeka ukuwa. Ngenxa yabantu abangase bangaphili isikhathi eside kangako. Angikwazi ukusho ukuthi bangangena baphephe. Ngikuzamile usuku lonke. Umhlaba awungivumeli."

"Ngizodinga lokho ebhalwe phansi. Indaba yokuqinisekisa." Wakhuluma kancane, engabheki ngqo. "Hhayi manje. Kodwa ngizodinga."

Wezwa kahle ukuthi leso sigwebo sisho ukuthini. "Uzothola noma yini idatha esekelayo."

"Kumele kube nendlela."

"Mhlawumbe ngokusebenzisa izindawo ezindala phezulu. Indlela engaphandle. Ingxenye evaliwe engenhla kwe-reef horizon, eshiywe, mhlawumbe ihamba ngokuhambisana, yehle eduze." Wazamazama ikhanda. "Izindawo ezineminyaka eyikhulu. Azikho izisekelo ezake zahlolwa umuntu ophilayo. Zincane kakhulu ukuthi ithimba lokuhlenga lingadlula ngemishini. Angikwazi ukuthumela abantu endaweni engingakwazi ukuyihlola, endaweni esaqhubeka ukuhamba, ngethemba nje. " Wayeka. Waziphoqa ukuthi aqhubeke, ngoba ungunjiniyela futhi okunye kungaba amanga. "Ayisiyo indlela yokuhlenga. Akuyona eyabantu."

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wabuka isithombe sokushisa isikhathi eside. Izimo ezishisayo bezinganyakazi imizuzu embalwa. Kwathi enye yazo yanyakaza, kwavela ukufudumala okusha, wathuthumela sengathi kuthintwe yena.

---

eGoli, endaweni yokusebenzela enuka i-solder nensimbi epholile, u-Arin Ndlela wayebuka lezo zimo ezishisayo esikrinini esihlukile.

Isikhathi sokuphenya besisebenza amahora angamashumi amathathu nanye, kanti i-Guardian ayikaze inyakaze phakathi kwawo wonke lawo mahora. I-IAOC yayiyivimbe lapho imisiwe khona. Umyalelo kaBrand, olethwe ngumuntu e-dock ngosuku bafika ngalo: akuvunyelwe ukuyivula, akuvunyelwe ukuyihlanganisa, akuvunyelwe ukuyishiye isakhiwo kuze kuphele uphenyo. Ngakho wachitha kusihlwa evule ama-crash logs, ewahlela ngendlela ohlela ngayo ukuvuma icala, enquma ukuthi umuntu kufanele atshelwe kanjani into embi ngawe. Kwabe sekuvela umkhondo wokuzamazama komhlaba awawugcina ekhoneni kwesikrini sakhe sesithathu—umkhuba, ukubhaliselwe kudala, inethiwekhi ye-geology yesifunda ayeyibuka kusukela esemncane ongakwazi ukuyeka ukubuka umhlaba owathatha uyise—wathumela i-spike eyamenza wayeka ukuthayipha phakathi kwamagama.

Wavula isiteshi sezindaba. Wavula idatha yomphakathi yokuzamazama komhlaba. Wavula igama lemayini, igama lehlika esifubeni sakhe njengesisindo aselinde impilo yakhe yonke engazi.

Iron Ridge.

Akazange akhulume isikhathi eside. Emgqonyeni ngemuva kwakhe, i-Guardian yayimile ithule, i-Assist Suit igoqekile efreyimini yayo, i-kill-switch relay iyinkinobho ebomvu embi ayeyibophele ngesandla sakhe.

Lapho i-SAGE ikhuluma, kwakuyiLibrarian. Ithule, inembile, imqaphele njengoba uqaphela umuntu omi emaphethelweni ento ethile.

"Iron Ridge," kusho uLibrarian. "I-decline isha. I-level yamashumi amathathu nantathu. I-reef yi-carbon-leader." Wathula, kwaba nzima. "I-geology ifana nesakhiwo esahluleka lapho uyihlo—"

"Ngiyazi ifana nani," kusho u-Arin.

IKhothi yathula. Ngisho noFool akashongo lutho.

U-Arin wahlala wathule. Isithombe sokushisa sashintsha esikrinini, izimo ezishisayo, ukufudumala kancane komuntu oshintsha ebumnyameni amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu phansi. I-news ticker yadlula ngaphansi kwaso. Umkhondo wokuzamazama komhlaba ekhoneni waphinda wathumela isenzakalo esincane, kwalandela esinye.

"Atlas," ekugcineni wakhuluma. "Khipha wonke amamephu i-AugmenTech engawathola. Imayini. Izindawo ezindala phezulu. I-geology engenhla. Konke okuvamile, konke okungaphansi kwezivumelwano zamalayisensi. Sakhiwo sonke."

"Kungani," kusho u-Atlas. Akubuzanga. U-Atlas emcela ukuthi akusho ngezwi.

U-Arin wabuka izimo ezishisayo ezaziyizindoda, ebumnyameni, endaweni ebilindele impilo yakhe yonke ukuba ibe yiyo.

"Ngoba bazoyeka," esho. "Bheka. Ekuseni sekuyinzame yokubuyisa izidumbu. Kanti labo bantu basaphila."

Esikrinini, isithangami sabezindaba siqoqwa ngaphansi kwezibani ezikhanyayo, indoda egqoke isudi enhle yahamba yaya epodium ubuso bayo buhlelwe izithombe. Ngemuva kwakhe i-headframe yayimi imnyama ebusuku, ngaphansi kwayo amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu omhlaba wasendulo ubambe amadoda ayisikhombisa esandleni sawo esivaliwe.

---

Isimemezelo safika ngo-6:40 ekuseni okulandelayo, ekukhanyeni okumpunga, izithombe zezindaba zisabukhoma nemindeni iqoqene ngemuva kothango emnyango.

Umqondisi wezokusebenza wema epodium wakhuluma, akazange aqambe amanga, impela. Lelo kwakuyilo okuzokhunjulwa umama kaMandla nomama kaThando nabafazi, abafowabo nezingane ezazimi ngemuva kothango, lapho bezobuka futhi bebuye babuke. Ukuthi lo muntu akazange aqambe amanga. Wakhuluma amazwi kancane, enesisindo, enokubonakala kude sengathi udabukile.

Wathi umhlabathi usalokhu uyingozi, ungazinzile. Wathi ukuqhubeka kokuzamazama komhlaba kwenza noma yiluphi uhlobo lokungena ezama ukusiza kube yingozi engamukelekile kwabasebenzi bokuhlenga.
