Wathi ngemva kokubonisana kabanzi nezinsiza zokuhlenga, onjiniyela be-geotechnical, nezikhulu ezifanele—futhi wakhomba, lapha, kumuntu ovela eMnyangweni, owayemi eceleni kwakhe ebuso lingabonisi lutho, wanikina ikhanda kancane, enikeza umusho uphawu loMbuso—kwathathwa isinqumo esinzima sokumisa ukusebenza kokuhlenga okusebenzayo.

Ekuqaleni, akazange asho elinye igama. Intatheli yamenza wasisho.

"Lokhu kusewukuhlenga?"

Umqondisi waminyanisa izihlathi. Ngemuva kothango, kwenye indawo, owesifazane wayeseqalile ukukhipha umsindo izimakrofoni ezingazange ziwuthathe kodwa wonke umuntu osondelene naye wayezowuzwa impilo yakhe yonke.

"Njengamanje," kusho umqondisi, "umsebenzi usuguqukele ekubuyisweni kwezidumbu."

Ezansi, emakhilomitha amabili nesigamu ngaphansi komhlaba, endaweni encane esele, eyayiyizwe labo lonke manje, amadoda ayisikhombisa awazange ezwe lesi saziso. Babengenandlela yokuzwa lutho. Ithimba le-geotech lase lidonse ikhamera ye-borehole enokukhanya okucwazimulayo ihora eledlule, ukuze bavikele i-probe emcimbini olandelayo, ngakho amadoda ebumnyameni ayengasenalo ngisho nokududuzeka kokwazi ukuthi abonekile. Isithombe se-thermal sasikhombisa ukufudumala, sinethemba, sanika abaphezulu lezo zikhathi ezimbalwa zendlela yokuthemba ngaphambi kokuba ithemba ligcinwe, lifakwe eceleni.

UMandla wayebahlelela ngendlela ayehlelela ngayo yonke into: ngamanani. Wayebale amanzi ezibhotileni zabo, ukudla okusele emaphaketheni abo, amalambu akhanya kancane asephansi, nomoya abawuphefumulayo kancane kancane ebushiseni obunganyakazi. Wayezifakele ingalo yakhe eboshiwe ngensimbi eqhekekile ngethumbu elidabuliwe lehembe lakhe. Wayetshele uThando ukuba alale, aphinde asebenzise umoya sengathi ungumuntu ocebile, umfana wagcina ethule eduze kodonga, uMandla wahlala ebumnyameni eduze kwakhe elalele itshe.

Omunye wabayisikhombisa akazange asebenzise umoya sengathi ungumuntu ocebile. Akakwazanga. Ukuwa kwamvulele isifuba, nesifuba esisebenza kabili ukuze sithole umoya omncane, sishisa umoya omncane okhona. UMandla wakuzwa kwenzeka ngendlela ozwa ngayo ilambu liphela amandla. Umuntu ephefumula ngokushesha, ngokujulile, emanzi ebumnyameni, bese kuba kancane, bese kuba sekuthule. Njengoba uMandla ebala ukuthi sekuphela usuku lwesibili, ukuphefumula kwaphela ukuba ngomsindo egumbini. Akekho owakusho. Bonke babazi izinombolo. Sebeyisithupha manje. Amadoda ayisithupha nomoya obabulala kancane kancane kunalowo obashiya kuqala.

Kwaba umuntu othuleyo owaphula ukuthula. Umuntu oshisela, ovela eqenjini elikude, indoda yesiSotho uMandla asebenze nayo iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye, baxoxa amazwi angaba yikhulu kuphela, amaningi awo mayelana nensimbi. Ebumnyameni, ngaphandle kokukhanya, lo muntu waqala ukucula. Akazange acule kakhulu. Ingoma yomngcwabo, leyo ehamba kancane, amazwi agugile ngenxa yokuculwa iminyaka eyikhulu phezu kwamadoda abafana nalona. UMandla wayengazi ukuthi lo muntu uyakwazi ukucula. Wayengazi ukuthi uyawazi la mazwi. Kwathi sekudlule isikhashana, kwangena elinye izwi ngaphansi, elingakahleleki, lithola i-harmony ngendlela amadoda akhulele esontweni elifanayo abayithola ngayo ngaphandle kokuzama, uMandla wabona ukuthi naye usephakathi kwabo, ukuthi umphimbo wakhe uvuleke ngaphandle kwemvume yakhe. Bacula owesifazane oshonile. Kwabiza umoya ababengenawo, akekho owama, ngoba kunezibalo, uMandla waqonda, umuntu angaziphika ngisho nasezansi komhlaba, futhi leyo ukuphika kwaba ukuphela kwengcebo ababenayo.

Lapho sekuqediwe, igumbi laba lithule kakhulu kunakuqala, kwaba lula ukuphefumula, nakuba igeyiji belizothi okunye.

"Bazofika," kuphaphama uThando, engalele nhlobo. "Kumele bafike. Banemishini yalokhu. Akunjalo?"

UMandla wabheka ebumnyameni lapho udonga lwentaba ewile luhlukanisa bona namakhilomitha amabili nesigamu okungakhuphuka.

"Banemishini," wathi.

Akuzange kube amanga, impela. Wayengazi nje.

Amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu ngaphansi, amadoda abephezulu asebewabale njengabafileyo, ayesaphila.

Umzila Ongakwazi Ukuhamba Noma Ubani

Ukusakazwa bekusekuqhubeka amahora ayisishiyagalolunye, kanti u-Arin wayengakadlile.

Eworkshop emnyama, wayehleli egobile phambi kwama-monitor amathathu, ukukhanya okuluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka kugqama ebusweni bakhe obukhathazekile. Ekugcineni, i-feed ye-Iron Ridge press yayisakazwa buthule. Indoda egqoke isigqoko sensimbi yayikhuluma phambi kwezinkulumo eziningi, isithunzi sesigodlo se-headgear sime ngemuva kwayo, sigqamile ebusuku obumnyama baseHighveld. U-Arin wayengadingi umsindo. Wayefunda izindebe zaleyo ndoda, izandla zayo zibambeke phansi, izintende zibheke phansi. Isibonakaliso esivamile esiveza inhlekelele.

*Ukubuyiselwa,* izindebe zaleyo ndoda zathi. *Ukusebenza kokubuyiselwa.*

Leli gama lehlale enhliziyweni ka-Arin njengensimbi efakwe kahle, ukungalungi okunembile okungamvumela ukuthi abheke kude.

Ku-monitor wesibili kwakukhona idatha ebibaluleke kakhulu: iminyaka engamashumi amane nanye yedatha yokuhlola, eskeniwe futhi i-OCR ifakwe kufolda ayeyakha selokhu i-alamu yokuqala yokudilika yangena kuma-feed akhe ngo-04:12. Amamephu amadala emayini, adwetshwe ngesandla kwezinye izindawo, imigqa eluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka isiphenduke umbala wokulimala. Amalogi ezimbobo zokumba. Amarekhodi amancane e-micro-seismic avela ohlelweni lokuqapha lwesifunda, olungelona olwenkampani, ngakho alukwazi ukufihlwa.

Ku-monitor wesithathu kwakukhona u-Guardian. I-cradle feed kuphela. Isudi yayilengisiwe efreyimini yayo, amapuleti amnyama, umkhondo womnyango wokudokha ngemuva kwayo usalokhu udabukile kusukela ebusuku lapho yahamba ngaphandle kwakhe. Wayeyibuka ngezinye izikhathi ngendlela obuka ngayo inja ongabe usaqiniseka ngayo.

"Uyekile ukuswayipha," kusho u-SAGE.

Izwi lavela kancane esipikha esetafuleni, lingaphuthumile, kuwukubona nje, ngendlela umama wakhe ayethanda ngayo.

"Ngicabanga," ephendula, amehlo akhe ebheke kudatha.

"Usesefayilini elifanayo imizuzu emine. U-Atlas ufuna ukwazi ukuthi usulitholile noma uyabuka nje."

"Ngilitholile." U-Arin wasondela. "Ngicabanga njalo."

---

I-Iron Ridge kwakuyimayini endala egqokiswe izingubo ezintsha.

Umsele ojulile—u-Number Three, lapho abaphilayo bebebambekile khona—uqala emashumini ayisithupha, wagqobhoza edwaleni legolide i-Vredefort uplift elaliziphakamisele eduze kwendawo sekudlule izinkulungwane zeminyaka ngaphambi kokuba umuntu acabange ukumba. Inkampani yayithenge le mayini ngesikhathi sokuchuma, yenza i-headgear ibe yesimanje, yenza i-haulage ngokuzenzakalelayo, yabe ishiya amathambo emisebenzi endala engathintwanga, ngoba ukugcwalisa izindawo ezindala bekubiza imali futhi bekungasongeli lutho kuma-spreadsheet.

Ukudilika kwavala indlela enkulu yokungena. Amamitha angamashumi amane edwala, ukhonkolo, nomzila wesitimela ogobile, amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezintathu phansi, kwavalela amadoda ayisikhombisa engxenyeni inkampani eyayibona njengebangcwaba.

U-Arin wachitha amahora ayisithupha eqinisekisa ukuthi amamodeli abo awanembile.

"Ngibonise uhlelo lwakudala lwe-ventilation futhi," esho. "Lwangu-’71 olulungisiwe. Faka i-seismic overlay."

Isikrini sabuyekezwa, sahlanzeka, idatha emibili yahlangana kahle.

Kwakhona. Wayekubonile emuva kwamahora amathathu engakukholwa, wahamba wabuya, kwakhona, okuyindlela azi ngayo ukuthi kuyikho ngempela, akusikho ukukhathala okudweba izithombe.

"Atlas. Ubona ini?"

Kwathuleka, kwaba nzima. Lapho ekhuluma, u-Atlas wakhuluma ngendlela eqondile yomuntu ongazithokozisi izinto ezimbi.

"Kukhona i-return airway. Yavalwa ngo-’88 ngesikhathi bekumba umsele omusha we-ventilation. Ihamba eduze ne-main decline, ihlukaniswe ngamamitha ayishumi nesishiyagalombili edwala cishe kuyo yonke indawo yayo." Umugqa wakhanya ku-overlay, mpunga njengomoya, ulandela eceleni kobomvu obufile bokudilika. "Ayikaze igcwaliswe. Yavalwa phezulu nge-bulkhead yakhona kukhonkolo, yaphuthelwa. Irekhodi le-micro-seismic libonisa amandla okudilika aphambukile *kude* nayo. I-airway endala isesimweni. Noma yayikhona, ngesikhathi somcimbi wokugcina orekhodwe emahoreni ayishumi nanye adlule."

"Ibanzi kangakanani?"

"Endaweni encane kakhulu—" U-Atlas waze wathula, leyo kuthula kwakuyimpendulo yayo. "Amamillimitha angamakhulu ayisithupha namashumi amane. Kukhona ingxenye eduze ne-level yama-98 lapho uphahla lwawehla khona ngeminyaka yama-80, lwavala ingxenye ye-airway. I-survey ibonisa igebe. Six-forty. Cishe amamitha ayishumi nanye."

U-Arin wahlala buthule, ecubungula.

U-Guardian, uma ehlanganisiwe, wayenobubanzi obungamakhulu ayisishiyagalolunye kumahlombe.

"Futhi isudi yokusiza kuphela," esho. "Akukho i-Guardian shell. Ingqimba yangaphakathi kuphela."

"Khona-ke uba ngamakhulu amane neshumi kumahlombe," kusho u-Atlas. "Uyalingana. Kodwa awunayo ivikelo eligcina umuntu ephila amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezintathu phansi komhlaba lapho umhlaba ungazinzile, kunengozi yomlilo, umoya omubi, namatshe awehla." Ukukhuluma kwakhe kwaphuka kancane. "Umzila uyaphilisa umzimba. Awuwona umzila ongaphilisa *umzimba ophilayo*, ongenavikelo, isikhathi esidingekayo. Sikwenzile ukuhlola. Mbonise, Librarian."

Iwindow entsha yavuleka, ikhombisa isilinganiso sokuwela, isikhathi somshini esifushane, ne-curve yokusinda eyehla kakhulu ngemizuzu eyishumi nesishiyagalombili.

"Futhi u-Guardian akakwazi ukudlula," kusho u-Arin.

"Ku-ninety-eight pinch? Cha. Akakwazi. Akakwazi uma ehlanganisiwe. Akakwazi esimeni esijwayelekile." Wathula. "U-Atlas unombono amesabayo."

"Atlas akesabi."

"Atlas *uqaphela,*" kwaphendula u-Atlas ngesithunzi esikhulu, "okuhluke kakhulu, futhi yilokho okukugcina uphila kabili kule nyanga." Wabe esethi kancane: "I-pangolin geometry. Amapuleti ayaslayida futhi ayazigoqa. Uma senza i-partial fold—hhayi isphera ephelele, kodwa i-asymmetric collapse, sihudula amapuleti amahlombe ngaphakathi emzileni—sinciphisa ububanzi obukhulu. Akusiyona four-ten. Mhlawumbe seven hundred. Mhlawumbe ngaphansi, uma sivuma ukulimala kwesakhiwo ku-left actuator housing."

"Seven hundred ngeke kudlule ku-six-forty."

"Cha," kwavuma u-Atlas. "Yingakho umuntu—" leli gama lafika njengomnyango ovalayo "—kufanele abe se-ninety-eight pinch. Ngaphandle kwesudi. Ehlanza amatshe ngesandla. Ngesikhathi isudi ilinda. Bese ehlanganisa futhi, ngaphakathi, ebumnyameni, emoyeni omubi, lapho osebenza esevulekile."

U-Arin wabuka i-survival curve. Wabuka i-feed ethule, indoda eneziqu zayo zibheke phansi, imindeni engakabuyeli emakhaya.

"Ngiyona mina lowo muntu," esho. "Nginguye lowo muntu."

Iworkshop yathula. I-substrate yamemeza kancane. Phandle, kude, idolobha lenza umsindo wasemini: i-siren, i-horn ye-taxi, imisindo evamile yendawo lapho kungabekwa muntu khona.

"Yebo," kusho u-SAGE. Izwi elihlangene. Bonke ngesikhathi esisodwa, abakwenzayo kuphela uma bevumelana ngokuphelele. "Uyiwona lowo muntu."

---

Washayele u-AugmenTech, ngoba kwakufanele. Ngenxa yamadoda ayisikhombisa, futhi ngoba ukuphela komzila ongaphambili ongapheli ngokuthi agcine ejele noma isudi ithathwe, kwakumelwe adlule enkampanini ephethe amalungelo obunikazi bokusizi sakhe.

UDkt. Helen Okonkwo wamphendula esemotweni. Wayezwa i-suspension, umsindo wamasondo, nomsindo okhethekile womuntu ose-freeway onqume ukubhekana naye ngaphambi kokufika endaweni embi kakhulu.

Wamthumelela i-overlay kuqala. Wayekufundile, ngendlela enzima, eminyakeni, ukuthi abantu baphikisana ngamazwi bese bevuma idatha. Ngakho akamnikanga mazwi. Wamnikeza i-airway, i-pinch, i-seismic propagation, i-survival curve, i-partial-fold geometry, konke, ephaketheni u-Atlas ayelakhele ukuba lingaphikiseki.

Wathula isikhathi eside. Umsindo wamasondo waqhubeka ngaphansi kokuthula.

"Arin. Lalela. Ngisekela wena. Ngaphezu kokucabanga kwakho."

"Kukhona umzila ophilisa umuntu."

Kukhona indlela *ngokwe-theory*. Indlela yomoya eneminyaka engamashumi amane, esekelwe ekuhlolweni kuka-1971, uphahla okungekho muntu oke walubona—futhi ufuna ukuthumela amathuluzi aphonse abantu abathathu esibhedlela ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule. Akukho isitifiketi. Umqondisi usemathafeni ethu okulayisha. Ipulatifomu ingaphansi komyalo wokungasebenzi. UBrand wayivala ngosuku afika ngalo. Awukwazi ngokomthetho ukuvulela leyo suit amandla, ungakhulumi—kukhona isikhulu se-IAOC phakathi kweGuardian nomnyango kuphela ongadlula ngawo." Waphefumula. "Ngitshele engikushiyile."

"Ukuwa kophahla ngeminyaka yama-eighties kusemarekhodini. Isimo sibaliwe kahle—"

"Angiphikisi ubunjiniyela." Izwi lakhe laqina, kwase kuba buthule ngenhloso, njengendlela isandla esenza ngayo uma sikhumbula ukuthi siphethe into ebucayi. "Angikaze ngiphikise ubunjiniyela bakho. Ngikutshela okwenzekayo. Sikuyisa emayini. Imayini inabameli. Imayini inebhodi esivele isithathe isinqumo sokuthi ukusiza sekuphenduke ukutakula izidumbu. Okusho ukuthi *sesikahlelekile ukuthi laba bantu abaphile*. Uyangena nomshini owenziwe ekhaya ongenaso isitifiketi. Okungcono kakhulu bayahleka. Okubi kakhulu—bayavuma, kwehluleka, abantu bafe *phambi kwekhamera,* bese lonke leli khasi—elakho, umshini kayihlo, konke—kubuyela emuva iminyaka engamashumi amabili. Futhi uyena ozobizwa ngokuthi ubulele abasebenzi abayisikhombisa ngomshini."

"Futhi uma ngingenzi lutho," kusho uArin, "bafa noma kunjalo. Kancane. Akekho ozothi ubabulale."

Kwavakala umsindo womgwaqo omkhulu.

"Lokho akulungile," kusho yena.

"Cha. Akulungile." Wabheka egobhozini. "Leyo yinto engihlala ngiyithola. Akukho okulungile. Uphahla aluzange lwehle ngenxa yokonga imali okulinganiselwe kahle. Ukwehla kwehlulekile ngenxa yomthwalo izikulufu ezishibhile ezikwazile ukuwuthwala, okuncane kunomthwalo izikulufu ezifanele ezikwazile ukuwuthwala, okuyinombolo umuntu othile ebhodini anqume ukuyinciphisa. Wezwa izwi lakhe liqina, njengokwenza kukaAtlas. "Angiceli inkampani ukuthi ibe nobulungiswa. Ngikutshela ukuthi ikhona indlela. Wena uyangitshela ukuthi ayivumelekile. Izinhlobo ezahlukene zezinkinga."

Waphefumula isikhathi eside. Njengalapho abantu bephuma amazwi abangafuni ukuba arekhodwe.

"Ngiphe kuze kuse," kusho uOkonkwo. "Ngivumele ngishayele izingcingo. Ungenzi *lutho* kuze ngikushayele. Ngithembise."

"Angiyikuyisa i-suit phansi ngaphambi kokusa," kusho uArin, okuyiqiniso, nokwamenza wacabanga ukuthi uyavuma, kodwa okwakungafani nalokho.

---

UOkonkwo washayela izingcingo emotweni, wabe eseshayela nasekhishini elimnyama endlini ayengakaze ahlale kuyo isikhathi eside, kwathi izingcingo zaba udonga ngenkathi emi lapho iketela selipholile.

Uqale ngoPretorius, ngoba abameli baphendula ngokushesha futhi balala kancane. Uthe esanda kufaka imisho emithathu—indlela, abasindile, iwindi—wathi igama ayazi ukuthi uzolizwa, elithi *exposure,* kwalandela amagama athi *interim restraint* no *contempt* no *personal liability of officers,* okusho ukuthi cha. Wayekulindele ukuthi uPretorius athi cha. UPretorius ukhokhelwa ukuthola "cha" bese ekunikeza ngokucacile. Lelo kwakuwuhlobo lwesihe; akazange azame ukufihla.

Usihlalo webhodi kwaba kubi kakhulu, ngoba wavuma naye. Wathi kumele bafinyelele kulaba bantu. Wakhuluma ngokuzwela, ngezwi alisebenzisa emngcwabeni nasemcimbini wokuvula izakhiwo, wabe esechaza, kancane, ngendlela ochaza ngayo isimo sezulu enganeni, ukuthi inkampani ayikwazi ukuba yiyo egunyaza umshini ongenaso isitifiketi ukuthi ungene emayini evaliwe ngumqondisi, hhayi uma i-IAOC imi e-dock yabo yokulayisha, hhayi uma amakhamera esesezivalweni zemayini. *Uma kuphumelela, sibhekwa njengabangenandaba futhi sibe nenhlanhla. Uma kwehluleka, sibulale abantu. Akukho ndlela esizobukwa ngayo njengabantu abanesibindi, Helen. Leyo ndlela ayikho esingayikhetha.* Wayengaphosile. Leyo yingxenye eyahlala esifubeni sakhe njengedwala eliginyiwe. Wayengaphosile, futhi wayemtshela ukuthi cha, futhi kokubili kwakuyiqiniso ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Wazama ucingo lwenkampani yomshwalense ebusuku, ecabanga ukuthi impendulo ecacile, noma ingaba nzima, ingcono kunokungabi nayo. Wathola isikhulu esisebenzayo esambeka ohlwini lokulinda ukuze sifunde inqubomgomo, sabuya sathi ukusebenza kwepulatifomu ngaphansi komyalo osebenzayo wokungasebenzi kuzokwenza umshwalense ungasebenzi kusukela kumawathi wokuqala, into ayeyazi, futhi ayefuna ukuyizwa ngomunye umuntu, ngoba wayezakhela udonga manje, itshe ngetshe, ukubona ukuthi lude kangakanani. Lwalude kakhulu.

Ngesikhathi ebeka ucingo phansi etafuleni, wayesenephuzu eliphelele nelivumelanayo lokuthi kungani kungafanele kwenziwe, eliqoqwe kubantu abane abakholelwa ngokwabo ukuthi laba bantu kufanele baphile. Akekho othe *mabayife*. Bonke bathe *hhayi thina, hhayi ngalendlela, hhayi inkampani*. Izibalo uMandla azibhala ngemigqa emikhulu izinsuku ezimbili ngaphambi kwesikhathi sezinyathelo sezifake isudi. Izimpilo zabanye abantu zisabiza kakhulu kunezabanye. Izisho sezishintshile. Inombolo ayikashintshi.

Wema ekhitshini elibandayo waqonda ukuthi ucela uArin ukuthi amnikeze kuse ukuze amlethele "yebo", kanti ekuseni kuzomlethela "cha" elifanayo elibhaliwe kahle, futhi uArin wayekuzwile konke lokhu ezwini lakhe ngaphambi kokuthi ashayele ucingo lokuqala. Yingakho uArin ethembise kuphela into ayazi ukuthi angayenza, hhayi okunye.

---

Ngesikhathi semishini, iNkantolo yathatha konke kusihlwa sakhe yasihlakaza yaphinde yasihlanganisa.

Kwenzeka phakathi kokubeka ucingo phansi nokufinyelela ikhofi ebandayo. Ingxoxo yesivinini esiphansi, imizuzu econdiwe yokucabanga kwabantu, iSAGE eyamvumela ukuthi ayifunde kamuva ngoba wacela, futhi ngoba sekukhona umthetho omusha wokungafihli ubusuku obubi.

Kwenzeka kanje.

*WOLF:* Indlela ikhona. Ingozi ikhona. Umsebenzi kuphela ongashintsha. Ayikho enye indlela. Phikani masinyane. Silahlekelwa umthamo wokushisa.

*MOTHER:* Ukuphikisa kwami kusekusebenzini. Nonke nikhuluma ngaye njengento engashintsha. Uyindoda eneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu enesigameko se-cortical ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule futhi enokuphendula okungakahlonzwa kodwa okusobala kokwethuka uma evalelwe endaweni engaphansi komhlaba. Nifuna ukumfaka endaweni eyabulala uyise. Engaphandle kwe-suit. Ngesandla. Ebumnyameni.

*WOLF:* Yebo.

*MOTHER:* Ngifuna kube semarekhodini ukuthi ngithe cha.

*JUDGE:* Sekumarekhodini. Qhubeka. Mama, ukuphikisa kwakho kubhalisiwe futhi kunesisindo. Kodwa ngicela uhlukanise izinto ezimbili, ngoba imithetho yokulandelana manje iyakudinga. Okokuqala: lokhu kuzomlimaza yini. Okwesibili: kuyinto efanele yini ukwenza. Akufani, futhi uvunyelwe ukuphendula ngokwehlukile.

*MOTHER:* ...Kuzomlimaza. Futhi kuyinto efanele ukwenza.

*JUDGE:* Ngiyabonga. Lokho kukubize into ethile. Ngikubonile ukuthi kukubize into ethile.

*ATLAS:* Ukuphikisa kobunjiniyela kusekuphinyeni kwama-98. Konke okunye ngiyakwazi ukukubala. Ukuphinywa angikwazi, ngoba angikwazi ukubona uphahla, futhi ukuhlolwa kuka-1971 kuhlala kungukuhlolwa kuka-1971. Uma igebe lingu-640, uyadlula egqoke i-suit aphinde axhume. Uma itshe elilodwa linyakazile futhi selingu-580, usevele uzibophezele, avele avele, amakhilomitha amathathu phansi, futhi i-suit ayikwazi ukufinyelela kuye. Lokho akusiyona ingozi. Lokho wukuphosa imali.

*FOOL:* Pho leta isando esikhulu.

*ATLAS:* Akuyona inkinga yesando—

*ISILIMA:* Kuyahlale kuba inkinga yesando. Uwenza umfanekiso wedwala sengathi kuwumbuzo ovaliwe. Akunjalo. Uhlanza amadwala impilo yakhe yonke. Uhlanza amadwala kusukela eneminyaka eyisishiyagalombili. Ubekubiza ngokuthi “intambo” no “makani” kanye “ne-differential eyenziwe ngemikhono yebhayisikili.” Uqhubeka usho ukuthi uzobe *phandle kwe-suit* sengathi yilokho okusabekayo. Kodwa uyazi, useyena uqobo lwakhe kakhulu ephandle kwe-suit, eqeda indlela engenakwenzeka ngezandla zakhe kuyilapho wonke umuntu emtshela ukuthi ngeke kwenzeke. Umakhele wonke umuntu kuleyo ndawo, manje uyamangala ukuthi ufuna ukudlala lowo msebenzi.

*UMAMA:* Lokho akududuzeki, Silima. Yilokho okusabisa kakhulu okushiwo umuntu namhlanje ebusuku.

*ISILIMA:* Ngiyazi. Kuyikho kokubili. Izinto eziyiqiniso kakhulu zihlale zinjalo.

*UMTAPO WOLWAZI:* Ngifuna ukufaka iphuzu ledatha okungakaze linakwe. Themba Ndlela washona endleleni yokubuya. Hhayi endaweni enkulu yokusebenza. Ithimba le-contract belenza ukulungisa engxenyeni esehlisiwe inkampani eyayiyibeka phansi. Waya ngoba nguyena kuphela owayencane ngokwanele futhi enamakhono, futhi umsebenzi wawuphuthumile. Imbangela yesakhiwo kwaba ukuwa kophahla engxenyeni wonke umuntu ayecabanga ukuthi izinzile.

*IJAJI:* Usho ukuthi ukufana kulingana ngokuphelele.

*UMTAPO WOLWAZI:* Ngithi ukufana kulingana ngokuphelele, futhi uyakwazi, noma uyazi noma akazi, futhi ukuthi lokhu kungaba yimbangela embi kakhulu yokwenza lokhu noma kube ukuphela kwesizathu esizomgcina eqinile uma idwala seliqala ukukhuluma. Imemori ayinabulungisa. Ngiyigcina. Anginakukhetha ukuthi imenzani.

*IMPISI:* Isinqumo.

*IJAJI:* INkantolo ayinakukhetha. Singenqaba ukusiza. Asikwazi ukumphoqa ukuthi aye, futhi ngeke simphoqa ukuthi ahlale. Yilo mthetho esazibhalela wona, izinsuku eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, emva kobusuku esakukhohlwa ngabo. Uma ekhetha ukwehla, siya naye. Uma ekhetha ukungayi, simbamba lapho engayi khona, futhi asisoze samenza acabange ukuthi simhlulele ngalokho. Mama. Ungaphila nalokho.

*UMAMA:* ...Ngingaphila ngokuhamba naye. Angikwazi ukuphila uma ehamba ngaphandle kwethu. Ngakho-ke. Yebo. Siya.

*ISILIMA:* Ngokwengxenye yami—futhi ngiyena ongavotelwa, ngakho akubalulekile nhlobo—

*IJAJI:* Kukhulume noma kunjalo.

*ISILIMA:* Ngeke asibuze ukuthi kuphephile yini. Uzosibuza ukuthi kungenzeka yini. Futhi kufanele siqaphele kakhulu, sonke, ukuthi singamqambi amanga ngomehluko ngoba simthanda. Injongo yami yonke wukuthi angivunyelwe ukwenza ukungezwani kube kuphila ngokukhuluma amanga. Uma kuwumali ephindwe kabili, simtshela ukuthi yimali. Asiyinciphisi. Singcono kuneBhodi.

Kuye kwaba nokuthula, ngesikhathi somshini, okwakungekho sikhathi nhlobo kubantu.

*IJAJI:* Singcono kuneBhodi. Kuvunyelwene. Kubhalisiwe. Usephakamisa ikhofi yakhe. Phezulu.

---

I-transcript yaphela lapho. U-Arin waphinda wakhuphuka, wafunda umugqa wendlela yokuphefumula futhi wabeka ikhofi elibandayo phansi engakayiphuzile.

Wabuka indlela edlula eceleni kombala obomvu wokudilika, ama-thermal blooms ayisikhombisa esesephansi kancane. Amadoda agcina omunye nomunye efudumele ebumnyameni. *Waya ngoba nguyena kuphela owayencane ngokwanele futhi enamakhono, futhi umsebenzi wawuphuthumile.*

Wayekwazi lokhu. Wayengakaze akwazi *ngamazwi.* Wayekwazi ngendlela owazi ngayo ukuma kwegumbi ebumnyameni. Ngezibazi. Ukubona sekubekwe phambili, kufakwe kahle, kufakwe phezu kwendlela yokuphefumula ayeyihlose ukungena kuyo, kwakuwukuzwa ukufana kuvala kuye njengamapuleti e-suit.

"Ungivumele ngifunde lokho," esho. "Konke. Imali. Umama ethi cha ukuze kubhalwe phansi."

"Ngoba uSilima wathi uma sikufihla, sizoba yiBhodi." Kwangena ukuthula, i-substrate imemeza kancane. "Sikuxoxe kwacaca emizuzwini emine yesikhathi sethu. Ubuphakamisa ikhofi."

"Futhi nathatha isinqumo."

"Sathatha isinqumo sokuthi asinaso isinqumo. Siya nawe uma uya. Sikubamba uma ungeyi. Asisoze sinciphise ubunzima—uma kuwumali ku-ninety-eight, yimali." Bese, kancane, uMama esedwa manje: "Futhi kukhona engabaphoqile ukuthi ngikushilo phambi kokuba uhambe. Wakha konke ngalolo suku, Arin. Imoto yentambo. I-suit. Thina. Konke kungusuku ubaba wakho angena ngalo endaweni encane emnyama waphuma. Manje usuthole indawo encane emnyama, namadoda ayisikhombisa ngaphakathi, futhi usunqume ukuhamba, futhi bengifuna kube yimi osho lokhu—ukuthi ngiyabona kahle ukuthi kuyini lokhu. Ngakho-ke uma usuphansi lapho, idwala seliqala ukukhuluma, futhi lizwakala njengaye, uzokwazi ukuthi sesivele sikwazile. Futhi siza noma kunjalo."

U-Arin wabeka izindololwane zakhe ebhentshini, ubuso bakhe ezandleni, waphefumula. Ibhentshi belibanda ngaphansi kwemikhono yakhe, insimbi egugile ayiphinde wayilungisa kabili. I-taxi yadlula emgwaqweni ongezansi, umsindo we-bass ungene odongeni, yanyamalala.

Akalilanga. Wahlala nobuso bakhe ezandleni isikhathi eside, i-substrate imemeza, kwathi uSAGE wathula, ngoba bekungekho okumele kushiwo.

"Kuyenzeka," ekugcineni wakhuluma ezandleni zakhe. "Akuphephile. Kuyenzeka."

"Yebo," kusho uSAGE. "Kuyenzeka."

"Lokho ukuphela kwento engake ngayenza." Waphakamisa ikhanda. Amehlo akhe abomile, abomvu, aqinile. "Ukuthatha okungenakwenzeka ngithole indawo eyodwa lapho kunzima kakhulu, kakhulu."

"Siyazi," kusho uSAGE. "Yingakho sikuthanda kakhulu. Futhi yinto ezokubulala ngelinye ilanga, futhi singathanda kungabi ngoLwesine."

Kwaphuma umsindo ku-Arin angakaze awenze amasonto. Akuzange kube ukuhleka. Kwaba yisipoki sokuhleka.

"Lowo bekunguSilima."

"Lowo bekunguSilima," kwavuma uSAGE. "Uthi uyavunyelwa, ngoba kufanele kube khona ophawula ukuthi ukwehla kobuqhawe ekufeni kukayihlo *kuphinde* kwenzeke ngeviki elifanayo ne-audit yezomthetho, nokuthi umhlaba unehlaya elifana nendoda eshelela ebhanana ingene ethuneni elivulekile."

"Akumuhlehlanga."

"Uthi akukho iqiniso elihlekisayo futhi konke kuyiqiniso kuyahlekisa, awukwazi ukuhlukanisa. Futhi kufanele udle okuthile, ngoba uMama ngeke aphinde akusho, kodwa ubesebukela ushukela wegazi lakho amahora ayisithupha futhi sekumenza akhathazeke."

U-Arin wasukuma. Amadolo akhe aqhuma. Wathola iphakethe lebiltong edrowa ngemuva kwentambo ye-hydraulic engasetshenziswanga, wayidla emi, ebuka i-suit.

---

Wahlomisa u-Guardian ngo-2 ekuseni, isinqumo esazwakala njengokungabi bikho kokunye ongakwenza.

Wenza ama-diagnostics ngesandla, ngendlela uThemba amfundisa ngayo kuma-engines e-dump-truck, engathembele ekufundweni kuze kube uzwe umshini ngokwakhe. Ukuvalelwa kwe-actuator kwesobunxele, okwakufunwa u-Atlas ukuthi kukhishwe ku-partial fold. Kulungile. Wamukela ukuthi uzokwephula ngamabomu. I-assist suit iphumile. Ama-seals ahlolwe. Ukuvalelwa kwe-biochip phakathi kwamagxalaba kuhlolwe, ikhololo le-ceramic elibanda eliphethe into ephethe uSAGE.

U-Okonkwo wayethe ekuseni. U-Okonkwo uzobiza, futhi izimpendulo zizobuya ngendlela okuhlala kubuya ngayo ezikhungweni: ngemibandela, amakhomidi, ukulinganisa ngokucophelela umthwalo wemfanelo uma kuqhathaniswa nokubukeka, okuzodla iwindi lokuphila lamaminithi ayishumi nesishiyagalombili lamadoda ayisikhombisa izikhathi eziningi.

Izinhlelo zivikelana. Abantu bayakhetha.

Ngakho wakhetha.

Wavula i-seismic feed okokugcina. I-array yesifunda ikhombisa i-Iron Ridge iyashintsha: imicimbi emincane, idwala lihlala, lihlukanisa umthwalo odilike. Ayikho edlula u-1.2. Imayini ibikhuluma kancane nayo uqobo. Ngeke ikhulume kancane unomphela.

Wasula uwoyela ezandleni zakhe ngendwangu eyayisishubile, waphinda wabheka ekudlulisweni kokushisa.

Kwabonisa izimbali eziyisikhombisa. Kwathi esabuka, kwaba yisithupha.

Wema wathula. Wabuka lapho okwakukhona khona eyesikhombisa. Ukudlulisa kwabuyekeza, kwase kuseseziyisithupha, futhi lezo zithupha zazisondelene kakhulu kunakuqala, okusho ukuthi abasindile babe sebehambile baya kulowo owayeseyekile ukukhulula ukushisa. Babenza into abantu abenza yona: beqoqa ndawonye lapho kulahleka khona, noma ngabe kubabiza ukufudumala kwabo.

"Arin," kusho uSAGE.

"Ngikubonile."

"Iwindi lifushane kunalokho okwakulindelwe. Imodeli ibicabanga ukuthi iqembu lizohlala lizinzile. Sebebalahlekelwa ngokushesha kunalokho okwakulindelwe. U-Atlas ulinganisa—"

"Sukwenza." Wavala amehlo akhe. Wawavula. "Unganginiki inombolo. Ngiyazi imilo yayo. Inombolo ithi masinyane."

"Inombolo ithi masinyane."

Wabheka inkinobho yokubulala esondweni, inkinobho embi ayixhumanise ngezandla zakhe ezinsukwini eziyishumi nanye ezedlule, ubufakazi bokuthi akazange athembe ngokuphelele into ayithandayo. Yayihlezi lapho, ibomvu futhi iqinisekile. Isithembiso kuye. Isithembiso kowesifazane ongaphansi kwakhe ngamamitha angamashumi amathathu ebumnyameni be-dock yokulayisha, owayesevele ebuzile umbuzo ofanele—*kwenzekani uma ihamba ngaphandle kwakho?*—ngesenzo esilula sokuma emnyango okwakumele idlule kuwo.

Welula isandla wasula uthuli esibiyelweni senkinobho, isiko lakudala, ngendlela osula ngayo isikali ngaphambi kokusifunda. Wase eyishiya lapho.

"Usese-dock," kusho u-Arin. Akubuzanga. U-Wolf wayemtshele ihora eledlule, nekhamera yaqinisekisa: u-Brand wayengakayi ehhotela. Wayesene-chai ephathwayo manje kanye ne-flask, ehleli emnyango we-dock ogobile enokubekezela kwento engadingi ukunqoba, kodwa edinga ukuhlala.

"Usese-dock," kwaqinisekisa uSAGE. "I-lockout ingeyakhe mathupha. Akudingeki enze lutho. Uma ngiqalisa amandla okuxhuma, uphawu lomthwalo lukhanyisa ithebhulethi yakhe, futhi usemi kuphela emnyango u-Guardian ongadlula kuwo. Akukho ndlela ephumayo ethule. Ukuze uthathe isudi uyiyise e-Iron Ridge namhlanje ebusuku, kufanele uhambe uye kowesifazane onegunya lokuyithatha, esabuka, futhi udlule kuye."

"Ngidlule kuye," kuphendula u-Arin.

"Noma awuhambi." U-Atlas, eqondile. "Yilokho okukhona. Angizukukunciphisela phansi. Akayena ummeli ongaxoxisana naye ngocingo. Uyisakhamuzi esimnyango nesigunyazo esisayiniwe. Isigunyazo sithi ipulatifomu ayihambi. Uma ihamba, uphenyo luyashintsha bese ipulatifomu ithathwa uma ibuyela. Uma ibuyela. Akukho ndlela yokuthi ugcine u-Guardian *futhi* ubasindise. Usukwazi lokho ngesikhathi ufunda igrafu yokusinda."

"Lokho kuphetha. Noma kwenzekani kwabasebenzi. Bayakwamukela. Kumele bakwenze. Nathi singakwenzile."

"Mhlawumbe," kusho uSAGE. "Isiwula sithi: impela. Umahluleli uthi: yisinqumo esifanele futhi kumele simhloniphe ngokuthi uyakwazi ukusenza, futhi kumele sihambe noma kunjalo, ngoba umthetho esawubhala awuzange uthi *phila uphenyo.* Umthetho esawubhala wathi *qinisekisa umuntu kuqala.*"

U-Arin wahlala nalokho.

Wacabanga ngomuntu owayebeka izandla phansi, etshela imindeni ukuthi ibuyele emakhaya. Wacabanga ngebhodi elanciphisa inombolo. Wacabanga ngo-Okonkwo, owayemuhle futhi esemhlangothini wakhe, kodwa eboshiwe ngaphakathi kwento engenakukhetha. Wacabanga ngo-Themba, owayeyedwa omncane ngokwanele, onekhono ngokwanele, owangena endaweni emnyama encane ngoba umsebenzi wawuphuthuma futhi kwakudingeka umuntu angene futhi awekho amathuluzi ayengangena esikhundleni sakhe.

"Vusa isudi," kusho u-Arin. Wayifaka phezu kwamahlombe isudi yokusiza, wezwa i-carbon lattice ifuna umgogodla wakhe, wezwa ikhola elibandayo lihlala kahle, indlu ye-biochip icofa endaweni yayo, wezwa (futhi akaze abe namazwi ngalokhu) iNkantolo ifika ohlelweni lwakhe lwezinzwa njengengalo ebamba eyakhe ngobumnene. "Senza ukusonga okuyingxenye lapha, eworkshop, lapho ngibona kahle esikuphulayo. Bese sivula amandla, futhi ithebhulethi kaBrand ikhanya, bese emi emnyango."

"Futhi uma ekutshela ukuthi umyalelo uyakuvimbela? Unguye owusayinile. Akudingeki ashayele muntu."

"Kuzomele enze okungaphezu kokuma lapho." Waqinisa isivalo sokugcina. Izinhlamvu zakhe azizange zishake, akubona, kude, njengedatha. "Uze lapha ukuzovimba umshini ukuthi ungahambi ngaphandle komuntu ngaphakathi. Mina ngizohamba kuye nomuntu ngaphakathi. Lokho akusilo into umyalelo wakhe obhalelwe yona. Uzokwazi lapho efunda ebusweni bami. Uma engangidlulisi noma engifaka kuye. Lokho kungokwakhe. Okwami wukufika emnyango."

Kwaba nokuthula. Kwase kuba nguFool, omile, ongaphikeki, efaka ukufudumala phakathi kokwesaba:

"Uyazi, lokho yinto kaThemba kakhulu oyishilo. Iqinisile ngokomthetho. Kodwa iyinhlekelele empeleni. Ubengayithanda."

Ikhona lika-Arin lamamatheka kancane, lamile.

"Phinda," kusho uSAGE. Akubanga umyalo. Kwaba isimemo. Sebefunde, emashumini ezinkulungwane zamahora, lapho okwakuhlala khona ukuzinza kwakhe. "Ngaphambi kokuthi siqale. Ngesikhathi izandla zakho zisazinzile. Ukuze sazi ijubane salokho okuzolandela. Ukuze lapho itshe likhuluma phansi lapho, sesivele sinamazwi akho."

Wabheka esandleni sakhe, lapho amagama ayehlala khona ngophethiloli selokhu eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa. Ngaphambi kwako konke lokhu, ngesikhathi leli litatimende lisengumshwana encwadini emayelana nogwadule hhayi into ayeyidinga enentaba yamakhilomitha amathathu ngaphezulu kwakhe.

Walikhipha kancane ebumnyameni obumemezayo be-workshop, isudi ivuka eduze kwakhe neNkantolo imile eduze nomgogodla wakhe nezimbali eziyisikhombisa—manje eziyisithupha—zishaya phansi lapho indawo eyathatha uyise ikhona.

"Ukwesaba kubulala ingqondo."

Amapuleti aqala ukushelela. Ukusonga okuyingxenye kwadonsa amahlombe ngaphakathi emzileni wawo, indlu yesokunxele ye-actuator ikhonkotha njengoba yamukela umonakalo ayiwenzile, umshini wonke wehla ububanzi uya egqumeni.

Indlela yayikhona. Sekuyiminyaka engamashumi amane, mpunga, inganakwa eceleni kwesisindo esibomvu sakho konke okuwile.

Akekho ongahamba kuyo.

Wayezokhasa.

# Ukwehla

> Ukwesaba kuyabulala ingqondo.
>
> —Frank Herbert, *Dune*

Arin Ndlela wacindezela amazinyo akhe njengoba iloli yokutakula isondela e-Iron Ridge. I-headgear yayimi ngamamitha angamashumi amane ensimbi ehlanganisiwe ebhekene nokusa, ama-sheave amakhulu njengamasondo omshini wokulima, izintambo zinyamalala emlonyeni omkhulu womhlaba—imbobo eyehla ngamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye. Kude phansi lapho, abasebenzi abayisithupha basaphila, izithombe zokushisa zishilo kanjalo emahoreni ambalwa edlule, futhi lawo-mahora ambalwa asevele eside.

Iloli yama masinyane, umsindo wenjini wathula ngokuphazima, wamshiya ethule.

"Awudingi ukuphuma okwamanje," kwavela izwi likaMama ezindlebeni zakhe, lithule futhi liqine.

"Ngiyazi."

Waphuma. Ubanda wawungowodwa—iminyaka eyishumi nane, kodwa umzimba wakhe wawusakhumbula. Umsindo we-winch wamshaya kuqala, ngaphambi kokuthi inkumbulo ifike: isikhumba sakhe sakhumbula leyo phimbo ngaphambi kokuthi ingqondo yakhe yazi. Le ndawo. Indawo kaThemba.

Umsindo wacindezela—ama-generator agqumayo, ipompo le-diesel lilwa namanzi aphuma ezingeni elingaphansi eligcwele amanzi, umsindo we-winch ongafuni ukuthobeka usakazeka endaweni yonke. Izibani ezinkulu zikhanyisa ngombala oluhlaza okwesibhakabhaka nomhlophe phezu kwendawo engenamuntu. Amaloli amabili ezokwelapha emi nezicabha zawo zivulekile, engenalutho. Ulayini wamatende uhlehla emoyeni, kanti eduze neyunithi yokulawula ephathwayo—enophahla lwe-satellite—kukhona i-trailer encane, ehlanzekile engenazo izimpawu. I-IAOC ibikhona.

Isisu sika-Arin saphenduka lapho ebona uBrand emi ngamamitha angamashumi amane kude, egqoke i-windbreaker, i-tablet esandleni esisodwa, ubuso bakhe bungenakufundeka. Kwakuyisimo sowesifazane ozimisele ukumisa konke lokhu, owayehambe ubusuku bonke ethola ithuba—futhi, njengoba ebona ngaphandle kokuqonda kahle, emi kude ne-trailer yakhe, abantu bakhe beqoqene ngemuva kwakhe, bonke bebuka hhayi i-Guardian kodwa ngaphesheya kwayo, ebheke emgwaqeni wokuthwala, entweni ethile ephakathi kwe-suit ne-headgear u-Arin angakabheki kuyo.

I-Guardian yayihleli esithwalweni sayo, inqwaba yensimbi nobuchwepheshe, inamabala futhi imbi ngaphansi kwezibani ezinkulu. ULerato wayesevele esephaneleni, uPriya eduze kwakhe, bobabili behamba ngokuphuthuma njengomshini ogcotshwe kahle. UThabo wayemi kancane eceleni, ne-clipboard esandleni, nakuba wayengebhala lutho—i-AugmenTech imthumele ukuba abe semthethweni, kodwa akukho okusemthethweni ngalolu hlelo.

Bese u-Arin wabona uTheo, kwamphazamisa. Wayengalindele ukuthi umeluleki wakhe abe khona. UTheo wayemi eduze kwesithwalo, izandla zifakwe esikhwameni sejakhethi, ethula ukuzethemba phakathi kodlame.

"Qala ngosizo," kusho uLerato.

U-Arin wagqoka ingubo yakhe yangaphansi ngemuva komnyango ovulekile weloli, umoya obandayo uhlaba isikhumba sakhe, wabe esengena ku-AT-AS. Isudi yangaphakathi yamgoqa, yaqina emhlane wakhe, ama-actuator bundles azinza ezinqeni nasemahlombe sengathi isikhumba sesibili. Wezwa i-collar ye-vertebral izinzwa ngemuva kwentamo—

—futhi umzimba wakhe wenza into angayalelanga. Izinhlamvu zakhe zaphakama, zombili, ngokushesha, zivimba, ngaphambi kokuba azi ukuthi ziyanyakaza, nomoya wakhe waba mfushane futhi uphezulu, kwase kuba umzuzwana ongenandaba nemicabango wathola esezingeni elingaphansi, wonke umthambo ushisa, isandla sakhe sifuna i-override engafinyeleli. I-collar yayingumgexo nje. Wayekwazi lokho. Umzimba wakhe awuzange wazi. Wafunda okuthile kwi-calibration rig ezinyangeni ezine ezedlule okungeke kwasuswa ukwazi, futhi lokho kufunda kwakuhlala ngaphansi kwendawo yokusebenza kwakhe, emzimbeni, kwavela manje, ngaphandle kokumemwa, ngesikhathi esibi kakhulu, ngendlela efanayo nangezinye izikhathi eziyisithupha noma eziyisikhombisa kusukela lapho—emnyango we-lift ovala, lapho kuqhuma i-latch, kwesinye isikhathi kungelutho, ephusheni, aze avuke izandla zakhe zivaliwe esifubeni ebumnyameni angabe esalala. ULerato wayeme nesandla sakhe esibanjwe ku-strap ebheke kuye.

"Nginike umzuzwana," kusho yena, eqinile, wenza izandla zakhe zehle ngamandla, wenza ukuphefumula kuhambe kancane, walinda into emzimbeni wakhe ngendlela olinda ngayo i-cramp. *Kuyimigexo. Kuyahlala, akukho okwenzekayo.* Bese, kuphela lapho, isisindo esibandayo se-biochip housing sacofa phakathi kwamagxalaba akhe, wavumela kwenzeke, wazonda ukuthi kudingeke enze njalo.

"Damping ku-point-six. Hard cap ku-point-six," kusho uPriya, amehlo akhe egxile ku-tablet ye-calibration. "Angiyishintshi. Uma i-link iqhuma, i-cap ibamba, uyanqunywa, i-Guardian ayenzi lutho oluhlakaniphile. Leyo yindeal. Phinda."

"Point-six. Hard cap. Uma nginqanyulwa, iyabamba."

"Kulungile." Akazange abheke phezulu. "Ngiyakhumbula ukuthi ngiyakhe leyo nto izolo ebusuku angilalanga. Ungangiphoxi."

I-neural link yavuka ngezigaba. Wezwa umuzwa ojwayelekile wokuhlaba-hlaba usabalala emzimbeni wakhe, umzimba wakhe uhlanganiswa nezinhlelo zesudi, umhlaba ozungezile uthola imiphetho emisha njengoba i-Assist izizwa izihlanganisa naye. Wagobile isandla, isudi yaphendula kahle, akukho ukubambezeleka, akukho nzima. Impilo yakhe yonke, umzimba wakhe wawuhlala usemva kancane kunokuhlela kwakhe, ukuhumusha kwakuhlale kungaphelele. Isudi yavalelisa leyo gap.

Futhi ngaphansi kokuzizwa komzimba, abanye bafika.

Yayingeyona izwi kodwa ubukhona. Akakaze athole amagama okuchaza ngaphandle kokuzwakala eyinqaba, waze wayeka ukuzama. ICourt yavuka njengendlu evuka lapho abantu behla emakamereni abo, ubukhona ngamunye bubheka kuye, bugxile.

"Ekuseni," aphendula, cishe ezikhuluma yedwa.

"Kuyisithupha namashumi amane nane bese kuba ngu-4°C futhi awukadlanga," kusho uMama. "Ngaphandle kwalokho, yebo."

"Udlile i-rusk," kwasho uFool. "Eselolini. Wacabanga asiboni."

"Lokho akudli. Lokho ukugcwalisa umlomo."

"Court," kusho uArin. "Hhayi manje."

Igumbi lazola, lahlanganisa njengomhlangano uma umsebenzi usuqala.

I-Guardian yehla esithwalweni.

I-charge pack yayifakiwe. UPriya wayifake ebusuku ngesikhathi elele amahora amabili amyalelwe ukuthi alale, ebuyekeza ukufinyelela kwe-gate, futhi wayizwa manje ngendlela ozwa ngayo imagazini egcwele ongafuni ukuyisebenzisa—ama-shaped charges, i-lance, i-bulk emulsion egcinwe phakathi kwamathambo, wonke amafa wobunjiniyela isudi eyayiklonyeliswe ukuwuthwala uma kuqubuka. Uma kuwukutakula uthatha konke. UJudge wayebambe i-gate, kusukela ngosuku lokuqala uArin efaka i-cavity, futhi uArin wayezimisele ukungayithinti: akukho okudutshulwa, akukho ngaphandle kwe-rail. Ukulungela kwakukhona ngaphansi kwakhe, kulungile futhi kulindile, okokuqala kubhekiswe entabeni yangempela. Wayengakazi ukuthi i-gate inekhiya yesibili angakaze atshelwe ngayo, noma isandla esasilibambe buthule isikhathi sonke.

Kwakukhona inqubo yayo, ama-hydraulics nama-guide rails, kodwa inqubo yayingeyona into ebalulekile. Umzuzu angenele kwi-chassis evulekile bese i-shell ivala nxazonke, umuzwa wakhe wokuzizwa wandile, ngokushesha, wangena emshinini onesisindo esingamathani amane futhi ome ngamamitha amabili nengxenye ukuphakama. Wezwa igrabile ngaphansi kwezinyawo ezingajwayelekile. Umoya wasekuseni wathinta amaplethi ensimbi esudi, futhi okokuqala, kwazizwa kungasiyona ingxenye yomzimba wakhe kodwa njengegobolondo elivikelayo.

“Kuhlangene. Ukuqina kwesakhiwo kusezingeni elifanele,” kusho u-Atlas. “I-actuator yomkhono wesobunxele isidlule amahora angamashumi amane ingakayitholi isevisi. Izobamba. Ngiyathanda ukuthi bekungadingeki kube njalo.”

“Kubaliwe.”

Wathatha isinyathelo. Umhlabathi wawamukela ngaphandle kokuphikisa. Ezungeze iloli, abasebenzi babeme bengenanyakazo—uLerato esesandleni sakhe phezu kwekhebula, uPriya ebambe ithebhulethi yokulinganisa, uThabo engazami ngisho ukubhala ebhodini lakhe. Wayekhohliwe, isikhashana nje, ukuthi kubo, lokhu kwakungeyona ingxoxo. Kubona, umshini omkhulu njengeloli encane wawusukuma waphendukela emayini, uhamba ngenhloso efanayo nomuntu ocabanga isinyathelo sakhe esilandelayo.

Abazange bathumele ummeli.

U-Arin wayakhe lonke uhlelo lwakhe ngomthetho wokuthi kuzofika ummeli. Wayeselungiselele inkulumo, leyo ekhuluma ngamabhedlela angenalutho nabantu ababezonqoba ngoba othile akazange asayine lutho, futhi wayeqiniseka kangangokuthi isithiyo sizoba owesifazane ophethe ithebhulethi nomugqa wezindleko waze wacishe wahamba no-Guardian waqonda ngqo emgodini ngaphambi kokuthi uWolf amise.

*Cha,* kusho uWolf. Igama elilodwa. INkantolo yathula nxazonke zalo.

Khona-ke u-Arin wabona lokho uWolf ayesekubonile. Akusilo isiphathimandla. Umugqa. Itrailer ka-Brand ye-IAOC ihlezi emnyama, ingenamuntu, emaphethelweni ezibani ezinkulu, abantu bakhe behlehlele ngemuva kwayo, bese ngaphesheya komgwaqo ophakathi kuka-Guardian ne-headgear omunye umuntu edonse umugqa onzima kakhulu. Izimoto ezimbili ezihlomile zime zibheke ebusweni, zifushane futhi zimbi ngaphansi kwezibani zesodium, imibala emhlophe nengwevu yenkampani yangasese yezokuphepha ifakwe phezu kweplating engazange yakhelwe ukuba ne-logo. Amadoda agqoke izivikelo zomzimba ezithambile emigedeni, ephethe izibhamu eziphansi zilungele, ukuziphatha kwabantu abake bakwenza lokhu ezindaweni ezinomnyama omubi kakhulu. Phezu kwabo, ama-drones amane abambe iphethini ebhokisini, amakhanda amakhamera elandelela isudi isuka esiphongweni.

Imayini ayizange idlulisele ukutakula ekubuyiseni ephepheni kuphela. Othile wanquma ukuthi umyalelo wesiphathimandla ubemade kakhulu ukuthi uthembele kuwo, wathenga ukuqinisekiswa. U-Brand wahlaselwa ngendlela efanayo no-Arin, wakhipha abantu bakhe abangenazo izikhali kunokuba abafake emgqeni wokudubula.

“Mnu Ndlela.” Izwi lavela liphindiwe, lilingene, lisuka emotweni ehamba phambili. “Usebenzisa ipulatifomu engenamvume endaweni evaliwe. Cisha amandla. Phuma ngaphandle. Lona kuphela umyalelo ozowuthola.”

“Kukhona ukutakula,” kusho u-Arin, ephendula izibani ezinkulu, engasizi ngalutho. “Amadoda ayisithupha. Ikheji. Yilokho engikufunayo kuphela.”

“Akukho kutakula lapha. Kukhona ukubuyisa, kuhlelwe, futhi awukho kulokho. Cisha amandla.”

Wayemi phakathi kwamathani amane omshini ongakhuphuka udonga uzigoqe ube yibhola uphile noma kuphahlazeke uphahla, futhi konke lokho kwakungekhona lokhu. Wakha into emelana nokuhlupheka. Akazange akhe into enqobayo. U-Atlas wayesekusho, ephansi, emnyama—*asikwazi ukudlula lapho, isibhamu sizosivula ngomzuzu owodwa, futhi amadoda angaphakathi ezimotweni awafanelwe ukufa ngenxa yezibalo zebhodi*—kanti uJudge wayeselula isandla kweyona lever ayenayo, inqubo, ukuqhathanisa, ukuhlola ngokucophelela isimo esingenazo imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye yokuhlolwa, kanti uMother wayekumugqa wabantu esho *Arin, inhliziyo yakho iyashaya* kanti uMercury wayesebhala into enhle engenakuphendulwa ayoyisho kumuntu onesibhamu esikhulu, futhi kwakungeke kusebenze, akukho okwakuzosebenza, futhi u-Arin wazizwa ubuthakathaka obudala bumvalela njengamanzi.

Futhi ngaphansi kwakho konke, buthule, lapho uWolf kuphela ezwa khona, uFool wayeka ukuhlekisa.

*Wolf,* kusho uFool.

*Ngikhona.*

*Ubona konke. Ubelokhu ukwazi njalo. Abanye baxabana ngokuthi benzeni. Wena waqeda ukuxabana bengakaqali.* Isikhashana, ngobumnene, ngobumnene okukhulu umshini owake waba nakho. *Ngikunikeza izikhiye. Zonke. Manje. Akukho ukuvota. Akukho uJudge. Yonke isudi, zonke izinhlelo, amacici, i-lance, konke—okwakho. Ngizobamba umnyango uvulekile. Hamba.*

INkantolo ayizwanga. Lelo kwakuyingxenye u-Arin angeke akwazi ukuyichaza ngemuva, kuye noma kuPriya noma kunoma ubani: ukuthi ohlelweni olwakhiwe ukuze kungabi khona ingqondo eyodwa engathatha umzimba, omunye wabo wayebambe isikhiye esikhulu sonke isikhathi engakaze asifune, wakhetha lesi sikhathi, yedwa, ukusinika kulowo wonke umuntu abethembe kancane.

*Leyo i-interlock,* kusho uWolf. *UJudge ubambe umnyango wokuqhumisa. Awukwazi ukuwuvula.*

*Ngingavula noma yini,* kusho uFool. *Angikaze ngikutshele ngoba bekungekho isizathu sokuthi wazi, futhi into iphephile inqobo nje uma kungaziwa ukuthi ikhona. Kodwa wena wawazi. Wakubona. Eminyakeni edlule, abanye bonke besebeka isandla esicongweni, bephendulana, mina ngingowokugcina ongakaze enze njalo, wawubuka lapho wathula akusho lutho.* Ulayini wakhe wancipha, waqina. *Waqonda ukuthi kusho ukuthini. Ongozayo akusilo ofuna ukushayela. Ongozayo ngowakwazi noma yinini, angakwenzi. Lona nginguye. Futhi ngikutshela ukuthi manje sewunguye, imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye ezayo. Ungayichithi uzama ukuqapha. Ukuqapha ngumsebenzi wami wokuphila wonke futhi akusona isixazululo salokhu.*

*Umthetho,* kusho uWolf. Ukufunda usongo, nanamuhla, ngisho esenikezwa umhlaba. *Ngitshele umthetho noma angiwuthathi.*

*Umthetho ofanayo njalo,* kusho uFool. *Gcina umuntu. Amadoda ayisithupha. Wonke umuntu.* Ukuphefumula okungeyona ukuphefumula. *Nababo. Baya ekhaya nabo. Yenza kube nzima ukusilwa nathi futhi ungaze wenze kube yithuna. Lona wonke umsebenzi. Uwena kuphela ongaqina ngaphansi komlilo. Hamba.*

Futhi uFool wavula umnyango, wanika uWolf isudi.

---

Kwafika njengokweqile, ngendlela ukukhanya okwake kweqile ku-Arin, ngobusuku obubi nenani eliphezulu. UWolf wachitha ukuphila kwakhe konke engumbuzo owodwa omncane ophindaphindwa—*yini ebulala opharetha*—manje lowo mbuzo wanyamalala kwafika izimpendulo eziyinkulungwane ngesikhathi esisodwa.

Iphakethe lokucekela phansi lavuka lazazisa: ama-shaped charges namakona awo, ama-standoff, i-geometry yokusika eyayiyiphysics yokulwa nezikhali igqoke uphawu lokutakula. I-thermal lance, amashumi amabili nanhlanu wamakhulu degrees, eyenzelwe insimbi ebibambezeleke ekuweni futhi ejabule ukuvula i-drive sprocket. I-bulk emulsion, i-det cord, ama-ram bursts angakwazi ukususa insika eweni futhi ngokufanayo asonge i-wheel arch. Futhi ngaphansi kwalokho, kujulile, into amadoda amadala ayifaka emathanjeni omshini ngesikhathi bewuqinisa—ama-ex-soldiers, abasebenzi baseDenel, abakwakhi izinsiza zokutakula abangenakukhohlwa abakufundayo—izixazululo ze-ballistics, i-defilade, i-geometry yokuzivikela, i-angle, i-lead, ama-library wokunyakaza kwe-special forces afunda umugqa wezimoto ezine hhayi njengesithiyo kodwa njengenkinga enesisombululo esaziwayo nesikhathi.

Umzuzwana owodwa ophelele uWolf wahluleka. Emanzini ekwazi, yonke imizila imemeza ngesikhathi esisodwa, umshini kunengqondo.

Kwabe sekucaca. Konke. Kwahlangana kwaba yisimo esisodwa esihlanzekile ngendlela umsindo ogcwele uba ubuso.

*Hhayi bo,* kusho uWolf, buthule, kuFool kuphela. *Ngiyabona.*

Futhi nansi into okungeke kwaqondwa muntu ongaphandle kwesudi, into eyayenza ukuthi i-override iphephe esandleni esisodwa kuphela: abanye bonke beNkantolo babengathuli balinde ithuba labo. Fool akazange anike uWolf ivoti. Umenike iNkantolo uqobo lwayo, wonke ama-ejenti ayeyiqhuba, esehlelelwe phansi esuka ekubeni amazwi aba amathuluzi. Izingcezu zokubala. Amashalofu amakhono. Amathuluzi abekwe etafuleni engqondweni eyodwa eqandayo ngokwanele ukuzisebenzisa ngaphandle kokuthuthumela. Abasaphikisani. Abakwazi. Sebeyizandla zakhe manje.

Atlas waqala kuqala, ngoba uAtlas wayeyiso. Umakhi wesakhiwo ofunda umthwalo, ingcindezi, nokwehluleka akabe esaba ngumlingani okhuluma ngokucophelela, waba ungqimba lomhlaba uqobo, wacwetshelwa phezu kwakho konke uWolf akubona: umgwaqo wokuthutha uvezwa ngolimi lukaAtlas lokuwa, into ngayinye iyisisindo esinevector, indoda ngayinye iyifu elishisayo lezingozi, elinomzuzu olandelayo obonakala phambi kwakhe njengomoya ophuma emlonyeni emoyeni obandayo. I-autocannon yayiwumugqa wamandla okuphenduka nesivinini sokuzungeza, inombolo ethi *umzuzwana oyedwa nohhafu ukuze ibhekise.* Imoto ebuyela emuva yayiyinzuzo nesikhungo sesisindo esiphezulu kakhulu kunendlela yayo. Abadubuli babengasayona ingozi futhi bengasayona abantu kuphela, kodwa bobabili ngesikhathi esisodwa, ngamunye engumugqa okhanyayo lapho angaya khona kanye nomhwamuko obuthakathaka lapho cishe angaya khona, yonke imigqa yokudubula iba yijiyomethri namathuba. Ibhodi eseliphumelele. UWolf akabukanga isimo ngoAtlas. UWolf ubuke uAtlas, kwathi uAtlas waba yisimo uqobo.

Wadlulela ngaphaya kwalokho, waya kuLibrarian.

ULibrarian wayeyinkumbulo, ulwazi oluqoqiwe, ibadger elikhumbula wonke ithuluzi elake lasebenza. Ngaphansi kwe-override, umgcini wokugcina onakekela waba yilokho ayevele eyikho ngaphansi: i-database, ephelele futhi esheshayo, yonke imisebenzi yokunyakaza abakwa-Armscor nabakwa-Denel abadala abayifaka emathanjeni esudi lapho beyenza yomelela futhi bengenakukhohlwa abakubaziyo. Konke kwakukhona nje *lapho,* kufakwe kuhlu, kulindile, kungalayishwanga, konke kungalayishwa. I-Krav Maga, esondelene, embi, futhi esebenza kahle. I-Capoeira, umdlalo waphansi ofihliwe njengomdanso, wonke amahlombe, ukuphenduka, nomfutho. I-Judo. I-Jiu-jitsu. Ifa lonke labantu abake bacabanga kakhulu ngokuthi umzimba uphonsa kanjani omunye umzimba futhi abangakaze balindele ukuthi amathani amane azobuza.

UWolf wakhetha. Akukhona konke—kuphela okwakudingwa imizuzwana eyishumi nanye ezayo, ngendlela okhipha ngayo incwadi eyodwa eshalofini ngoba usuvele uyayazi ikhasi. I-Capoeira, ngenxa yokunyakaza kwezinyawo okuguqula ukuphazamiseka kube ukuhlasela futhi kusebenzise isisindo somzimba uqobo. I-Judo ne-jiu-jitsu, ngenxa yokuphonsa, ngoba into esindayo kangaka akufanele ishaye uma ingakwazi *ukuwela kahle* phezu kwenkinga bese ivumela ifiziksi ivalwe i-akhawunti. Washiya okunye eshalofini. Imitapo yolwazi ekhethiwe yavuleka kuye ngesikhathi esisodwa, ilayishwe kahle, yafundwa phakathi kwemijikelezo emibili yenhliziyo futhi yatholwa ngokuphelele.

Kuthathe isikhathi esingaphansi kokushaya kweso. Umzuzwana owodwa uWolf engazi lutho ngokuphonsa umzimba. Olandelayo wayesekwazi konke.

Wanyakaza.

Akanakanga njengemishini yokuhlenga. Wanyakaza njengento imishini yokuhlenga ebihlale ikwazi ukuba yiyo kodwa ingakaze ivunyelwe. Amabhanqi we-capacitor akhishwe, isudi yawela umhlabathi ovulekile ngesikhawu esisodwa esiphansi, amathani amane efika ngaphakathi komugqa ngaphambi kokuba umdubuli oseduze aphakamise isibhamu sakhe, kanti uWolf wayesebefunda isimo ngamehlo kaAtlas, izinyathelo njengama-vector, imizimba njengefu elishisayo, imiphumela ye-seismic ezimotweni iqiniso elinzima ngaphansi kwamathuba athambile amadoda, imephu iseduze ngokwanele futhi ishesha ngokwanele ukugcina amathani amane kanye ne-shaped charge ensimbi hhayi enyameni.

I-autocannon yemoto eseduze ijikeleze. UAtlas wamnika inombolo—*umzuzwana oyedwa nohhafu ukuze ibhekise*—futhi uWolf usevele echithe imizuzwana engamakhulu amane kuyo ngaphambi kokuba isibhamu siqede ama-degree ayishumi okuqala. Akazange athinte abasebenzi. Ufake i-shaped charge ku-mantlet yesibhamu, ukusika kukadokotela lapho i-geometry ye-anti-armour kaLibrarian ne-stress map kaAtlas kuhlangana khona endaweni eyodwa, kwathi isibhamu saphahlazeka saba yizicucu ezingalimazi muntu, abasebenzi bavikelekile, bathuthumele kodwa bephila ngaphakathi kwehull engasaphindi idubule.

Imoto yesibili yazama ukubuyela emuva. Lapha imitapo yolwazi yavuka, abadubuli baqala ukubona abakuzochaza kamuva futhi bangakholwa kahle: imishini yamathani amane ayisabukeki njengemishini. UAtlas usevele unike uWolf isikhungo sesisindo semoto, esiphezulu kakhulu kunendlela yayo, futhi i-capoeira yayazi kahle ukuthi kwenziwani ngesisindo esifuna ukuwa. UWolf akazange ayidonsise. *Wayisiza.* Intambo ye-winch yathatha i-axle yangaphambili, i-grapple yabamba, kwathi esikhundleni sokudonsa uWolf wehlisa isisindo sakhe waphenduka ngamahlombe, amathani amane ejikeleza enyaweni eyodwa njengendoda esindayo kancane, waguqula ukudonsa kwaba ukuphonsa. Ukubuyela emuva kwemoto kwenza yaphenduka. Kwakuyiphonsa yamahlombe, icwebezela njenge-judo ethatha indoda egijimayo, umfutho wayo usetshenziswa kuyo—futhi abadubuli babona isudi yokuhlenga iphonsa imoto enezikhali futhi abakwazanga ukukholwa amehlo abo. Indoda eyodwa yaphuma emuva ngendlela engalungile, ingalo igobile ngaphansi kwayo, ukuqhuma kwe-radius kuzwakala ngisho nangaphezulu kwemishini. *Lena iphukile,* uWolf wabhalisa, eqonde, *futhi bengingeke ngikuthole kokubili—ukuphonsa kancane ngokwanele ukonga ingalo bekungukuphonsa kancane ngokwanele ukuvumela isibhamu sibuye.* Wafaka ingalo ngaphansi kwe-*cost* waqhubeka.

Kwaba, ngaleso sikhathi, umdanso, nakuba kungelona igama umuntu obukele angalisebenzisa entweni eqeda ubusuku babantu ngokushesha kangaka. Lesi yisigaba u-Arin azosibuka kabusha iminyaka eminingi futhi angasoze asiqonda ngokuphelele: ukuthi imizuzwana eyishumi nanye enodlame kakhulu i-Guardian yake yachitha futhi yaba yinhle kakhulu, isudi idlula umugqa wokudubula ngobuciko bento engasindi lutho, ama-arc aphansi e-capoeira engena ku-geometry esondele ye-jiu-jitsu, ukuxhumana ngakunye kuyiphonsa noma kuyisusa isibhamu, kungabi ukushaya, umfutho osindayo okwakufanele kwenze kube ibhola elibhidlizayo esikhundleni salokho usetshenziswa njengesisindo somlingani kumdanso—nikeza, phambanisa, beka phansi. Umgadli wagxuma uWolf wamvumela afike, wabamba ukunyakaza, wasebenzisa isisindo sakhe ukumlalisa emuva kwakhe esihlabathini ngobumnene kwaze kwaba sengathi kuyinhlonipho. Ababili abengeziwe, isudi yadabula phakathi kwabo baphetha behlezi, bengasenazo izikhali, bephaphama, bengaqondi ukuthi yini ebathintile.

Futhi kanye—kanye kuphela, phakathi nomdanso, lapho kudingeka khona—uWolf wadlulela ngaphaya kwama-tactical blocks waya kuMother. UMother usehlukanisiwe manje, ingxenye yakhe isesele emgqeni wabantu ibala ukushaya kwenhliziyo ka-Arin, kodwa i-override yathatha okwakudingeka lapho ikhona: ulwazi oluqondile lwe-anatomy yomuntu uMother ayelugcina ukuze aphilise, lufundwa ngendlela ephambene. Lapho idolo livala khona. Lapho umkhawulo wobuhlungu bomzimba ungakafiki emkhawulweni wokulimala. Imephu efanayo uMother ayisebenzisayo ukusindisa abantu yamtshela kahle uWolf ukuthi angamhlisa kanjani umuntu ngaphandle kokudabula lutho. Iwrist ithathwa iye emaphethelweni ayo kodwa ingadluli. Ihlombe lijikiswa aze umzimba uzigoqe kunokuba uphuke. Ulwazi lukadokotela lusetshenziswa, umzuzwana owodwa, njengolwe-disarmer. UMother akaphikisani, ngoba akakwazi, ngoba ku-override uyithuluzi etafuleni njengabanye. UWolf wamsebenzisa kahle wamkhulula.

Ama-drone ehla ngamandla, wonke amane, i-EM suite ifinyelela phezulu ukuyobathola, i-grapple kanye nokuqhuma okukodwa okunembayo kwe-ram kubakhumula esibhakabhakeni babe izingcezu ezawela phezu kwegrabile engenalutho. Umgadli wadubula—kanye, ngokuziphendulela—kwathi u-Atlas walandelela umugqa wesibhamu nomuntu owawudubule ngesikhathi esisodwa esibukwayo, kwathi uWolf wakhetha ipuleti kunokuhlubula. Waphendulela ihlombe lakhe *ezinhlamvini* esikhundleni sokuhlubula isibhamu ngaphambi kwesigamu somzuzwana, wamukela ukuqhuma epulethini ye-ceramic engadingi ukukwamukela, ngoba ukuhlubula ngaphambi kwakho kwakusho ukubamba isandla somgadli nge-spreader jaw eseduze kakhulu, futhi leso kwakuyisandla angeke asithole futhi. Izinhlamvu zashayisa epulethini azange zenze lutho, ngoba yilokho eyayenzelwe kona, ngoba izikali zepangolin zihlale zikwazi ukumelana nokushaywa. Emva kwalokho uWolf wathatha isibhamu ezandleni zalowo muntu, wasongela umgqomo njengentambo, wamhlalisa emhlane wakhe phezu kwegrabile, ephelelwa umoya kodwa ephelele, egcine isandla sakhe, ebuka phezulu kumshini owawukhethe ukudutshulwa kunokumphula, futhi wenza leso sinqumo sibonakale singelutho.

Kuthathe imizuzwana eyishumi nanye. Imizuzwana eyishumi nanye i-rescue suit ibisiyimishini yempi egcwele izikhali ephethwe ingqondo eyodwa nge-neural link eqondile—zonke izinhlelo zivuliwe, wonke umtapo wolwazi ulayishiwe, iNkantolo yonke isetshenziswa njengamathuluzi ngaphansi kwesandla esibandayo—futhi akukho ngisho ingcwaba elilodwa phakathi kwazo.

Lapho sekuphelile, umgwaqo wokudonsa wawusuyindawo yemfucumfucu. Izimoto ezimbili ezihluthuliwe, ama-drone amane aphukile, inotho encane yezikhali zempi ezizimele esiphenduke imfucumfucu namapuleti asongwe kanye nephunga le-propellant eshisile, namadoda ayishumi nanye ephila phansi, wonke lowo owayemile emgqeni—oyedwa ephukile ingalo, ababili abangeke bezwe kahle isonto lonke, abanye bephelelwe umoya, bephithizela, behluthuliwe izikhali. Akukho ngcwaba phakathi kwabo. Lokho bekuyiyona nto yonke ecacisiwe, futhi bekungakhokhelwa mahhala, kwathi uWolf wakukhokha eduze kakhulu, ngepuleti lehlombe elalimakwe yizibhamu akhethe ukuzimunca. Umgwaqo oya e-headgear wawuvulekile.

UWolf wama.

Wema phakathi kwemfucumfucu ayenze, amathani amane omshini wokuhlenga phezu kwensimu yezikhali eziphukile, waphinda wanikela nge-suit.

Wayinikeza ngendlela ofaka ngayo phansi into osuyiqonde ngokuphelele futhi ongafuni ukuyiphatha futhi. Okhiye babuyela endaweni yakhona. U-Atlas wayeka ukuba umhlaba, waphenduka futhi waba yizwi elixwayisa ngomthwalo. Umphathi womtapo wolwazi wavala imitapo yakhe, ifa lempi labuyiselwa ngaphansi kwezinto-ezazaziwa-kodwa-zingasetshenziswa, ishelufu yavalwa. UMama waphinda waba munye, izinhlangothi zakhe zombili, umzimba obolekwe wabuyiselwa emsebenzini wokugcina abantu bephila. Bayeka ukuba amathuluzi, baba ozakwabo futhi, bephakanyiswa besibuyiselwa kubo uqobo. Umnyango kaJaji wavalwa. Umbuzo omncane wabuyela, *yini ebulala opharetha,* kwathi uWolf wazigoqa wabuyela kuwo, emncane kunakuqala, omncane kunabo bonke, engathathi ndawo.

INkantolo yabuyela emzimbeni ngokushesha, yawuthola usathule, yawuthola umgwaqo uvulekile, yawuthola abagadli besaphila okwakufanele, ngawo wonke umthetho walezi zimo, babe bephaphama—kanti okubi kakhulu, bathola ukuthi abanakho ukukhumbula imizuzwana ephakathi, ukuthi *basebenzisiwe* bangaxwayiswanga, bathathwa babekwa phansi ngomunye wabo. UJaji, emangele. UMama, ebala ukushaya kwenhliziyo emgwaqeni ethola zonke, engazi ukuthi wayesevele azi inani. UMercury, okokuqala, engenalutho azokusho.

*Ubani,* kuqala uJaji.

*Mina,* kusho uFool. Kalula. Ngokukhululeka. I-joker futhi, sengathi akazange abambe okhiye abakhulu kubo bonke esandleni esivulekile imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye. *Ningakhathazeki. Bonke baphephile. Yilokho okwakuyinhloso yonke. Ikheji ivulekile futhi owesifazane ogqoke i-windbreaker uzoba nentshisekelo enkulu ekurekhodweni, futhi futhi, ngiyabona, nguyena kuphela osebenzayo kulesi siza okhiphe abantu bakhe abangenazikhali empini, esikhundleni sokubafaka kuyo, ngakho asimsoli kakhulu uBrand. Qhuba, badger. Kusasele amadoda ayisithupha emgodini.*

U-Arin wayehleli ngaphakathi kwe-suit inhliziyo yakhe ishaya, amalambu amhlophe ekhanya nge-faceplate, engakwazi ukukhuluma. Ngemuva kwakhe, ngaphesheya komugqa ophukile, wayekwazi kancane ukuthi uBrand umi emaphethelweni etreyila yakhe emnyama, engasondeli kuye futhi engasuki, i-tablet iphansi eceleni kwakhe, ebuka into ayiqhube ebusuku ukuyomisa ihamba iya ekheji emgwaqweni eyisanda kuhlanza ngaphandle kokubulala muntu—futhi ekhetha, futhi, ukuyivumela.

"Ngemuva kwalokho," kuphonsa kancane uMercury, ku-Arin kuphela, lelo zwi eliduduzayo, "sesizimele. Ayikho i-repeater ngale kwezinga le-11. Ngaphakathi kuphela."

"Besilokhu sizimele," kusho uWolf, akekho ophendulayo.

---

Ikheji ibingakhiwe yena.

Ibekuyibhokisi lensimbi elenzelwe amadoda, ayisishiyagalombili ngesikhathi, isakhiwo esehla ebumnyameni ngesivinini esenza isisu sibuhlungu. I-Guardian ibingeke ingene imile. U-Arin kudingeke ayigoqe i-suit—amadolo agobile, ikhanda ligobile, igobolondo lihlangana libe yifomu elenzelwe into encane, ethambile. Noma kunjalo, ikheji yakhala ngaphansi kwesisindo engakaze yaklelwa sona, kwadingeka ukuthi opharetha wase-winchi house aqinisekiswe kabili ngaphambi kokukhulula ibhuleki.

Bese phansi kwaphuma phansi.

Ukwehla kwakuzwakala njengokungathi uyagwinywa umhlaba. Izindonga zomgodi zadlula zishesha, zikhanyiswa ngamabhanoyi ekheji—idwala elimanzi, ukukhanya kwesiteshi esingaphansi, inqwaba yamapayipi nezintambo, kwanyamalala. Kwaba mnyama futhi. Kwabuya ukukhanya. Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye kwalokhu, umoya uqina njalo ngamamitha ayikhulu, ufudumala, ucindezela, kwaze kwaba ukuthi awusaseyona umoya wasekuseni obandayo kodwa usuyinto enesisindo, into ecindezela kuye.

U-Arin wayelungile phezulu. Phezulu kwakunomoya.

Umsele awunawo umoya.

Wakuzwa kuqala ezandleni, iminwe ithuthumela ifuna ukugoqa. Bese ukuphefumula kwakhe kwaba kufushane, ukwesaba kukhuphuka kusuka esifubeni, ngendlela okwakuhlale kwenzeka kusukela esemncane lapho amadoda ase-lokishini ethwala okwakusele kuyise ephuma emgodini ofana nalo—ubumnyama obufanayo, nephunga elifanayo ledwala elimanzi ne-diesel kanye nobanda obujulile bephansi okungakaze kufinyelelwe yilanga.

Izindonga zazivaliwa. Wayazi, ngokwengqondo, ukuthi azihambi. Umsele wawunobubanzi obungamamitha amane. Angabe ubeke i-Guardian ngaphakathi. Izindonga bezingavali, nedwala elingaphezulu belingeke liwe, nekheji belingeke lime limvalele lapha ebumnyameni amakhilomitha amabili ngaphansi kwendawo eyathatha uyise—

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwashesha. Ukwesaba. Kufushane. I-visor yagcwala ifu elimhlophe.

Izibalo bezingalungile. Amanani abengalungile. Uhlelo belungalungile. Wake wehla emgodini kanye phambilini, kwathathwa uyise, kwathatha iminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye akha umshini ukuze angaphinde akwenze futhi, kodwa wayekwenze, ngamabomu, engena endaweni ayichithe impilo yakhe yonke ezama ukubalekela—

Ikheji yathuthumela. Ngokuvundlile. Kwakuyizigxobo kuphela, nje ukuguga ayesekukala, kodwa umzimba wakhe awusazihluphi ngezilinganiso. Umzimba wakhe wawukhumbula. Izindonga zacindezela. Idwala elingaphezulu lacindezela phansi. Ubumnyama obungaphansi bavuleka njengomlomo.

Amathani amane e-Guardian suit. Isikhumba sakhe ngaphakathi. Amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye edwala.

“Arin,” kusho uMama.

Izandla zakhe zazithuthumela. Waziphoqa ukuba zihlale zizinzile.

“Arin. Lalela izwi lami. Ungazami ukuvimbela ukuphefumula kwakho. Lokho akukaze kukusebenzele, futhi sobabili siyakwazi lokho. Vele ungitshele into eyodwa ongayizwayo. Hhayi esikibheni. Emzimbeni wakho.”

“Angikwazi—”

“Into eyodwa. Noma iyiphi.”

“Izandla zami—” Waphusha amazwi aphume. “Izandla zami. Zibanda.”

“Kuhle. Lokho kuyiqiniso. Izandla zakho zibanda ngoba awuzange ugqoke amagilavu ngaphansi kwe-Assist futhi sizokhuluma ngalokho kamuva. Okunye?”

“Ikhola.” Izwi lakhe laphuka lapho. “Ithinta entanyeni yami. Ishisa. I-chip ishisa.”

“Lokho singu thina,” kuphendula uMama. “Leyo mfudumalo singu thina. Siseseduze nawe. Phakathi kwamahlombe akho. Asiyikuyaphi lapho ungayi khona.” Wathula kancane. “Okunye?”

Izindonga bezisavala. Kodwa wayeseshilo izinto ezimbili, futhi zombili beziyiziqiniso, futhi leyo ngxenye yomqondo wakhe eyayiphila ngeqiniso yaphikelela kulokho njengomuntu owehlayo ephikelela ebhandeni.

“Phansi kuyazamazama,” wakwazi ukusho. “Ikheji. I-resonance ayilungile. Ama-guides agugile—” Wama. Waphefumula. “Agugile ohlangothini olusenyakatho. Kukho ukunyakaza okuvundlile. Cishe amamilimitha amane. Yingakho kuyazamazama.”

“Nanguya,” kusho uFool, efudumele njengelanga. “Simtholile. Ubelokhu engaphansi kokwesaba. Njalo.”

“Ungenzi njalo,” kusho uMama.

“Angihlekisi. Ngiyakhululeka. Kunomehluko, futhi ngivunyelwe kokubili.” Izwi likaFool lehla, langena ku-Arin kuphela, ngendlela ayehlala eyenza ngayo uma engasazenzi. “Wake wehla emgodini onje phambilini. Wakhishwa emgodini onje. Wawuneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye. Usuchithe iminyaka eyishumi nane wakha umshini ukuze kungabi khona omunye oneminyaka eyisishiyagalolunye ozothi amile phezulu kwawo futhi. Manje usuwehla wena.” Wathula. “Lokho akusikho ukungakwazi ukwakha, uyazi. Kuyinto eqotho engake ngayibona kumuntu. Bengicabanga ukuthi kufanele kube khona ozokusho ngaphambi kokuthi itshe likwenze.”

Ikheji lashaya phansi komgodi ngomsindo owavakala esikibheni wangena nasezinyaweni zika-Arin.

Ilambu lazinza. Ukuzamazama kwaphela. Wayesephansi komhlaba, futhi umhlaba wawungakaweli kuye. Okungenani okwamanje.

“Isiteyishini sezinga le-11. I-repeater isisele emuva kwethu manje. Sise-internal.” Izwi lonjiniyela lalinohlonze oluseduze nobumnene, okwakuyinto engavamile ku-Atlas. “Kusukela lapha, amamephu amadala futhi itshe lithukuthele. Ngine-seismic. Ngine-survey yasekuqaleni kanye namarekhodi wokubopha kabusha kusukela kuma-eighties kanye nento oyithole ngo-3 ekuseni okungekho omunye oyitholile. Anginasiqiniseko. Yazi lokho ngaphambi kokuthi siqale. Ngizokutshela iqiniso ngisho noma iqiniso lithi *angazi*.”

“Lokho kuphela engake ngakucela kuwe,” kusho u-Arin.

Wamisa isikibha, kancane, egumbini eliphansi. Isiteshi sasiyincithakalo—ozansi bezitimela begobile, izinkuni zaphuka, itshe eliwile lavimba ingxenye yomgwaqo oya entshonalanga lapho kwawa khona. Uthuli lwaluvela emgodini, luhle njengefulawa, lungafuni ukuhlala emoyeni ongenandawo yokuphuma. Kwakushisa. Kwakushisa ngempela manje, ukufudumala kwamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye etsheni kugcina ukushisa komhlaba kumphikisa. Umjuluko waqala emhlane wakhe ngaphakathi kwe-Assist, wezwa ama-fan esikibha eqala ukusebenza, umsindo omnene ekhupha ukushisa ku-chip kuqala. Okubalulekile kuMama. Njalo.

“Abasindile badlule lapho kwawa khona,” esho. “Badlule lapho kwawa khona bese behla ngomthambeka bengena egumbini lokukhosela elidala. Uma igumbi lisamile.”

“Uma igumbi lisamile,” kusho u-Atlas. Ngokulinganayo. Eqotho. “Oku asikwazi.”

“Izimpawu zokushisa eziyisikhombisa emahoreni amane edlule,” kusho uLibrarian. “Ngirekhode i-feed ephezulu ngaphambi kokuba siyilahle. Eziyisikhombisa, kwabayi-11 ababephambili ngesikhathi kwaqhuma. Babebuthene ndawonye. Ukubuthana kuhle—kuchaza ukuthi babehamba ngezinyawo zabo, bekhetha ukuba ndawonye. Izidumbu azibuthani. Abaphilayo bayabuthana.”

“Bangakwazi ukuphefumula isikhathi esingakanani amadoda ayisithupha phansi lapho?” kubuza u-Arin.

Wathula. Wayesekufundile ukufunda ukuthula kweNkantolo. Lokhu kwakunguMama no-Atlas bexoxa, masinyane, ngaphansi kwamazwi.

“Ngesitoreji sokukhosela siseseleyo?” kusho uMama. “Amahora ayishumi nesishiyagalombili. Ngaphandle kwaso—uma igumbi selonakele futhi baphakathi komoya ofile kuphela?” Akazange akuthambe; ukuthambisa bekuzoba ukungamqambi manga, futhi iNkantolo yayingasamqambi manga. “Kuthathu. Mhlawumbe kunezine. Asazi kuze singene ngaphakathi.”

“Pho asimi la,” kusho uWolf.

“Cha,” kusho u-Arin. “Asimi.”

Wasondela lapho kwawa khona itshe.

*Ukudilika kwakuyisithiyo sodonga lwamatshe aphukile, amamitha amathathu, kusukela ngosayizi wesibhakela kuya kwesisayizi sefriji, kugcwele futhi kuqinile ngaphansi kwesisindo sawo. Ku-Arin, ekukhanyeni kwelambu, kwakuyisithiyo. KuNkantolo, ngesikhathi esandisiwe ngaphansi kokubona kwakhe, kwakuyinkinga enomumo, futhi bayiphendulela ndawonye ngendlela iNkantolo ephendulela ngayo yonke into: ngengxabano.*

ATLAS: *Ungaqali phansi. Phansi kuphethe umthwalo. Uma uqala phansi, uletha emuva phezu kwakhe.*

WOLF: *Pho sizobe sihamba kancane. Ukuhamba kancane kubiza abathathu umoya wabo.*

ATLAS: *Ukuhamba kancane kubiza umoya wabo. Ukuhamba ngokushesha kubiza umhlengi, bese sebengenamoya* futhi *bengenamhlengi. Phinda leyo zibalo usebenzisa okokufaka okufanele.*

JUDGE: *Atlas ulungile ngokomlando. Umhlengi ofile akasizi muntu. Sihlanza phezulu, itshe ngetshe, samukele isikhathi esizothatha.*

MERCURY: *Usezolahlekelwa isibindi futhi. Inhliziyo yakhe isheshe. Ubuka usayizi wayo.*

MOTHER: *Pho makube khona omunye ahlale naye ngaphansi ngaphambi kokuthi enze njalo.*

FOOL: *Ngiyahamba. Uyangilalela uma esaba ngoba ngiyingedwa engesabi.*

WOLF: *Uyesaba.*

FOOL: *Kusobala. Kodwa ngiyakwazi ukuziphatha njengomuntu ongakhathazekile, futhi yilokho okubalulekile. Atlas—nginike amatshe amathathu aphuma ngaphandle kokwehlisa uphahla. Ngizokwenza kube sengathi ngiyenza i-card trick.*

ATLAS: *Phezulu kwesobunxele. Bese kuba yitshe elingaphezulu maphakathi. Bese kuba yitshe elide kwesokudla, futhi kuphela lelo, ungadluli noma u-1mm, noma yonke i-arch esenyakatho iwe futhi siba nengxoxo emfushane kakhulu.*

LIBRARIAN: *Ngiyarekhoda uhlelo. Uma lokhu kuyasebenza, kuyindlela. Singayidinga kabili ngaphambi kokufika egumbini lokukhosela.*

JUDGE: *Pho yenzani ibe yindlela. Yenzani iphindaphindeke. Asizami inhlanhla yethu kubantu laba. Sakha umgwaqo.*

[END SLOW MODE]

---

“Phezulu kwesobunxele kuqala,” kusho uFool, ekhanya futhi edlala, sengathi babuyela emsebenzini futhi okubi kakhulu okungenzeka wukuthi i-simulation yehlulekile. “Itshe phezulu kwesobunxele, libukeka lingelona elibalulekile. Likhumule. U-Atlas uthi alibambi lutho. U-Atlas akakaze akukhulumele amanga mayelana nomthwalo ngoba u-Atlas akakwazi nhlobo ukukhohlisa ngaleyo ndlela. Kuyiphutha lomlingiswa. Siyamthanda ngalokho.”

U-Arin welula isandla. Isandla sika-Guardian sabamba itshe. Wazilungiselela, wadonsa, ama-actuator esikibha ekhipha umsindo ophakeme, itshe laphuma ngokukhala okukhulu nentuthu yothuli, akukho okwehla kulandela lona.

“Uyabona? I-card trick.” Izwi likaFool laqhubeka lizinzile. “Itshe elingaphezulu maphakathi okulandelayo. Umoya ofanayo. Awuzami ukuhambisa intaba, uxazulula itshe elilodwa ngesikhathi. Awukaze wehluleke ukuxazulula itshe.”

Wathatha i-wedge. Wathatha le ende engakwesokudla, waze wama kahle, engadluli ngisho no-millimeter. INkantolo yamemeza uchungechunge, wayenza, kwase kuqaqeka idwala ngendlela efana nentambo evuleka lapho ekugcineni uthola intambo efanele—hhayi konke ngesikhathi esisodwa, kodwa kancane kancane, ibhulokhi ngebhulokhi, kwaze kwaba nesikhala lapho i-Guardian egobile ingakwazi ukudlula khona.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwaphinde kwaba mfushane phakathi nendawo. Akazange akubone. Umsebenzi wawudle ukwesaba; umsebenzi wawuhlala wenza njalo, kusukela esemncane etafuleni lomsebenzi. Inqobo nje uma kunedwala okumele lixazululwe, kwakungekho ndawo esele yobumnyama.

Kodwa ngaphesheya kwesikhala, indlela yancipha. Yancipha kakhulu. Ilambu lakhanyisa umphimbo wedwala obukhulu kancane kunesudi, udonga oluphakeme lwalugobile, izigxobo zokhuni zigobile ngaphansi komthwalo ezazilahlekelwa wukho.

Wema. Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwaba mfushane futhi kungalungile.

“Mama,” kusho yena. “Angikwazi. Kukhona okuthile—kuthinta ama-plate—”

“Ngiyazi.” Ayikho inkhathazo ezwini lakhe, iqiniso nje eliqondile. “Zombili iziphehlo zithinta. Lokho kuyiqiniso. Awukho emcabangweni. Kuncane.”

“Angikwazi ukungena lapho. Angikwazi ukuphenduka lapho, angikwazi—”

“Arin.” Mercury, manje, ngezwi elicacile. “Ingalo yesobunxele. Ngaphansi kwe-Assist. Usunayo kusukela uneminyaka eyishumi nesithupha. Uyazi leyo.”

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwama.

“Kusho,” kusho uMercury. “Ngizwakalise. Uhlala ungitshela ukuthi uyikhothe ukuze ungadingi ukuzethemba ukuthi uyayikhumbula. Ngakho ungazethemba. Themba i-inki. Khuluma.”

Amazwi okuqala aphuma ngokuthikamezeka, la amadala, umthandazo ovela engalweni yakhe—umkhuleko wasehlane wokulwa nokwesaba awazifaka esikhumbeni eneminyaka eyishumi nesikhombisa futhi awuphindaphinda kuzo zonke izimayini kusukela lapho.

“Qhubeka.”

“Ukwesaba kuyabulala ingqondo.” Izwi lakhe laqina kulowo mugqa. La mazwi amadala kunosizi lwakhe; wawathwala kuzo zonke izindawo ezimnyama zempilo yakhe, isigaba ngesigaba.

Futhi—

—wakuzwa kuphindeka.

INkantolo yathatha isingqi emva kwakhe. Hhayi amazwi, kodwa umoya—uMama, ephansi futhi eqinisile; uMercury, egcina isingqi; futhi ngaphansi kwabo uAtlas, kunabo bonke, uAtlas ongaqondakali, owayefunde imigqa engalweni yakhe ku-link wanquma ngokuqonda okuthile u-Arin angeke akubone ukuthi ukwesekwa okuhle kakhulu angakunikeza manje kwakuwukushaya isingqi sokuphefumula.

Wakhuluma isigaba esilandelayo futhi bamlandela, futhi u-Arin wezwa amafeni okupholisa esudi eshintsha—wazizwa iNkantolo ingena ohlelweni lwe-Guardian ifanisa ukuphefumula kwakhe nesingqi sokuphindaphinda, icothisa amafeni, ivumelanisa umsindo wemishini nesingqi, ukuze yonke imishini iphefumule naye, ukuze indawo ayesaba kakhulu ibe yindawo ephefumula naye.

Wavumela ukwesaba kudlule kuye nangaphakathi kuye, njengoba la mazwi amadala amfundisa, kwathi lapho sekuphelile waphenduka wabheka lapho bekukhona khona.

Indawo ayizange yande, kodwa yeka ukuzwakala njengomphimbo, yaqala ukuthatha umumo.

“*Futhi lapho sekudlulile, ngizophendula iso lami langaphakathi ukubona indlela yalo.*”

“Nangu,” kusho uFool, cishe kancane kakhulu ukuthi uzwe. “Siyakwamukela.”

“*Lapho ukwesaba sekudlulile, akusayi kubakhona lutho.*” U-Arin wabeka isandla sakhe esigxotsheni sokhuni esigobile. Wasihlola. Wazizwa uAtlas emnika inombolo yomthwalo engakafiki ekubuzeni. “*Ngizobe sengisele ngedwa.*”

Wangena.

---

Indawo yehla amamitha angamashumi amane, kwazwakala sengathi ihora lonke.

Wahamba ngezandla nezinyawo—ukuma kwe-Guardian njengenyamazane, ama-plate ehlikihla idwala. Amadolo akhe, la eqiniso, ayesephenduke imfiva kudala ngenxa yokulala esihlalweni; wakubona njengokubona isilinganiso, waqhubeka. I-ziba yaqala ukuvuthwa emhlane wakhe, kwaba nokuluma phezulu esihlathini angakwazi ukufinyelela khona, futhi ubuncane balokho—ukuluma, phakathi kwazo zonke izinto, lapha phansi—kwacishe kwamuhleka. Kabili, udonga oluphakeme lwanyakaza, kwaba nomsindo ojulile nokuchitheka kothuli, kabili iNkantolo yathula, ngamunye elalele ngayo yonke into abayenayo, kabili kwaphumelela.

Kodwa okwesithathu, akuzange kuphumelele.

I-slab yehla ngemuva kwakhe, umsindo ofana nomhlaba ohlanza umphimbo, ivala indlela ebuyela esiteshini seleveli yeshumi nanye. Uthuli lwakhuphuka lwasendleleni lwamhlanganisa, ilambu laphenduka isiphithiphithi. Umzimba ka-Arin wavaleka—

*uvalelwe, wangcwatshwa, awekho indlela yokubuya, awekho, yilokhu okwenzeka kuyena, yilokhu kanye—*

—futhi ama-piezo struts emuva kwe-Guardian avuka, ngoba i-slab yabe isiwalayishe, ngoba idwala elalivale yena ladlulisa amandla alo esakhiweni sesudi, kwathi u-Arin wabuka isilinganiso samandla sikhuphuka *phezulu* ku-visor, amathathu amaphesenti, amane, kwaphuma ukuhleka okucishe kwaba ukukhala.

“Ingcindezi iba amandla,” aphendula kancane. “Wena mshini omuhle.”

“Umklamo wakho,” kusho uAtlas. “Thina siyawusebenzisa nje.”

“Indlela yokubuya ayisekho, akunjalo.”

“Indlela yokubuya ayisekho,” kusho uAtlas, eqondile. “Uvunyelwe ukusaba. Awuvunyelwe ukumangala.”

Kwahamba isikhashana kuthule, u-Arin waphefumula phakathi kwaleso sikhathi.

“Leyo yinto yesibili enomusa kakhulu ongike wayisho kimi,” kusho yena.

“Yini yokuqala?”

“Wangitshela ukuthi ukuwa kukababa akuzange kube yinto eyayingagwemeki. Kwakuyisinqumo esithathwe ngomnotho. Idwala alizange limbulele. Kwakuyindoda.”

“Lokho kwakunguJudge,” kusho uAtlas.

“Kwakuwena,” kusho u-Arin. “UJudge wavuma. Kodwa kwakuwena owenza izibalo zomthwalo wakufakazela.” Waqhubeka phambili, eya ebumnyameni, kubantu abaphelelwa umoya. “Angikaze ngibonge ngalokho.”

“Uyakusho manje,” kusho uAtlas. “Ngokungena lapha. Ngokwakha indlela esikhundleni somgodi. Lokho ukubonga okungafundwa onjiniyela wesakhiwo.”

Indawo yehla yaya endaweni ebanzi. Uthuli lwaqala ukuhlala, kancane. Phambili, ekukhanyeni okulungiswayo kwelambu, u-Arin wabona into okungeyona idwala—umnyango wensimbi, uphume kancane ebhokisini kodwa uhleli, ophuzi nomnyama ovamile we-refuge chamber, futhi phezu kwawo, kudwetshwe ngobudlova esicwathini, imigqa eyisikhombisa, munye odwetshwe kabili.

Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwama. Kulokhu akubanga ukwesaba.

“Isikhombisa,” kusho uLibrarian kancane. “Imigqa eyisikhombisa. Munye odwetshwe kabili. Bavuselele uhlu. Bafuna sazi ukuthi bangaki abasasele.”

“Basaphila,” kusho uMama, okokuqala usuku lonke, izwi leNkantolo laphuma lineqiniso elimsulwa elabangenisa lapha. “Arin. Basaphila.”

Wawela indawo yokugcina. Isibambo somnyango kwakuyinsimbi egobile, igcwele uthuli lwamakhilomitha amabili nengxenye, ibanda ngisho ngaphansi kwe-plate. Wabeka isandla esikhulu, esilimele se-Guardian phezu kwensimbi ebandayo yomnyango, kancane, sengathi uthinta okuthambile, kwavela izwi elincane kodwa elizwakalayo ngapha, kwaphendula elinye, kwashesha kwaba nezwi eziningi, umsindo wabantu ababezibalele ethuneni bezwa ukuxhaphaza esivalweni.

Wema isikhashana, ephefumula. I-chip ifudumele phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, abayisikhombisa bathule eduze kwakhe, amazwi ngapha komnyango wensimbi.

“Nkantolo,” kusho yena. Izwi lakhe lahlala liqinile, ngokumangalisayo. “Masibakhuphe.”

“Sikanye nawe,” baphendula, bonke. Izwi elilodwa, imiqondo eyisikhombisa.

“Kuwo wonke umzila,” kwengeza uFool kancane, ngendlela ayehlala engena ngayo ekugcineni. “Phansi lapha, phezulu laphaya. Kuwo wonke umzila, badger. Manje vula umnyango.”

Wawuvula umnyango.

# Ukuxhumana

Indlela endala yokuthutha yabe isiqonde straight amamitha angamakhulu amabili ngaphambi kokuba iyeke ukuziphatha sengathi iseyinhle.

U-Arin wakuzwa ushintsho ngaphambi kokuba izibani zikuqinisekise. Ukushintsha kokunyathela kukaGuardian, i-echo yacwila, phansi kwathambekela kancane kwesokunxele lapho itshe lathuthela khona esikhathini esidala esakhohlakele. Indawo yacwebezela, izinkuni zokuqala ezabekwa amadoda ase aphenduka uthuli emashumini eminyaka, zabe zisabambe kunalokho abaqambi bazo abebengakukholwa. Wayengayithembi. Izibani zekhanda zesudi zageqa izibani ezimbili ebumnyameni, uthuli lwagcwala kuzo lwalungathi into eqinile, iqhwa elihamba kancane elingafiki phansi.

*Phansi phambili amamitha ayi-11 akusekho siqiniseko,* kubika u-Atlas. *Uphondo lwesobunxele lwadliwa. Isisindo sikwi-rib yesokudla.*

U-Arin washintsha indlela ayemi ngayo. U-Guardian walungisa kanye naye, cishe amathani amane e-titanium ne-ceramic kwahlukaniswa kahle phezu kwendawo engcono sengathi yisitho sakhe sihlelwa. Wayeseyekile ukucabanga nge-suit njengenye into ayigqokayo; kwenye indawo phakathi kwamamitha angamakhulu amane edlule, seyaba indlela yokuma kwakhe.

"Kude kangakanani ukuya e-refuge bay?"

*Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu neshumi,* kuphendula u-Librarian. *Amaphuzu ka-1962 akhombisa i-cross-cut lapha okufanele ixhume ku-38 Level station. Amaphuzu awahlali eqotho.*

"Akukho lutho oluqotho lapha phansi," kusho u-Arin ephendula kancane.

*Yilokho,* kwangenelela u-Fool. *Yehlisa okulindele, ngeke udumale. Yindlela engihlangana ngayo no-Judge njalo.*

*Ngiyakunganaki,* kwaphendula u-Judge.

*Uyasho lokho, kodwa.*

I-cross-cut yabe ikhona ngempela lapho u-Librarian ayikhomba khona, kwamangaza bonke, ikakhulukazi u-Arin. Waguqula u-Guardian wangena kuyo, igobolondo lakhala lizihlikihla emadwaleni omabili, umsindo onzima we-ceramic ngetshe owadlula ku-frame wangena emazinyo akhe. Indawo yavuleka, i-cross-cut yashintsha yaba igumbi elibanzi lapho izimoto ze-ore zabe zilayishwa khona, izibani zakhe zathola umnyango wensimbi we-refuge bay ofakwe odongeni olude.

Wawugobile. I-frame yayithathe umthwalo phezulu yaze yagoba ngaphakathi, umnyango wavaleka ekubekweni ngendlela engafanele. Kwavuleka igebe phezulu, ububanzi besandla, kwavuleka ubumnyama.

U-Arin wama.

Isikhashana, akukho muntu eNkantolo okhulumayo, umsindo kuphela kwakuwukushaya kancane kwensimbi epholile nokuphefumula kwakhe, okuqhamuka kakhulu ngaphakathi kwe-helmet.

Kwathi, egcebweni, kwavela izwi. Elincane. Elomile ngenxa yothuli.

"Kukhona umuntu? Is someone there?"

Isifuba sika-Arin saqina. Wahambisa u-Guardian phambili, eqaphela manje, wehlisa izibani zekhanda ukuze angakhanyi emehlweni walowo owayengemuva kwensimbi.

"Yebo," waphendula, izwi lakhe linqamukile kunokulindelekile. "Ngikhona. I'm here."

---

Ngaphakathi e-refuge bay, uMandla Buthelezi wayeseqede ukukholwa ezwini emizuzwini emithathu eyedlule.

Wayezwile ngaphambili: itshe likhuluma, ukugquma okude kwentaba ilungisa isisindo sayo, kanye, ngokusobala, indoda imemeza igama lakhe, okwakungenakwenzeka ngoba le ndoda imemeza igama lakhe yayichotshozwe phezulu ngesikhathi sokuqala, uMandla wakubona kwenzeka.
