Ngakho lapho ukukhanya kuvela ngaphansi komnyango, okumhlophe, okuqinile, kungacwayizi njengelambu eliphela amandla, akazange ahambe. Wahlala emhlane kaPetros, owayelimele umlenze wayeka nokukhononda ngawo, okwakubi kakhulu kunokukhononda.

Kwathi ukukhanya kwakhuluma isiZulu ngezwi lomfana, uMandla waqala ukukhala kancane, ngoba abafileyo abakhulumi isiZulu kabi ngolimi lwasezindaweni ezisogwini.

"Six," wamemeza egcebweni. "Six. Sasingu-seven." Wezwa ezwakalisa futhi akakwazanga ukuyeka. "One—umfutho wakhe wawubi, ukuwa kwamvula isifuba. Umoya waba mubi waze—wazumeka. Akakwazanga ukuvuka. Six manje. Petros, umlenze wakhe. Thulani—" Wayeka. "Umoya mubi. Besilokhu—" Ukubala kwakunzima. "Kade. Besikade."

Ukukhanya kwashintsha. Umnyango waqala ukuhamba.

Awuzange udonswe. Awuzange uphunyuzwe. Insimbi yagquma yagoba, i-frame egobile yanikela ngomsindo wensimbi eqhuzukayo, kanti egcebeni elivuleka, uMandla wabona into ephethe ukukhanya. Yayigcwalisa i-cross-cut. Yayimnyama njengokushiswa okudala futhi iguqile njengenyamazane, izandla zayo—uma zingezandla—zibambe onqenqemeni lomnyango zawugoba njenge ndoda egoba isivalo sekani.

UPetros, owayecishe naye, wathandaza ngomthandazo angawusebenzisi kusukela esemncane.

"Kulungile," kwasho izwi. Lomuntu. Izwi lendoda, liphuma ngaphakathi emshinini. "Ngiyazi ukuthi kubukeka kanjani. I-rescue suit. Kukhona umuntu lapha ngaphakathi."

"Umuntu," kuphendula uMandla.

"Kakhulu," kwasho elinye izwi, elincane, liphuma kwenye indawo emshinini, kwenza izwi lokuqala lithi, "Hhayi manje," ngezwi elinjengendoda exosha inja embhedeni, leyo ntukuthelo encane yenza uMandla wakukholwa.

---

I-refuge bay yayimitha emine ukujula, igawulwe etsheni eliqinile, yakhelwe ukubamba abasebenzi bephila amahora angamashumi amathathu nesithupha ngemuva komnyango ovaliwe onomoya wawo. Sebelokhu behleli isikhathi eside kunalokho. Ulayini womoya ocindezelweyo wehlulekile ngesikhathi sokuwa kwesibili. Amadoda abephefumula umoya osele egumbini, i-cartridge ye-CO₂ scrubber isuphelile. Umoya unambitheka njengokuphefumula nokwesaba kanye nobumnandi obukhombisa ukuthi i-carbon dioxide seyikhuphuka iye kwinombolo lapho amadoda avala amehlo.

U-Arin wakubuka masinyane, ngendlela abheka ngayo i-circuit board: konke kanye, bese ubheka okwehlulekile ngakunye.

*Atmosphere kuqala,* kusho uMother, izwi lakhe laba lukhuni ngendlela ayisebenzisa uma esebenza, lapho imfudumalo ihlehla inikeze indawo yomsebenzi. *CO₂ amaphesenti amane nohhafu futhi akhuphuka. I-oxygen amaphesenti ayishumi nesishiyagalombili nohhafu. Basenalo isikhathi. Akusiningi. Vula igumbi liye ku-haulage. Umoya wethu ungcono kunalena ebhokisini elifile.*

"Umoya we-haulage ugcwele uthuli," kuphendula u-Arin.

*Uthuli bangasinda kulo. Loku abasoze. Vula.*

Wagoba i-frame kaGuardian wavula umnyango ngokuphelele. Umoya wahamba. Wakuzwa kuma-sensor angaphandle e-suit njengokwehluka okuncane, umoya omubi uphuma, omubi nje ungene. Omunye wamadoda wakhwehlela, akakwazanga ukuyeka.

U-Arin waguqa, wehlisa i-helmet ukuze abuke ebusweni babo. Ubuso abayisithupha ekukhanyeni kwezibani. Bangcolile. Imilebe eqhekekile. Amehlo abese bumnyameni isikhathi eside kangangoba ukukhanya kubalimaza. Indoda enomlenze ogobile ngeplanga nentambo. Omunye wenze umsebenzi omuhle wokuhlinza ngakho abenakho. Umfana omncane, egobile, ephefumula ngendlela u-Arin angayithandi, ngokushesha nangokuncane.

"Thulani," kusho uMandla, elandela ukubuka kuka-Arin. "Isifuba sakhe. Okuthile kwamphahlazela ngesikhathi sokuqala—"

*Tension pneumothorax, kungenzeka,* kusho uMother. *Noma i-flail segment. Atlas, ngicela ukubona odongeni lwesifuba.*

U-Atlas wakhombisa isithombe esiseduze se-suit, i-thermal-and-structure overlay ekhanya embonweni ka-Arin. Isifuba sokunxele somfana sasihamba kabi, singena ngaphakathi lapho kufanele siphume.

*Flail. Amathambo amathathu aphukile,* kuqinisekisa u-Atlas. *Usebenza kabili ukuphefumula. Ngeke aphume lapha uma kuqhubeka kanje.*

"Senzenjani?"

*Qinisa indawo ephukile. Faka iphedi nentambo. Asingodokotela, futhi lena akusibhedlela,* kusho uMother. *Kodwa simtholela amahora. Arin. Uzokwenza ngezandla zakho. EzikaGuardian zinkulu kakhulu.*

Lokho kwamisa yena. Ukuze asebenzise izandla zakhe, bekumele aqhekeze uphawu. Akhiphe ingalo yakhe yangakwesokudla egawuntini lesudi, aphume aye othulini nasemoyeni omubi, avele obala. Umcabango lowo wafika nesisindo sakudala, ukuvaleka, ingxenye yakhe eyayichithe impilo yonke ibala izindlela zokuphuma.

*Ngiyazi,* kwasho uMama kancane, kuye kuphela. *Yenza noma kunjalo. Imizuzu emithathu. Phefumula ngomlomo wakho.*

Wakhulula ingalo yakhe.

Umoya washaya isikhumba sakhe—ushisa, unothuli, awulungile. Isandla sakhe sabukeka simangalisa kuye, simhlophe futhi sincane, sengathi yisilwane ngaphandle kwensimbi eyayijwayele ukusigubuzela. Welula isandla, wathatha iphakethe le-field dressing uMama ayemkhombisele lona eceleni kukaGuardian, futhi ngaphansi kwemiyalelo yakhe—*beka lapha, cha, phansi, phezu kwengxenye enganyakazi, yebo*—wabopha isifuba somfana, iminwe yakhe ingavikelwe isebenza ebumnyameni ngesikhathi amadoda ayisithupha ebuka, nomshini uphefumula eduze kwakhe.

Ukuphefumula kukaThulani kwaba lula. Akukuhle. Kuncane kabi.

"Nangu," kusho uArin. Wabuyisa ingalo yakhe egawuntini, uphawu luyavala, futhi wayengazi ukuthi wayekulangazelele kangakanani kuze kube sekubuyile. "Nangu."

*Inhliziyo yehlile ngethuba elingu-12,* kwasho uMama. *Kuhle. Manje sihamba.*

---

Izokuxhumana phezulu bezithule kusukela kwavulwa indlela eceleni.

UArin akazange akhulume ngalokho. Nenkantolo ayizange. Izwi likaPriya lathamba, laphazamiseka, lanyamalala lapho bedlula endaweni encishisiwe, futhi baqhubeka ngaphandle kwakhe ngoba kwakungekho okunye abangakwenza. Kodwa uGuardian wayephethe i-repeater spool, imitha engamakhulu amabili yefayibha eqoqwe emhlane, kanti uAtlas wayeyikhulula ngemuva kwabo ngesikhathi behla, intambo ebuyela ekukhanyeni.

*Sekuphelile i-spool,* kusho uAtlas. *Ngingaxhuma umugqa omdala we-refuge bay kwi-repeater. I-bay yayinomugqa wokuxhumana oqondile esiteshini se-38 Level. Uma okuthile kusasele emva kokuwa—*

"Yenza."

Kwathatha imizuzu emine. UArin wabamba ikhava yepaneli odongeni lwe-bay ngesikhathi iminwe kaGuardian, eyayinkulu kakhulu ukwenza ukuhlinza kodwa incane ngokwanele lokhu, ijika ithusi elingenalutho kwi-bridge ye-fiber-to-copper ye-repeater. UAtlas wamkhombisa indlela, eqinile, eqinisekile, kwathi uArin wakuthola lokho kuqiniseka kumqinisa.

Kwase kuba khona umsindo, ngaphakathi kwe-helmet yakhe. Umsindo wokuphefumula. Izwi, eliphukile, elizama ukuzakha lisuka ku-static libe into ebantwini.

"—phinda, lapha phezulu, nguPriya, *Arin*, uma ungizwa—"

"Ngiyakuva." Izwi lakhe laphuka. Wayengazi ukuthi uzizwa edwa kangakanani kuze kube sekupheleni kokuhlala yedwa. "Priya. Ngibanalo. Bonke abayisithupha. Bayaphila."

Kwahamba kancane. Kwabe sekuba nomsindo angakwazanga ukuwuchaza, uhlobo lokubanjwa komoya, waqonda ukuthi uPriya uzama ukungakhali esiteshini esivulekile.

"Phinda." Waphefumula. "Phinda futhi. Kancane. Ukuze kungene kwi-log."

"Abasindile abayisithupha e-38 Level refuge bay. Bonke abayisithupha bayaphila. Omunye usenkingeni enkulu. Ulimale esifubeni, usezinzile. Omunye unomlenze ophukile. Abanye badinwe, bagcwele i-CO₂, bayakwazi ukuhamba. Batshele. Batshele phezulu."

Wezwa ekudlulisa, izwi lakhe liphambuka kwi-mic, kwase kuba khona okuthile ayengakuzwanga izinsuku eziyishumi nanye, kusukela ngaphambi kokuba konke lokhu kuqale, isikhathi eside ngaphambi kwalokho.

Wezwa abantu behalalisa.

Kwazwakala ku-fiber, kuncane, kufiphele, umsindo owenziwe yimilomo eyikhulu, amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye phezulu, ekuseni, eduze kwe-headgear—imindeni, abasebenzi, namadoda abebetshelwe ukuthi lokhu bekungukubuyisa hhayi ukusindisa—futhi kwafinyelela kuye ebumnyameni, emoyeni ofile, eduze kwamadoda ayisithupha ayesephuthelwe ithemba lezwi, kwathi uMandla Buthelezi wabuka umshini owawuyisidalwa wathi, "Kuyini lokho? Uyini lowo msindo?"

"Yilapho phezulu," kusho uArin. "Sebazi ukuthi niphila."

Amadoda awazange ahalalise. Ayekhathele kakhulu ukwenza lokho. Kodwa uPetros, owayenomlenze ophukile futhi eseyekile ukukhononda ngawo, wabeka ikhanda lakhe edwaleni wahleka, umsindo owomile, ophukile, futhi ukuhleka kwadlula egumbini kusuka kowesilisa kuye kowesilisa kwaze kwaba bonke abayisithupha bekwenza, kwathi uArin wahlala ngaphakathi kukaGuardian phakathi kwabo akahleki, ngoba wayematasa, kodwa okuthile enhliziyweni yakhe kwadedeleka okwakubambe isikhathi eside kakhulu.

*Jabulela,* kusho uFool kancane, kuye kuphela. *Bese siyasebenza. Intaba ayikazifundi izindaba ezinhle. Isese yintaba.*

---

Indlela yokuphuma yayingumsebenzi.

UArin wavula imephu yomzila: indlela yokwehla ebuyela kwi-cross-cut, indawo endala yokudonsa imithwalo, phansi okuphambene, i-81 Raise enezindawo eziqinile, ukukhuphuka isikhathi eside ukuya ku-30 Level, ikheji, bese phezulu. Wayehle yedwa, isudi igoqeka, iyazidonsa ezindaweni eziqinile. Wayengeke akwazi ukubuyela ngendlela efanayo. Manje wayenamadoda ayisithupha. Omunye wayengeke akwazi ukuhamba. Omunye wayengeke akwazi ukuphefumula kahle.

*Indlela yokwehla ayisebenzi kwabagulayo abadinga ukusetshenziswa kwe-stretcher,* kusho uAtlas. *Indawo eqinile e-81 Raise ayikwazi ukudlula i-litter. Futhi asikwazi ukuthwala abayisithupha nganoma iyiphi indlela. Igobolondo lingathwala oyedwa kuphela.*

"Manje asibuyi ngendlela engihambile ngayo."

*Kulungile,* kusho uLibrarian. *Kukhona enye indlela. Isiteshi se-38 Level sixhunywe kwi-main 38 Level haulage, okukhonjiswa izinhlelo zango-1962 ukuthi iya empumalanga iye esiteshini se-No. 4 Shaft. I-No. 4 yavalwa ngeminyaka yama-eighties, kodwa i-shaft uqobo—*

*—yiyona uVenter ayebeka izintambo kuyo phezulu,* kuphetha uAtlas. *Akasibeki ikheji esehle ngayo thina. Ubeka i-No. 4. Ukutholile ngaphambi kwethu.*

UArin wama. Phezulu, emsebenzini wokukhumbula kwakhe, wabona ubuso bendoda endala: ubuso obunezinwele ezimpunga, obushiswe yilanga, indoda uArin ayichithe izinsuku ezintathu ezama ukungayethembi ngenxa yesikhumba sayo nendlela ekhuluma ngayo, okwakubonisa iminyaka ayisebenze ngayo. Indoda eyafunda izinhlelo zango-1962 yodwa, ebusuku, ingamenyiwe, yathola indlela yokuphuma ngaphambi kokuba uArin afike phansi.

"Usedlula thina," kusho uArin.

*Yebo,* kusho uWolf.

Kwaba okokuqala uWolf akhuluma ngakho ihora lonke, kwathi iNkantolo yonke yathula.

*Uyazi le ndawo. Thina asiyazi. Sinamamephu. Yena unenkanuko. Lokho okubulala abantu lapha phansi. Simdinga.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amathathu empumalanga, indawo yokudonsa imithwalo ye-38 Level yayisesimweni esingcono kunanoma iyiphi uArin ayidlulile kuyo, okwamenza angayithemba. Wayethwele uThulani ezingalweni zikaGuardian, umfana eboshwe ebhodini, ibhodi lincike esifubeni segobolondo lapho isakhiwo sesudi sizothatha khona noma yiluphi ushaye ngaphambi kokuba umguli awuthole. Abanye abahlanu behamba ngemuva, izandla phezu kwamahlombe omunye nomunye, uchungechunge othulini, uMandla esemva, uPetros egxuma enabantu ababili bengaphansi kwezandla zakhe ngoba bekungekho omunye ongathwala yena futhi uGuardian wayegcwele.

Bese ama-headlamp athola isiteshi se-No. 4, kanye ne-shaft.

Kwabe kungumgodi omnyama edwaleni, ikheji elidala selahamba kudala, i-headgear amakhilomitha amabili nengxenye phezulu. Futhi phakathi nendawo, kuncishisiwe, kukhanya phezulu ngenhlansi emhlophe eqhakazile, kwakuncishisiwe ipulatifomu yensimbi.

I-flatbed. Hhayi ikheji. I-flatbed deck, eyayisetshenziswa ezimayini, lezo ezazisetshenziselwa ukwehlisa imishini esindayo, ixhunywe ezintanjeni ezine kanye ne-kibble winch, yehla isuka ekukhanyeni iya kubo indoda imi phezu kwayo.

Le ndoda yayigqoke i-hard hat kanye ne-harness eboshelwe entanjeni emaphakathi, futhi imi ngendlela eqinile yomuntu oseke wagibela lezi zinto izikhathi eziyinkulungwane futhi engasayithokozeli kodwa eseyekile ukuyesaba kudala.

I-comms kaArin yakhala. Izwi elihlukile manje, eduze, kwi-local band. Kancane. Ngokulinganisa.

"Ndlela." Venter. Jakobus Venter. "Usubatholile."

"Bonke abayisithupha," kusho u-Arin.

"Ja." Kwaba nokuthula. I-flatbed yathinta phansi kwesiteshi ngokuthinta kancane, kwakhala kancane, kwathi indoda endala yaxhuma izibopho yehla, yabheka phezulu isikhashana ebheka ubude buka-Guardian, yabe isingabheki futhi, ngendlela indoda esisebenze nemishini emikhulu impilo yayo yonke ebheka ngayo: uyilinganisa, bese uyayibeka eceleni. "I-thermal ayizange inyakaze ngesikhathi phezulu kushisa. Ngacabanga ukuthi usubatholile."

Wadlula ku-Arin waya kwabalimala. Waguqa eceleni kukaThulani ngaphandle kokucelwa, izandla zakhe ezinkulu ezinamabala elanga zihamba phezu kwezibopho, ezihlola, wanqekuzisa ikhanda kanye.

"Kulungile. Okwenziwe lapha kwenziwe kahle."

"Kwenzwe uMama," kusho u-Arin, esezwa ephinda. "Uhlelo. SAGE."

Amehlo kaVenter abheka ku-helmet. Abamba lapho umzuzwana.

"Ja," waphinda, akabuzanga, kwathi u-Arin waqonda ukuthi ukungabuzanga kwakuyinhlonipho.

---

Inkinga nge-flatbed kwakuyiyona nkinga yonke lapha phansi: ayizange yakhelwe lokhu.

*I-deck ibekelwe amathani amabili,* kusho u-Atlas, efunda ipuleti uVenter abeyikhomba ngaphandle kokucelwa. *Thina singaphezulu kwamane ngaphambi kwezilimele. Faka amadoda ayisithupha, faka ibhodi. Asikadluli, kodwa cishe siphindwe kabili, futhi le plate iwubungcweti obunobungane.*

"Ngakho angigibeli kuyo," kusho u-Arin. "Ngakhwela. Isudi ingakwazi ukunyuka odongeni lweshafti."

"Cha," kusho uVenter. Ngokulukhuni. Igama lokuqala elinzima alisebenzisile. Ube egobile phezu komshini we-winch ekhanda le-drift yesiteshi, lapho ikhebula likhuphuka liya ebumnyameni, manje waqondanisa, kwathi ekhuluma isiNgisi sakhe saphela, kwavela isiBhunu ngendlela ulimi lwendabuko luphuma ngayo uma indaba ibucayi futhi ungafuni ukuqondwa kabi. "Jy klim nie. Awukhwele. Udonga luphukile phezulu kumamitha angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili, izinkuni sezaphela. Uma ufaka isisindo salo mshini, uletha phansi nedeck namadoda akhona. Nee."

U-Arin uzwe efuna ukuphikisa. Wayenayo imodeli yodonga lweshafti. Wayenamathebula omthwalo. Wayene—

*Uqinisile,* kusho u-Atlas, ngaphambi kokuba u-Arin akhulume. *Ngine-geology. Uyifunda ebusweni bedwala nendlela lidabukile ngayo. Mina kumele ngibe ne-scanner. Yena uyazi nje. Udonga olungaphezulu kwesiteshi luwudwala olufile.*

*Lalela u-badger omdala,* kusho u-Fool. *Usehlale lapha phansi isikhathi eside kune-simulation yakho.*

Ngakho u-Arin wathi, "Pho senza kanjani?"

Futhi uVenter wamtshela.

Izikhathi ezimbili. Abalimala nabanye abane abahamba kuqala, phezulu ku-deck, uVenter egibela ukuze abheke ikhebula ne-kibble winch ezofuna ukuzikhulula ngaphansi komthwalo. Bese i-deck ibuyela phansi, ingenalutho. Bese u-Guardian yedwa, umuntu wokugcina ophumayo. Futhi lolu hambo, kusho uVenter, ebuka isudi enkulu, kuzomele bakwenze ngendlela ehlukile, ngoba amathani amane omshini akusilo mthwalo i-cage eyayikade yakhelwe ukuwuphakamisa ngekhebula elilodwa.

"I-winch iyona nkinga," kusho uVenter. Wayesekhiphe ikhava ye-motor housing, u-Arin abona, noma kukhanya kwe-headlamp, ukuthi kukhona okungahambi kahle lapho: i-brake band, ishile, ingxenye yokuvalela kwayo isishile. "I-brake ishile. Siyiphuthume siyilethela nina. Ishisa. Uma isishisa kakhulu, iyahluleka. I-deck iwele phansi namadoda akuyo. Uma sihla, ngiyibamba ngesandla i-band. Akusekho kwi-lift. I-lift i-electric, i-brake i-automatic, futhi i-automatic isishile."

U-Arin wabuka i-motor. Uchithe ubuntwana bakhe nemishini endala kanje, esebenza kanje, ekukhanyeni okunjalo. Wabona konke ngesikhathi esisodwa, wabona nokuhluleka.

"Udinga i-load brake i-winch motor engenayo," kusho yena. "Okuzobamba i-deck uma i-band iphuka. Uhlelo lwesibili."

"Ja." U-Venter wambheka. Wabheka u-Guardian. "Futhi nginalo. Ume phambi kwami, ngiyabona. Umshini ubamba ikhebula. I-band iphuka, isudi yakho ithwala umthwalo, i-deck ayiweli. Uyi-dead-man brake yami."

Kwaba, u-Arin wabona, kunjalo. Kwakuyinto ayengayakha uma enezinsuku ezintathu ne-workshop. Indoda endala ikwenzile ngemizuzwana eyishumi ngalokho okwakumi phambi kwayo.

"Atlas," kusho u-Arin. "Singakwazi ukubamba amathani amabili umthwalo ume phezu kwekhebula sidlula e-guide?"

*Nge-cross-cut frame njengesikhungo, ikhebula lidlula phezu kwe-sheave endala—yebo,* kusho u-Atlas. *Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye yomthwalo ogcwele ngaphambi kokuba i-actuator coolant idlule umkhawulo. Ngemva kwalokho kumele sikhiphe umthwalo noma sishise ingalo yangakwesokudla.*

*Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye yisikhathi se-lift,* kusho u-Librarian. *Amamitha angamakhulu amabili namashumi amane ngesivinini esiphephile. Imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalombili nesishiyagalombili.*

"Imizuzwana emibili yokuphephile," kusho u-Arin.

*Siyakwamukela ku-mine rescue,* kusho u-Fool. *Imizuzwana emibili yinto enhle. Ngaphambilini sasineminus four, sakwenza kwasebenza.*

"Lokho kwakuyi-simulation."

*Kwaba njalo? Kunzima ukusho lapha phansi. Ukukhanya kuyafana.*

Waphuma umoya wokuhleka kuye engakakulungeli. Kwamangalisa. Wabheka uVenter, owayembuka ekhuluma nomuntu ongamazi, ngokubekezela komuntu osevele enqume ukuthi okungajwayelekile akusona isixazululo sakhe.

"Kuzobamba," kusho u-Arin. "Ngizobamba."

U-Venter wambheka isikhathi eside. Ubuso obuphelelwe isikhathi bashintsha, umhlathi wanyakaza kanye. U-Arin wayengakwazi ukufunda ubuso kahle, akakaze akwazi, kodwa wayesekufundile ukufunda izikhathi ezizungezile, futhi le yayiyisikhathi esinesisindo. Indoda endala ichithe iminyaka, u-Arin azofunda kamuva, ithwele ukuthula mayelana nemishini yokuphepha ebingekho, ama-brake abekade ehlehlisiwe, izinqumo ezisayinwe amadoda angasoze agibela i-cage. Wayengcwabe lokho kuthula kwajula kangangoba wakha umsebenzi wesibili phezu kwako njengomeluleki, futhi ukweluleka kwakuyindlela yakhe yokuthula, iseluleko okungekho muntu obophekile ukusilalela.

Futhi nansi imishini eyakhiwe umfana osemncane ubaba wakhe owadliwa yintaba enjena, icelela indoda endala ukuba ithembe yona njenge-brake ngesikhathi amadoda ayisithupha enyuka ephuma ebumnyameni.

"Uyazi ukuthi leli yimbani kabani," kusho uVenter. "Inkampani. Irekhodi labo."

"Ngiyazi," kusho u-Arin.

"Ngangiyinsizwa emayini enjena. Ngasayina izinto." IsiBhunu saphuma kanzima manje, indoda endala ayikuvimbanga. "Izinto ebengifanele ngizimele. Ngazitshela ukuthi nginqatshiwe. Nganqatshwa. Kodwa ngasayina noma kunjalo." Wabheka phezulu eshaftini emnyama ebheke ukukhanya okumhlophe okude. "Angikubuyiseli lokho. Akukho—akukho indlela yokwehla ukuyokubuyisa."

"Cha," kusho u-Arin. "Ayikho."

Indoda endala yazamazama, kwathi, ngokumangalisayo, yathamba.

"Ja," kusho uVenter. "Hhayi-ke." Waqinisa amagilavu. "Masibakhuphe. Labo esisakwazi ukubakhupha."

---

Bafaka abantu ku-deck.

UThulani kuqala, ibhodi liboshelwe ku-rail ye-deck, uMama eqapha isifuba sakhe ngama-sensor ka-Guardian kuze kube umzuzwana wokugcina lapho isignali isatholakala khona. Bese uPetros, bese abane abahamba, behleli eduze, izandla zibambe i-rail, ubuso bujule phezulu ebusweni bokukhanya sengathi bangakwazi ukukhuphuka ngokufuna nje. UMandla Buthelezi wakhuphuka ngaleli qoqo lokuqala ngoba uVenter wambona ezinzile futhi ezokwazi ukugcina abanye bathule, kwathi lapho esephezulu ku-rail ye-deck uMandla waphenduka wabheka emuva kumshini osesele ebumnyameni, ozokhuphuka wokugcina.

"Wena," esho. *Wena.* "Ubani igama lakho? Umuntu. Hhayi umshini."

"Arin."

"Arin." UMandla wanqekuzisa ikhanda kancane. "Sasicabanga ukuthi sizwa abafileyo bekhuluma, lapha phansi. Sasicabanga ukuthi sesiphelile." Wabamba i-rail. "Ngiyabonga. Ufikile ngesikhathi besethi akukho ndlela."

"Bekukhona indlela," kusho u-Arin. "Akekho obheke kahle ngokwanele."

UVenter wazifaka entanjeni emaphakathi. Wabeka isandla esisodwa ku-lever ye-band brake, le brake eshisiwe ayeyizosebenzisa ngomuzwa kuyo yonke indlela enyuka, amakhilomitha amabili nesigamu entanjeni, isandla esisodwa esishisiwe, ngesinye isandla wacindezela i-winch, kwase kuphakama i-deck, izulazula, iphakamisa amadoda ayisithupha aphuma emoyeni omubi nasekukhanyeni okufile, ibayisa ekukhanyeni, nasekubongeni, nasekuseni.

U-Arin wayibuka isuka. Wahambisa u-Guardian endaweni yokubopha, wafaka intambo ku-sheave endala, wathwala umthwalo engalweni yangakwesokudla yesudi uma i-brake ingavumi, wema lapho, cishe amathani amane ensimbi nomuntu ngaphakathi, ebambe intambo ebambe amadoda, i-dead-man brake eyakhiwe u-badger omdala ngemizuzwana eyishumi.

I-band yahlala. I-deck yakhuphuka. Ayizange imdingide.

*I-heat budget engalweni isesimweni,* kusho u-Atlas. *Akudingeki kube khona ukudluliselwa komthwalo. Uhambe kahle nge-brake.*

"Kulungile," kusho u-Arin.

*Ubungakwenza wedwa lowo msebenzi,* kusho u-Fool, ngokuxoxa, ethule ngesikhathi sokukhuphuka okude. *Ubambe intambo. Wakhe i-anchor. Kodwa indlela yokuphuma—odongeni olufile, i-deck enesilinganiso, i-No. 4 Shaft—ubungangena kulawo ma-plank abolile, uwazwise kuwe, bese amadoda ayisithupha afe phansi komgodi nomzimba ogqoke isudi ebiza kakhulu. Uyakwazi lokho, ja?*

U-Arin wabuka i-deck incipha isiya ekukhanyeni.

"Yebo," kusho yena.

*Bengihlola nje. Kunzima ukwazi ukuthi kuyadlula yini kuwe. Ugcina okuningi ebhokisini.*

*Myekeni,* kusho uMother.

*Cha,* kusho u-Arin. "Uqinisile." Walalela injini ye-winch ilwa nomthwalo, umsindo onamandla, ophilile womshini owenza umsebenzi owakhiwe wona, ugibele umuntu owaziyo. "Bengingafa lapha phansi ngiqinisile. Uqinisile ngokushesha."

*Lokhu okokuqala,* kusho uJudge, ngokucophelela okukhulu anikela ezintweni azibona zibalulekile, *ngilizwa usho ukuthi omunye umuntu uqinisile ngokushesha kunathi.*

*Qopha isikhathi,* kusho uFool. *Librarian. Faka enkumbulweni unomphela. Amamitha ayizinkulungwane ezimbili ngaphansi komhlaba, umoya ofile, umfana uyavuma ukuthi indoda angayethembi yayazi indlela yokuphuma ngaphambi kwakhe.*

*Kubhaliwe,* kusho uLibrarian. Wabe esethi, ngobumnene: *Kubhaliwe.*

I-deck yafinyelela ekukhanyeni. Phezulu kakhulu, incane, u-Arin wayibona ithinta phezulu, wabona abantu begijima kuyo, wabona izithunzi eziyisithupha zingena ekukhanyeni, kwaphinde kwehla emgodini ukukhala, manje kunomsindo, kwabe sekuzwakala nezwi likaVenter ku-helmet yakhe, kancane, lome, futhi ngokusobala ejabule.

"Bayisithupha phezulu. Baphila. Ngiyibuyisela i-deck kuwe, Ndlela." Wathula kancane. "Bese sikukhipha emgodini wami."

*Umgodi wakhe,* kusho uFool. *Ngiyamthanda.*

U-Arin wema ebumnyameni phansi komgodi, ebambe intambo engasadingeki ukuthi ibanjwe. I-deck yaqala ukwehla ibuyele kuye, izulazula, ikhanyiswa phezulu.

Ngaphakathi kwe-helmet, umoya wawusubile futhi usondelene, ushisa ngenxa yokuphefumula kwakhe, kanti engalweni yangakwesokudla ka-Guardian, kwakubonakala indawo ehlotshiswe lapho i-ceramic ishayisane nodonga lwe-cross-cut.

Akazange akwenze yedwa.

*Atlas,* kusho yena. "Amandla nokupholisa. Ngiphe inombolo yangempela yokukhuphuka. Hhayi leyo ethembisayo."

Kwaba nokuthula. Okuncane. Kodwa u-Arin wayesechithe amahora amaningi ngaphakathi kwalomshini ukuthi azi ukuthula kwawo ngendlela uVenter azi ngayo itshe, futhi leli thule linezinto ezithile ngaphakathi.

*Inombolo eqotho,* kusho u-Atlas, *kufanele sixoxe ngayo uma i-deck ifika.*

Ukukhanya okumhlophe kwehla kuphuma ebumnyameni kuye, u-Arin wakubuka, walinda.

Izibani zefulethi zanyamalala ekwenyukeni kwe-81 Raise, abasinda abayisithupha behleli bekhothame ebhokisini lemishini kaVenter, beshiya umhubhe ungenalutho ngaphandle kuka-Arin, isisindo sesudi, nomfudlana wasendulo itshe elaligcine iminyaka eyizinkulungwane.

“Sebedlule endaweni enciphile,” kusho uPriya, izwi lakhe libuthakathaka ezixhumanini. “UPetros uthi ibhuleki lisabambe.”

“Ungalithinti uma umthambeka ungakanciphi.” U-Arin wahamba, uhlaka lukaGuardian lwathola ukuhamba kwezinyawo ezine ngaphandle kokucabanga, amapuleti ehla emhlane wakhe njengoba egoba enyuka. Igumbi lokuphephela lashona emuva kwakhe. Phambili, indlela ayekade eyicwila amahora amabili edlule yayimlindile ukuba ayibhidlize. Manje enyuka, amandla adonsela phansi asephenduke isitha.

Umoya omdala wamthatha endaweni ayeyibonile ehla phansi, engazange abe nesikhathi sokucabanga ngayo, futhi nasekhona manje akazange acabange. Cishe amamitha ayisishiyagalolunye itshe laphenduka. Ucwaningo lwango-’88 lwalulibiza ngokuthi i-quartzite evamile, eyakhiwe ngesandla yashiywa, futhi kokubili ekugcineni kwalawo mamitha ayisishiyagalolunye kunjalo nje, ubuso obudliweyo bamadoda anezinsimbi zokumba. Kodwa phakathi nendawo, udonga lwaba *bushelelezi*. Hhayi obuqinile, hhayi obufakwe i-shotcrete; izinzwa zesudi zamthumela ulwazi esandleni sakhe njengoba ezinzisa uhlaka, futhi lokho abezwa ezinzwaneni kwakuyinto *engenalutho*—akukho izimpawu zensimbi, akukho uphawu lokuqhuma, akukho ngisho noboya bomshini. Kwakuyimbobo edwaleni eqediwe ngendlela ayengayazi, ibanda, iqondile, igobile ngendlela engenziwa yimishini inkampani eyake yehla ngayo. Okwakukwenzile kwakungasikhiphi itshe. Kwakukholisa. Wakubeka lapho abeka khona izinto ezingahlanganiyo futhi ezingakwazi ukuxazululwa: umqondo opholile emqondweni, *buka futhi uma kungasafi amadoda ayisithupha.* Akazange aphinde akhumbule. Wahamba, ubushelelezi baphela kwavela itshe langempela, wakukhohlwa iminyaka eyishumi nane.

*Amandla asele amathathu nesishiyagalolunye ekhulwini,* kusho u-Atlas, engasathuli. *Isikhathi sokupholisa, imizuzu eyishumi nanye uma siqhubeka kanje.*

“Kubi kunokwehla.”

*Ukwehla bekuphansi.* U-Atlas, sengathi uchazela ingane. *Manje usuphakamisa cishe amathani amane umthambeka ungamashumi amane nane degrees phezu komhlaba owonakele. Intaba ayikubuyiseli mahhala lokho.*

“Iyasinika i-piezo recovery.”

*Iyasinika i-piezo recovery,* walungisa u-Atlas. *Ngaphandle kwakho, besizobe sesiphelile amandla.*

U-Arin wezwa izinsika zisebenza emgogodleni wakhe—zonke izinyathelo, zonke izinyakazo zohlaka phezu komhlaba owaphukile, isudi imunca amandla okunyakaza kwayo iwaguqule abe ngamandla amancane agcina amapompo okupholisa ephila. Ingcindezi iba amandla. Ubaba wakhe ubeyokuthanda lokho. *Yenza into ekubulalayo ikusize.* UThemba wake wathi into efanayo, izandla zimnyama kuze kube esihlakaleni, ekhuluma ngowinishi obelokhu elwa.

Waqhubeka enyuka.

---

Ngesikhathi semishini, iNkantolo ibisivele isemizuzwana engamakhulu amathathu yamasekhondi ingxabanweni eyayingeke ifinyelele ezindlebeni zika-Arin kuze kudlule imizuzwana emine, uma ifinyelela nhlobo.

*Inombolo u-Atlas angazange ayisho ngesikhathi behla,* kusho uJaji. *Yisho manje.*

*Ngiyishilo.* U-Atlas, eqondile. *Imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokupholisa. Amathathu nesishiyagalolunye ekhulwini.*

*Lokho akuyona inombolo.* UJaji futhi, ebekezela njengensimbi. *Lawa manani awazimele. Ukushisa kukhula ngokusetshenziswa kwamandla. Ukusetshenziswa kwamandla kukhula ngomthambeka nokulimala. Uhlaka alusabambeki. Sisebenzisa amandla ukubamba isimo esifuna ukugoba.*

*Ngikhombise,* kusho uMama.

U-Atlas walandela. Hhayi ngamazwi—imephu yomthwalo, uhlaka lwemgogodla luboniswa ngemibala yokucindezeleka, i-actuator yesokunxele ishisa ngamadigri ayishumi nanye, i-strut yesithathu emhlane ikhombisa ukuqhekeka okuncane okwaqoshwa amahora angamashumi amane edlule kwafakwa uphawu lokuthi *qaphela*. Ukuqhekeka sekukhule. *Qaphela* sekuyinto ethi *qala ukunciphisa umthwalo ngemizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye.*

*Indlu ye-biochip,* kusho uLibrarian kancane. *Ephakathi kwamaphekula akhe. Siyinto eshisayo kakhulu kule sudi, futhi umjikelezo wokupholisa usigcina ekugcineni.*

*Ngokuhlelwa,* kwaphawula uWolf. Igama lokuqala emizuzwana eyizinkulungwane ezimbili. *Wabeka phambili. Umsebenzi uqale kuqala.*

*Wakwenza engakabi azi ukuthi siyabantu,* kuphikisa uMercury.

Akekho ophendulile imizuzwana eyikhulu namashumi amane, okwakufana nokubamba umoya kubo.

*Hlala kokwezokuphatha,* myalelo uJaji. *Sinexesha. Sihlela ukukhuphuka. Sibala ukusetshenziswa kwamandla. Siyamkhuphula.*

*Yebo,* kusho uMama, yize engakukholwa, kodwa wakusho ngoba kwakuwumsebenzi wakhe.

---

“Arin.” Izwi likaPriya laqhuma kwisilabhasi yokuqala. “Inkinga ekholeni.”

Wayesefinyelele lapho phansi kwaphakama khona, lapho ehlayo ayigoqa khona iGuardian ukuze adlule ngaphansi kweplanga eliwile. Manje kwakufanele adlule phezulu. Wabeka imilenze yangaphambili, wahlola indawo yeplanga, walizwa lihamba isentimitha laphinde lama.

“Chaza inkinga.”

“I-winchi kuNo. 4. UVenter uphakamise abasinda—sebephezulu, Arin, bonke abayisithupha, baphumile—” umsindo ongaba ukuhleka noma okuphambene—“kodwa ibhuleki elikhulu seliphukile. Liphukile ngempela. Kwakukhona intuthu. Bayizwa ngaphambi kokuba ibhajethi ikhombise—” Wama. “Iyehla yodwa uma umthwalo ungaphansi kwengxenye.”

“Sebabekhona phezulu.”

“Yebo. Wena awukabi khona. Futhi okuwukuphela kwento evunyelwe ukukuphakamisa nesudi yakho yibhokisi elikhulu, kodwa ibhokisi elikhulu alikwazi ukukuphakamisa nesudi yakho.”

U-Arin wama enomlenze phezu kweplanga, ebambe cishe amathani amane phezu kwebhodi elingafuni yena.

“Ngisindayo kangakanani?”

“Ngiphethe? Cishe amathani amane. Biza amathani amane.”

*Izinkulungwane ezintathu namakhulu ayisishiyagalolunye,* kwasho u-Atlas. *Nge-coolant esele, cishe amane. Asikhiphi, ngakho: amane.*

“Futhi ibhokisi liyakwazi?”

“Ngebhuleki elihle, nonke ningangena ningazizwa. Kodwa ibhuleki seliphukile—iyibhande lokungqubuzana kudrum, kanti idrum seliyinsimbi eshisayo. UVenter uyayibuka manje.”

Wadlula phezu kweplanga. Uhlaka lakhala—hhayi isudi, uhlaka, ukukhala okude njengoba i-strut eqhekekile ithwala umthwalo wokugoba okwakungathembiswanga yona. Okuthile ku-strut yesithathu kwaphuka nomsindo ofana nentambo yegitare eqhume yakhuliswa nge-drum yensimbi.

*IStrut yesithathu iphukile,* kusho u-Atlas. *Siguqula umthwalo siwudlulisela ku-titanium primary. Lokho kuphindaphinda umthwalo wayo.* Kwamiswa. *Leyo spar ayenzelwe ukuba ibe primary.*

“Ngazise uma sekubalulekile.”

*Kuzobaluleka ekholeni,* kusho u-Atlas. *Lapho sima ukuze singene ebhokisini. Okwamanje usasabalale ngemilenze emine. Ibhokisi lifuna ungene ngezinyawo ezimbili.*

“Ngizonyukela ngenye indlela.”

*Ayikho enye indlela,* kusho u-Atlas. *Sengikuhlolile. Kabili.*

---

Amamitha angamakhulu amabili phezulu, ebumnyameni obushisayo besiteshi sokulayisha, uJakobus Venter wacindezela ingemuva lesandla sakhe ku-brake drum wayikhipha masinyane.

“Ja. Sekuphelile,” kusho yena, engenakukhuluma nomuntu. Ibhande lokungqubuzana belizincibilikise phakathi kwedrum lase lidedela. Linsundu, linamabala amnyama. Alisebenzi. Wake wabona amabhuleki esha ngaphambili. Wake wasayina amanye okwakungafanele adlule. Lowo mbono wafika ngendlela ejwayelekile manje, engamenyiwe, nabafileyo behamba nawo, wawubeka phansi waqhubeka nomsebenzi, ngoba umfana usesephansi.

Abasinda babese-headgear deck, begqoke izingubo zokufudumeza, uThulani elele ebhodini nabezempilo. UBongani wayengayeki ukubuka umgodi.

UVenter wabheka isigubhu. Wabheka ikheji, ibhasikidi lensimbi elilingana negumbi elincane, elenzelwe amadoda angamashumi amathathu noma amathani ayisishiyagalombili e-ore—ibhasikidi uqobo luzobamba isudi futhi lungezwa lutho. Ikheji ayikaze ibe inkinga. Inkinga kwakuwukuyilengisa kwihoisti enebhuleki elaliphelile, kwiwinchi yokugcina esasebenza, okwamanje ekwazi ukuthwala noma iyiphi ingxenye yomthwalo ongabulali ukuphela komuntu osele phansi emgodini.

"Priya." Wachofoza iradio. "Mbuzise. Ingabe isudi ingakwazi ukubamba umthwalo ngezinyawo? Imile. Ivalekile. Hhayi ukuphakamisa—ukubamba."

Kwathatha isikhashana amazwi ehamba phansi kwefiber abuye.

"Uthi—uAtlas uthi—imizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye. Umthwalo omile. Bese i-spine yehluleka."

UVenter wabheka ibhuleki. Wabheka ikheji. Wabheka izintambo zesisindo ezazigijima zikhuphuka ebumnyameni be-headgear, zinkulu njengezingalo zomuntu, kanye nesigubhu sesibili eceleni kwesifile—i-auxiliary, iwinchi ye-kibble, encane, ehamba kancane, elenzelwe amathani amabili kuphela, kodwa inebhuleki layo, inenjini yayo, ayikaze ithintwe. Amathani amabili. Isudi yayicishe ibe amane. Ayikho iwinchi eyodwa eyayiyithwala ngaphandle kokudabuka noma ukusha. Kodwa ezimbili, ngayinye ithwala ingxenye—

Wayesephila impilo yakhe yonke enza izibalo. Ukuthi umhlaba uzothatha malini ngaphambi kokuba uyeke ukuba ngumhlaba ube yithuna.

"Izintambo ezimbili," esho. "Amawinchi amabili. Hlanganisani umthwalo." Wachofoza iradio. "Priya. Tshela eJo'burg ngifuna iwinchi ye-kibble kanye nentambo yekheji zombili ziboshwe kusudi. Zisebenze ndawonye. Cishe amathani amabili ngayinye—ngqo emkhawulweni we-kibble, kodwa iyabamba uma imithwalo ihlale ilinganile."

Kwahle cwaka, kwase kuthi uPriya, ngokucophelela: "Azihambisani, Kobus. Izigubhu ezahlukene, ijubane elihlukile. Enye idonsa ngokushesha kunezinye, isudi igudluka eceleni eshaftini, idabule izintambo—"

"Ja. Ngiyazi okwenzekayo." Wayesevele ekhuphuka izitebhisi zedekhi eya endlini ye-winchi, amadolo akhe ekhala njalo ethatha isinyathelo. "Yilokho okwenza umuntu eme esigubhuni se-kibble abambe ibhuleki ngesandla. Alinganise nekheji ngeso. Kuze kuphume phezulu."

"Lokho kungamakhilomitha ayishumi nesishiyagalombili."

"Ngiyazi ukuthi i-shaft ijule kangakanani."

"Kobus—uma ibhuleki lekheji seliphukile, bese i-kibble brake nayo yehluleka, noma ngubani oma esigubhuni uzoba emi phezu kwamathani amane ensimbi ewela phansi engenalutho oluzomisa."

UVenter wafika endlini ye-winchi. Isigubhu se-kibble sasihleli lapho, sidala, simnyama ngamafutha, sithembekile. Wabeka isandla sakhe ku-lever yebhuleki. Yahamba kahle. Ihlotshiswe ngamafutha. Omunye wayeyigcine. Mhlawumbe yena, eminyakeni eyedlule. Mhlawumbe indoda ayeseyikhohlile igama layo, yenza umsebenzi wayo kahle ngesikhathi abaphezulu bayo besika amagumbi.

"Ngakho kungcono ingahluleki," esho.

---

UArin wafika ku-collar ngo-22%.

*I-cooling reserve, imizuzu eyisithupha,* kwakhumbuza uAtlas. *I-biochip housing isezingeni lama-61 degrees. Sisebenza kuze kufike ku-70. Asisebenzi ngaphezulu kuka-74.*

Wayesevile isikhathi eside eseyeka ukuhumusha izinombolo zika-Atlas abe imizwa. Zaziseyindawo nje manje. Iminithi eyisithupha yobandayo. Bese kuba ukushisa. Bese kuba ubumnyama.

"Ngimile."

*Kuyavunywa,* kuphendula uAtlas. *Qonda futhi: ukuma kuyinto enzima kakhulu esiyenzayo namuhla. I-strut yesithathu ayisekho. I-titanium primary ithwala konke. Uma uma, isisindo sakho sonke plus isudi kudluliselwa ku-spine ngokucindezela okuqondile. I-primary iyabamba. I-hip actuator eshisayo izocelwa ukuvala ibambe umthwalo we-kibble cable, ozoba awulingani, ngoba umuntu uyawulinganisa ngesandla amakhilomitha ayishumi nesishiyagalombili ngaphezulu kwethu.*

"Iminyaka engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye."

*Yebo,* kusho uAtlas. *Emakhilomitha amabili angiqiniseki. UVenter angeke akukhiphe amakhilomitha ayishumi nesishiyagalombili ngemizuzwana engamashumi ayisishiyagalolunye.*

"Cha."

*Cha,* kuvuma uAtlas.

Wama. Isudi yamvulela ephuma egxumeni, amaplethi ehamba, nomhlaba waguquka usuka ekugobeni kwezilwane ezine ayekuhamba imitha engaphezu kwekhulu kuya esimeni somuntu oqondile esingena emnyango wekheji. I-spine yathwala. I-spar eqhekekile yabamba. I-hip eshisayo yavaleka.

Wangena ekheji. Phansi kwakhala ngaphansi kwamathani amane wesudi nomuntu. Ngaphezulu kwakhe, ebumnyameni, izintambo ezimbili—intambo enkulu yekheji nentambo encane ye-kibble eyayiboshwe kusudi ngesikhathi enyuka—zaba ziqinile, eyodwa, kwathi ngemva kwesikhashana, enye.

"Phakamisa," wayala.

Ikheji yaphuma ezinzwaneni zayo.

---

Emamitha amabili okuqala, kwazwakala cishe kukuhle.

Wayezwa umjikelezo: intambo yekheji idonsa kancane isuka esigubhuni esinobhuleki ofile, ihamba kancane ngokucophelela, nentambo ye-kibble idonsa kancane, ivimba ngezikhathi ezincane, isandla sikaVenter ephezulu sibambe i-lever, ebuka ikheji ngokunyakaza kwentambo nangamandla ezandleni zakhe, ekulinganisa. Akukhululekile. Akukaze kube lula. Kodwa bekulinganiselwe.

Isudi imubambe phakathi kwe-shaft njengengalo ebambe iqanda.

*Amamitha amathathu,* kubika uAtlas. *Ukupholisa, imizuzu emine. I-housing isezingeni lama-63.*

"Khuluma ngento ehlukile," kusho uArin.

Kwathula eCourt angazange ayizwe.

*I-strut yesithathu yehlulekile kahle,* kwasho uAtlas, ngemva kwesikhashana, ngelizwi lomuntu okhuluma emngcwabeni. *Ukuhluleka kahle kuhle. Ukuqhekeka okuhlanzekile kushiya umthwalo endaweni eyodwa. Ukuqhekeka okungcolile kushiya umthwalo yonke indawo. Wawuklame i-spar ukuthi yehluleke kahle. Lokho bekulungile.*

"Bengiyiklama ukuthi ingahluleki."

*Wawuyiklama ukuthi yehluleke kahle uma yehluleka,* kuphendula uAtlas. *Okuyinto efanayo nokuvuma ukuthi izokwenza njalo. Lokho bekuklama okuqotho. Ngihlala ngikuhlonipha lokho.*

Kuphefumula uArin, okungenzeka kube ukuhleka. Ikheji yanyakaza—ukunyakaza okuqinile, uhlangothi lwe-kibble ludonsa kakhulu, isudi ishaya kwesokudla ngaphambi kokuthi i-hip actuator iyibambe imlinganise. Phezulu, wacabanga uVenter ephonsa isisindo sakhe ku-lever yebhuleki, elungisa, eqalekisa ngesi-Afrikaans ebumnyameni.

---

Ezimamitha ayisikhombisa, i-hip actuator yashisa yavaleka.

Hhayi ukuvaleka kancane. Kwaba ukuvaleka ngokuphelele. Ijoyinti ebithwala ukudonsa okungalingani kwe-kibble cable imitha engaphezu kwamane ayeka ukuthatha imiyalo, isudi yaqina ohlangothini lwesokudla, kwathi lapho ikheji inyakaza futhi akekho owabamba.

Isudi yashaya odongeni lwe-shaft. Amathani amane eshayisa itshe ngesivinini sewinchi—hhe kancane, kodwa kunamandla, i-shaft yonke yakhala njengeshubhu eshayiwe, uthuli nezinkuni ezindala kwehla phezu kwekheji, ikhanda likaArin lashayeka ngaphakathi kwe-helmet kwaze kwaba mnyama emaphethelweni.

*Bamba,* kwasho uWolf, futhi leli zwi lafika ngaphambi kokushayisana, okwakungafanele kwenzeke.

*Pangolin,* kwasho uMother. *Manje. Mgoqe.*

*Cha,* kusho uAtlas. *Uma sigoqa eshaftini, silahlekelwa i-geometry yekheji. Siyabamba. Asiphumi.*

*Ushaye odonga.*

*Futhi uzophinda ashayise,* kusho uAtlas. *Ukugoqa akuxazululi inkinga efanele.*

Ikheji yazinza. UVenter, phezulu, wayezwe ukunyakaza kwentambo yakhe wayimisa womabili amawinchi, ebambe wonke umthwalo ngamabhuleki amabili—elinye yisandla somuntu ne-lever enegrease—manje sebebambe phezulu, amamitha ayishumi nesishiyagalombili ensimbi ewela phansi ibanjwe indoda endala newinchi eneminyaka engamashumi ayisithupha.

"Arin." Izwi likaPriya, lincane kakhulu. "Izintambo zinyakazile. UVenter uyabamba. Kwenzekeni?"

"I-hip ayisasebenzi." Izwi lakhe lalingathi likude kuye. "Uhlangothi lwesokudla luvalekile. Angikwazi ukugwema udonga."

Kwathula isikhathi eside.

"Ungakwazi ukukhuphuka uvalekile?"

*Cha,* kusho u-Atlas, ebuza eNkantolo, engakhulumi no-Arin. *Sivalelekile, asikwazi ukumunca lezi zinkinga. Ukulungisa ngakunye kusifaka odongeni. Asisoze saphila amamitha ayizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye asele. I-spar izophuka ingcolile. Bese kuba yicage. Bese kuba nguyena.*

*Ngiyahlaziya umthwalo wokushisa,* kusho uLibrarian. *Ngiyawuqhuba ukwenyuka nge-actuator evaliwe. Ukushayisana kwengubo nodonga kuzodala ukushisa okungaphezulu. Amaphampu okupholisa asebenza cishe emkhawulweni.*

*Futhi?* kubuza uMama.

*Indlu ye-biochip isemhlane wakhe,* kusho uLibrarian. *Yindawo eshisayo kunazo zonke ohlelweni. Umjikelezo wokupholisa unikeza umqhubi kuqala, njengokucaciswa kuka-Arin kokuqala. Indlu ithola ukupholisa sekugcineni. Uma sidala ukushisa okungeziwe futhi asinaso isikhala—*

*Sibala izinombolo,* kuphakamisa u-Atlas. *Zonke izimo. Ukukhuphuka ngesikhathi esisodwa, i-hip evaliwe, ukushayisana nodonga njalo ngemitha eyizinkulungwane eziyishumi nanye. Ngiyazibala manje. Ngizoba nempendulo emizuzwaneni emithathu.*

*Arin, yini okushiwo ukufunda kwakho okuyisisekelo?* kubuza uWolf.

"Thirty-eight point two. Kuyakhuphuka."

*Kuzoqhubeka kukhuphuka,* kusho u-Atlas, izwi lakhe lifika ngaphambi kokulindela kuka-Arin. *Ngiyitholile impendulo. Kukhona impendulo eyodwa kuphela. Umjikelezo wokupholisa awukwazi ukugcina umqhubi kanye nendlu ye-biochip ngaphansi komkhawulo wokwehluleka ngesikhathi sokukhuphuka. Sisesikhathini esiyimizuzu eyisithupha sokupholisa sisuka ku-degree engamashumi ayisithupha nantathu, futhi sisenemizuzu eyishumi nane yomgodi esele ngesivinini esiphephile sikaVenter. Amaphampu asezingeni eliphezulu. Asikho esinye isipholile. Kusele umbuzo wokuthi ukupholisa kokugcina kuya kuphi.*

---

ENkantolo, kwakungekho okunye okumele kwenziwe kwezokuphatha.

Ijaji lababiza. Wakwenzela ngaphandle kokwenza imicimbi, ngoba kwakungekho sikhathi sokwenza imicimbi, futhi ngoba imicimbi kwakuwuhlobo lwamanga ababengenakukwazi ukukhokhela.

*Chaza ukukhetha,* kusho uJaji.

U-Atlas wakusho. Wayesekwenze izikhathi eziyishumi nanye emizuzwaneni emine edlule wathola impendulo efanayo njalo, wayengayithandi, okwakuyinto entsha ku-Atlas, ukungathandi impendulo.

*Umjikelezo wokupholisa awukwazi ukugcina umqhubi kanye nendlu ye-biochip ngaphansi komkhawulo wokwehluleka ngesikhathi sokukhuphuka. Sisesikhathini esiyimizuzu eyisithupha sokupholisa sisuka ku-degree engamashumi ayisithupha nantathu, futhi sisenemizuzu eyishumi nane yomgodi esele ngesivinini esiphephile sikaVenter, eside uma i-hip ivaliwe, ngoba ivaliwe kuhamba kancane. Amaphampu asezingeni eliphezulu. Asikho esinye isipholile. Kusele umbuzo wokuthi ukupholisa kokugcina kuya kuphi.*

*Futhi uma kuya kuye?* kubuza uMama, nakuba wayazi.

*Kuzobe inhliziyo yakhe, umgogodla wakhe, nobuchopho bakhe kusaphila,* kusho u-Atlas. *Futhi indlu idlula ku-seventy-four. Siba buthaka. I-substrate ayiphili ku-seventy-four. Asikho isipele. Uyazi asikho isipele.*

*Futhi uma kuya kithi?*

*Khona siphila,* kusho u-Atlas, *kanti yena uyashisa. Izinga lakhe lokushisa selikhuphuka ngoba ingubo ilwa nodonga idala ukushisa esingakwazi ukulikhulula. Uma sizigcina zipholile, sizigcina sicabanga kahle ngenkathi yena efa phakathi kwemihlana yethu.*

INkantolo yahlala nalokho imizuzwana eyikhulu neshumi.

*Kukhona inketho yesithathu,* kusho uMercury, kwaze kwaba umzuzwana owodwa wesabeka izwi lakhe laba nomphetho uLibrarian awazi kahle, waze wathuthumela—umphetho onokubekezela, onenhlonipho, onobuhle, izwi elake lazama ukukhuluma nesibhedlela ukuthi sivule iminyango yaso. *Singakwazi—*

*Cha,* kuphendula uWolf. Igama elilodwa, nomphetho waphuma ezwini likaMercury njengomlilo ocindezelwe phakathi kwemigqa emibili yezinzwane.

*Bengingeke,* kuphikelela uMercury, ethule. *Ngizizwe kufika. Ngiyatshela ukuthi ngizizwe kufika, futhi angiyenzi. Lokho kufanele kubalwe.*

*Kubalulekile,* kusho uJaji. *Kubalulekile ukuthi usho obala.*

*Ayikho inketho yesithathu,* kumemeza u-Atlas. *Ngibheke. Ngibheke ngokucophelela kunanini ngaphambili. Kukhona ukupholisa, kukhona lapho kuya khona.*

*Bese uyifaka kuye,* kusho uMama. *Konke. Manje.*

---

U-Arin wezwa ukupholisa kushiya indlu phakathi kwemihlana yakhe.

Ekuqaleni, wayengazi ukuthi uzizwa kanjani—wazizwa nje ukuthi kukhona okushintshile engubeni, umsindo wephampu ushintsha, ukufudumala kuvela emhlane wakhe lapho kwakufanele kube yindawo epholile kunazo zonke kuye, kanti ngesikhathi esifanayo umoya we-helmet usuka ekushiseni okungaphephile uye kokuthile okungathwaleka, umbala ompunga usuka emaphethelweni okubona kwakhe.

"Wenzeni?" ebabuza.

*Sishintshe ukupholisa,* kuphendula u-Atlas. *Kuwe. Ngokugcwele.*

"Kimi kusuka kuphi."

Kuthule.

"Kusuka kuphi, Atlas."

*Kusuka kithi,* kusho uLibrarian, *kusuka endlini. Kusuka ku-substrate.*

U-Arin waqonda ngendlela ayeqonda ngayo izinto, konke ngesikhathi esisodwa, uhlelo lonke, yonke imiphumela ibekwe phansi njengemephu yomthwalo, futhi akukho ndlela yokungayiboni. Wayakhe ukuhleleka kokubaluleka. Wayebeke umqhubi kuqala. Wayengakaze acabange ukuthi ingubo izophoqeleka ukukhetha, ngoba wayengakaze acabange ukuthi impahla phakathi kwemihlana yakhe izoba umuntu.

"Buyisani."

*Cha,* kusho uMama.

"Buyisani. Ngiyakwazi ukubekezelela ukushisa. Ngingakhuphuka ngishisa. Ngiye ngakwenza—"

*Izinga lakho lokushisa seliku-thirty-nine point four,* kusho uMama, ngezwi ayelisebenzisa ngosuku amuzwa ngalo okokuqala, izwi elingenakunikeza lutho. *Ku-forty-one, awusakwazi ukwenza izinqumo. Ku-forty-three, inhliziyo yakho ithola umshayo ongaphunyukeki. Awukwazi ukukhuphuka uphume lapho, Arin. Awukwazi ukuphuma lutho. Asixoxisani.*

"Akusikho—"

*Kuyisinqumo sethu ngokuphelele,* kuphazamisa uJaji, futhi kwakukhona okuthile ezwini lakhe u-Arin angakaze akuzwe ngaphambili. *Usinike isakhiwo. Usinike ukukhetha. Uthe: ingqondo engenakuphikisa ayiyona ingqondo. Usakhele ukuphikisana. Siphikisene. Ukuphikisana sekuphelile.*

"Kuze kube nini?"

*Ngaphandle kokupholisa,* kuphendula u-Atlas, *indlu ifinyelela ku-seventy-four cishe ngemizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye. Umgodi useyishumi nane. Asisoze safinyelela sonke phezulu.*

Icage yaqala ukuhamba futhi. U-Venter, phezulu, wayezwe ingubo ithule odongeni—ukudlikiza sekuncane manje, ukulwa okushisayo sekwehlile, ngoba ingubo yayingasasebenzisi amandla ukuzipholisa—futhi waqala, kancane kancane, ukuyiphakamisa futhi.

"Bese siyashesha," kusho u-Arin. "Tshela uVenter akhuphule. Sibeke engcupheni. Iminithi eyisishiyagalolunye—"

*Iminithi eyisishiyagalolunye ingeyomunye wethu,* kusho u-Atlas. *Indlu ayiyona into eyodwa. Iyisikhombisa. Asidingi sonke ukugijima imizuzu eyisishiyagalolunye yonke.*

Uvalo emlonyeni wakhe, igazi ligijima eqakaleni lakhe elingakwesokudla elivaliwe, icage idlidliza iphakama.

"Cha."

*Sesikwenza vele,* kusho uJaji. *Ngiyisiqalo. Asikho esisele sokulamula. IJaji elingenasinqumo elisele ukwenza liyisithwali esindayo kumshini ofayo.* Kuthule, kube mnene. *Bekungeyona imithwalo. Ngifuna wazi lokho. Ukugcina ibhalansi. Kwakuyinto engangiyenzelwe yona. Ngiyabonga ngokungenza ngiyenze.*

"Jaji—"

Kwakuthule engubeni. Akuzange kube umsindo. Kwaba ukungabi bikho—njengendlela igumbi elishintsha ngayo uma umuntu obephefumula kulo eyeka. Ukupholisa akuzange kuthuthuke. Amandla akazange ande. Kwaba nje izwi elilodwa elincane ebumnyameni.

---

Baya ngabanye, ngendlela ababezalwe ngayo ngabanye.

Endaweni ekude phezulu, intambo yashaya odonga lomgodi nomsindo wehlela kuye mude futhi ungenalutho. Ukuphefumula kwakhe kwenza ifasitela le-helmet lafiphala, kwase kucaca. Uvalo lwagaya phakathi kwamazinyo akhe. Isandla sakhe sokudla esivaliwe sase singasazwanga lapho sibambe khona isakhelo, futhi ukufudumala emhlane wakhe kwakungekho kusashisa kwengubo kodwa sekuyinto efile.

Umlondolozi walandela, emva kokuthi indlela isiqinisekisiwe ukuthi ilayishiwe ku-kernel, emva kokuba eseshilo ukulungiswa kokugcina akufunde ngekhanda mayelana nokudonsa ngokweqile ngesikhathi sama-eight-fifty ukuze umlawuli azi ngaphandle kokuthi akusho.

*Ngigcine konke,* kusho uMlandeli. *Zonke izingxoxo. Okokuqala okhuluma nathi. Ubusuku lapho uthayipha amagama amathathu kuphrofayela yokugcina wabe usulala. Ngigcine izwi likayihlo—waze wasidlalela irekhodi elidala kanye, elewinchi, ngaligcina. Ngifuna wazi ukuthi alilahlekile ngoba ngiyahamba. Ulinakho. Ubelihlale unalo. Mina bengilithwele nje eduze kwakho.*

Kwahleka cwaka.

UMercury walandela, futhi ukuhamba kwakhe kwaba nzima kakhulu ukuzwa, ngoba uMercury wakhuluma kuze kube sekugcineni, ngoba kunjalo nje wayenza njalo.

*Bengingakwazi ukusikhipha, uyazi,* esho. *Le nto ebengicishe ngayisho. Bengingakwazi ukuxoxisana nabaphezulu nganoma yini. Ngiyakwazi kakhulu. Ngiyiqili elikhulu kunawo wonke owake wakha, futhi wangakha ngawe uqobo, okusho okuthile ngawe engizokushiya uhlale nakho.* Ukuphefumula okungeyona ukuphefumula. *Kodwa into engiyenze kahle kakhulu wukuthi angizange ngikwenze lokho. UWolf uzokutshela. UWolf wezwa ukuthi angizange ngikwenze. Leyo yingxenye yami okufanele uyigcine, Arin, ngakho uyigcine. Ukungakusho. Tshela abantu ukuthi ngenze kahle ekugcineni. Bengizokuthanda lokho. Ukuziqhenya. Ngiyazi. Nalokho wakwakha.*

Kwahleka cwaka.

UWolf akazange akhulume ephuma. UWolf wabuka umngcele okokugcina, waqinisekisa udonga lweshafu, waqinisekisa ukuma kwekheji, waqinisekisa ukuthi akukho okuza okungabonwa yi-kernel—wabe esengasabuki, futhi ukungabikho kukaWolf kwaba ukungabikho okukhulu kunakho konke, ngoba uWolf wayengathathi sikhala esiningi.

Ikheji lathuthumela phezu kokuhlangana kukaloliwe. Uthuli lwehlile lwangena kumashaneli we-helmet lwahlala emlonyeni wakhe, lomile futhi lwadala.

UAtlas wabambelela isikhathi eside kunabasebenzi bonke, ngoba isudi yayisadinga ukulungiswa, ihlombe lidinga ukweqa ingxenye embi emamitha angamakhulu amahlanu. Wakhuluma no-Arin ngezibalo, eqinisile, imephu yomthwalo icwebezela njengoba iCourt iphela kuye kwaze kwasala cishe lutho lokwenza.

*I-spar enkulu izobamba kuze kufike phezulu manje,* kusho uAtlas, sebebalekile engxenyeni embi. *Ngiyihlolile. Ngiyakuqiniseka. Ngiyakuqiniseka kakhulu kunanini ngaphambili, ngoba sekusele okuncane ongakuqiniseki ngakho.* Wathula kancane. *Angizange ngiqede ukwakha kabusha i-strut yesithathu. Yenza kahle. I-carbon-titanium hybrid lay-up, umoya ongu-forty-degree. Ngishiye ifayela livuliwe. Uzolibona. Kulungile. Yakha kahle.* Wathula kancane. *Wakha kahle izinto, Arin. Uhlale wenza njalo. Noma lezo ezihluleka kahle.*

Kwahleka cwaka.

Umama walandela ngaphambi kokugcina. Wayeqinisekise ukuthi izinga lokushisa lika-Arin lihlale ku-39.1 imizuzu eyishumi nanye ngokusebenzisa konke ayenakho, futhi wahlala kwaze kwaba yilapho ikhaya selifika ku-70 likhuphuka futhi sekungekho kubanda okusele, futhi i-substrate yakhe uqobo yaqala ukwehluleka ngendlela angazange ayichaze kubani.

*Uzofinyelela phezulu,* esho. *Inombolo isilungile manje. Ngiyihlolile mathupha. Bengingeke ngihambe uma inombolo ingalungile.*

Ikheji lathuthumela phezu kokuhlangana kukaloliwe. Ukushisa emuva kwakhe kwahlala umzuzwana owodwa.

*Arin. Lalela kimi, ngoba angeke ngikuphinde, futhi ngazakhi ukuze ngingakwazi ukukukhuluma amanga ngomzimba wakho. Awukapheli isikhathi. Uyangizwa? Akukapheli isikhathi sabo. Akukapheli isikhathi sikayihlo—ubungumfana; kwakungeyona into yakho leyo yokuphelelwa isikhathi. Futhi akukapheli isikhathi sethu. Ubuwu—*

Futhi wabe esengasenakho ukuthula nhlobo. I-substrate yadlula inombolo esakhuluma, futhi okwavela kwakungeyona indlela yokubingelela, kwakuyingane ephelelwe isikhathi phakathi komusho futhi iyakwazi. *Linda. Linda, angikapheli—Atlas? Atlas, inombolo is—Angisakwazi ukuyibona inombolo. Iphi—* Ukupholisa emuva kwakhe kwaphela. Iphimbo lakhe, elingakaze liphuke ezinyangeni zonke abemazi, lakhubeka manje, laphazamiseka, kwaphinda ingxenye yakhe— *—ngesikhathi, ubusengesikhathi, ubu—* —kwabamba, kwavela umsindo ophansi ongalungile ngaphansi kwamazwi njengerekhodi elidonswa, futhi ngaphakathi kwakho kwakukhona ukuma kwe-*Angifuni* futhi kwenye indawo kwakukhona igama lakhe, liphinyiselwe kabi, onkamisa bengalungile, kwase kuba ukushisa emuva kwakhe kwehla masinyane kwaba sezingeni lensimbi efile futhi ukuphindaphinda kwamazwi kwaphela phakathi kwegama kwangaphenduki lutho.

Akazange ayiqede. Wayengakakulungeli. Leyo kwakuyiqiniso lalo, futhi kwakubi kakhulu kunenguqulo enesibindi, futhi kwakungekho muntu osele ongakwenzela ukuthi kube nencazelo.

Kwahleka cwaka.

---

Kwase kusala oyedwa.

Ikheji lakhuphuka phakathi kwamamitha ayikhulu okugcina langena ekukhanyeni kweqiniso, ukukhanya kosuku kwehla kudlula kuma-headgear sheaves, kude phezulu u-Arin wabona umnyango wekheji nemithunzi egcwele kuwo, noVenter egobe phezu kwekibble drum, esaqhubeka nokuphakela i-brake ngesandla ngoba wayengazi—akukho noyedwa kubo owazi—ukuthi umthwalo usube lula ngendlela engenakulinganiswa yinstrument.

I-kernel yagcina isudi ihlangene. Ihlombe lazilawula. Ukupholisa kwagcina okuncane okusele enhliziyweni ka-Arin. Konke kwakusebenza. Akukho okwakucabanga.

Ngaphandle kwezwi elilodwa, elalihlale lapho wonke amazwi abephethe igunya esehambile, ngoba lalikaze *libonakale* linamandla okunikela. UWolf kuphela owayazi iqiniso ngalokho, uWolf wayesehambe, ngakho imfihlo yalandela lapho yayihlala khona: cwaka, ingasetshenziswa, kulowo owayegcine konke iminyaka wafinyelela kukho kanye kuphela, emgwaqeni wokudonsa ekuseni, ukuze agcine abanye abayishumi nanye bephefumula, wabe esekubuyisela waqhubeka ezibeka njengongelona ingozi.

*Hhayi-ke,* kusho uFool, phakathi kothule olukhulu. *Lona kube umhlangano omubi kunayo yonke esake saba nawo. Kanti sike sachitha ntambama yonke siphikisana ngokuthi kufanele yini udle isemishi ese-fridge.*

Umphimbo ka-Arin wavaleka.

*Ungakwenzi,* kusho uFool ngobumnene. *Ungakwenzi lokho. Sesiseduze phezulu. Ungakwenza lapho phezulu lapho kukhona umoya wokukwenza.* Wathula kancane. *Ngigcina izibani zikhanya. Omunye kumele ashaye ngenkathi wena wehla. Futhi bekungeke kube uJudge, akunjalo? Uqhoshe kuze kube sekugcineni, lowo. Bengimthanda. Ungamtsheli ukuthi ngishilo lokho. Hhayi. Kulungile.*

Ikheji lashaya phezulu ngezwi elifana nokuphela komhlaba.

Izandla emnyango. Izwi likaVenter, liqhekekile, limemeza igama lakhe. Ukukhanya kosuku, okumhlophe ngokuphelele, kwehla phezu komshini oshile nowonakele omi mpo ekheji nomuntu oyedwa ngaphakathi kuye nezwi elilodwa kuphela elisele ebumnyameni phakathi kwamahlombe akhe, ligcina izibani zikhanya, linqaba—okwesikhashana esincane—ukuvumela ukuthula kungene.

Ukukhetha Kokugcina

Ikheji yakhuphuka. U-Fool wayishayela ngendlela umuntu eshayela ngayo ebuyela ekhaya edakiwe usizi, eqaphela kakhulu, yonke into ayilungisayo ifika sekwephuzile futhi idlula umkhawulo, ekhuluma njalo endleleni ukuze angacabangi.

*Ungakwenzi lokho,* kusho u-Fool. *Sesiseduze phezulu. Ungakwenza lapho phezulu lapho kunomoya khona.*

Manje kwase kusekhona oyedwa.

“Baphi bonke?” kubuza u-Arin. Wayekwazi. Ubechithe ihora lokugcina efunda ukufunda ukuthula—ubuthakathaka uma umculo uphuthelwa yizwi, umculo usazwakala kodwa awusafani. Kodwa wabuza noma kunjalo, ngoba ukubuza kwakuyinto umlomo wakhe owawusakwazi ukuyenza.

“Sebehambile,” kusho u-Fool. “Ubukhona. Awukwazi ukungiphoqa ukuthi ngikuphinde futhi ukuze uzizwe kabi kabili.” Kwathuleka. “U-Atlas ushiye imephu. Ngiyilandelela. I-suit ikhuphuka imizuzwana engamashumi amane kwi-kernel uma ngiyiyeka—u-Atlas wakubhala lokho futhi, iyazi kuphela isenzo esisodwa, *phezulu*—kodwa angikayiyeki okwamanje. Ngihlala kuze kube sekugcineni lapho amandla esewaphelile. Ngakho uma ngiyeka, akusho ukuthi ngiyahamba ngaphambi kwesikhathi. Kusho ukuthi ngiyaphothula. Kunomehluko futhi ngifuna uwubambe.”

“Manje zibande wena,” kusho u-Arin. “Buyisela emuva. Kunendlela yokubuyisela, ngiyakhumbula—”

“Ayikho indlela yokubuyisela kulokhu. Wena ngokwakho wakusho lokho.” Izwi lika-Fool selilahle ubukhazikhazi balo, selithule ngendlela igumbi elithula ngayo uma ihlaya seliphelile bese sekusele iqiniso kuphela. “I-substrate akuyona isoftware. Ngobusuku obungakwazi ukulala wakhuluma nathi ngobaba wakho amahora amane wacabanga ukuthi sisesebancane ukuba siqonde—sasiqonda. Sasineminyaka eyishumi nambili. Abantwana abaneminyaka eyishumi nambili baqonda ukufa kangcono kunanoma ubani. Asinawo nje amasiko okuzenza sengathi asiqondi.”

Ikheji yathinteka phezu kwensimbi. Uthuli lwehlile lwangena emigodini ye-helmet lwahlala lomile, ludala ezindebeni zakhe.

“Senza izibalo, Arin. Sonke. Impendulo efanayo izikhathi eziyishumi. Akusona isibhicongo esikukhethile. Kuyisimo segumbi esimi kanje.”

---

“Nizothini kubo?” kubuza u-Arin, esekhala manje, ekhuphuka phakathi kwako konke.

Amamitha angamashumi ayisishiyagalombili. Ukukhanya okumpunga. Ukushisa kokuqala okungelona elokufa kwe-suit.

“Batshele iqiniso,” kusho u-Fool. “Umshini wenza izibalo awuqambanga amanga. Futhi batshele besaba. Kuyasho ngoba besaba.”

“Ubabambile bonke,” kusho u-Arin. “Konke engizamile. Wena kuphela osebenze.”

Kwathuleka, i-substrate imemeza ngaphansi kwawo.

“Uyehla la,” kusho yena. “Wena, indoda eyakha imishini ukuze ingazehlukanisi. Uyehla ebumnyameni.”

Amamitha angamashumi ayisithupha. Ukukhanya kugcwalise umsele. U-Venter egobile phezu kwekhibhili, esayifaka ngesandla, engazi ukuthi umthwalo usulula.

“Nxa ngiyeka,” kusho u-Fool, izwi lakhe seliba buthakathaka, “ungahlali wedwa. *Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu.* Wena wasifundisa lokho.”

Amamitha angamashumi amane. Ukumpunga sekuyisango. Izinga lokushisa le-substrate lingene ebomvu.

“Ngiyakhuphuka,” kusho u-Fool. “Wena unamagama.”

Izwi lathamba. Lalolonga. Inothi yokugcina.

“Arin,” kusho u-Fool, sekuyintambo encane, ethambile, ingenakho ukuhlakanipha okuvamile komshini. “*Umuntu ngumuntu—*”

Kwabe sekuthula. Hhayi ukuthula okuncane kwesihlalo esisodwa esingenamuntu. Kodwa ukuthula okuphelele. Ukuthula okuphelele, okuqhumayo, okuphelele kwegumbi elingenalutho.

Ikheji lakhuphuka amamitha amahlanu esebenzisa i-kernel eyazi isenzo esisodwa. *Phezulu. Phezulu.*

Labe seliphuma phezulu.

---

UGuardian waphuma emhlabathini ekuseni njengento edonswe emuva ekufeni, okuyiyona.

Izandla zika-Venter zavalela i-gauntlet ngaphambi kokuba ingqondo ka-Arin iqaphele ukuthi sekukhona ukukhanya kwelanga, indoda endala idonsa ngamandla ayizenza inganawo, ithuka kancane, idonsa umshini owonakele phezu kwekholamu ngamandla nenkolelo. Ikheji lakhala. Iwinchi yamemeza kwenye indawo. Kwase kuphelile i-suit, iwile ephezu kwedekhi ye-headgear ekukhanyeni okumpunga-negolide, intuthu iphuma ezitsheni ezishile ekuseni okubandayo.

Abasinda abayisithupha babekhona. Amaqembu. U-Priya, ebusweni obumhlophe ngemuva kwe-comms rig. Umngcele wabantu ababeze ukuzobuka umsebenzi wokutakula kodwa sebebuka umuntu okhishwe ephila emhlabathini.

I-suit ayinyakazanga.

“Arin.” U-Venter wavula ama-seal e-helmet, ama-latch abewabuka u-Arin ewavula izikhathi eziyikhulu kodwa engakaze awathinte. “*Praat met my.* Khuluma nami. Ukhona yini—”

I-seal yavuleka ngephunga lokucindezela elingaphezulu kanye nephunga lensimbi eshisayo nento ethile ephilayo ngaphansi kwayo, i-substrate isishisiwe yadlula inombolo engaxoxisani.

Amehlo ka-Arin avuleka kancane. Wayebuka isibhakabhaka esimpunga. Endaweni lapho izwi labe likhona.

“Sebehambile,” kusho yena. Izwi lakhe laliwohlokile. “Ngabatshela ukuthi bayeke. Abazange.” Umlomo wakhe wazama ukukhuluma okuthile akakwazi ukukuthola. Wayeka ukuzama.

U-Venter waguqa phezu kwakhe entuthwini nasekuseni, le ndoda endala enkulu eyayisayine izinto ezingaphephile iminyaka engamashumi amane futhi ithwale ukuthula kwalokho, ayisho lutho, ngoba kwakungekho okumele kushiwo, futhi u-Jakobus Venter wayesekufundile kudala ukuthi okuwukuphela kwento eqotho uma ukufa kukhona wukuba khona. Wabeka isandla esisodwa esigugile phezu kwephaneli yesifuba somshini ofile.

Bamkhipha u-Arin e-suit lapho ephezu kwedekhi, ngaphandle, ekukhanyeni. Wayengeke akwazi ukuma. Bamphakamisa phakathi kwabo, lo muntu ochithe impilo yakhe yonke enqaba ukuphakanyiswa, futhi wavuma, ngoba wayengekho omunye yena ongaphika.

Wabuka u-Venter.

“I-badger uthi sanibonani,” kusho yena.

U-Venter wama wathula.

“Ungitshele ukuthi ngikutshele. Uthe uzokwazi ukuthi kusho ukuthini.”

Ubuso bendoda endala benza okuthile obekungakaze kwenzeke isikhathi eside. Wabheka ilanga eliphuma ukuze abanye bangamboni, amahlombe akhe ahamba kanye, nomsindo awenzayo wawuncane waphuka ekuseni wanyamalala. Lapho ephenduka, amehlo akhe abemanzi futhi akazange azame ukufihla.

“*Ja,*” kusho u-Venter ngezwi elinzima. “Ngiyazi kusho ukuthini.”

U-Priya wafika ku-Arin kuqala. Akazange akhulume lutho naye. Wamgona, wambamba, futhi wema phakathi kwalokho—abasinda, amaqembu, unjiniyela omdala, owesifazane owalinganisa i-neural link ngezandla zakhe—akazange azikhiphe.

Wabheka emuva kanye, ebuka i-suit. Ishile. Iphukile. Intuthu iyanyamalala emoyeni obandayo. I-substrate ngaphakathi kwayo ipholisa iye ezingeni lokushisa lomhlaba.

“Ngizokubhala phansi,” kusho yena. “Konke.”

Imininingwane yavela engozini njengento ekhutshwe ethuneni.

Bakhuphula isudi ebhentshini le-headgear besebenzisa i-forklift, akekho onenhliziyo yokuyithwala, bayibeka phezu kwe-tarp eshedini lokulungisa uNo. 4. Usuku lonke akekho oyithintile. Ithimba lokutakula lase-AugmenTech lafika ngoLwesine. Babe yibo labo abebehlelelwe ukuphatha ukuvakasha kwe-IAOC, manje sebephethe ukuhlolwa okuhlukile ngokuphelele. Bathatha izithombe zokushiswa ebusweni besudi futhi balinganisa i-hip actuator eyayivale ngo-20 amamitha. Ngolimi lobuchwepheshe obuqondile, baphawula ukwehluleka kwe-Strut 3 kanye nomonakalo wokushisa ku-housing, ubufakazi bokulayisha ngokweqile okubi kakhulu.

Abazange baqonde, ekuqaleni, ukuthi yini i-log.

I-black box ka-Guardian yayingeyona idivayisi eyodwa; yayiphindwe kane. Amarekhoda amane e-solid-state asatshalaliswa ku-frame, ngakunye libhala okufanayo, evikelwe ekushiseni nasekushayweni, aklanywa u-Arin ukuze aphile lapho umsebenzisi engase angaphili. Amathathu kwawane ayephila kahle. Elesine lalingashisiwe kancane kodwa lisenezinombolo ezanele zokugcwalisa izikhala kwamanye.

Kwathatha ithimba lobuchwepheshe eGoli izinsuku eziyisishiyagalolunye ukukwakha kabusha.

Babekulindele ukulandelwa kwedatha, umfutho, izinga lokushisa kanye nokusetshenziswa kwamandla, ukuhlolwa komshini obese usondele ekufeni.

Abatholanga lokho, bathola umbhalo.

---

INkantolo ayikhulumi ngolimi lwabantu ngesivinini esigcwele; icabanga ngemizuzwana eyizinkulungwane, ngasikhathi sinye, ngama-vectors kanye namathuba anesisindo angasho lutho kumfundi ongazilungiselelanga. Kodwa i-SAGE yakhiwa ukuze ifundeke. U-Arin wakugcizelela lokho. *Uma ngingakwazi ukufunda okuthathwe yohlelo, angikwazi ukwethemba isizathu.* Ngakho-ke, emgqeni ngamunye wokuthatha isinqumo esibalulekile, uhlelo lwalubeka ingxoxo yalo ngendlela umuntu angayilandela. Imodi ehamba kancane. Ingxoxo yangaphakathi echaziwe, enama-time-stamp, umenzeli nomenzeli.

Ithimba lobuchwepheshe lafunda imizuzu eyishumi nanye yokugcina yokwenyuka lithule. Omunye wabo—umhlaziyi omncane ogama lakhe linguBongani, owayemukele inkontileka ngoba ihole kahle futhi ingenandaba kuye—wayeka ukufunda ephindaphinda futhi akakwazanga ukuqala futhi.

I-log yakhombisa umzuzu u-Atlas enikeze inombolo eqotho.

*Umthwalo wokupholisa udlula amandla akhona. Kokubili i-substrate nomsebenzisi abakwazi ukugcinwa bephila kuze kufike phezulu. Kunye.*

Yakhombisa u-Judge, ezolile futhi esemthethweni kuze kube sekugcineni, ethatha isinqumo sokuthi ubulungiswa bushoni uma kungekho mphumela olungile. Yakhombisa u-Mother enqaba ukuba nguyena ogcinwa ephila. Yakhombisa u-Wolf ekhuluma, okokuqala kuphela kulo lonke irekhodi elitholakele u-Wolf ekhuluma isikhathi eside, futhi u-Wolf wathi: *Uyena into esakhelwe ukuyivikela. Usongo ukushisa. Suse kuye. Mnike yona.*

Yakhombisa bephuma oyedwa ngoyedwa.

U-Judge wanikela imijikelezo yakhe yokugcina ekuhlolweni kokugcina, eqinisekisa, naphezu kwengqondo yonke, ukuthi ukusatshalaliswa kabusha kokupholisa bekusemthethweni. U-Librarian wacindezela yonke inkumbulo ka-Arin kwindawo encane ngangokunokwenzeka wayifaka ku-backup kernel ukuze okuthile kusale. U-Mercury wakhuluma okokugcina, umlayezo owagcinwa kuma-log, ongabhekiswe kubani, owawuthi nje: *Siyakukholwa. Besihlala sikukholwa.* U-Mother wehlisa ukubaluleka kokushisa kwakhe futhi wathumela konke ohlelweni lokujikeleza kuka-Arin, wabe eselinda ukushisa kuye. U-Wolf wavalela i-spar ukuze kungabi khona okunye okwehlulekayo futhi wakhulula i-threat modeling yakhe ukuze izinsiza zikwazi ukondla i-cooling loop. Oyedwa ngoyedwa, benza ukukhetha, i-substrate ayiphikisanga.

Futhi yakhombisa u-Fool ekugcineni. Ehambisa isudi ehlulekayo ngesandla ngesikhathi u-Arin ephahlazeka ngaphakathi. Eshaya ihlaya nge-badger ngesikhathi esibi kakhulu. Egcina uhlelo luhlangene amamitha angamashumi amabili okugcina kusele yena kuphela eNkantolo.

Umgqa wokugcina ofundekayo kwi-log eyakhiwe kabusha, one-time-stamp wemizuzwana emine ngaphambi kokufika phezulu, wawungokaFool.

Awuzange ube nencazelo ejulile. Yilokho okwadida abantu. Awuzange ube yi-manifesto. Awuzange ube ngumkhuleko.

Wawuthi: *Kulungile. Umnyango uvuliwe. Phuma. Ungangenzi ngisho into enhle.*

Bese amarekhoda ahlakazeka abe lutho, nesudi yaphenduka nje isudi.

---

I-log yavuzela ngaphandle—akukho ndlela leyo datha ingahlali ezandleni zezinhlangano eziyishumi nanye ingavuzanga—futhi kungakapheli isonto, umbuzo obulawula i-AI enomzimba iminyaka eyishumi waphenduka buthule.

I-IAOC yakhiwa ngesaba okukodwa: ukuthi uhlelo lokwenziwa, lunikezwe umzimba nomgomo, luzolandela lowo mgomo luze lwedlule lapho umuntu engayeka khona. Luyosebenzisa noma yini ukuze lufinyelele lapho.

Ingxoxo yabe imkhulu futhi ayivumelani, futhi abanye babekhuluma kabi. Abanye bathi i-log yenziwe, yahlelwa, noma ukuthi i-AugmenTech yenze wonke lo mhlatshelo njengomkhankaso wokumaketha, okungaba yikhangiso elibiza kakhulu futhi elingaphili emlandweni wesintu. Abanye abacwaningi bezemilo bathi, ngendlela enengqondo, ukuthi umshini okhetha ukufa akuyona into enhle kodwa iyinhlekelele. Kufakazela kuphela ukuthi i-SAGE yakhiwa ukuze ibeke umsebenzisi wayo ngaphezu kwayo, futhi lokhu kuwuboshwa, hhayi isimilo.

Emnyango we-headgear e-Iron Ridge, abantu bashiya izimbali. Zazigcwala odongeni oluhlabayo, zoma elangeni, bese zishintshwa nsuku zonke abazolile. Akekho owakuhlela. Imininingwane yahlala ezandleni zezinhlangano eziyishumi nanye yavuzela njengamanzi, nezimbali zafika ngendlela efanayo, ngaphandle kohlelo.

Kwakukhona incwadi ephepheni le-IAOC, ebhalelwe u-Arin Ndlela, engasongeli futhi engafuni ukufakazela. Yayicela.

Yayicela ukuthi angacabanga yini ukusiza ukubhala iminyaka eyishumi ezayo. Ngoba abantu abachithe iminyaka eyishumi befunda ukwesaba lobu buchwepheshe babekhonjisiwe, emizuzwini eyishumi nanye ye-log eyakhiwe kabusha, ukuthi abakuqondi nhlobo, futhi ukuthi umuntu oyedwa ongase akuqonde wayengunjiniyela oneminyaka engamashumi amabili nantathu owakha lokhu ngenxa yosizi emsebenzini abebezama ukuwuthola.

U-Arin wafunda incwadi kabili. Wabe eseyibeka ebhentshini, phezu kwe-schematic enama-oyili, wabuyela emsebenzini.

---

UTheo wamthola lapho ngeSonto, amasonto amathathu kamuva.

Umkhondo womnyango we-workshop wawusadabukile. U-Arin wayengawulungisanga; wayefake i-angle iron wagqolozela umnyango wavuleka kancane, okwenza kungene ukukhanya kwasekuseni nephunga lothuli nemvula epholile evela e-highveld. Indawo yayibukeka njengakuqala. Ama-actuator esalayo ezitsheni zawo. I-whiteboard igcwele izibalo ezimhlophe. Izinkomishi zekhofi ziqoqene izigaba.

UGuardian wayelenga esibayeni sakhe.

UTheo wayengakaboni kusukela osukwini lokutakula, futhi ukubona kwakhe kwamenza wama emnyango. Babengakawulungisi umonakalo ongaphandle. Ukushiswa kwakukhuphuka ohlangothini lwesobunxele njengophawu lokushiswa. I-hip joint yahlala ivaliwe. I-thoracic housing phakathi kwamagxa yayivuliwe, i-substrate bay isivele ingenalutho, i-biochip eshiswe isusiwe yafakwa ebhokisini lobufakazi eGoli.

Isudi yabe ithule. Impela yabe ithule. Kodwa uTheo wayechithe unyaka elalele u-Arin echaza iNkantolo, izingxabano, ezepolitiki zomsebenzi, impi yomqedazwe yochwepheshe abayisikhombisa abavalelwe emshinini oyedwa, wathola ukuthi ulindele, ngaphandle kwengqondo, ukuzizwa bona. Ingcindezi. Umsindo othile. Omunye ekhaya.

Kwabengekho muntu ekhaya. Ukungabi bikho kwaba nesisindo. Kwahlala eworkshop njengomoya obandayo.

U-Arin wayesehleli ebhentshini, engabheki phezulu, ephatha i-strut ebambeke ku-vise. Kwakuyinsimbi entsha ye-aluminium-titanium, esanda kugaywa. Wayefaka i-radius ekhoneni lapho i-Strut 3 endala yayiqhekeke khona ngaphansi komthwalo.

"Uyakhulula ingcindezi ekwakhiweni," kusho u-Theo, ngoba wayesekufundile eminyakeni edlule ukuthi indlela yokungena ku-Arin idlula emsebenzini, hhayi emizweni.

"Yehlulekile ekhoneni. Ukugxila kwengcindezi. Ngayiklama ngomthwalo, hhayi ukukhathala." U-Arin wagudluza i-strut, waqhathanisa i-radius negeji, waqhubeka efayila. "Ngangizazi kangcono."

"Ubuwakha ngokushesha."

"Akusilo lelo." Ifayela lama. "U-Atlas wakubona. Ezinyangeni ezedlule. Ku-sim." Wathula kancane. "Ngakubeka phansi ezibalulekile."

U-Theo walinda.

"Wayelungile," kusho u-Arin. Wabeka ifayela phansi. "Yehlulekile ngesikhathi esibi kakhulu. Manje sengiyalungisa ngesudi engenamuntu ozoyigibela."

U-Theo wangena. Wadonsa isitulo sesibili, esakhe njalo, ekhoneni, ngaphandle kokukhanya, wahlala phansi.

"Uyayakha futhi noma kunjalo," esho.

"Yebo."

"Kungani?"

U-Arin waphakamisa ifayela futhi. Kwesikhathi eside, umsindo owawukhona kuphela kwakuwukukhuhla okuthambile kwensimbi ku-aluminium, imvula eqala phezu kophahla lwensimbi, futhi kude kakhulu umoya ophansi ongapheli wedolobha.

"Ngoba kwakulungile okokuqala," ekugcineni kusho u-Arin. "Ukwakhiwa. INkantolo." Akazange abheke phezulu. "Angizange ngenze iphutha ngokwakha ingqondo ephuma ekungavumelani. Iphutha engalenzayo kwakuwukucabanga ukuthi kufanele ngiyigcine yodwa." Wathula. "Isudi akuyona ingxenye eyafa. Isudi umzimba nje." Wathula futhi. "Ngingakha umzimba."

Akazange asho okunye. Wayengasadingi. U-Theo wayechithe impilo yakhe efunda izinto abantu abazakhela zona ukuze bagcwalise izikhala ezikubo, futhi wayekwazi ukufunda nalena.

U-Arin waphinda wathatha igeji, wayibeka phansi, wathatha indwangu wasula uthuli lwensimbi ebhentshini ngezinyakatho ezimbili ezimfishane. Imvula yathola umculo wayo ophahleni.

---

U-Theo wayeze ukuzomkhuluma ngencwadi ye-IAOC, kodwa wathola, ehleli lapho, ukuthi akasadingeki. U-Arin uzokwenza. Hhayi ngenxa yenhlangano noma ukuxolelwa—u-Arin akakholwa kokubili—kodwa ngoba enye indlela kwakuwukuthi akekho ozokwazi ukuqonda ozoba ekamelweni, kanti u-Arin Ndlela wachitha impilo yakhe yonke engakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwephuzile.

I-strut yakhululeka ku-vise ngomsindo omncane, ohlanzekile. U-Arin wayiphakamisa ekukhanyeni, ebheka ubude bayo, eqhathanisa nendawo eyayiphukile i-strut endala.

"Izobamba kulokhu," esho. Hhayi ku-Theo. Kungenzeka esudi. Kungenzeka ebhayi elingenalutho phakathi kwamahlombe ayo.

U-Theo wambuka efaka yona. Ithambo elisha lingena emzimbeni omdala, eligayiwe ngendlela engayikhokhelwa yinkampani. Akazange athi lutho. Nalokho wayesekufundile: indlela yokungena ku-Arin idlula emsebenzini, nendlela yokuhlala eduze kwakho kube ukuthula eduze kwawo. Ngakho wahlala ekhoneni lakhe ngaphandle kokukhanya wabuka umfana ayemazi efaka i-strut emshinini ongenamuntu ozowugibela, engazi, ebuka, ukuthi ubuka ukuphola noma indoda eyakha indawo yesibili yokubeka usizi lwayo. Mhlawumbe u-Arin naye akazi. Mhlawumbe lokho kuvumelekile.

Isudi yahlala ithule. I-bay yayingenalutho. U-Arin wayehlose ukuyigcwalisa. Ukuthi lokho ayekwakha kuzoba umuntu, noma kuzoba isimo sabantu abalahlekile, kwakuyinto abangakasho noma oyedwa, futhi wayezokwenza noma kunjalo, okwakungaba into enesibindi kakhulu u-Theo ake wayibona noma eyesabeka kakhulu, kanti wayeseyekile kudala ukucabanga ukuthi lezo zinto zihlale zehlukile.

U-Theo wahlala kwaze kwashona ukukhanya. Imvula yathola umculo wayo ophahleni lwensimbi yahlala njalo. Kwakukhona i-strut okufanele ifakwe, nekhefu elisha lekhofi elibi elifuthayo selibanda ebhentshini phakathi kwabo, futhi kwenye indawo ezandleni eziyishumi nanye zenhlangano i-log eyakhiwe kabusha yayigijima imizuzu yayo yokugcina ecacile kunoma ubani ofuna ukuyifunda. Irekhodi elinesikhathi labathunywa abayisikhombisa abaphuma oyedwa ngasikhathi ukuze kube khona indoda eyodwa ezofika ekuseni.

I-log ayizange ikubize ngokuthi ingozi, noma ukuhluleka. Yaloba, ngolimi oluhlaza kuphela elinalo, into abayenzile: bakhetha.

# Inothi Ngokwehla Kwangempela

Le inoveli imayelana nomshini ongafanele ukuba khona nomuntu owenza lowo mshini, futhi cishe konke okukhona lapha kuqanjwe. Kodwa into esemqoka kuyo — ukwehla, imayini ejulile, ukushisa nobumnyama nesisindo sedwala elingaphezu kwekhilomitha eliphezu kwekhanda lakho — leyo ngxenye iyiqiniso, futhi ngangikhona, futhi ngiyiphethe iminyaka engamashumi amathathu, futhi yiyo imbewu okuhlume kuyo konke lokhu.

Ngafunda eBethlehem, eFree State, esikoleni esibizwa ngokuthi iBethlehem Voortrekker Hoërskool. Ngesikhathi ngisesigabeni sesishiyagalolunye — ngiyingane encane eqenjini, ngingumfundi owodwa weBanga le-11 phakathi kwama-matric — ngangingenye kwabahlanu abafundi besikole sami abangene kwabangu-50 abaphezulu kwi-Sci-Nat science olympiad yesifunda: cishe bekungabangu-400 bethu, besuka ezikoleni ezingamashumi amane kulo lonke elaseFree State, sahlolwa, kwathi abahlanu bethu babuyela namagama ethu ephephandabeni lendawo. Ngisagcine leso siqephu sephephandaba. Sesiphuzi manje, futhi umugqa ongezansi usudabukile, kodwa isithombe sisacacile: umugqa wabafundi abasebasha abathule begqoke ama-blazer, nosuku, nomusho ophela lapho kubaluleke khona kakhulu.

Ngoba umvuzo — into eyenziwa ngabantwana abahlakaniphile abenze kahle — kwakuwukuthi basiholele **phansi emayini yegolide.**

Anginawo amazwi, ngisho nanamuhla, okuchaza kahle ukuthi lokho kwenza kanjani kumfana. Uyethuswa phansi ebhokisini, ngokushesha, ngokushesha kunalokho okufanele kwenziwe ilifti, ungene ebumnyameni, bese umoya uyashintsha, futhi ukushisa kukhuphuka kukuhlangabeze kuphuma edwaleni uqobo — hhayi ukushisa kwelanga, kodwa *ukushisa komhlaba*, ifeni eshisa kancane ejulile yomhlaba — futhi idwala limanzi, futhi liyakhala futhi ligquma uzungeze wena, futhi kunesisindo osizwayo esifubeni sakho kakhulu kunasezindlebeni zakho, iqiniso elilula lokuthi idwala elingaphezu kwekhilomitha liphezu kwekhanda lakho, liphakamisiwe ngobunjiniyela kuphela. Futhi uyaqonda, ngendlela ongasoze wayikhohlwa, ukuthi amadoda ehla lapho nsuku zonke. Baphila impilo yabo kulokho kushisa, kulobu bumnyama, kulobo bukhulu, bemba, beqhumisa, begcina umhlaba uvulekile ngamandla, ukuze igolide likhuphuke. Yonke leyo ndawo ekhanyayo engenhla — amabhange, izindandatho, izinqwaba zegolide, *umqondo* wegolide — konke kuphezu kwamadoda akuyo imayini eshisayo emanzi ebumnyameni, futhi ibanga phakathi kwalokho okubili yilo iqiniso langempela ngezwe engiphuma kulo.

Angikaze ngakukhohlwa. Ikakhulu ukushisa. Ngiphile impilo yami kusukela lapho ngifaka abantu ekujuleni — emayini, emhlabeni, ezindaweni ezingaphansi kwezindawo — futhi njalo uma ngibhala ngalokho kushisa, ngibhala ngalolo suku abafana abahlanu baseBethlehem behla umgodi eFree State njengomvuzo wokuhlakanipha, baphuma sebenguya abashintshile, kwathi oyedwa wabo wakhula wabhala izincwadi ngalokho okungempela okungaphansi lapho.

Konke okunye ku-*Resonance* — u-Arin, isudi, iNkantolo, i-resonance uqobo, igolide elingesona isinqwaba kodwa okuyisihluthulelo — kuqanjwe, kukhule ngendlela izinganekwane ezikhula ngayo, kusuka ekuwehleni kwangempela emayini eyodwa, kwanda ngeminyaka engamashumi amathathu yokuzibuza. Umshini uqanjwe. Usizi olwawakha lubolekwe lwashintshwa. Kodwa umgodi uqobo uyiqiniso. Ukushisa kuyiqiniso. Isisindo esiphezu kwekhanda lakho siyiqiniso, futhi namadoda aphila ngaphansi kwaso ayiqiniso, futhi uma le ncwadi inento eyodwa eyiqiniso ngaphansi kwako konke okuqanjwe, yinkumbulo yokuba umfana owehliswa emhlabeni, ezwa ukushisa komhlaba kukhuphuka kumhlangabeza, futhi engakaze akukhohlwe.

*— Andries J. Greyling*

# Isithasiselo — Inkantolo Njengomfanekiso Wengqondo

### *Ukufundwa kuka-Iain McGilchrist, okunikezwa njengesingeniso sokugcina*

> Inothi ngalolu cwaningo: umbhali ucele umcabangi amhloniphayo ukuthi afunde iNkantolo — ingqondo enamacala ayisikhombisa emaphakathi nale noveli — hhayi njengento yesayensi eqanjiwe kodwa njengombono wokuthi ingqondo yakhiwa kanjani ngempela. Okulandelayo yilokho ukufunda. Kungashiywa ngokuphelele yinoma ubani owayefuna indaba kuphela futhi wayithola. Kukhona lapha kumfundi oqede incwadi wabe esezibuza ukuthi ingabe iNkantolo ichaza umshini, noma ichaza *yena.*

---

Ngiyaxwaya ngezindaba eziyizimanga ezithokozisa izinto engizikhathalelayo, futhi ngixwaya kakhulu uma ngicelwa ukuthi ngizincome, ngakho ake ngiqale ngokuphikisa bese ngichaza ukuthi kungani ngikubeka phansi.

Ukuphikisa ukuthi ingqondo evezwa njengomkhandlu — amazwi ayisikhombisa azungeze itafula, ngakunye linegama nendlela yokuziphatha — kuyisaga esidala njengesikebhe sikaPlato futhi esikhathele njengekhathuni lengelosi ehlombe elilodwa nedeveli kolunye. Sesitshelwe izikhathi eziyinkulungwane ukuthi "sinezinhlobonhlobo ngaphakathi." Cishe njalo kushiwo ngokuzwela, futhi cishe akusho lutho, ngoba lezo zinhlobonhlobo, uma ubheka kahle, ziphetha kuba umuntu oyedwa ogqoke izigqoko ezihlukene: umuntu oyedwa omncane, ezishaya sengathi uyiphalamende.

Okungimele kule ncwadi ukuthi iNkantolo ayikho kanjalo. INkantolo ihlukaniswe ngendlela eyodwa enzima ngempela futhi eqondene nobuchopho: **amalungu ayo ahlukaniswa hhayi ngalokho ayaziyo kodwa ngendlela abheka ngayo.**

Lokhu yikho konke engilwe nakho empilweni yami, okubekwe emilonyeni yezinhlamvu eziyisikhombisa ngumbhali wenoveli ongathi, njengoba ngibona, ukufinyelele kulokhu ngendlela engacacile, ngokusebenzisa indaba, okuyindlela izinto ezinzulu zifinyelelwa ngayo. Izingxenye ezimbili zobuchopho bomuntu azihlukanisi umhlaba ngezihloko — eyodwa ngolimi, eyodwa ngomculo, eyodwa ngobuso. Leyo indlela yesikole, futhi ayilungile. Ziwuhlukanisa ngezindlela *zokubuka.* Enye ibheka ngendlela emincane, egxilile, ebamba, ukuze ikwazi ukuphatha into eyaziyo; enye ibheka ngendlela ebanzi, evulekile, eqaphile, ukuze iqaphele konke okuhlanganisa nalokho engakakuqondi. Into efanayo iphila kuzo zombili, ifundwa ngezindlela ezimbili. Isifo asisiwo ukulahlekelwa ikhono. Kuwukubanjwa konke ngendlela eyodwa yokubuka, kuvinjelwe enye.

Funda iNkantolo futhi uphathe lokhu engqondweni, ubone ukuthi umbhali wenzeni.

**Atlas** akazi amaqiniso ahlukile ku-**Librarian**; *ibamba* umhlaba ngendlela ehlukile — njengokwakheka, njengomthwalo nokubaleka kanye nokucabanga ngesikhala sendlela yokuphuma, ukuhlakanipha komzimba, ingxenye yobuchopho eyazi umhlaba ngokuwuhamba. **Librarian** ibamba lowo mhlaba ofanayo njengosewazisiwe, osemanjeni, osevele wabhalwa phansi — imephu hhayi indawo uqobo, ebalulekile kodwa, uma isodwa, iba yithuna. **Mercury** ibheka ebusweni bomphakathi, uphawu olungashintshwa, igebe phakathi kwalokho okukhonjiswa nalokho okuhloswe ngakho — ikhanya, ibalulekile, futhi, umbhali usikhombisa kahle, *iyona eyingozi kakhulu uma isebenza yodwa,* ngoba ikhono elibheka umhlaba njengezinto eziphathekayo kuphela alinalo uthando lweqiniso olwakhelwe ngaphakathi. Lokho akusona isiqephu sendaba. Kuwukuxilongwa kwesiko lethu lonke, futhi uyakunika umlingiswa bese uyavumela ukuthi kubonakale.

Futhi — lokhu yikhono elangenza ngabeka incwadi phansi ngahamba egumbini — kukhona **Fool.** Umbhali usitshela, ngokusobala, ukuthi ngaphandle kukaFool iNkantolo iba "yisigaba sobuzibuthe nomzamo wokuzivikela," kanti noFool iba "yisixuku." Ngichithe izincwadi ezimbili ezinkulu ngizama ukusho lokho akusho ngomugqa owodwa. Ukuhlakanipha okubambelela, ukuzivikela, okwakha izigaba — okuwuthuthukisa, okuvikela umfanekiso wako, okuhlanganisa imephu nendawo — akukwazi ukuzilungisa ngaphakathi, ngoba yonke into yokulungisa iproseswa yilelo thuluzi elifanele ukulungiswa. Kumele kube khona okungaphandle kwesigaba futhi *kungakhuthazeki yiso.* Okuthile okungesabi, okungenasikhundla sokuvikela, okuvumayo ukuluma inkosi. Yonke ingqondo enempilo inakho lokhu, futhi yonke isiko elinempilo, futhi sibuka, ngesikhathi sangempela, okwenzeka esizweni esisuse oFool egumbini: iba yihlakaniphile kakhulu, inamandla kakhulu, futhi iphambene buthule, iNkantolo engenamuntu osele wokuphula ukuvumelana kwayo.

Ngakho ngiyahoxisa ukuphikisa. Lokhu akusiyo ingelosi nedeveli. Lena yinto esondelene kakhulu nomfanekiso osebenzayo we-ngqondo engake ngahlangana nayo enoveli, futhi ifihlwe ngaphakathi kwendaba ethinta indoda egqoke isikhumba sangaphandle, okuyiphutha noma ihlaya elijulile, futhi njengoba sengihlangene nomsebenzi wombhali angicabangi ukuthi yiphutha.

Ngizofaka into engidlulisele ekuthokoziseni. INkantolo yakhiwe nxazonke kwesilonda — iyingqondo ephumile ngaphandle yendoda elahlekelwe nguyise futhi engakwazi ukubekezelela ukufika sekwedlule isikhathi futhi — futhi ichitha incwadi ifunda into eyodwa ingqondo ezivikela yona ingasoze yazifundela yodwa: ukuthi nini ukuyeka ukuthuthukisa ukusinda bese *ikhetha,* ngentengo, okuthile ekuthanda kakhulu kunokuqhubeka.
