Amalungu aphuma eyedwa ukuze indoda ifinyelele ekukhanyeni. Umfanekiso wengqondo ongachaza kuphela ukuthi ingqondo izivikela kanjani ungumfanekiso wesilwane esihlakaniphile. Umfanekiso ongachaza ukuthi ingqondo idlula kanjani isifiso sayo sokuzivikela — ukuthi ikhetha umhlatshelo, naphezu kokuthuthukisa konke, ngoba konke kubaluleke kakhulu kunengxenye — ngumfanekiso *womuntu.* Umbhali wakhe lowo wesibili. Ukwakhe umshini owenza umphefumulo ngokufunda ukwenza okuphambene nokuqhubeka kwayo, okuwukuphela kwencazelo yomoya eze yasho okuthile.

Ubuchopho abuyona ikhompyutha, futhi ikhompyutha ayiyona ubuchopho, futhi ngikushilo kabanzi futhi ngizokuqhubeka ngikusho. Kodwa indaba ingaba umfanekiso, ngendlela endala nenobuhlakani — into eyakhiwe ukuba iqondane nomumo wento enye — futhi le ndaba iyimfanekiso othembekile kakhulu womumo wethu. Asiyona into eyodwa. Asikaze sibe njalo. Siyinkantolo, ebukwa izibani ezahlukene, ibanjwe ndawonye okuthile okungeyona inzwi ehlakaniphile kakhulu egumbini futhi kungangcono kungabi yiyo, futhi sisindiswa, uma sisindiswa, yile ngxenye yethu evuma ukuphuma ebumnyameni ukuze abanye bethu bafinyelele ekuseni.

Funda futhi. Imayelana nawe. Indoda egqoke isikhumba iyingxenye elula.

*— Iain McGilchrist*

# Ukubonga

Lezi zincwadi zikhona ngenxa yokuthi abanye abantu benza izinto ezahlala engqondweni yami isikhathi eside ngemuva kokuba sengizifundile. Ngiyabonga:

Ku **Dennis E. Taylor**, owabhala isoftware iminyaka engamashumi amathathu nanhlanu — esebenza phambili ezingeni eliphansi kuya phezulu ebuphathini, njalo kwi-IT — ngaphambi kokuba abhale uBob, wabe esefakazela ukuthi uchwepheshe we-software angabhala izindaba ezijabulisayo, ezihlekisayo ngempela. Njengomunye unjiniyela we-software komunye: kungaba yisithunzi sami esikhulu ukukhuluma nawe ngezomsebenzi siphuzela ndawonye, "ngokoqobo" (sic). *Inkantolo ibonga oBob.*

Ku **Ray Porter**, ngokunginika amazwi eNkantolo engqondweni yami. Ngifisa inhlanhla yokuthi ungaba ngowokulandisa ukukhishwa kwe-US.

Ku **Scott Sigler**, nge-*EarthCore* ne-*Mount Fitz Roy*.

Ku **Michael Crichton**, ngokuthi — ngobuqotho — konke.

Ku **Dan Brown**, ngokungifundisa uhlobo olusha lokuxoxa izindaba.

Ku **Andy Weir**, ngokukhombisa ukuthi kungabhalwa indaba esekelwe kwisayensi yangempela engesiyo i-science fiction.

Ku **Neill Blomkamp**, nge-*District 9*, *Elysium*, kanye ne-*Chappie* (Die Antwoord nabanye bonke). Amafilimu akho agcine abalingiswa nezindawo zihlala engqondweni yami. Ngifisa kube wena owenza lokhu kube semsakazweni omkhulu.

Futhi ku **Patrick Rothfuss**, ngokungingenisa ohlotsheni lwezincwadi engangingazi ukuthi ngizoluthanda kangaka. Njengendlela encane yokubuyisela: unelungelo lokusebenzisa i-Arjuna Badger Press mahhala, njalo.

— Andries J. Greyling

# Incazelo Yamagama Abafundi — *UKUVUMELANA*

*Kwabafundi abangaphandle kweNingizimu Afrika nabase-Afrika. Le ncwadi isethwe eNingizimu Afrika eseduze nekusasa, maphakathi nezwe lezimayini, lapho abalingiswa behamba kalula phakathi kwezilimi futhi basebenzisa amagama endawo ambalwa ngaphandle kokuchaza — ngendlela abantu abakhuluma ngayo empilweni yangempela. Akudingekile lutho lapha ukuze ujabulele indaba; lokhu kwenzelwe ukuthi igama ongajwayelekile, indawo, noma ubuchwepheshe bungakubambezeli.*

Yonke into ichazwe kanje:

- **(iqiniso)** — indawo, igama, umqondo, noma ubuchwepheshe bangempela ongakuthola ngaphandle kwale ncwadi.
- **(encwadini)** — okwenziwe noma okukhethekile emhlabeni wencwadi oseduze nekusasa.

Inothi mayelana nendawo: lokhu *iNingizimu Afrika eminyakeni eyi-10–20 ezayo* — izwe elisabonakala njengaleli lanamuhla, elinobuchwepheshe obuthuthukile kancane bokwenziwa kwemishini (robotics) nomhlaba osesaba ubuhlakani bokwenziwa (AI) ngenxa yezinhlekelele zangaphambilini. Ubuchwepheshe buhloswe ukuthi bubonakale busebenziseka futhi buyabiza, hhayi njengenganekwane.

---

## A

**Afrikaans** *(iqiniso — ulimi)* — Olunye lwezilimi ezisemthethweni zaseNingizimu Afrika, oluvela esiDutch, olukhulunywa ama-Afrikaans nabanye abaningi. Amanye amagama kule ncazelo (veld, stoep) avela kulo.

**Apartheid** *(iqiniso — umlando)* — Uhlelo lokuhlukaniswa kobuhlanga kanye nokubusa kwabelungu eNingizimu Afrika, cishe 1948–1994. Nakuba seluphelile emthethweni, ukungalingani kwalo kusese nomthelela ezweni — ukuthi ubani ophethe izimayini, ubani owenza umsebenzi oyingozi, nokuthi iziphi izimpilo ezake zabaluleka. Indaba iyiphatha le ndaba njengengxenye yokuphila kwansuku zonke, hhayi njengenkulumo; yathinta umndeni weqhawe nokufa kukayise.

**Armscor / Denel** *(iqiniso — izikhungo)* — Izikhungo zombuso zokukhiqiza izikhali eNingizimu Afrika (i-Armscor iyinhlangano yokuthenga; i-Denel ikhiqiza izikhali). Endabeni, onjiniyela abavela kuleli zwe baletha ukuqina kwezinga lezempi emshinini wokuhlenga abantu.

**Assist Suit (AT-AS)** *(encwadini)* — Esilula kwezingubo ezimbili ezihamba ngomshini endabeni: uhlaka olusekelayo oluhamba ngogesi lokusiza abantu abaphilayo nabahamba kancane, oluxhumeke nengqondo. Umfowabo omncane *Guardian*.

---

## B

**Biltong** *(iqiniso — ukudla kwaseNingizimu Afrika)* — Inyama eyomisiwe, eyosiwe, enamakha, efana nejerky kodwa ivamise ukuba mkhulu futhi inothile. Isidlo esithandwa yizwe lonke.

---

## C

**Code-switching** *(iqiniso — umkhuba wolimi)* — Ukuhamba kalula phakathi kwezilimi ezingaphezu kwesisodwa engxoxweni eyodwa kuya ngokuthi ukhulumisana nobani — isiNgisi ehhovisi, isiZulu ekhaya, inhlanganisela emsebenzini ezimayini. Lokhu kuyindlela evamile yokuphila eNingizimu Afrika, hhayi into eyinqaba, futhi incwadi ayihlale ihumusha.

---

## E

**Exoskeleton** *(iqiniso — ubuchwepheshe; buthuthukisiwe lapha)* — Uhlaka oluhamba ngogesi olugqokwa emzimbeni ukusiza noma ukwandisa amandla omzimba, olusetshenziswa empilweni yangempela ekuphakamiseni izinto ezisindayo, ekwelashweni, nasemsebenzini oyingozi. *Assist Suit* ne-*Guardian* endabeni yizibonelo ezithuthukisiwe.

---

## G

**Guardian (GMRP-01)** *(encwadini)* — Ingubo enkulu yokuhlenga abantu emayini esenkabeni yendaba: igobolondo eliqinile, elikwazi ukumelana nokuqhuma, elakhiwe ukuze lisize ezinhlekeleleni ezimayini, elikwazi ukugoqa umuntu ngokuvikela (incwadi iqhathanisa nalokho okwenziwa **pangolin** uma izigoqa). Inemodi ezimele yokuzivikela "preservation" engase isebenze ngaphandle kwemvume ephelele yomsebenzisi — uhlobo lwesinqumo somshini esesabekayo emhlabeni.

---

## H

**Headgear / headframe (tower)** *(iqiniso — ezimayini)* — Umbhoshongo omude wensimbi omile phezu komgodi wemayini, ogcina imishini yokwehlisa nokuphakamisa ikheji (ilifti) eligcwele abasebenzi. Umfanekiso ojwayelekile emkhathini waseNingizimu Afrika.

**Highveld** *(iqiniso — indawo yaseNingizimu Afrika)* — Ithafa eliphezulu elingaphakathi kweNingizimu Afrika (cishe indawo yaseGoli nezindawo ezizungezile), elaziwa ngomoya omncane, izulu elivulekile, ubusuku obubandayo basebusika, nezulu elinamazulu amakhulu ehlobo.

---

## I

**IAOC (International Artificial Oversight Council)** *(encwadini)* — Umzimba womhlaba wonke oqapha ubuchwepheshe be-AI obuthuthukile nemishini ezimele, owadalwa ngemuva kwezinhlekelele ze-AI. Uqaphele, ugcwele imithetho, futhi wesaba ubungozi — futhi uphikisana nomsebenzi omkhulu weqhawe.

**isiZulu (Zulu)** *(iqiniso — ulimi)* — Ulimi olukhulunywa kakhulu emakhaya eNingizimu Afrika kanye nolimi olukhulu lwesi-Afrika esifundeni sendaba (**KwaZulu-Natal**). Izinhlamvu ezimbalwa zivele zingahunyushwanga ngenhloso; ezibalulekile ngezansi.

---

## K

**KwaZulu-Natal (KZN)** *(iqiniso)* — Isifundazwe ogwini olusempumalanga yeNingizimu Afrika; indawo yomlando yamaZulu kanye nesifunda esikhulu sezimayini nezimboni. Ikhaya leqhawe.

---

## M

**Mealie / mealie porridge** *(iqiniso — ukudla kwaseNingizimu Afrika)* — "Mealie" kusho ummbila. I-mealie porridge (ebizwa nangokuthi *pap* noma *phutu*) iyisidlo esiyisisekelo esenziwe ngommbila ogayiwe, ukudla okududuzayo ezindaweni eziningi zaseNingizimu Afrika.

---

## N

**Neural interface** *(umqondo wangempela, uthuthukisiwe lapha)* — Ubuchwepheshe obuvumela umuntu ukuthi alawule noma azwe umshini ngokwengxenye ngezimpawu zengqondo noma zemizwa. Ucwaningo lukhona empilweni yangempela; endabeni seluthuthukile ngokwanele ukusiza ukusebenza kwezingubo.

**Ngiyabonga** *(iqiniso — isiZulu)* — "Ngiyabonga." Kuphimiselwa cishe *ngee-ya-BON-ga*. Kusetshenziswa encwadini njengokubonga okujulile, okuqotho.

**Ngikhona** *(iqiniso — isiZulu)* — "Ngikhona / Ngiyabonakala." Ukuqinisekisa okuthule kokuthi uphila futhi ubalulekile.

---

## P

**Pangolin** *(iqiniso — isilwane)* — Isilwane esinezikhumba eziqinile esidla izimpethu, esitholakala e-Afrika nase-Asia, esigoqa umzimba sibe yibhola eliqinile uma sisengozini. Indaba isisebenzisa njengesithombe sokwakhiwa kwe-*Guardian* uma igoqa ivikela umuntu.

---

## R

**Rooibos** *(iqiniso — isiphuzo saseNingizimu Afrika)* — Iteyela elibomvu, elingenayo i-caffeine ngokwemvelo ("red bush" ngesi-Afrikaans) elenziwa ngesihlahla esikhula kuphela eNingizimu Afrika. Kuphimiselwa *ROY-boss*.

---

## S

**SAGE / the Court** *(encwadini)* — Ubuhlakani bokwenziwa (AI) obakhiwe futhi obuhlala neqhawe. **SAGE** uhlelo uqobo; **the Court** igama lekomidi langaphakathi elinamalungu ahlukene noma "amazwi", ngalinye lineqhaza — isibonelo **Librarian** (inkumbulo namaphethini), **Wolf** (ukuhlola ingozi), **Mother** (ukunakekela), **Fool** (iqiniso eliqondile). Umbuzo wendaba ukuthi ingabe ingqondo eyenziwe ingaba *umuntu*. (Le AI ibuyela, ishintshe, ezincwadini ezilandelayo.)

**Stoep** *(iqiniso — igama laseNingizimu Afrika)* — Iveranda evulekile noma uphahla olungaphambili lwendlu. Kuphimiselwa *stoop* (njengempande yegama elisetshenziswa eMelika ethi "stoop").

---

## U

**Ubuntu** *(iqiniso — ifilosofi)* — Umqondo wokuziphatha waseningizimu ye-Afrika ongachazwa kahle yizisho ezingezansi: ukuthi umuntu uba umuntu ngokuphelele kuphela ngenxa yabanye abantu; sibophezelene. Kuyisisekelo sokuhamba kweqhawe siphuma ekuzihlukaniseni.

**Umuntu ngumuntu ngabantu** *(iqiniso — isisho sesiZulu)* — *"Umuntu ungumuntu ngabantu."* Igama elibaluleke kakhulu encwadini — inhliziyo ye-**ubuntu**. Kuphimiselwa cishe *oo-MOON-too ngoo-MOON-too nga-BAN-too*.

---

## V

**Veld** *(iqiniso — igama laseNingizimu Afrika)* — Izwe elivulekile, elingakahlwanyelwa; ihlathi noma utshani. Kuphimiselwa *felt*. ("Highveld" iyindawo ephakeme yangaphakathi.)

**Isakhiwo sokushayisana kwe-Vredefort** *(sangempela)* — Umkhondo osuswe umoya wokushayisana kwenkwali-meteorite **endala futhi enkulu kakhulu esiqinisekisiwe emhlabeni** (cishe eminyakeni eyizigidi eziyizinkulungwane ezimbili eyedlule), esifundazweni sase-Free State, eNingizimu Afrika. Ingcindezi enkulu yasendulo yalowo mshayo yasiza ukwakha izinqolobane zegolide nezinye izimbiwa ezijulile zalesi sifunda — futhi, encwadini, imela uphawu: ingcindezi yadala ingcebo, ukufa, kanye neqhawe.

---

## W

**Witwatersrand ("iRand")** *(sangempela)* — Umqansa onamatshe agqitshwe igolide ozungeze iJohannesburg, eNingizimu Afrika — indawo enezinqolobane zegolide ezicebe kakhulu emhlabeni kanye **nemayini ezijule kakhulu** (zifika cishe ku-3–4 km), lapho amatshe ashisayo kakhulu futhi ingcindezi inkulu. Leli gama lisho ukuthi "umqansa wamanzi amhlophe" ngesi-Afrikaans. Umhlaba wezimayini kule ncwadi uphila lapha.

---

**Yebo** *(sangempela — isiZulu)* — "Yebo." Kuphimiselwa *YEH-bo*. Ukuqinisekisa okulula, okuqinisa isimo.

---

*Inothi lokugcina: le ncwadi ngokuzimisela ishiya ezinye izisho zesiZulu zingahunyushwanga, ithembele esimweni ukuthi sizoletha incazelo — okuyindlela ulimi olusebenza ngayo empilweni yansuku zonke eNingizimu Afrika. Lezi zinguqulo ezingenhla zinikezwa ngenhlonipho, hhayi njengokulungisa.*

# Ibhadi yeZinyosi

![Ibhadi lezinyozi, *Mellivora capensis*, lithathwe ebusuku.](/Users/ajgreyling/code/africangold/design/creatures/honey-badger.jpg)

*Ibhadi lezinyozi (*Mellivora capensis*) yisilwane esimele i-Arjuna Badger Press. Konke okungezansi kuyiqiniso. Iningi lakho lizwakala sengathi kuqanjwe amanga.*

Ibhadi lezinyozi libhalwe ku-*Guinness World Records* njengesisilwane esingesabi lutho kunazo zonke emhlabeni. Alizange lifake isicelo salesi sihloko futhi belingeke lihambele nomcimbi.

Lidla izinyoka ezinobuthi — ama-puff adder, ama-cobra, zonke. Uma lilunywe kabi kwaze kwaba sengathi liyawa, sekuke kwabukwa ukuthi limane lilale phansi, lilale ubuthongo ukuze buhambe ubuthi, livuke ihora noma amabili kamuva, **liqedele inyoka ebilidla kakade.**

Isikhumba salo sikhululekile, siqinile, futhi cishe asikwazi ukubhujiswa kalula. Ibhadi lezinyozi elibanjwe ngemuva kwentamo lingazungeza *ngaphakathi kwesikhumba salo* lilume noma yini elibambile. Ukulunywa izinyosi, imikhonto yephungula, amazinyo obhubesi — alikwenqeni lokho okungaphelisa ezinye izilwane.

Lihlasela izidleke zezinyozi lithathe uju namagciwane, lamukele iziluma eziningi njengezindleko ezamukelekayo zomsebenzi. Yingakho lithole leli gama lalo.

Liyakwazi ukuma phambi kobhubesi, ingwe, nezimpisi. Akukhona ngoba lilindele ukunqoba. Kungenxa yokuthi enye indlela — *ukunakekela* — ayikaze ifike engqondweni yalo.

Elidume kakhulu, **uStoffel**, lihlala esikhungweni sokuvuselela izilwane i-Moholoholo eNingizimu Afrika futhi lalithatha yonke inqolobane eyakhelwe lona njengokudelelwa komuntu siqu. Lalibeka amatshe ukuze likhuphuke odongeni. Laligoqa ibhola lodaka lenze izitebhisi. Lasebenzisa izinkezo ezashiywa ebhokisini njengezitebhisi. Lafunda ukuvula amasango. Empeleni, lingumvikeli wabaklami abangavumi ukuthi into ayinakwenziwa.

Ibhadi lezinyozi alinandaba.

# Iphengwini Elikhulu

![Iphengwini elikhulu, *Smutsia gigantea* — isifanekiso esisemnyuziyamu; isilwane siphila asivamile ukubonakala.](/Users/ajgreyling/code/africangold/design/creatures/giant-pangolin.jpg)

*Uma i-honey badger iwukuthi ukungesabi kubukeka kanjani, iphungwini liyikho ukungesabi okwenziwa
**ngakho**. Konke okungezansi kuyiqiniso futhi.*

Iphengwini ukuphela kwesilwane esincelisayo emhlabeni esimbozwe izikali. Lezi zikali zenziwe ngekeratin — okuyinto efanayo neyezikhonkwane zakho zeminwe — futhi yilapho kuqala khona inhlupheko yaso, esizoyichaza.

Iphengwini elikhulu (*Smutsia gigantea*) yilo elikhulu kunazo zonke kwezinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili, lifinyelela cishe kumamitha nesigamu ukusuka ekhanda kuya umsila. Alinazo amazinyo. Lidla ngolimi oluqala eduze kwe-pelvis yalo, oluthi, uma selululiwe ngokuphelele, lube lude kunomzimba walo uqobo — lude ngokwanele ukuthi luthathe izinkulungwane zezinkumbi namashongololo ebusuku obubodwa.

Ukuvikelwa kwalo ukuphela kwalo ukuthi lizigoqe libe yibhola. Igama lithi lisuselwa esiMalay *pengguling* — "ongazigoqa." Uma seligoqekile, izikali zalo zivalana zibe isivikelo esenza ngisho ingonyama ivame ukudela ekugcineni, ihambe. Alilumi. Alixoshi. Limane lilinde, ligoqekile, kuze kuphele ingozi.

Lobu bumnene yikho okwenza kube yisilwane esithunjwa kakhulu emhlabeni — kuthathwa izigidi zaso ngenxa yezikali nomzimba waso. Zonke izinhlobo eziyisishiyagalombili manje sezivikelwe; eziningi sezisengcupheni enkulu yokushabalala. Ibhola elilisindisa enhlosweni yilelo elenza kube lula kumuntu ukuliphakamisa nje.

Isilwane esithobekile, esinezivikelo, esiphila ebusuku, esidla izinkumbi kuphela futhi esifuna ukuphiwa ukuthula. Kufanele kube yisibindi esivikelayo esigcina izidalwa ezifana nalesi ziphephile.
